### 35.1 Settlement headings, button art and equipment slots (#745)

- **Seen:** @YuriRestia's #745 reports translated outcome headings overlapping the official layout, buttons mixing
  official sprites with CSS decoration, and equipped items overflowing their two slots. The dev mock had no gear.
- **Official / decision:** retain @isomerpoi's #698 screen and the maintainer-approved official look (§34.34).
  The official client's `autochessouter/settlegame/auto_chess_settle_game_state` sprites are indexed in
  `docs/research/07-assets.json` → `autochessUi.settle`: `btn_next_bg`, `btn_bg_02`, `operator_frame` and
  `operator_trap_bg` (asset pipeline: `docs/ASSETS.md`). No match, reward or equipment rule changes.
- **Cause:** absolutely positioned headings had no width budget; button CSS always painted behind the sprite;
  percentage-sized images could determine the flex/grid slots' intrinsic minimum sizes, with the screen's broad
  `max-width: none` image rule removing the normal cap.
- **Now:** both headings scale uniformly inside fixed boxes, remeasured when language, fonts, viewport or text size
  changes. The three buttons render CSS decoration through the existing Sprite missing/failed-image fallback only;
  keyboard focus still has a visible ring. Equipment slots have zero minimum sizes and bounded grid tracks, with
  contained images capped to the slot. `game-mock.html?phase=RESULT&variant=equipped` supplies two/one/no-item rows.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the browser's title budgets (personal 7.8 × 1.04rem, alliance 2.8 × 0.9rem) and shrink-to-fit behavior
  are translation/accessibility accommodations, not official client measurements. Keep #698's short-label size,
  screen positions, two-slot layout and fallback artwork. This change adds no gameplay assumptions.
- **Tests:** `test/ui/settle.test.js` adds title fitting, mutually exclusive button decoration and equipped-card
  regressions. `test/ui/settle.e2e.test.js` checks both pages in zh-CN/en/ja/ko/zh-TW, 1280×720 and 844×390, with
  loaded/failed art and all four text sizes; `SP_SETTLE_SHOTS=<dir>` writes personal screenshots
  at that path and alliance screenshots under `team/`. The test loads the real language index before switching:
  the dev mock skips `main.js`'s `initLang()`, so otherwise only Chinese is registered and unknown codes normalize
  to Chinese without fetching a pack. Language warnings are included in failures. All 20 cases passed three runs in a row before the merge.
  Golden results must remain unchanged.

### 35.2 Minimum-Python parsing guard for the local extractor (#744)

- **Seen / cause:** `tools/local-extract/fx.py:2987` uses nested same-quote f-string syntax that Python before 3.12
  cannot parse; the Node checks do not compile the extractor. @zasx77771's #744 fixes that line separately.
- **Official / contract:** this is tooling, with no official gameplay rule. `tools/setup.mjs` → `findPython()` accepts
  Python 3.8+. The [setup-python action documentation](https://github.com/actions/setup-python#basic-usage) names
  major v7; checkout stays on v7 like `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.
- **Now:** the separate `local-extract-python.yml` workflow runs `python -m compileall -q -f tools/local-extract` on
  Python 3.8 for pushes, pull requests and manual runs, with read-only contents permission and a five-minute timeout.
  It needs no extractor dependencies or official files. `ci.yml` and its exact `test/ci.test.js` contract are unchanged.
- **[ASSUMED]:** five minutes is a CI resource budget, not a game rule. The guard checks syntax, not extractor runtime
  compatibility. It intentionally remains red until #744 merges; `fx.py` is not edited by this change.
- **Tests:** `test/local-extract-python-ci.test.js` ties the interpreter floor to setup, checks triggers/permissions,
  action versions and the compile command; `test/ci.test.js` retains the local CI contract. Locally, Python 3.14.3
  compiles the tree; Python 3.9.6 fails at the expected `fx.py:2987` f-string. Python 3.8 is not installed here.

### 35.3 Optional selected-unit bond timers (#746)

- **Seen:** @Realzhazha's #746 asks to see the remaining duration of Siracusa, Sargon and similar timed effects.
- **Official / decision:** local `activity_table.json` → `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess.bondInfoDict` →
  `sargonShip.desc` explicitly gives each stack an independent duration, with equal-duration ATK at six members.
  `siracusaShip.desc` separates deployment ASPD, equal-duration stealth at six members, and the ten seconds after
  stealth for the attack proc. `server/sim/content/bonds/core.js` implements those effects with `timeLeft` and
  `mem.siraStealthEnd`; the post-stealth length comes from `act2autochess_bond_eff_siracusa.end_duration` in the
  generated bond blackboard, the same data used by the proc. The maintainer approved this optional convenience
  on 2026-10-18 (round49 triage D3 / #746), OFF by default, following the ammo/energy count switch (§34.3).
- **Cause:** the simulation kept these times but neither the render snapshots nor the selected-unit card exposed
  them. The existing live-stat RPC alone would omit server-run fields.
- **Now:** 设置 adds「盟约效果剩余时间」beside the ammo/energy switch, persisted as boolean `bondTimers` in
  `sp.pref.settings`. The selected card shows Sargon's active stack count and next stack expiry; Siracusa has separate
  deployment ASPD, deployment stealth and post-stealth proc-window rows. Early stealth removal starts only the
  post-stealth row; ASPD may continue independently. Expired/removed effects and dead/undeployed/hidden units have
  no rows. `shared/bondTimers.js` declares the four effect identities, sources and translated labels; another mapped
  effect can be added by declaration. Only these two bonds are wired.
  Optional snapshot `bondTimers` rows carry remaining simulation seconds. Local, Worker and server fields use the
  same data; pause freezes it and speed changes simulation progress. Reconnect needs no event history, spectators
  use the watched field, field re-entry clears old data, and absent rows clear the card. Prep previews show none.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the official game has no such numeric HUD. The convenience readout, labels, stack-count/next-expiry
  summary, rounding upward to 0.1 seconds and 250 ms card refresh are presentation choices, not official rules.
  The card holds the latest received values between snapshots, including network stalls; it never extrapolates with
  wall-clock time. A missing/junk preference stays off; only boolean `true` enables it. This adds no gameplay rule.
- **Tests:** `test/content/bond-timers.test.js` covers independent expiry, layers, cap, early removal, natural expiry,
  death, redeployment and read-only snapshots; `test/match/bond-timers.test.js` covers real Worker parity, 1×/2×,
  pause, watched fields and fresh catch-up; `test/ui/bond-timers.test.js` covers defaults, filtering and translations.
  `test/ui/bond-timers.e2e.test.js` is `SP_E2E=1` gated: settings persistence, live card updates, missing/dead rows,
  foreign-field isolation, five languages and 1280×720 / 844×390 at XL text; both cases passed before the merge.
  Golden scenarios are unchanged: `tools/golden.mjs`'s existing canonical snapshot explicitly selects gameplay
  fields and excludes this optional display metadata; neither the tool nor any golden JSON was edited.

### 35.4 Picture-only outfits (B6)

- **Seen:** the 0.3.1 known issue excluded outfits with avatars / portraits but no Spine, including Forge skin-catalogue
  outfits already imported into the skins layer. Cards and panels also continued reading the default pictures.
- **Official / decision:** the maintainer chose option A on 2026-10-18: those outfits become wearable, labelled
  「仅更换头像与立绘」, with the default battle model and independent missing-picture fallbacks. The official
  [skin_table.json](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Kengxxiao/ArknightsGameData/master/zh_CN/gamedata/excel/skin_table.json)
  `charSkins` separates `skinId`, `charId`, `avatarId`, `portraitId`, `illustId` and `battleSkin` (for example
  `char_285_medic2#1`). Local `activity_table.activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess.charShopChessDatas`
  `chess_char_3_01_a` still maps `char_103_angel` to `backupCharId: char_603_csnipe`. Missing plugin models are a
  remake presentation policy, not an official gameplay rule.
- **Cause:** `availableSkins().playable` required `skinHasModel`, and the picker filtered out everything else.
  DOM card URL helpers never received an owner's selection; result and foreign-detail paths dropped the owner.
- **Now:** a model or pictures enables an outfit; the global picker gate stays global. `OperatorImage` and the pure
  URL helpers apply each owner's public choice to cards, details, bond members and result pictures; the local picker
  uses its own saved choices. Stand-ins / DIY resolve their effective body first. Open images follow choice, visibility
  and manifest changes; public clears beat stale unit metadata. The combat meter retains effective-body picture
  metadata; the DOM fallback field accepts the same seat / choice updates. The waiting room's 名片干员 seat cards and the
  strategy draft's name cards follow the same rule (the maintainer's decision 4 of 2026-10-21; `ui/seatArt.js
  seatCardSkin`): the lobby publishes each seat's map as `room.state.seats[].skins`. Spine resolution retains default Front / Back for picture-only skins and skin models for model
  outfits. No importer, simulation or generated-data changes; the one wire addition is `room.state.seats[].skins` (decision 4). Normative updates: SKINS.md, PLUGINS.md §9,
  design/client.md §9, DESIGN index and PLAYING.md; the previous model-only limitation is removed.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the existing E2 and illustration fields count as pictures, while metadata alone does not. An unfinished Spine
  map without Front uses both default views. E2-only art replaces only elite images in play. Picture fields keep the existing normal / elite fallback order; portrait
  may use the outfit's illustration, avatars never do. Card updates follow the renderer's latest public map (including
  clears), then unit metadata if the owner is unavailable; they never borrow the viewer's choice for another seat.
  Unowned catalogue / studio comparison / historical statistics images retain defaults; the 名片干员 picker is one's own
  picker (one's own outfits), and the room carries a seat's whole public map, not one skinId per card. These choices do not alter gameplay.
- **Tests:** `test/ui/picture-skins.test.js` covers picker eligibility, two owners / spectators / visibility / clears,
  individual image fallbacks, normal / elite art, default Front / Back and model outfits, effective stand-in / DIY
  bodies and owner propagation through cards / details / results. `test/match/combat-metrics.test.js` adds
  effective-body / elite picture metadata coverage. Updated picker and stand-in UI tests retain prior behavior. `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/picture-skins.e2e.test.js` mounts the production picker, shop card and avatar
  with original synthetic PNGs and checks note layout (desktop / landscape phone), selection, persistence, distinct
  seats, public changes / clears, visibility off/on and plugin removal.
  The browser check passed before the merge. Golden scenarios unchanged.

### 35.5 Re-entrant buff removal

- **Seen:** round49's API reproduction staggers two Sargon stacks, lets the older expire, then explicitly removes
  the last stack; the call throws after clearing its ATK aggregate. The same happens after overflowing the 25-stack
  cap. An extension clearing all six members stops after the first; no current vanilla caller is known to reach it.
- **Official / contract:** local `activity_table.json` → `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess.bondInfoDict.sargonShip`
  specifies independent stack durations and equal-duration ATK for six different members.
  `effectBuffInfoDataDict.bondeffect_sargon` → `act1autochess_bond_eff_sargon` gives ASPD +12, ATK +12%, duration
  `5 + 0.22L` seconds and cap 25, disabled ATK in `band_narant`. These gameplay rules remain unchanged.
  The requested fix defines the remake's helper traversal; official tables do not specify JavaScript callback order.
- **Cause:** `removeBuff` walks the mutable list backwards by index. Sargon's synchronous `onRemove` calls `syncAtk`,
  which removes the ATK aggregate when the final stack disappears. After `[S1, A, S2]` becomes `[A, S2]` by expiry,
  removing S2 also removes A; the outer loop then reads `undefined.key` through its stale index.
- **Now:** fix the matching objects in reverse order before callbacks, then find each in the current list by identity.
  Only present objects pass through `_removeBuffAt` and count toward this call's return value; nested removals have
  their own counts and skipped objects receive no second callback. The existing path preserves intermediate stack
  counts, dirty stats, flags, status events and `onRemove` versus `onExpire`. A clearing hook can finish all six units
  and continue; its `once` handling remains intact. Normative additions: design/engine.md §5.3, SIM.md §3 and DESIGN index.
- **[ASSUMED]:** fixing this call's candidate set, retaining reverse callback order, skipping already removed objects
  and leaving new same-key instances for later calls are extension API policies, not additional official game rules.
  Explicit clearing in these tests models a plugin / kit call, not a demonstrated vanilla gameplay trigger.
- **Tests:** `test/content/sargon-remove-buff.test.js` covers the staggered two-stack / ATK case and a six-member
  extension batch at the 25-stack cap, including unique reverse callbacks, per-removal ATK sync and continuation.
  `test/sim/remove-buff.test.js` covers nested matching / unrelated removals, actual counts, flags, status events,
  callback-added same-key instances and absent-object / null no-ops. All four fail before the fix.
  Golden scenarios must remain unchanged; neither the golden tool nor its JSON files are modified.

### 35.6 Strategy-draft match information (#484, PR #561)

This is a post-release addition and is not included in published 0.3.1.

PR #561 by @hesperine completes the remaining part of GitHub #484: the banned-member tooltip was already added
by PR #494 in 0.2.5, but the strategy draft could not revisit the briefing's special enemies, leader and battlefield.

The maintainer accepted this as a convenience in the [initial review](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/561#issuecomment-6144292092)
and confirmed the existing modal interaction on 2026-10-16
([follow-up](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/561#issuecomment-6198380140)). The official client has
no such button; it only repeats information already announced in the briefing, without changing the game rules.

Now the entry under the strategy order reads 本局信息. The shared `ui/matchEnemies.js` `MatchEnemies` blocks show
the announced leader's icon, name, abilities and final-assault round, the battlefield, and each special enemy type's
icon, name and description. They read the same existing `m.public` fields (`bossId`, `stageId`, `factions`) and
localized lookups as the briefing. The existing `ui/matchInfo.js` blocks and banned-member hover tooltips are reused.
With #828 (PR #643's pre-game flow) the entry moved into the step header beside the difficulty, and `MatchEnemies`
took the event's 本局遭遇敌方 layout (portrait over 敌方领袖, the text with the battlefield, the special enemies as round
icons with name and description). The briefing renders it in its enemy section and the dialog stacks the same markup
into its column (`css/screens/briefing.css` scoped under `.brief`, `css/screens/draft.css` `.minfo-dlg`), so the
two screens still share one copy.

The dialog stays modal and read-only while choosing or waiting for a teammate: close, Escape or the backdrop closes
it; clicking the backdrop over the confirmation button closes only the dialog and does not pick a strategy. Closing
preserves the highlighted strategy, the countdown keeps running, and a turn change closes the dialog automatically.
On smaller windows and landscape phones its body scrolls while the close button and turn status remain visible.
There is no layout change to expose or operate the background controls while the dialog is open, as the maintainer
confirmed. No new [ASSUMED] rule, data, protocol or simulation change. Normative: [client.md §10](../design/client.md)
and [PLAYING.md](../PLAYING.md).

Tests: `test/ui/match-info.test.js` (the shared information and localized/fallback enemy data),
`test/ui/match-info.e2e.test.js` (real server and Chrome: briefing/dialog consistency, banned-member hover,
close/escape/backdrop without a selection, selected strategy preserved, live countdown and turn-change closing;
1000, 1001, 1024, 1120 and 1280 px windows with every information block reachable by scrolling and background
controls usable after closing; 1920×1080 desktop and 844×390 / 756×366 landscape phones).
Golden: unchanged (client information only).

### 35.7 AI strategy extension interface (#673, @HankDUMPLINGZhong)

- **Seen:** alternative AI policies previously required changing the built-in bot. PR #673 by
  **@HankDUMPLINGZhong**, cherry-picked as `4908a73b`, provides the strategy framework.
- **Official / decision:** maintainer review of #673 permits the implementation extension; no official gameplay
  rule or default policy changes.
- **Cause:** match decisions and tooling were coupled to the original functions.
- **Now:** per-match validated strategy definitions, inherited hooks, registry selection, startup validation,
  sliced prep/rehearsal and journal identity. The original implementation remains `legacy`.
- **[ASSUMED]:** this server implementation contract and benchmark experiment selection are engineering policy,
  not an official alternate AI rule. The original #673 description and validation follow.

Source: maintainer review of PR #673. Normative contract: DESIGN §6.6; implementation reference:
[META §1.6](../META.md#16-selectable-ai-implementations) and [the strategy guide](../BOT_STRATEGIES.md).

Match resolves a trusted server-side AI definition per match: an explicit `botStrategy` option (ID or definition)
overrides `SP_BOT_STRATEGY`. An unset or empty selection uses the original bot, `legacy`, the only built-in at #673.
`botStrategyContract.js` provides frozen definitions that inherit unspecified hooks and pure registries that
reject duplicate IDs. Implementations import the contract, separately from the registry, to avoid import cycles.
`startServer()` validates the environment selection before loading data or listening, reporting the variable,
rejected value, available IDs and how to restore the default when the ID is unknown.

Band/card/private-bounty picks and both sliced prep stages use the selected object. Match still owns cancellation,
host-delegated rehearsal, cleanup and Ready, including exceptions thrown before an iterator exists. Prep generator
steps are counted by `testSlice`, and local rehearsal jobs receive the same slice controller. Replays use the
journalled step budget instead of wall-clock timing, so inputs between slices keep their original order.
The input journal records the resolved `botStrategy` ID; replay explicitly uses it, with `legacy` for older records
that omit it, regardless of `SP_BOT_STRATEGY` on the replay host. An unavailable recorded ID fails explicitly.

Matchrun and botbench select registered IDs; benchmark records and bounty calibration use the selected bot.
Solo `--band` forces only the opening picker, preserving `gd.bandIds()` / `gd.bandBondIds()` for later effects and
skipping the original picker's RNG draw. The earlier override replaced both methods for the entire match;
these are different experiments and require fresh comparison runs.

No alternate policy, new weights, extra legacy RNG draws or priority AI seats ship with this interface. Band
selection keeps the existing draft order, including the room's existing AI-last option. General and specialist
policies are future, separate contributions. The original `bot.js` policy and legacy Golden results stay unchanged.

- **Tests:** real HTTP/WebSocket room startup with unset/empty/explicit legacy selections, inherited hooks,
seeded choices/RNG, match isolation, private drafts, sliced prep/rehearsal replay and failure cleanup. Recorded and
old journals are replayed after changing the environment, checking strategy identity, state and RNG.

### 35.8 External AI strategy (maintainer request 2026-10-19)

- **Seen:** #673's synchronous pickers and prep generators cannot exchange decisions with a host program or
  third-party service; calling network IO there would block or race phase progression.
- **Official / decision:** binding maintainer decision **2026-10-17**, requested **2026-10-19**: an external AI
  API, “observation → intents, validated like a human, timeout → built-in bot”, as a #673 strategy. This is a
  remake extension; existing official economy, placement, drafts and phase rules remain in DESIGN §6 / META.
- **Cause:** `Match.handle` authorizes human seats, while the original bot mutates through trusted hooks; the
  test journal previously recorded only the selected implementation and scheduler slices.
- **Now:** registered opt-in `external`, asynchronous band/card/prep/offer decisions, exact public and own-private
  wire views, incremental human intents, shared semantic validation and human schema/error codes. HTTP(S) POST
  accepts no redirects, bounds replies and time; stdio multiplexes one persistent child per match, kills it on
  failure and restarts on demand after backoff. Accepted actions survive fallback; legacy completes the decision
  with existing sliced prep/rehearsal. Stale replies cannot apply across turns/phases or manual takeover.
  Journals store external replies/failures and non-secret limits; replay does not contact the AI. Default
  `legacy`, #673 authorship, human seat authorization and normal golden results stay unchanged.
- **[ASSUMED]:** `sp-ai/1` envelope and gameplay-action allowlist; 1000 ms total decision/request budget, at most
  half the remaining phase time; 64 intents per decision, 64 KiB replies, 3 consecutive failed decisions before
  match-long legacy for that seat, 1000 ms restart backoff; configurable bounds and seat selection. Stdio has
  no shell, discards stderr and uses SIGKILL for cleanup; examples choose simple public-view actions. These
  are engineering choices, fully specified in [External AI API](../EXTERNAL_AI.md), with a
  [Chinese server-owner guide](../EXTERNAL_AI_ZH.md). No new gameplay assumption.
- **Tests:** `test/match/external-ai.test.js`, `test/match/external-transports.test.js`, startup/strategy tests,
  related match, test-mode and documentation suites; envelope bounds, view equality/isolation, human rejection,
  both transports/examples, all failure paths, cutoff, stale replies, personal/plugin choices, offline replay
  and full real-simulation bot matches with one external seat and a legacy peer. Golden unchanged; no UI msgids.

### 35.9 External AI advisor and knowledge

- **Seen:** §35.8 forwards raw wire views and requests low-level intent batches; language models repeatedly
  spend tokens on purchases and tile moves without the legacy bot's procurement/rehearsal knowledge.
- **Official / decision:** binding maintainer approval **2026-10-19**: opt-in advisor mode, versioned knowledge,
  readable observations, real-IO benchmarks and a language-model example; `legacy` remains the default and
  golden results stay unchanged. This is a remake extension, not an official AI protocol. Existing rules come
  from `activity_table.json` (`autoChessData`, `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess` shop/band/bond/chess/item/
  boss records), character/skill/enemy tables and PLAYING §1–§7 / DATA. `aceffect_band_34` gives 坎诺特 capital 5,
  interest 1, max 1; no universal interest or EXP. Copy-weighted shop odds keep their existing pool.js evidence;
  no fixed odds or hidden live-stock disclosure is invented.
- **Cause:** the #673 handoff had no round-level preferences or public static primer; virtual benchmark clocks
  advanced through decision deadlines before real HTTP/stdio responses could arrive.
- **Now:** owner-selected `plan` beside default `direct`; one plan per seat/prep round plus draft/card/offer
  decisions. Bounded buy/keep/sell/bond/economy/item/placement preferences weight legacy scores, then its sliced
  procurement/rehearsal finishes Ready. Empty/unusable plans use legacy; malformed replies clear preferences.
  A shared acknowledged hello carries a language-aware SHA-256 knowledge pack from current data/docs, room rules
  and plugin offers; restarted stdio children receive it again. Raw views remain, with a named seat digest,
  public scouted boards and observation-local short IDs that use human validation. Journals replay offline.
  Botbench/matchrun wait for actual IO and report survival, final LP, damage, leader damage, wins, calls, reported
  tokens, latency and fallback counts by strategy. A self-authored JSON-plan prompt and environment-only chat
  adapter use cached knowledge prefixes and compact digests; no real API is contacted by tests.
- **[ASSUMED]:** plan bounds and soft weights, conservative shortcuts (effect-dependent actions/swaps retain raw
  intents), original strategy prompt, language fallback, cache limits, handshake and benchmark accounting are
  engineering policy, specified in [EXTERNAL_AI](../EXTERNAL_AI.md) and its [Chinese guide](../EXTERNAL_AI_ZH.md).
  Provider prefix-cache hits are not guaranteed; absent token usage means unknown. No new gameplay assumption.
- **Tests:** `external-advisor`, `external-knowledge`, `external-llm`, existing external transport/decision suites,
  bot/match/strategy/startup/replay and documentation checks. Covers plan bounds/weights/expiry, impossible
  targets and human validation, plugin/language/hash changes, observation isolation, short IDs, one hello,
  full matches via both transports, real-IO tools and the example adapter against a loopback mock API. Golden
  unchanged; no UI msgids or browser assets added.

### 35.10 Wild Mane S2 rear push (#782)

- **Seen:** #782 reports 野鬃 S2 pushing an enemy behind her along the deployment direction. The maintainer's
  **2026-10-19** first-hand official play and two recordings show that enemy moving away from her instead.
  Primary video: `原版野鬃s2效果以及转身动画无遮挡.mp4` (32.600771 s, 1920×1282); corroboration:
  `原版野鬃s2效果以及转身动画.mp4` (33.297370 s). Both show 2X; timing below uses original frame PTS.
- **Official / decision:** local `skill_table.json` → `skchr_wildmn_2`: `attack@force = 1` at every rank,
  duration 20 s. The client evidence already recorded in §21.13 is `wildmn_s_2[force]`, `knockback[dir]`,
  `_useSourceDirection: true`, `_decreaseForceLevelWhenNotInDirection: 2`.
  [PRTS 推与拉](https://prts.wiki/w/推与拉): >45° off deployment or <0.25 tile becomes radial away with
  force level −2. The maintainer's new observation supersedes round 40's §32.14.9 exception, not this reduction.
- **Cause:** `keepDir: true` suppressed the radial direction specifically for rear targets while retaining
  the two-level reduction. Only the shared normal/elite Wild Mane kit passed it; no other kit users were found.
- **Now:** remove that option and its branch. Rear hits (negative dot against field deployment direction)
  move away, including the mirrored right boss half, using the existing effect distance table: weight 1
  0.085 tile, weight 0 0.374, weight ≥2 zero. Her skill still uses deployment `fwd`, independent of attack
  animation. Front, side and near-front distances, all other kits, fixed-direction exceptions and the
  renderer remain unchanged. Normative updates: design/engine.md §5.4; SIM.md §6 helper contract.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the explicit task scope keeps front targets unchanged, so only rear hits select the effect
  column. This is a scope compromise, **not an official rear-only classification**: the video also suggests
  the effect column in front. Other rear weights/angles extend that same PRTS column [ASSUMED]. No arbitrary
  fitted force or multiplier is introduced. The grey-hooded purple-sword enemies are visually consistent
  with [法术近卫](https://prts.wiki/w/法术近卫) (weight 1); identity is an inference, not decoded video metadata.
- **Tests:** `test/sim/feedback1d-push.test.js` now has 10 cases (5 additional): four facings, front/rear/side/
  near targets, the 0.25-tile boundary, coincident centres, normal/elite and weights 0–4, right-half boss
  mirroring and real-wave rear hand-overs with invariants. The old forward-behind assertion is replaced.

Measurement: in the un-tilted lane, floor seams around y=527 occur at x≈963/1118/1274/1431;
interpolating toward the enemies' ground anchors gives **154±4 px/tile**. Track fixed enemy HP-bar edges,
cross-check body translation, and stop before normal walking resumes. Front shifts are **240–242 px ≈
1.56–1.58±0.05 tiles**; rear shifts **14±2 px ≈0.09±0.02 tile**, about **6%** of front.
PRTS level 0 / −2: effect **1.56247 / 0.08492** (5.4%); projectile **1.6958 / 0.11825** (7.0%).
The ratio supports a two-level reduction; absolute distances better fit the effect column.

All S2 targets observed are the same front enemy A and rear enemy B; no lower-lane enemy is hit by her S2.
Each recording has one activation, unobstructed ≈15.8 s / crowded ≈18.9 s, ending ≈28.9 s (the first has
selection-menu pauses). Unobstructed hit intervals, last pre-slide → plateau, in video seconds:

| Target | Frame times (s) | Distance |
|---|---|---|
| A front 1 | 15.977 → 16.377–16.393 | ≈240 px / 1.56 tiles |
| B rear 1 | 16.506 → 16.602–16.635 | −14 px / 0.09 tile, away |
| B rear 2 | 16.990 → 17.088 | −14 px / 0.09 tile, away |
| A front 2 | 17.490 → 17.876–17.893 | 242 px / 1.57 tiles |
| A front 3 | 18.618 → 19.002–19.018 | ≈242 px / 1.57 tiles |
| A front 4 | 23.535 → 23.906–23.938 | ≈242 px / 1.57 tiles |
| A front 5 | 25.998 → 26.337 | ≥234 px / ≥1.52 tiles before menu tilt; endpoint obscured |

Crowded corroboration, impact → plateau: A 19.132→19.519–19.534, 21.145→21.515–21.532,
21.643→≈22.04, 24.253→24.625–24.656, 26.719→27.102–27.134 (each ≈240–242 px);
B 19.631–19.646→19.727–19.743 and 20.130→20.227–20.259 (each −14 px).
B's 20.630 hit is lethal (COST +1), so no complete displacement can be measured. Far-right front endpoints
have gate overlap; the first two front pushes in the unobstructed video are the clean samples.
Eight full source frames and the per-hit pixel coordinates are saved locally under
the maintainer's local measurement frames (not shipped).

For **#730**, unobstructed normal attacks: last rear hit **7.517 s**, still facing left **7.838**,
return begins **7.854**, edge-on **7.870**, fully right **7.918**, next front hit **7.982**.
The visible turn is **0.064±0.016 video seconds ≈0.13±0.03 game seconds** at 2X. Last rear hit to return
start is **0.337 video seconds ≈0.67 game seconds**; to fully right **0.401 ≈0.80 game seconds**.
This is also a switch to an approaching front target: it does **not** establish a fixed no-target return
timeout, three-second delay or clip-end policy. The initial turn overlaps deployment; S2's visible thrust
continues right even on rear hits. No attack-turn implementation changes are included here.

### 35.11 Committed example LLM plans

- **Seen:** the maintainer's first real advisor run (solo HARD, seed 7, reasoning effort low) shifted from
  不屈/卡西米尔/迅捷/助力 to 萨尔贡, then 维多利亚/拉特兰 and 绝技. The local round49 plan report shows up to
  eight targets, duplicated purchases, item IDs in operator lists, one rejected plan and 3–28 s replies
  (reported median 12 s). The report is local evidence, not bundled official data or a repeatable model benchmark.
- **Official / decision:** maintainer request **2026-10-19** to improve the opt-in §35.9 example within §35.8's
  seat visibility, validated execution, fallback and journal guarantees. Official data remains local
  `activity_table.json` → `autoChessData`, `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess` → `bondInfoDict`,
  `charChessDataDict` (bond IDs, normal→elite and merge count), `shopLevelDataDict`, band/effect records;
  §35.9 records the other catalogue/economy sources. Existing bond builders, purchase prices and interest
  calculations remain authoritative. The advisor strategy has no official prescription.
- **Cause:** the example discarded earlier plans, omitted its own LP/outcome feedback, sent unnecessarily
  verbose dynamic state and allowed up to eight unrelated bonds. A single obvious schema error rejected all
  preferences. Reasoning was not separated from the applied plan.
- **Now:** retain confirmed/pending prep snapshots per opaque match ID, seat and knowledge version. Host
  acknowledgements prevent unaccepted replies becoming history; drafts and failures preserve the prep direction.
  Feed back actual own buy/sell/merge hooks (including legacy execution), bond counts before→after, current
  board/bench/overflow, LP and last battle's counted leaks/LP loss. Keep at most three held, publicly reachable
  bond targets; require explicit pivot plus reason to change targets or priority order, otherwise retain viable
  old targets. Lost feasibility produces a logged automatic pivot. Compact shop cost/tier/bonds, stars,
  thresholds/layers, economy, enemies/factions/boss and choice effects follow the stable cached knowledge prefix.
  Model replies separate brief `why`, `pivot` and `pivotReason` from `plan`; only validated plans reach the
  game/journal. Optional `SP_LLM_DEBUG_FILE` records reasoning, repairs and latency privately. Local repairs
  drop wrong/unknown IDs and stale choices, deduplicate/cap lists and clamp numeric bounds; malformed structures
  still fail normally. No extra provider calls, changed game rules or real API calls in tests.
- **[ASSUMED]:** all summary/prompt/repair policy; three targets, 32 seat memories, 256 own events with sequence
  gaps, three reasoning sentences/360 characters, one-line pivot reason/160 characters and 128 logged repair
  details. Candidate feasibility uses owned/displayed pieces and the current-level public catalogue, bans and
  deployment capacity, not hidden stock. Bench progress is potential; golden-only targets require owned elites;
  special bonuses/plugin effects are not forecast. Missing snapshot baselines after restart/eviction are `null`
  and incomplete history is explicit. Generic plan validation still allows eight bonds; legacy/direct defaults,
  deterministic sim, human handlers and replay are unchanged. Explicit empty plans still delegate unweighted
  to legacy. No measured strength/latency improvement claimed.
- **Tests:** authored `test/fixtures/llm-advisor.json` records synthetic multi-round mock replies/outcomes;
  `external-llm-continuity.test.js` covers confirmed continuity, three-bond cap, justified/missing/automatic pivots,
  per-seat/per-match isolation, draft/failure handling, private/bounded reasoning, repairs, compact choices,
  actual host transactions/results and feedback gaps. Existing external/advisor/knowledge/transport tests,
  match/strategy/startup/replay and docs checks exercise the surrounding guarantees. Golden unchanged; no UI msgids.

### 35.12 Regenerable advisor strategy playbook

- **Seen:** §35.11 keeps round plans consistent but lacks a version-derived build catalogue, staged targets and
  a repeatable method for matching a professional team to bans, teammate claims and special enemies.
- **Official / decision:** maintainer request and first-hand/community guidance **2026-10-19**: compare several
  non-competing professional-team directions, consider 罗素/three 坚守 as a safety net or 芬 as a supplier, and
  preserve the method for other versions. This guidance is not official text. Mechanics use local official
  `activity_table.json` → `autoChessData` / `act2autochess` band/effect, bond, chess, shop and enemy/boss records,
  character/skill/enemy tables, generated `bands.bondIds/buffs`, `chess` capabilities and the matching band,
  pool, item and damage code. The official client leader limit is `MAX_BATTLE_DAMAGE = 300000`: qualifying
  final/hidden leader hits are voided, not clamped. Sources and exact trigger caveats are in
  [AI-STRATEGY](../AI-STRATEGY.md). Existing pool reconstruction and maintainer decisions retain their sources.
- **Cause:** a raw catalogue does not derive cheap bridges, threshold access, enemy coverage or public shared-pool
  contention. Community pairings were not recorded separately from mechanical associations.
- **Now:** the hashed knowledge pack includes a data-derived playbook with members by tier, baseline threshold
  estimates, capability hints, a tier-weighted bond graph, band role classes and mechanical implications.
  An editable overlay preserves per-entry sources and reports mechanical/community disagreements; the
  follow-ups below resolve the economy, freeze and redeployment builds. The example sends up to four scored legal
  band/main/sub variants at draft and prep, with team roles, stage keys, missing counters, supplier options and
  pivot conditions. It uses existing public teammate boards/bands, announced bans/enemies and own progress,
  never hidden live stock. Prior-plan continuity and the held/reachable three-priority-target gate remain.
  An offline CLI archives consumed input/policy hashes, the overlay, playbook and optional observed score trace.
- **[ASSUMED]:** all strategic recommendations, role classifications, cheap-tier graph weights, text-derived
  coverage hints, baseline shop schedule/expected encounters, score weights, candidate diversity and stage
  boundaries. These are not strength claims, guaranteed rolls or actual remaining-copy estimates. Optional
  skills, stand-ins/DIY, custom scripts and special counting modifiers need review when regenerating. Fewer than
  two alternatives are explicitly reported when legal directions run out. No gameplay rules, legacy defaults,
  provider-call count, protocol plan bounds or golden outputs change.
- **Tests:** `test/match/ai-playbook.test.js` covers current-data mechanics, graph/coverage/timing, sourced overlay
  conflicts and sourced builds, changed content, bans, professional-team variants, public contention,
  continuity, enemy/boss counters, team roles, hidden-state isolation, deterministic CLI output and draft/prep
  prompts through an injected mock transport. External advisor/knowledge/LLM/continuity, match mechanics and docs suites
  validate the integration. No real API calls or new UI msgids.

### 35.13 Advisor economy floors and expiring funds

- **Seen:** in the maintainer's live 娜仁图亚 / co-op 绝境 match, the language-model advisor never refreshed
  when targets were absent, left funds unused, and only reached shop L3 at R7 without a deliberate low-level
  build. Diagnostics showed `maxRefreshes:0` each round and `level:1/2/3` through R0–2/R3–6/R7–8.
- **Official / decision:** maintainer's first-hand strategy guidance **2026-10-19**, not official text:
  spend expiring funds, refresh for targets, freeze unaffordable wanted cards, store sale value in spare
  operators, and level on time; 罗素 elite farms / 电表倒转 holds or other justified ideas may differ.
  Official `activity_table.json` sources: `autoChessData.constData.noMoneyTipsBand`, 坎诺特's
  `aceffect_band_34 / coin_carry_over`, and `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess`'s
  `constData.shopRefreshPrice`, `shopLevelDataDict`, `shopCharChessInfoData`. Existing PRTS hand-capacity,
  copy-weighted pool, upgrade discount and freeze rules retain their sources (§6.2, §29.8, §32.14.7).
- **Cause:** `bot.js` capped upgrades at the plan's number and capped every purchase pass's refresh count.
  The compact model context did not make disappearing funds, frozen slots and stored sale value explicit.
- **Now:** `level` is a floor followed by legacy leveling. Only `holdLevel:true` with a level and nonblank
  `economy.why` caps it. Non-carry advisor seats buy wanted/useful operators, preserve unaffordable targets
  via the normal `g.freeze` handler, search while a stocked purchase is affordable, then buy cheap bank
  singles. Bank sales fund wanted purchases; carry seats retain interest behavior. Digest/compact context
  expose expiring funds, effective refresh/upgrade cost, frozen indices and bank UIDs/base refunds. Knowledge
  explains the mechanics, sell values, data-cost/tier recommendations and the measured legacy curve; prompt
  requires explaining delayed development. No-plan/empty-plan legacy and all official gameplay are unchanged.
- **[ASSUMED]:** wanted/useful classification, one reserved bench slot, cheapest distinct bank singles with
  available stock, base-refund-only liquidation estimates, 64-action spending-pass bound, recommended
  R3/5/7/10/13 tier access deadlines and small-sample legacy reference. Model preferences do not override
  actual affordability/hand/pool rules. Frozen targets, unusable residuals and bounded effects may leave funds.
- **Tests:** 13 new advisor-economy cases cover floor/hold validation and execution, preference override,
  buy-before-refresh, bounded useful search, freeze across rounds, carry interest, bank acquisition/sale,
  unavailable stock/bench capacity, empty-plan equivalence and digest fields; two new model/playbook tests
  cover hold repair and tier/cost/share curves. Reached advisor/LLM, bot, economy/pool/merge suites: **214 pass,
  0 fail**. Docs checks: **32/32**; strict i18n: **2150/2150** in all four packs; lint/import/typecheck pass
  (148 existing warnings, three existing allowed import findings).
  All **288** golden scenarios match; none regenerated. No real API or browser run.

Offline `botbench` (real sim, example `tools/ai/stdio.mjs`, solo/co-op 绝境, seeds 1–3, rehearsal 0): advisor
funds lost/prep **0.4762 / 0.5092**, legacy **0.7143 / 0.6667**; advisor R4/7/10/13 shop levels
**2/4/5/6** and **1.9167/3.9167/5/6**; legacy **2/3.6667/5/6** and **2/3.9167/5/6**.
Refreshes/prep **1.6190 / 1.5521** versus **1.7857 / 1.7358**; mean survival rounds **14 / 13.5833** versus
**14 / 13.25**. Advisor wins **1/3, 1/3**, legacy **2/3, 1/3**, zero errors/fallbacks. Draft policies select
different bands; this is a small integration regression check, not proof of better combat strength. Commands,
metric denominators and sources: [AI-STRATEGY](../AI-STRATEGY.md#advisor-economy-3513).

### 35.14 Advisor execution fidelity

- **Seen:** a manual playtest of the language-model advisor requested by the maintainer on 2026-10-20 (co-op
  HARD, seed 11, seat 0, the example agent's prompt pipeline answered by hand, then a deterministic replay of the
  same replies with a caller trace of every buy, sale, level-up, refresh and freeze) produced sensible plans that
  execution lost: R3 spent all six funds filling before a three-cost L2 upgrade;
  R6–8 froze incidental normal copies named only by elite keeps; R10–14 sold elites, high tiers and kept
  members for hand room; R12 bought redundant copies and upgraded past the intended one-prep delay.
  The schema could not request item purchases or a last-fund refresh followed by freezing.
- **Official / decision:** maintainer **2026-10-20** explicitly limits the hand-room correction to advisor
  seats. Official `activity_table.json`: `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act2autochess.shopLevelDataDict`,
  `shopCharChessInfoData`, `charChessDataDict`, `bondInfoDict`, `constData.shopRefreshPrice`, plus item prices
  and forms in `trapChessDataDict` / `trapShopChessDatas`. The local tables were read, not copied into tests. Existing
  [PRTS help](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议/帮助) hand capacity, overflow, merge and freeze rules (§6.2–6.3)
  remain authoritative; this changes no gameplay primitive or battle rule.
- **Cause:** fill-only procurement ran before the floor, `planContains` conflated normal and elite IDs,
  `keep` doubled as a buy signal, and bond weights ignored owned elites. Cleanup callers used independent
  legacy sale heuristics with no protection policy. Item buying and search-to-freeze were absent from the API;
  the prompt treated holds as persistent low-level builds although plans already expire each prep.
- **Now:** advisor fill passes complete merges then leave an affordable floor to level before singles.
  Keep/sell match exact forms; buy accepts either form but wants useful normal merge/distinct-member progress
  only until its elite is owned. Bond purchase/lineup weights obey that rule. Only unaffordable explicit buys
  or merge completions freeze; incidental bonds/keeps and owned-elite copies do not. Shared cleanup protects
  elites, tier ≥4, exact keeps, explicit targets actually bought this prep and active plan-bond thresholds.
  Sell unprotected owned-elite duplicates, banks, off-plan low-tier singles and other unprotected pieces in
  value order; next destroy the cheapest unequipped hand item costing ≤2; finally use legacy fallback. The
  room step keeps the legacy shed's guards: a pre-emptive shed never sells a piece bought this prep, and a live
  pair stays together (copies of an owned elite remain the first sale).
  Item purchases equip between buys and keep room. API adds `items.buy` (≤8) and `economy.freezeSearch`
  (boolean; extra searches share the prep-wide refresh cap, including free refreshes). A reasoned hold can
  defer just this prep; its existing lifetime needs no runtime change. English/Chinese API and the stdio/HTTP
  example policy document the fields. No-plan/empty-plan/AI takeover seats keep legacy execution.
- **[ASSUMED]:** all execution priorities, weights, useful-copy classification (a first copy starts merge
  progress), protection/value ordering, UID ties, cheap-item cutoff, two-slot incidental/one-slot requested
  item margins and 32-action room bounds are engineering policy, not official AI advice. Actual PlayerState
  purchase/sale/equip/refresh/freeze/level validation remains final. Protection may bench a piece; if all room
  options are protected and no cheap item remains, legacy cleanup can still sell one. Search requires an
  eligible explicit target and effective refresh funds, never hidden future rolls. None of these choices
  grants the advisor hidden state.
- **Tests:** `advisor-execution.test.js` constructs the R3 regression, merge precedence, exact forms,
  useful-copy/bond weights, freeze exclusions, each protection class, every cleanup caller, item procurement,
  room margins, schema bounds, last-fund/free-refresh caps, one-prep holds and no-advisor takeover equivalence.
  New execution tests **25/25**; with existing economy tests **38/38**. Reached advisor/LLM/knowledge,
  bot/strategy/economy/pool/merge/playbook/community and docs run: `# tests 237`, `# pass 237`, `# fail 0`.
  Lint/import/typecheck pass (148 existing lint warnings, three existing allowed sim import findings); strict i18n **2150/2150** in all four packs.
  All **288** golden scenarios match without regeneration.

A legacy-only `node tools/botbench.mjs --configs solo:HARD,coop4:HARD --seeds 3 --json <file>` run gives the
same six records before and after (measured timing fields aside). Replaying the playtest's replies on seed 11:
**R3 reaches L2**, the only freeze is R3's for the explicit `buy` target `chess_char_1_06_a`, R6–8 do not freeze,
and **no protected piece is sold** (18 advisor calls, no errors or fallbacks). The match diverges after the floor
fix, so only these execution points are compared, not the full-match outcome.

### 35.15 Advisor input for skilled play

- **Seen:** On 2026-10-20, at the maintainer's request, one coop4 HARD match (seed 11, seat 0) was played by hand
  from the same JSON the example model receives: `STRATEGY_PROMPT` plus the whole `sp-knowledge/1` pack as the system text, and
  `{kind, digest, continuity, strategy, choices}` as the user text. That system text was 621983 characters
  (about 150–160k input tokens), which a 128k-context model cannot run. Operators were stored twice (190289
  characters, plus `playbook.operators` 139351) and enemies twice (160896, plus `playbook.enemies` 26613).
  The digest listed chess and items by id only, so every decision needed a lookup; two rows share the name
  锡人. The band draft offered four candidates (哈洛德, 贾维, 娜仁图亚, 罗素) and left the usual mains out.
  Prep offered four near-copies of one band and main. Bounty cards carried name, description, enemy key and
  coin, and no kill estimate. Enemy `effect` strings ended in `[object Object]`. Adding the just-activated
  sub-bond 灵巧 was reverted as an unexplained direction change. A single Victoria-6 focus fielded about half
  the legacy bots' activated layers (round 13: 104 versus 168–217) and leaked 29 on the round-12 air wave.
- **Official:** Operator caps use `canHitFly`, `dmgType`, `position`, `stats.blockCnt`, `profession` and
  `subProfessionId` from `charChessDataDict` / `character_table` (`data/chess.json`). Two rows share 锡人:
  `chess_char_1_16` (T1) and `chess_char_2_19` (T2). Enemy description and ability text come from the enemy
  handbook (`data/enemies.json`); fly is `isFlyEnemy` when that flag is boolean, otherwise `motion === 'FLY'`,
  the same test as `waves.js`. Bond thresholds come from `bondInfoDict`. `bountyKillChance` is the existing
  legacy exposure model, not a new combat rule. What the example is shown is a maintainer decision from this
  play, not an official client screen.
- **Cause:** The example concatenated the full pack. The compact digest dropped names and never sent class or
  caps. The draft cap of four filled before later community mains. Prep kept every sub-pair as its own
  candidate. Ability objects were joined with `String`. `focusPlan` treated any bond-list change as a pivot.
  The prompt counted layers on one bond and did not compare the announced fly wave with anti-air units.
- **Now:** The example system prompt is a slim view of the unchanged pack: one operator row (id, name, tier,
  cost, class, bonds, caps, the first trait clipped to 120 characters, skill name), bonds, bands with
  insights, items, compact bosses, compact enemies, and the playbook without its operator and enemy copies
  (community, cycles, graph, pairings, schedules, `legacyReference` and band insights stay). Hello still
  sends the full pack. `strategyCandidates` still reads the full local playbook. Measured on the current
  data: Chinese hard system text is 177997 characters and still carries 249 compact enemies; the English
  funny catalogue would exceed 200000, so that view omits compact enemies (193822 characters) and the
  round's `upcoming` list still names them. Digest chess on the board, bench, overflow and shop gain `name`,
  `class` and `caps`; items gain `name`. Bounty cards gain `killChance` when the estimator returns a finite
  number, plus catalogue `hp`, `def`, `res`, `motion`, `fly` and a short effect. The band draft returns at
  most eight directions and prefers every uncontended community or maintainer main that fits. Prep collapses
  generic variants of the same band and main into one stable `band:main` id with `subOptions`. Only a change
  of the primary (highest-priority) bond needs `pivot:true`. The prompt documents §35.14's
  `items.buy` (at most eight), `economy.freezeSearch`, a one-round `holdLevel` with `economy.why`, exact
  `keep`/`sell` ids, and `buy` only to finish a merge or add a distinct member. It also counts activated
  layers on every bond and asks each prep to compare `digest.upcoming` fly and elite counts with `caps.air`.
  `repairPlan` keeps those fields (item ids checked against the catalogue and the current shop).
  Legacy decisions and golden results are unchanged.
- **[ASSUMED]:** The slim field set, the 200000-character budget, and omitting compact enemies only when that
  budget would break. The draft cap of eight: on an empty round-1 绝境 draft, 杜遥夜 scores 28.8 and is ninth
  (eighth place is 29) and 埃芒加德 scores 13 because that band has no data bond engine, so both stay out
  rather than raising the cap or hard-coding a main. Stable collapsed ids, the 160-character bounty effect,
  and caps reported false when the playbook has no row for that chess id. The one-round hold, the breadth
  rule and the air check are prompt policy. This branch does not execute `items.buy` or `freezeSearch`.
- **Tests:** `test/match/advisor-input.test.js` covers slim size, effect text, the eight-direction draft and
  collapsed prep, digest names and `killChance`, the sub-bond pivot rule, and repair of the parallel fields.
  With the reached playbook, community-build, cycle, external-knowledge, LLM and continuity suites: **61 pass,
  0 fail**. Docs checks: **32/32**. Strict i18n: **2150/2150** in all four packs. Lint/import/typecheck pass
  (148 existing warnings, three existing allowed import findings). All **288** golden scenarios match; none
  regenerated. No real API call. No browser run.

### 35.16 Finished battle visuals (PR #821, @Eimyy)

Explicit field completion now releases surviving models from the final combat/status pose into idle while preserving flight and stealth-driven forms. Persistent official/plugin effects stop and one-shot effects finish on a bounded presentation clock (two seconds [ASSUMED]); paused/stalled feeds do not trigger completion, and studio retains its supplied clock. Simulation time, result delivery and golden outcomes are unchanged. Regression coverage: `test/render/battle-finish.test.js`.

### 35.17 Simplified-board recovery after load timeouts (PR #664, @2994432392)

- **Seen:** GitHub #8 item 5 — after a cold start or a re-entry into a running match, a renderer stage that hit its
  12 s timeout left the player on the simplified board for the rest of the match.
- **Now:** only a timeout (`DOMException` `TimeoutError` from the engine import or `createFieldView`) schedules
  automatic retries, after 2 s and 8 s; missing WebGL is an error with no retry and no flash back to loading. Event
  retries are capped separately at 2, and the fallback notice is shown once per mounted match screen.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the 2 s / 8 s retry delays and the retry caps (no official counterpart; presentation only).
- **Tests:** `test/ui/fieldHost-retry.test.js`, `test/ui/fieldHost-notice.test.js`, `test/ui/fieldHost-lifecycle.test.js`
  and the browser tests `test/render/reconnect.browser.test.js` / the initialization recovery cases.

  ::placeholder font against the input's inner width, checked again as the field resizes and as fonts arrive) —
  English and Japanese at 特大 on every desktop and at 中 / 大 in a 960–1024 px window, Chinese, Traditional Chinese
  and Korean at 特大 in a 960–1024 px window, Korean at 大 at 960×540 —, the whole sentence everywhere else.
  The review of those fixes (2026-10-22): **the room's 开始模拟 / 准备就绪** (`screens/room.js` GoButton, `proto-start rs-go`)
  no longer takes 选择模拟协议's label rules — they are the dialog's own now (`.proto-foot .proto-start__label` / `__rook`,
  `css/screens/lobby.css`; the shared rule keeps the one-line label) — and makes its own choice in `css/screens/room.css`:
  the label wraps onto a second line and the button grows (`.proto-start`'s min-height) rather than cutting it, .46rem
  at most, the mark's rook at .4rem and its STRONGHOLD PROTOCOL at .1rem; on a short screen the button is at least 46 px
  (taller for the second line: 52–56 px in English and Japanese) and the label 18–20 px. Measured with
  headless Chrome, the host's 开始模拟 and a guest's 准备就绪 in five languages at four sizes: every line inside the
  button at 640×360 / 667×375 / 740×360 / 844×390 and at 600×540 – 1920×1080. **选择模拟协议's column** has a 300 px
  floor (`min(max(5.6rem, 300px), 46vw)`): in a window whose 1rem is under 54 px the cards not chosen keep their names
  whole at the compact levels (960×540, 1024×600, 1100×530, 800×600, 700×560 and 600×540 in five languages at four
  sizes; the room's 选择模拟协议 too); the 1280×720-and-up windows are unchanged.
  The text-fit review (2026-10-22, after the 特大 screenshots of the room on a 667×375 phone): **the room** — an
  occupied seat card's art holds its text in flow (`screens/room.js` SeatCard: `.rseat__top` with 创建者 / AI 队友, 更换立绘
  under it at the right and the tags, `.rseat__low` with the operator's name and the state beside its box), so a larger
  文字大小 or a longer language wraps the labels and pushes them apart instead of drawing one over another; the avatar
  frame (a bot, a card without the art pack) shrinks, square, before any text is squeezed. On a short screen the badge
  stops at 15 px, the state at 17 px (15 px when not lit), the name plate at 18 px, 邀请 at 18 px, the avatar frame at
  1rem; the empty seat's 添加 AI 队友 wraps onto a second line and the chip grows; 复制 stops at 18 px, the difficulty
  pill and the mode line at 20 px, the ping line at 15 px and the status line at 14 px (a spectator's long one on two
  lines). A spectator's 观战中 keeps its width: its eye takes the STRONGHOLD mark's place, and the bottom bar's side
  columns are at least their content (`minmax(min-content, 1fr) auto minmax(min-content, 1fr)`, `minmax(0, 1fr) auto` up
  to 900 px wide) so the status line wraps in what is left and the button (`flex: none`) is never squeezed. 独立模拟's
  card is as wide as a co-op one (3.4rem; at 2.7rem it was the narrowest) and its briefing is wider in a short window
  (7rem up to 640 px tall, 8rem / 54vw on a short screen, with 20 / 14 / 13 px text there), scrolling only as a last
  resort. ⚙ 同盟设置's rows, the shared ping pill and the 原版 / 插件 tag grow with their text (`min-height`,
  `css/components.css`). The 名片干员 picker's tiles are at least 6.6em of the names' font (11 px at most on a short
  screen, five tiles a row at 特大), so operator names wrap whole instead of ending in an ellipsis. **The hub** — the
  entries' names wrap between words and stop at .3rem (小's size), the card growing for the second line (min-height
  1.34rem); on a short screen the entries are at least 128 px wide (2.1rem was 84 px), the names at most 18 px, the
  decorative English 10 px; the plates' labels take a second line (and the tag moves under them) instead of an ellipsis,
  18 px at most on a short screen; the title stops at .56rem and keeps clear of the side columns, the latin one at
  .46rem (one line in any landscape window); the tips' count and arrows move together to a line of their own
  (`.tips__pager`) when the head is too narrow; the line above each entry, the 成就统计 card's label and its trophy count
  (.15rem at most) grow or wrap. Measured with headless Chrome (an audit of every visible line against each box that
  clips it or draws one, the screen, the other labels' text and breaks inside a word, an ellipsis allowed only for a
  player's name): the room in 1540 cases — a co-op room's host, doctor and spectator views, the room before anyone
  joined, ⚙, the doctor's picker and the 独立模拟 room at 640×360 / 667×375 / 740×360 / 844×390 and 800×600 –
  1920×1080, five languages, four sizes — went from 8292 problems (4375 on the phones, 3917 on desktops, most of the
  latter the picker's ellipses) to none; the hub in 220 cases (the same screens and languages) from 704 to none.
  The review of the text-fit fixes (2026-10-22): **Korean** breaks between words — `word-break: keep-all`, with
  `overflow-wrap: break-word` as the last resort, on the hub's stage and the room only (`.lobby-hub:lang(ko) .hub-stage`,
  `.rs:lang(ko)`; 选择模拟协议's 「독립 시뮬레이션 시작」 took three lines by words at 特大 in a 1024×600 window) —,
  so 「독립 / 시뮬레이션」, the tips, the room's 「시뮬레이션 / 시작」, the picker's names and the briefing no longer leave a
  syllable alone on a line; the checker (`test/e2e/text-fit.mjs`) now counts a break between two Hangul syllables, or a
  syllable and a latin letter or digit, as inside a word (keep-all or not: under keep-all such a break is the fallback of
  a word too long for its box). **The hub** — the entries follow 文字大小 as a whole: the card 2.3 × `--t` wide (the right
  column's plates with it) and at least 1.34 × `--t` tall, its icon .42 × `--t`, the name .3 × `--t` (no longer stopped
  at 小's .3rem; 小 is unchanged), the decorative English line clear of the icon (below it since the next review; first
  a padding beside it), both cards one height (`.hub-entries` stretches, the card fills its column); the title follows 文字大小 while it fits on
  one line beside the side columns — at most (100vw − 9.52rem) / 8.2, the latin one / 20.2 (the project does not use
  container queries, `client-static.test.js`), 小 unchanged —; a plate's label takes the row's free width
  (`flex: 1 1 0; min-width: min-content`), so it wraps beside its glyph and only the tag drops under it; on a short
  screen the plates' labels, the names and the decorative English are their size at 中 and a pixel more a step

  screen every px cap grows above 中: the seat card's badge, state, name plate, 更换立绘, 邀请 and a spectator's empty-seat
  line (now centred and capped too) and the status line half a pixel a step, the mode line, the ping line, 复制, the
  pill, the briefing and the big button's label a pixel; the AI teammate's avatar frame is 2rem at 小 again (80 px — the
  short screen's 1rem at every size had halved it) and `clamp(1rem, 3rem − --t, 2rem)` above (56 / 44 / 40 px), still
  giving way by flex, its AI tag with it; 复制's `line-height: 1` now comes after `font` (`font: inherit` had reset it to
  1.5, which set the label at the top of its 78% button and cut its lower half at 大 / 特大 — the cut 「Copy」 / 「复制」
  of the handoff's 特大 room shots, which the earlier cap had not fixed); 独立模拟's card is 2.7 × `--t` (2.7rem at 小, as
  before the text-fit review, and wider with the text; it had become 3.4rem at every size); up to 900 px wide the status
  line and the pill sit at the start of the bottom bar again (`.rs-foot__center { justify-self: start }`; the centre
  column stays 1fr beside the button's auto one, so 观战中 keeps its width); the shared ping pill is a fixed .32rem again

  ran over the state and the tags once the card was short), hidden on a short screen, where the state says the same and
  an offline doctor's state drops its empty box; on a short screen the badge and 更换立绘 share a row, the tags wrap under
  them. Measured with the same checks and, on the hub, the people icon against the text, a plate's label against its
  glyph and the two cards' tops: the hub (220 cases, five languages, four phones and seven windows, four sizes) from 318
  problems at c8554391 (101 Korean breaks inside a word, 169 lines under the icon, 44 cards' tops apart, 4 labels under
  their glyph — counted per card) to none (the phones' entry line wider than its card stays: below); the room
  (1100 cases: the co-op host's, doctor's and spectator's views, the 独立模拟 room and the picker, the same languages,
  screens and sizes) from 700 problems at c8554391, all Korean breaks inside a word (the picker's names 640, the big
  button's 「시뮬레이션 시|작」 36, the briefing 19, the status line and 邀请's line 5), to none; a modded room with a
  doctor who readied, and then offline, fits on the four phones but for the leftover below.
  The review of those fixes (2026-10-22): **独立模拟 on a phone** — the card is no wider than a co-op one there
  (`min(2.7 × --t, 3.4rem, 34vw)`: 108 px at 小, 136 px above; the 2.7 × `--t` of a 64 / 76 / 88 px `--t` had made it

  the 640–740 px phones for another reason: it was already at its 320 px cap whatever the card's width, and its text grows
  a pixel a step above 中 while the bars above and below take height from it (214–217 px of text in 197–212 px; 202 in
  201–203 at c8554391, whose caps stopped at 中). It may now be `min(11rem, 62vw)` wide (at 小 it stays as wide as its
  text: 247 px in English on 667×375, as at c8554391), and above 中 its padding, the title's and the list's line heights
  give a pixel, three and a pixel a step (`max(0px, --t-step)`; 小 / 中 unchanged): at 特大 196 px of text in 197 at most,
  no scrolling in any language on the four phones [ASSUMED: the width and the spacing]. **Japanese** never starts a line
  with a small kana, ー or a closing mark — `line-break: strict` in the room (`.rs:lang(ja)`) and on the hub's stage

  at 特大 on the phones), a spectator's 「ドクタ|ーの画面」, the briefing's 「シークレ|ットコア」, the hub tips'

  **The hub's entries** keep their text below the icon rather than beside it: the card's top padding is the icon's

  one line at 小 in every language there, the cards as at c8554391. **A modded room on a phone:** the 插件内容就绪 tag
  gives way, the expected state (载入中 / 校验失败 stay, and the status line waits for every doctor's content), so the

  The review of that review (2026-10-22, of 02eebe5b): **独立模拟's briefing title** broke inside the difficulty code in
  a window ≤ 640 px tall at 特大 (「Perilous Simulation AC-」 / 「2」 in the reviewer's 1024×600 screenshots, since the
  wider briefing of 242fcc11; `text-fit.mjs` allowed a break after any hyphen): the code is `nowrap`, the space before it
  outside its span so the title still breaks there, and the title wraps plainly (`text-wrap: wrap` — with the room's
  `pretty` below and the nowrap code, Chrome broke 「Perilous Simulation / AC-2」 at 小 in a 1024×600 window, where it fits
  on one line); the checker counts a break after a hyphen before a digit as a break inside a word. **A last line of one
  character** (139 in the review's 216 pages, most of them the 名片干员 picker's names since they wrap — 20 Japanese names
  a picker such as 「アンジェリー|ナ」 and 「维娜·维多利|亚」 at 小 too —, and at 特大 「添加AI队|友」, a spectator's

  Firefox has no `pretty` and wraps as before (scoped to paragraphs, with `balance` — which Firefox 121+ has — on short labels, in the review of that fix below). **The seat's state** in flow had lost the art's side padding: it takes it back (`margin: 0

  break before ー in a 1024×600 window at 特大).
  The review of that fix (2026-10-22, of 98f0c194): **`pretty` cut a short label in Safari.** WebKit's `text-wrap:
  pretty` adds lines to short labels, which Chrome's never does (the reviewer's sweep: 0 of 3305 width cases in Chrome,
  350 of 5288 in WebKit; 「等待博士加入」 at 61 px 2 → 6 lines): in Playwright WebKit (Safari 26.6) on a 640×360 phone in
  Japanese the empty seat's 「AI同盟者を追加」 took 3 lines at 大 and 4 at 特大, 「追加」 cut at the bottom and 「招待」
  pushed into the art's top edge — the browser tests run Chrome only. And `pretty` did not fix the picker's names: Chrome
  kept 「ファートゥー|ス」 (8 pages), WebKit all 16 Japanese names and 「维娜·维多利|亚」 at 小, as without it. `pretty`
  now stays on the paragraphs — the room's status line (`.rs-hint`), 独立模拟's briefing, the hub's tips, 选择模拟协议's
  description and hint — and on the big button's label, which grows with it (WebKit kept its lines with `pretty` in the
  reviewer's 56 room cases; `balance` broke it inside a word, 「シミュレー|ション開始」 at 小 on the phones and Korean
  「독립 시뮬|레이션 시작」); the short labels take `balance`, which keeps their line count — 添加 AI 队友, 等待博士加入
  and the picker's names (no name ends in one character in Chrome or WebKit). Neither rule is on the whole room, the hub
  or every dialog any more: the lobby's other dialogs and the match's were never measured with it (the dialogs keep
  Japanese strict). 选择同盟方式's note 「…或直接打开邀|请链接」 at 特大 is back as before `pretty` [open: `balance` broke
  the Japanese one inside a phrase at 小, 「…教えてもら|うか」; the Japanese note breaks between phrases since the review
  of 6f34f380, the Chinese one unchanged]. Measured after the change: Chrome, five languages × nine
  windows (640×360 … 1920×1080) at 小 and four at 特大, the hub, 选择同盟方式, 选择模拟协议, the settings dialog, a co-op
  room seen by its host, a long-named doctor and a spectator, ⚙, the picker and a 独立模拟 room — no text-fit record in
  the room or on the hub, no last line of one character but 「Standing / by」 at 特大 (a two-letter word, as before);
  WebKit, four languages × 640×360 / 667×375 / 844×390 / 1440×900 at 小 and 特大 — no record in the room or on the hub
  (the chip two lines again). In both, the dialogs' records are those without the rules (alliance key line's ellipsis,
  English 「Standard Simulation」 cut on the protocol cards at 特大, the settings dialog in Korean / 「English」 [open,
  older]) less Korean 「독립 시뮬레이션 시|작」 on 选择模拟协议's button, which Chrome now breaks between words on desktops;
  in WebKit `pretty` moves that break to another place inside the word (「독립 시뮬레이|션 시작」 on 1440×900 at 小,
  「독립 시뮬레이션 시|작」 without it — corrected in the review of 6f34f380, which measured WebKit without the rules too)
  [open: no keep-all in that dialog, see the hub's]. **The seat's state on a phone** takes 2 of the art's 3 px back, beside a 16 px box and a 3 px gap
  (18 and 4 px before): with all 3 px 「Standing by」 ran from frame to frame in 独立模拟's 108 px card, the box and 「by」
  0.2 px from the card's border on the 640 / 667 px phones (with 2 px and the 18 px box it took two lines); now 1.7 px on
  either side, one line [ASSUMED]. **Your plate on a phone**: the corner mark is `role="img"` named 你 and the tag
  `aria-hidden` (the tag's `display: none` had left a screen reader on a phone nothing that says which plate is yours).
  **The hub's matrix** reports `only n/21 tips checked` when the pager stops short (it had passed silently).
  The review of that fix (2026-10-22, of 6f34f380): **`pretty` reached the room's big button.** The room's 开始模拟 /
  准备就绪 button uses the dialog's `.proto-start__label` span, so `.proto-start__label { text-wrap: pretty }` applied to
  it too, and in WebKit at 小 on 667×375 it moved the Japanese host's and 独立模拟's 「シミュレーション|開始」 to
  「シミュレー|ション開始」 — the break inside the word given as `balance`'s failure (the 56 room cases had compared line
  counts only, and Chrome is unaffected). `pretty` is now on 选择模拟协议's own button only (`.proto-foot
  .proto-start__label`); the room's button wraps plainly, as at 856b976c. Chrome breaks it exactly as with `pretty` in
  every measured window at 小 and 特大 (desktops 「シミュレー|ション開始」 / 「シミュレーショ|ン開始」, phones
  「シミュレーション|開始」 at 小, as before); WebKit at 小 on the phones 「シミュレーション|開始」 again, on 1440×900 and at
  特大 「シミュレー|ション開始」, as without the rules [open, older: the label is one word to BudouX, so `word-break:
  auto-phrase` ran it past the button in all 16 Chrome cases tried]. **`balance` broke 「AI同盟|者を追加」** inside the
  word at 特大 on the 640 / 667 px phones (「AI同盟者を|追加」 at 98f0c194), in Chrome and WebKit: Japanese
  `word-break: auto-phrase` (BudouX phrases, Chrome 119+; Safari and Firefox ignore it) on the chip and on 选择同盟方式's
  note gives 「AI同盟者を|追加」 in Chrome, and the note 「仲間から4桁の同盟コードを教えて|もらうか、招待リンクを|直接開いてください」
  at 特大 on those phones (「…教えてもらう|か、招待リンク…」 before; three lines for two, no record) and 「…招待リンクを|直接開いて

  Firefox wraps the short labels as before: Firefox has `balance` since 121 (only `pretty` is missing), and `pretty` came
  in Chrome 117 / Safari 26, not with `balance` (Chrome 114 / Safari 17.5) — the comments, client.md and the CHANGELOG

  The review of that fix (2026-10-23, of 7aa59703): **English 「Copy」's 4 px** applied at every 文字大小, so at 小 on the
  phones, where the label had been centred (from its cap top to its baseline; the ink numbers above include the
  descenders of p and y), it sat 2 px high. The shift now grows with the step, `calc(2 * max(0px, var(--t-step)))`: none
  at 小 / 中 (the label as at c8554391: its ink 8.4 / 6.4 px from the button's top / bottom at 小 on 667×375 and
  640×360), 1 px higher at 大 (5.6 / 0.8), 2 px at 特大 as before (3.3 / 2.1) [ASSUMED]. **The test meant to keep
  `text-wrap` off the room's button** saw every second rule of lobby.css only (its pattern used up a rule's `}`, so the
  rule right after another and the first in an @media block were skipped: an inserted `.rs-go .proto-start__label
  { text-wrap: pretty }` was missed at 281 of 539 places), and its room.css check missed an ancestor the label inherits
  from; it now reads every rule of both files (the count checked against the sheet's blocks) and also refuses

  **An AI teammate's split name** is `botNameParts` in room.js, unit-tested (「AI·华法琳」 → 「AI·」 + 「华法琳」, `AI·2`
  and a doctor named 「AI·x」 unsplit), and the phone's rule that hides the prefix is checked. Seen and not changed: the
