  one look across sizes, the owner's call [ASSUMED]; Korean 「복사」 sits about 1 px high at 特大 on the phones (2.2 / 4.9 px
  of gap); on desktops English 「Simulation」 runs over the big button's hatch, as at c8554391.
- **Cause (our fixes):** #811 broke under B6 (the card view still imported the removed `chessPortraitUrl`); #812's
  maxlength cut a pasted invite link to the key "HTTP" and the native input sent an IME Enter; #813's room.card gave up
  after RATE / TIMEOUT / OFFLINE although its comment promised a retry; #814's late decode recorded a size for an evicted
  entry, which made the 64 MB PCM budget evict every new sound; #827's white LP rule covered the pending red and its
  caption was CSS text found in no official art; #828's briefing referenced variables 0.3.2 had removed, its global
  `.brief-*` rules broke the 本局信息 dialog, its badge marked a strategy after one clear, and its 1.6 s + 3 s overran BATTLE_CHECK by ~2 s,
  which left the round-1 kettles ~0.4 s. In integration: the host's in-room pick was never remembered, so a co-op-only
  player got every room at 标准; a plugin window over BATTLE_CHECK made the HUD play its entry twice.
  The leftovers fixed on 2026-10-22: the card view's top note, its sync chip and the bar's chip each said the lock (and
  the drawer's detail a fourth time); the short-screen rules hid the create card's `small` (and the first fix, a
  wrapping flex row, put the bare name text on a row of its own in English at 大, a three-row bar that pushed 加入同盟 off
  the screen in en / ja / ko at 大 / 特大 — `.ally-side` centred with `justify-content: center` and nothing scrolled — the
  review of 2026-10-22); the seat card's `onError`
  went straight to the frame; `TURN_BANNER_LATE_MS` 1 s skipped a band whose sprites the match screen had only begun to
  fetch (an early `preloadBootArt` before the art list would also have marked them missing for good); the deco text is
  wider than its sprites (69 vs 66 px and 47 vs 34 px at 667 px), so the three ran into one another.
  The second pass: the `spMine` effect and the phase-change effect fired on the same change, so the 1.2 s disc started
  under the 1.5 s banner (`--z-banner` above it) and its words were covered for its whole life; the 特训敌人 Tooltip
  measured its own anchor, the cell — in the dialog a full-width row —, and centred over it, ~half a dialog away from the
  icon and over the 战场 line; 选择模拟协议's cards grew with `--t` (names .4, descriptions .26: 35 / 23 px at 特大) in a
  224 px column, so at 中 and above the list (a scroll container nobody could see was one) ended under the button with
  终极模拟 out of view, and the label (.5 × `--t`, nowrap) was cut — even at 小 in English, and the list overflowed by
  9 px at 360 px tall; the key field's placeholder was .22 × `--t` (19 px at 特大) beside a 观战 of .26 × `--t`
  (84–133 px wide), leaving 95–170 px for a 160–390 px sentence in every language from 中 up (English already at 小).
  The review of the second pass: the cue's wait started with the turn, but the banner's own 1.76 s only started as it
  mounted, held back by the round plate (`!roundPop`) — in the server's flow (`afterRoundStart`: ROUND_START 2 s, then
  SP_DRAFT) the two raced with ~20 ms to spare, so a plate 100 ms late had the disc play while the banner left, and 1 s
  late under the whole banner (measured in the dev mock: disc at 3723 ms, banner gone 3783 ms; disc at 2836 ms, banner
  2019–3536 ms); the new browser tests had opened the draft from PREP, which the server never does. Above 520 px the
  second pass had changed nothing: English 开始独立模拟 was cut at every desktop size (1440×900 at 小: "Start Solo
  Simula"), the list overflowed at 特大 (zh by 57 / 189 px, en by 145 / 306 px at 1440×900 / 1920×1080) and at 中 in a
  small window (1024×600: zh 53 px, en 112 px), and even at 小 the last card was cut by 2–26 px in 16:9 windows; the
  key field's whole sentence ended in an ellipsis in many more cases than the one noted (English 特大 at every desktop
  size: 539 px of text in a 468 px field at 1920×1080; the default Chinese at 特大 in a 1024 px window: 267 in 256 px).
  The review of those fixes: the new label rules were written on `.proto-start__label`, which the room's GoButton shares;
  on a short screen room.css's `.rs-go.proto-start { height: 46px }` beat the dialog's `height: auto` and its label stayed
  .46 × `--t` (40.5 px at 特大), so 开始模拟 wrapped to 2–4 lines in a 46 px button with overflow hidden and every line
  was cut (zh / zh-TW at 特大 had fitted on one line; en / ja / ko went from cut on the right to unreadable half-lines),
  and on a desktop it wrapped and grew unreviewed. 选择模拟协议's 5.6rem column was 280 px at 960×540 and 233 px at
  800×600 while the glyph keeps 18 px and 文字大小 its px floors, so English 「Standard Simulation」 / 「Ultimate
  Simulation」 (201 px of text in a 200 px box at 960×540 中; 168 in 163 at 800×600 特大) ended in an ellipsis on cards
  not chosen — a 1100×530 window cut names a 640×360 phone shows whole; the desktop browser test stopped at 1280×720.
  Seen while measuring, older than these fixes and not changed: a spectator's 观战中 button is squeezed to a few px by
  the long status line beside it (`.rs-foot`'s auto centre column) on phones and in small windows (fixed in the
  text-fit review, below).
  The text-fit review: the reviews before it had measured only the room's big button. The seat card placed its badge,
  更换立绘, tags, operator name and state at fixed offsets (top .14 / .5 / .56rem, bottom .14 / .7rem), and its short-screen
  sizes were .26–.34 × `--t` uncapped — 23–30 px at 特大 in a card about 120 px wide and 100 px tall —, so 「Standing by」
  wrapped past the card and under 创建者, AI 队友 and 添加 AI 队友 ran off it (the chip, nowrap in a .36rem button, also
  dropped below the art), and in 800–1100 px windows at 大 / 特大 the operator's name ran over the state and 创建者 over
  更换立绘; 复制 (22.9 px at 特大) sat in a 26 px button and the pill's name (24.6 px) in
  a 31 px one; the uncapped status line (.28 × `--t`) took four lines for a spectator on a 640×360 phone and left the
  cards 40 px tall; the bottom bar's `minmax(0, 1fr) auto minmax(0, 1fr)` (`auto minmax(0, 1fr)` up to 900 px) gave the
  status line its max-content and shrank the button (the older 观战中 leftover, at 小 too); 独立模拟's briefing was
  taller than its row and was cut at both ends (older: the old room's briefing was cut too); the picker's names were one
  line with an ellipsis in a .96rem tile (「Ang…」 at 特大 on a phone, 「Blemishi…」 at 1440×900); ⚙'s rows and the ping
  / 原版 tag had fixed heights their text outgrew (older). The hub's entry names were nowrap at .3 × `--t` in a 2.3rem
  card, 2.1rem (84 px) on a short screen: English 「Solo Simulation」 / 「Team Simulation」 ran 25–40 px past it at 小 in
  every window and 57–100 px at 大 / 特大 (Chinese 57 px at 大 on a phone); the plates' labels ended in an ellipsis at 大 /
  特大 on a phone and in Japanese at 特大 in 800–1280 px windows; the title, centred over the whole stage, ran under the
  left column's tips in English at 大 / 特大 in 800–1280 px windows; in 800–1100 px windows at 大 / 特大 the line above
  each entry (a .22rem height), the 成就统计 label (.46rem) and the tips' count (older) were cut.
  The review of the text-fit fixes: the hub's names, now `white-space: normal`, broke Korean between syllables
  (「독립 시뮬레이|션」 in 40 of 44 desktop and phone cases at 小 / 中; the checker exempted Hangul) and their raised first
  line ran under the person icon; 「ALLIANCE SIMULATION」 sat under the people icon (39–206 px² at 小 / 中, 6–18 px²
  before) — the checker compares text with text only; the caps that pinned the title (.56rem / .46rem) and the names
  (.3rem) at 小's size made 中 smaller in small windows (800×600 in Chinese: the title 35.8 → 23.3 px, the names 19.2 →
  12.5 px) and 大 / 特大 change nothing; the plate's label kept its one-line flex-basis, so it moved whole under its glyph
  (en 特大 and ja 大 / 特大 at 800×600); the cards' tops were apart in 61 cases (25 before). On phones the room's caps
  were flat from 中 (the status line 17.9 → 14 px at 中) and `--av: 1rem` at every size halved the AI teammate's frame at
  小 (80 → 40 px); the report had not listed the room's default-size changes — the phone footer centred (`minmax(0, 1fr)
  auto` at ≤ 900 px), 独立模拟's card 3.4rem at 小 (1440×900: 200 → 255 px), the picker's 6.6em tiles; the base `.ping`'s
  `min-height` leaked to every pill; `font: inherit` after `line-height: 1` in 复制's rule; a modded room and an offline
  doctor had never been measured. The review of those fixes: 独立模拟's 2.7 × `--t` took the short screen's 64–88 px
  `--t` while its text stays capped there, and its briefing's text outgrew its box at 特大 — the checker lets a scrolling
  box through; the decorative line's padding took the icon's width from the whole column, though the icon sits above the
  bottom-aligned text; the checker accepted any break between kana; the modded leftover said the host's card was taller
  than its art, while its state was entirely below it (English, Japanese) and 「Disconnected」 cut; the hub's checks of
  one height and of the plate's label passed with their rules removed, because once the cards and plates follow `--t`
  the matrix's own texts no longer reach those rules; the default-size list missed the room's briefing and badge row.

  draft opening; the research cites no order); the tooltip's placement rule (above first, below to keep clear of the

  column, one-line cards, the description moved to the brief, and the caps (names 16–20 px, the brief's text 11–13 px,
  the title 24 px, the mode line 16 px, the label 20 px with a second line, the rook 16 px, the STRONGHOLD mark 6 px),
  the key field's short placeholder (the field's name) with its 11–13 px and 观战's 14 px. The review of the second
  pass — the per-overlay wait and its bounds (2 s for the plate, 1.8 s for the banner); above 520 px the label's .46rem
  cap and second line, the rook's .4rem and the names' .36rem caps, the list's tighter spacing, the three card levels
  (full, no descriptions, names only with the effects and rounds in the brief) and their order, the chosen card's
  ellipsis at the two compact levels; the field's name as the key placeholder wherever the sentence does not fit, decided
  by the field's width. The review of those fixes — the room's button wrapping like the dialog's on every screen (its
  .46rem, the rook's .4rem, the mark's .1rem), at least 46 px with a 20 px label on a short screen; the cards' column's
  300 px floor. The text-fit review — the order of the seat card's text (the badge, 更换立绘 at the right under it, the
  tags; the operator's name over the state), the shrinking avatar frame, the spectator's eye in the mark's place, and
  every cap and width: on a short screen the badge 15 px, the state 17 / 15 px, the name plate 18 px, 邀请 18 px, the

  3.4rem card, the picker's 6.6em tiles; the hub's .3rem names, 128 px short-screen entries with 18 px names and 10 px
  English, 18 px plates there, the .56rem title, the .46rem latin title and the .15rem trophy count. The review of those
  fixes (replacing the 1rem frame, the .3rem names and the .56rem / .46rem title caps above) — a short screen's caps at
  their 中 size and half a pixel (the seat cards, the status line) or a pixel (the rest) more a step (`--t-step`); the AI
  frame's `clamp(1rem, 3rem − --t, 2rem)`; 独立模拟's 2.7 × `--t` card; the hub's 2.3 × 1.34 `--t` card with its .42 `--t`
  icon and the decorative line's padding; the title's one-line bounds (8.2em, the latin 20.2em); the short screen's 7 px a
  step for the entries; Korean keep-all on the hub's stage and the room only; the 连接中断 band hidden on a short
  screen. Their review (replacing the decorative line's padding on desktops) — the short screen's 3.4rem cap on

  the icon; Japanese `line-break: strict` on the hub's stage and in the room; the 插件内容就绪 tag hidden on a short
  screen. Also from the text-fit review and not listed there: 更换立绘's chip is rounded at `max(.18rem, 11px)` rather
  than a 999px pill, since its label may wrap (visible at every size). None changes a game rule.

  CSS background) showing no art when an outfit file fails to load — the seat cards fall back since 2026-10-22; in the

  1440×900 in Chinese, English and Japanese at 小 / 大 / 特大, 226 cases): 成就统计's banner (78 problems) — the

#### 卡片晋升图标复查（#811，维护者截图，2026-10-24）

- **问题与原因：** 第一期下半卡片视图的普通 Lv.55 与精锐 Lv.50 都画固定双条 CSS 图形；阶级徽章只加宽，
  未同步高度，1600×900 实测容器高 18.33 px、图片高 36.66 px，侵入下方等级区域约 11.67 px。
- **来源：** 官方 `activity_table.json` 的 `activity.AUTOCHESS_SEASON.act1autochess/act2autochess`
  → `charChessDataDict.status.evolvePhase/charLevel`；四个版本生成的 `chess.json.status` 与
  `backups.json.units[].forms[].status`；已有官方 `assets.ui` 的 `elite/elite_0..2` 图片。逐条对照默认缓存及
  历史版本固定的 activity 表（第一期上半 `ea164ef7`、下半 `21b1f240`、第二期上半 `1457587e`），共 906 条
  棋子记录的阶段与等级全部一致，无需重建数据。
  各版本均为一阶普通精一 Lv.55／精锐精二 Lv.50，二阶普通精一 Lv.60／精锐精二 Lv.55，三至六阶普通精二
  Lv.1／精锐精二 Lv.60。替补和自选沿所选棋子的 status 取对应 form，不按稀有度推断。
- **修改：** 调配两行各自读取记录阶段，共用 `PromotionIcon` 选择官方图标；缺图显示对应 `E0/E1/E2`。
  阶级徽章按侧栏宽度保留等高方框，图标与数字保持间距。结算去掉按阶级硬编码的阶段／等级表，读取已解析的
  干员、替补或自选记录；记录缺失时不虚构阶段，等级显示 `—`。
- **同类审查：** 调配列表、调配详情、局内详情与商店显示阶级／普通精锐标签或技能等级，没有另一套晋升阶段图标；
  名片选择器显示稀有度星数，没有晋升阶段图标；成就统计的 `achievementElite` 已按 `chess.status.phase` 取图。
- **范围：** 无新增玩法假设；侧栏颜色、间距沿用本节的 [ASSUMED] 界面布局。未修改生成数据或模拟代码，默认版本
  数据保持原字节，golden 无移动。规范同步 `design/client.md` 与 `DATA.md`。
- **回归：** `loadout-cards.test.js` 覆盖四版本所有阶级／普通精锐、全部 backup forms、E0 与缺图回退；
  `settle.test.js` 覆盖记录状态与缺失记录。`loadout-cards.e2e.test.js` 用真实 Chrome 切换四版本、五语言、
  四种文字大小；小字检查 75/80/85/90/95/100%，其余文字检查存储 75/100% 时实际按 100% 布局。三种桌面和
  三种手机尺寸检查真实图片边界、等级行互不覆盖及 `test/e2e/text-fit.mjs`，共 1440 组合。
  前后截图和说明另存维护者指定的 `handoff/round52/shots-cardview/`，不入仓库。

验证结果：定向 UI／文档检查 `# tests 70 / # pass 70 / # fail 0`；卡片真实浏览器
`# tests 11 / # pass 11 / # fail 0`（含 1440 布局组合）。结算浏览器加载素材的 10 例通过；缺图用例首次在全量
测试负载下触发 8 次焦点边框断言，单独重跑完整结算套件 `# tests 20 / # pass 20 / # fail 0`，未改焦点逻辑。
四语言包各 2334/2334，0 missing、0 errors；默认 **288** 个 golden 全部一致，未更新期望。
`npm run ci`：`# tests 10174 / # pass 10146 / # fail 2 / # skipped 26`。其中重载首领性能测试为
0.537 ms/tick（阈值 0.5），按交接要求单独重跑 `# tests 1 / # pass 1 / # fail 0`；另一项为未改动的
`test/local-extract-fx.test.js` 本机素材检查：`projectile_enemy_dwlock_line.main` 实际为
`enemy_dwlock_skill_line_01`，期望 `null`，单独运行仍失败。素材由工作树外的共享目录提供，本任务禁止改动，
因此保留并报告。CI 后续 smoke、guards 另跑通过（守卫 28/28）；lint、imports、typecheck 通过，
保留原有 145 个 lint warning 与 3 条 `sim/nodeData.js` 导入提示。

### 35.19 Quintus's tide takes the attack's turn (maintainer recordings 2026-10-22)

- **Seen:** the maintainer's two co-op recordings of **2026-10-22** (「BOSS反复释放同一技能」 1 / 2, 1920×1282, the boss
  field's fixed 2×). In recording 2 (盐风主教昆图斯, third form, shared pool ≈ 32 %) the full-field 【大潮】 dome comes
  every **1.7504 real s** (σ 0.019) = 3.50 game s, with the normal attacks going on between. Recording 1 (假想敌：胄)
  casts on its data schedule (灭顶之灾 every 25 s — the shells 12.5 real s apart —, 死亡集群 every 50 s, the ray every
  4 s): no defect found there. The boss code was the same in 0.3.1 (`b3deebda`) and the 0.3.2 development head before
  this fix, so either build shows it.
- **Official:**
  - Data: `enemy_database` → `enemy_1521_dslily` level 0: `TidewaterG2` cooldown 3.5, initial cooldown 1, `atk_scale`
    / `ep_damage_ratio` 0.2; `baseAttackTime` 3.5; every skill priority 0. The pair level `level_act1autochess_h07_04`
    `overwrittenData` changes three values: 物种爆发 (`Doom`) cooldown / initial cooldown 100 / 200 → 999 / 999, atk
    380 → 480, res 50 → 40; every other skill value is the database's. The solo template `_s` has no override.
  - Prefab (`public/assets/local/fx/index.json` → `units.enemy_1521_dslily.skills`, from the client's
    `battle/enm_pfb_18.ab`): the ability of `tidewater*` and `rockfall*` is a `MeleeAttack`, the class of the boss's own
    Attack; 断裂生殖 / 物种爆发 are `AnimatedActionToOwnerAbility`. Each ability names its clip, and the model gives its
    length (`data/assets.json` → `enemies.enemy_1521_dslily.spine.animations`; not looping): 大潮 `Skill_02` 1.667 s,
    崩坍 `Skill_01` 3.0 s, 断裂生殖 `Skill_03` 2.0 s, 物种爆发 `Skill_04` 4.467 s. The abilities' own fields in the same
    bundle (not carried into fx/index.json), on all four skills: `_maxAnimScale` 1.0, so attack speed never plays a
    clip faster, and `_affectedBySlowDown` 1 (whether a slow stretches the clip: see [ASSUMED]); those of 大潮, 崩坍 and
    断裂生殖 have the timing fields of the boss's own normal attack (`Modes/*/Body/Attack`: `_timeMode` 0; 物种爆发
    `_timeMode` 1). Each skill's `EnemySkill` has `_castLikeAttack` 0 and `_resetMainAbilityCdWhenCastEnd` 0 (see
    [ASSUMED]), and each ability `_ignorePalsyInterrupt` 0.
  - [PRTS 盐风主教昆图斯](https://prts.wiki/w/盐风主教昆图斯): 大潮 and 崩坍 are both 「触发索敌和普通攻击相同」;
    断裂生殖 「仅仍有未执行的小队动作指令时可触发」. The page's 「关于技能」 rules (the site's general enemy-skill notes):
    「大多数技能自动触发会占用攻击间隔（即可以攻击时不进行攻击，改为触发技能…）」,
    「同时存在多个可触发的技能时，优先级最高的技能优先触发，优先级相同则随机触发其中之一」,
    「同一技能的冷却时间于该技能结束时开始计时，不同技能之间互不影响」, 「绑定模式的技能只能在对应模式下进行冷却和触发」
    (each form's skills start from their initial cooldown at the change).
  - 断裂生殖's squad actions: its blackboard `branch_id` `dslily_dstnta` is a level branch of **7** phases in both
    `level_act1autochess_h07_04` and `_s` (`data/waves.json` `branches`), stepped by the prefab's `MoveNextLevelBranch`
    (`_isLoop` false) and shared by the three forms' skills.
  - [arknights.wiki.gg, Sal Viento Bishop Quintus](https://arknights.wiki.gg/wiki/Sal_Viento_Bishop_Quintus), third
    form: though the cooldown matches the attack interval, he never uses Great Tide twice in a row, "always either
    attacking once or using another ability between them".
- **Cause:** `content/enemies.js onTick` ran every content skill on its own cooldown timer beside the engine's normal
  attack (`ai.js enemyAttack`), and `kitQuintus` gave each 昆图斯 skill only its form as condition. In the third form
  that is two actions per 3.5 s: a tide every 3.5 s **and** an attack every 3.5 s, tides back to back, a tide and 断裂生殖
  in one tick — and tides with no operator to attack (a solo repro went 51 s without an attack, tides still coming).
  断裂生殖 also went on after the branch's 7th phase, repeating the last one (all four tentacles) every 18 s, as boss
  fields have no time limit.
- **Now:**
  - A content skill marked `atk: true` is cast from the attack's turn (`enemies.js attackSlotSkill`, on the module's
    `beforeAttack`): at a swing's damage frame, a ready, castable attack-class skill whose condition holds is cast
    instead of the strike, and the attack cooldown restarts as after a strike. No target, no swing, no skill. Several
    ready: the highest data priority (`ab.sk[*].pr`), equal ones at random (`b.rng`).
  - A kit can give a skill's clip length (`clip`, `enemies.js castNow`): its cooldown then counts from the clip's end.
    `kitQuintus` marks 大潮 and 崩坍 of every form `atk`, and gives all four skills their clips (`bosses.js
    QUINTUS_CLIP`). 断裂生殖 casts only while the template's branch has phases left (`P.tent` < its length); a template
    without the branch keeps the random-target fallback with no limit.
  - Third form at the 3.5 s interval: tide, attack, tide … — the tide is ready 3.5 + 1.667 = 5.167 s after its cast, so
    it comes every **7 game s** (3.5 real s at 2×). At attack speed +75 (interval 2.0) it comes every third turn, 6 game
    s. The clip keeps its length under a slow (the rule chosen here, [ASSUMED] below), so an attack interval slowed
    past 5.167 s gives the tide at every turn and no normal attack at all; the tide rate doubles at that point. 寒冷 alone
    (攻击速度 −30: interval 3.5 × 100 / 70 = 5.0 s) is 0.167 s short of it: a tide every second turn (10 s), 10 tides
    and 8 attacks per 100 game s. Any further cut on top of 寒冷 crosses it: with −3 (5.224 s), 17 tides and no attack
    per 100 game s; with 引星棘刺's 【视界】 −5 (5.385 s) a tide every 5.4 s, with −10 (5.833 s) every 5.8 s, still no
    attack. The CHANGELOG states this condition in game and real seconds. After a stretch with no target, the first
    turn goes to the tide when it is ready.
  - Forms 1–2: 崩坍 / 大潮 come at the first attack turn after their (initial) cooldown, up to one interval later than
    before, in place of that attack. A skill is ready again its cooldown plus its clip after its cast (崩坍 43 s, 大潮
    41.667 / 36.667 s, 断裂生殖 42 / 37 / 20 s, 物种爆发 104.467 s in the solo template; the pair template overrides
    物种爆发 to 999 / 999), an attack-class one then at the next attack turn.
  - A repro match (2 humans on 托管 + 2 AI, ABYSS, pool 32 %, seeds 1–3, both pair fields): tides per field over
    120 game s **34 → 12–16** (14, 12, 13, 16, 15, 13), none in the tick of an attack. Two pairs came back to back, both
    on a slowed boss, the every-turn case above: seed 1 field b2 under 寒冷 −30 and 引星棘刺's 【视界】 −5 (interval
    5.385 s, the tides 5.4 s apart), seed 3 field b1 under 伊内丝's attack-speed theft at −60 (8.75 s). 断裂生殖 per field
    over 200 game s **11 → 7**.
  - The client: each skill slot plays its official ability's clip (`tools/assets/spine.mjs PREFAB_SPINE_ROLES`;
    `data/assets.json` regenerated with `node tools/fetch-assets.mjs --offline --add-only`, its disk-dependent
    `stats.bytes` then kept at the committed value, as the `hash` covers only the body). The resolver paired numbered
    clips with slots by name (`Skill_n` = slot n − 1), which these prefabs do not follow: 昆图斯's 大潮 and 崩坍 had
    swapped clips, the third-form tide played 崩坍's 3 s `Skill_01`, 大潮 (second form) played the 4.467 s 物种爆发 clip;
    假想敌：胄's 死亡集群 played the 2.533 s `Skill_2` for the 3.833 s `Skill_3` (both 胄 records), and `_2`'s skill "3"
    (slot 2, `EmptyAnimatedAbility`) the 3.833 s `Skill_3` for its 4.433 s `Revive`; 阿利斯泰尔's `storeA` / `Suicide`
    played `Skill_3` / the primary clip for `Skill_2` / `Skill_3`. `applyRoleFix` now checks every clip of a `skills`
    map. With 官方特效 on, the generic `tide` zone no longer draws over the tide's own wave (`render/vfx/skills.js
    ENEMY_FX_COVER`, skillfx.js rule 5: the window of `tidewater*` with its cast / range rows installed); with the
    effects off or not installed it stays.
  - Normative: SIM.md §1.2 (enemy attacks), design/engine.md §7 (content modules), ASSETS.md (`skills` roles).
- **Other leaders (known deviation at the time; fixed in §35.29 — the attack's turn — and §35.48 — the slot clips):**
  - Their attack-class skills still run on their own timers (or SP) beside the normal attack: each cast is an action
    on top of the attacks instead of in place of one. The cooldown skills have cooldowns of at least 3 attack
    intervals, so none comes at every turn: 卢西恩 / 不祥幻影 `aoe` (MeleeAttack, 10 s vs an attack interval of 3 s:
    about 3.3 attacks plus an aoe per 10 s, where the PRTS rules give the aoe in place of an attack about every
    12–13 s), 假想敌：胄 灭顶之灾 (RangedAttack, 25 vs 4), 假想敌：弦 断弦之奏 (RangedAttack, 25 vs 5), 假想敌：管
    裂管之奏 (MultiRangedAttack, 50 vs 3), “萨米的意志” `Lasso` (RangedAttack, 60 vs 6). Marking them `atk` with their
    clips moves their goldens.
  - 阿利斯泰尔's 【王权号令】 `equip` / `equipA` (RangedAttack `Skill_1`, priority 1) is SP-driven, the largest case left:
    the levels' spCost 10 (`act1autochess_h07_06` / `_s`), filled by `kitLion`'s own counter at 1 SP/s
    (`bosses.js ENEMY_SP_PER_SEC`, itself [ASSUMED]), against an attack interval of 8.2 s. It comes about every 10 s on
    top of the attacks, where the PRTS rule would have it replace about every other attack. How official enemy SP
    accrues is a separate open question. Long cooldowns, attack-class too: 阿利斯泰尔 `Suicide` (MeleeAttack `Skill_3`,
    999 / initial 999, priority 5) and “萨米的意志” `Doom` (MeleeAttack `Skill_02`, 600 in `enemy_database`, 999 /
    999 in the levels).
  - “帝国的甲胄” `MultiCombat` (MultiMeleeAttack, 15 vs 5 = 3.0) already lands on its next attack (the kit arms that
    strike), and 炎佑 (an ally summon here) already casts on its attack's turn.
  - The same slot-clip cause, not changed here (each to be checked against its kit's states): 泥岩 (its two slots swapped),
    “自在” `CrossAttack` (`Skill_01_02`), 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 (`Launch_Begin` / `Back_End`), 碎铳之簧 (`Skill_b` /
    `Skill_c`), 杰斯顿·威廉姆斯, 锏 — fixed in §35.48.
- **[ASSUMED]:** (no source settles these)
  - The skill is chosen at the swing's damage frame (0.367 s into 昆图斯's 1.667 s `Attack`), not when the swing
    starts. PRTS's 「可以攻击时不进行攻击，改为触发技能」 reads as a choice at the swing's start; choosing there would
    remove both consequences below at once.
    (1) A skill that becomes ready during the wind-up takes that turn, where the swing-start reading lets that attack
    go ahead and the skill wait one turn. In an unbroken attack cycle at the 3.5 s interval no recast is affected:
    each is ready a fixed time after the turn of its previous cast (the third-form tide 5.167 s and the second-form
    tide 36.667 s after it: 1.667 s past a turn; 崩坍 43 s: 1.0 s past a turn), outside the last 0.367 s before the
    next damage frame; the first-form 大潮 / 崩坍 never recast within the form's 75 s (initial cooldown 45 / 37, then
    41.667 / 43). Only a first cast after a form change (its initial cooldown runs from the change, on the 0.25 s
    ability tick) or a cast after a broken cycle (no target, an attack-speed change) is ready at an arbitrary point
    of the cycle; each of those comes one turn early with a chance of about 0.367 / interval (≈ 10 % at 3.5 s, an
    estimate).
    (2) The swing has already fired `enemyAttackStart` when the skill takes it, so 酒神 堕梦 deals its neural damage on
    skill turns too. Against that: the tide's `EnemySkill` has `_castLikeAttack` 0, and only 14 enemy skills in the
    client's `battle/enm_pfb_*.ab` have 1 (`enemy_1567_pope` `M1Attack` / `M2Attack`, `enemy_1572_folchr`
    `PowerAttack`, `enemy_10148_nsmos` `KeepAttack` …), which weakly suggests a tide turn is not a normal attack for
    「普通攻击时」 triggers. Footage with fewer 堕梦 triggers in the third form would settle it.
    Either way a swing that 麻痹 interrupts passes its turn and the skill waits for the next (the skill abilities'
    `_ignorePalsyInterrupt` 0: 麻痹 can interrupt them).
  - The attack cooldown restarts at the skill, as after a strike, not at its clip's end. The next swing does not wait
    for a clip longer than the interval (崩坍's 3 s clip at intervals under about 3.4 s) — superseded by §35.54: a clip
    longer than the interval now holds the next attack until it ends (the cooldown still counted from the cast, so the
    two points below hold; 昆图斯's turns change only when an attack-speed gain brings the interval under a clip). Two
    points support it. A
    restart at the clip's end would make the attack and the tide ready together, both 5.167 s after a tide's cast,
    and under 「可以攻击时…改为触发技能」 the tide would take every turn, against arknights.wiki.gg's "never use Great
    Tide twice in a row". And the `EnemySkill` of every 昆图斯 skill has `_resetMainAbilityCdWhenCastEnd` 0, where 59 enemy skills
    across `battle/enm_pfb_*.ab` have 1 (in the same bundle `BounceAttackP1` / `BounceAttackP2`, `ChargeAttack`,
    `Shotgun`).
  - The skill's effect lands at the attack's damage frame, not at its own clip's (`Skill_02` 0.333 s, `Skill_01`
    0.933 s), and no attack-clip stand follows a skill turn (昆图斯 is 自缚).
  - A skill's clip keeps its length under an attack-speed cut. This is the rule chosen here, inferred from the prefab
    fields and PRTS's two rules, not observed: the abilities carry `_maxAnimScale` 1.0 like 昆图斯's own `Attack`, and
    the engine already keeps the normal attack's clip at full length under a slow (`ai.js clipSpeed`: faster only
    when the attacks come quicker than the clip), so one rule covers both clips. With 「同一技能的冷却时间于该技能结束时
    开始计时」 and 「可以攻击时…改为触发技能」 the third-form tide is then ready 5.167 s after its cast whatever the slow,
    and past about 5.17 s per attack it takes every turn: 寒冷 alone (5.0 s) still alternates, 寒冷 plus any further
    cut does not (figures under **Now**). `boss-quintus-attack-slot.test.js` (寒冷 / 寒冷 + −5) pins it.
    Alternative not taken, (a): follow the abilities' `_affectedBySlowDown` 1 and stretch the skill clip with the slow
    (`castNow`: the clip × max(1, 100 / attack speed)). The every-turn threshold would move to about 6.68 s
    (3.5 / (1 − 1.667 / 3.5); attack speed −48 or lower), so 寒冷, 寒冷 + −5 and 寒冷 + −10 would still alternate; the
    goldens `boss-boss_4-pair` and `coop2-HARD-4` would move.
  - "The skill ends" = its clip ends, for all four skills.
  - 断裂生殖 / 物种爆发 kept their own timers and did not take the attack's turn. This departed from PRTS's default
    「大多数技能自动触发会占用攻击间隔」 (no exception is noted for them) and from arknights.wiki.gg's one action ("attacking
    once or using another ability") between two tides: an attack and 断裂生殖 could both come between two tides, and
    断裂生殖 could land in the tick of a tide, 崩坍 or attack. Known deviation at the time; fixed in §35.55 (both take the
    attack's turn, with a trigger of their own: `_allowNoTarget` 1).
- **Unchanged:** the third-form ATK, +70 % [ASSUMED: inferred from where the talents sit, not from a value; medium
  confidence]. The official talents are mode-scoped: `AtkUp1` (`PassiveBuffAbility`, buff `dslily_atkup_1`) sits only
  under `Modes/G1/Talents` and `AtkUp2` (`dslily_atkup_2`) only under `Modes/G2/Talents` (fx/index.json
  `units.enemy_1521_dslily.talents`), whereas a unit's always-on talent sits under every mode it applies in
  (`char_340_shwaz` talent 1 under Default / S2 / S3; `enemy_1535_wlfmster` `Passive` under M1 / M2, `Passive2` under M2
  only); PRTS 「第三形态：攻击力+70%」. arknights.wiki.gg's +110 % (= 40 + 70) cites no source. Official footage with the
  third-form panel ATK at base × 2.1 would change this (`boss:grow` would then get one key per form).
- **Tests:** `test/content/boss-quintus-attack-slot.test.js` — third form: never two tides in a row, the tide never
  passed over for an attack once its cooldown has run from its clip's end, every other turn at the 3.5 s interval and
  every third at 2.0 s, every second under 寒冷 (5.0 s) and every turn with no normal attack under 寒冷 + −5 (5.385 s); one
  action per interval among tide / 崩坍 / attack; the tide still global; no target → no tide; after an idle stretch the
  first turn is a skill; two ready skills → either, by seed, and the one raised to priority 1 first whatever the seed
  (either way round); 断裂生殖 exactly 7 casts with the branch (20 s apart in the third form), more without it;
  `QUINTUS_CLIP` = the slots' clips in `data/assets.json`; first form: 崩坍 / 大潮 at the first turn after the initial
  cooldown, 断裂生殖 on its own timer; the solo template (no override): 物种爆发 at 200.5 / 305 / 409.5 s, its initial cooldown
  after the third form starts, then cooldown 100 + its 4.467 s clip apart; an attack-class skill its leader cannot cast
  (silenced, the skill made silenceable for the test) leaves every turn to the strike and takes the first turn after the

  `applyRoleFix` on a `skills` map, the prefab animKeys when the fx index is extracted); `test/assets.test.js` (昆图斯 / 胄
  / 阿利斯泰尔 slots); `test/render/vfx-areas.test.js` (the tide covered by its window; not by 崩坍's, nor with the prefabs

### 35.20 Verified skill attack clips

- **Seen:** §34.31's deferred sweep found casts or a death clip selected for attacks while a skill was on:
  德克萨斯 S1/S2 Front `Skill`, 伺夜 S1/S2 Front/Back `Skill1/2`, 归溟幽灵鲨 S1 Front `Skill_1`,
  焰影苇草 S2 Back `Skill_2`, 耀骑士临光 S2 Front `Skill_2_Die`. These are seven skill slots on five operators;
  the additional 郁金香 row needs the separate identity correction in §35.21.
- **Official:** read the installed `public/assets/local/fx/index.json` units' modes, skills' uses and switch links,
  `.cache/gamedata/excel/character_table.json` and `skill_table.json`, and parsed the installed Front/Back `.skel`
  animation lists / OnAttack events. Cross-check the local client's `charpack/<charId>.ab` mode abilities and
  AnimatorHooker `_replaceAnimPairs`, `chararts/<charId>.ab` DefaultSkin `_animations`,
  `battle/prefabs/[uc]skills.ab` and `config/buff_template_holder.ab`. The relevant chains are:

  | Operator / skill identity | Official cast / mode / skin chain | Attack while the skill is on |
  |---|---|---|
  | 德克萨斯 `char_102_texas`, S1 `skcom_charge_cost[3]` (prefab `skcom_charge_cost`), S2 `skchr_texas_2` | Both duration 0; no switch. S1 ActiveBuffAbility grants DP; S2 MultiRangedAttack casts `Skill`. Default's raw `_animKey` is empty; its attack animator uses `_oneshotAnim=Attack`, `_beginAnim=Attack_Begin`. Default has no hook; DefaultSkin maps Attack → Attack_Loop, Attack_Begin → Attack_Start, Attack_End → Attack_End. | Normal `Attack_Loop`; Front changes, Back already uses the normal role. |
  | 伺夜 `char_427_vigil`, `skchr_vigil_1/2` | Both abilities are AnimatedActionToOwnerAbility casts (`Skill1/2`); neither switches mode. Default raw `_animKey` is empty, attack animator `_oneshotAnim=Attack`, `_overrideDownAnimation=1`; no Default hook. DefaultSkin maps Attack / Attack_Down identically. S3 alone switches to mode 1 with its own idle. | Normal `Attack`, Front DOWN `Attack_Down`, both models. |
  | 归溟幽灵鲨 `char_1023_ghost2`, `skchr_ghost2_1` | `ghost2_s_1` switch_mode_restart_fsm initially selects mode 2 Empty (no attack). `ghost2_s_1[trigger_ability]` starts ExchangeHp (`Skill_1`) and on its trigger switches to mode 3 S1. S1/Body/Attack uses Attack, with no hook; DefaultSkin maps Attack / Attack_Down identically. | Normal `Attack` on both models, downward role when available. Never infer an attack from Empty or repeat ExchangeHp. |
  | 焰影苇草 `char_1020_reed2`, `skchr_reed2_2` | Skill_2_FireBall is AroundTargetRangedAttack with `_animKey=Skill_2`; no switched S2 mode. Default/Body/Attack uses Attack, no Default hook, identity skin mapping. S3's hook instead maps Attack → Skill_3_Attack and Idle → Skill_3_Loop. | Normal `Attack` on both models; S3's held loop / own strike stays unchanged. |
  | 耀骑士临光 `char_1014_nearl2`, `skchr_nearl2_2` | AppearSkill, passive 27 s at level 10; template nearl2_s_2 switches to mode 2. S2/Body/Attack uses Attack_Loop; its hook replaces Die → Skill_2_Die and Stun → Die, with no Attack replacement. DefaultSkin maps Attack_Loop identically. | Normal `Attack_Loop`; Front changes, Back already uses the normal role. |

  The prior no-OnAttack sweep missed 归鲨 Back (`Skill_1` event 0.2 s) and 苇草 Front (`Skill_2` event 0.167 s).
  The same verified mode/skin mapping applies there too, so both are corrected with their other facing. A cast's
  event does not turn it into the later attack. 灰毫 S2 is a further guard: its raw Attack is replaced by
  Skill_Loop / Skill_Loop_Down / Skill_Loop_Up by the respective S2(LR/D/U) hooks; do not generalize raw keys.
- **Cause:** the name-based skill family was reused for every strike. The legacy index omits attack-animator
  `_oneshotAnim`, mode replacements and skin key-to-clip tables; simply reading its raw animKey is insufficient.
  In particular, Specter's first switch describes the cast interval and Texas/Vigil's empty keys hide the animator.
- **Now:** `skillAttack.js` adds only those seven verified slot/skill identities to the existing `SpineActor._attackClip`
  normal-role path. It works with no index, missing entries and late index/model arrival; no fetch is added. A
  different explicit plugin skill is excluded. The installed model supplies its attack triple and downward variant,
  preserving Front/Back selection, mirroring and skin roles. Casts, held loops, own idles, other skill slots and
  substitute forms retain their existing paths. This changes attack selection only; in particular it does not
  redesign Nearl's skill-start/death handling or claim a new simulation attack during an instant skill window.
  Normative updates: design/client.md §9, ASSETS.md post-processing and the DESIGN index.
- **[ASSUMED]:** as in §34.31, cosmetic models use their declared normal attack role, and a missing normal clip
  retains their old skill fallback. The existing downward-role convention is retained, including Specter's
  Attack_Down even though S1's raw `_downAnimKey` is empty. Unverified operators, custom replacement skills and
  other slots retain their prior logic. No new gameplay rule, downloaded art, generated-data or golden change.
- **Tests:** `skill-attack-candidates.test.js` adds 18 tests: each mapping with/without an index, repeated
  wind-up/attack and recovery, both models/four facings, cast events, common-skill / DIY / record resolution,
  alternate skins / missing clips / held loops, own idles and real UnitView late-load/slot/facing changes.
  `skill-attack-mode.test.js` moves its unverified no-event guard from now-verified Texas to Gnosis.
  The optional real-Spine browser test has 14 cases (seven slots × two index paths), each checking four directions,
  visible strike pixels, the next attack, skill off and return to Idle. It writes 56 PNGs to
  `$SP_CLIP_SHOTS` (when set) using installed art at runtime; no official files become test fixtures.
  All 14 cases passed before the merge (`SP_E2E=1` or `RENDER_E2E=1` runs it). In a real battle, check the normal swings on every facing, especially 归鲨 Back / 苇草 Front,
  and the preserved Texas cast, 苇草 S3 held loop, 灰毫 S2 and other skills' own idles in a real battle.

### 35.21 Tulip's S2 candidate remains unimplemented

- **Seen / cause:** §34.31's candidate table labels 郁金香's `Skill_2 → Attack` as S2. A numbered animation is
  not sufficient evidence of an equipped skill slot; that row conflated the two.
- **Official:** local character_table `char_513_apionr` has only `skchr_apionr_1` (只余芬芳); skill_table contains
  no `skchr_apionr_2`. The installed effects index and committed operator Spine manifest have no char_513_apionr
  entry. Reading her actual charpack/chararts gives Default Attack, identity skin keys Skill / Skill_2 / Skill_End.
  In `[uc]skills.ab`, `skchr_apionr_1/Skill` attacks with Skill, while its `Empty` ability uses Skill_2 without
  waiting for OnAttack and ends with Skill_End. Both are branches of S1, not a second skill.
- **Now:** leave the named S2 candidate unchanged on Front and Back; add no slot, renamed operator or guessed
  mapping. All other named candidates are implemented in §35.20. This supersedes the earlier row's claimed S2
  identity without rewriting the historical 0.3.1 account.
- **[ASSUMED]:** none added for Tulip. A future plugin with different skill data needs its own verified mapping.
- **Tests:** the resolution guard asserts char_513_apionr slot 1 has no override. No gameplay or golden change.

### 35.22 Official versions groundwork (M0)

- **Requested:** the maintainer's goal after 0.3.1 (2026-10-22): besides today's game, play the earlier states of
  卫戍协议：盟约, chosen by a lobby switch and in a room by vote, like 重刷. This section records M0, the invisible
  groundwork that ships in 0.3.2; the switch itself (M1) followed in 0.3.2 too (§35.23), and the earlier versions'
  rules (M2) come later.
- **Official:** the event ran twice. `act1autochess` (2025-11-14 – 12-12) is the official 「卫戍协议：盟约」 that the
  community and PRTS call the 上半期 (PRTS [卫戍协议：盟约](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议：盟约)); `act2autochess`
  (2026-03-14 – 04-25) its 下半期 (PRTS [卫戍协议：盟约 下半](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议：盟约_下半), news 9697).
  Each has two sub-versions in `autoChessData.versionInfoDict`: V1_1 from 2025-11-14 16:00, V1_2 from 2025-11-25 16:00
  (the second batch of 10 strategies), V2_1 from 2026-03-14 16:00, V2_2 from 2026-03-27 16:00 (更新#2: 终极模拟, 4 new
  strategies, the operator rotation; PRTS 下半, 3-27). Official data: Kengxxiao/ArknightsGameData, `zh_CN/gamedata/`:
  - `8303c922` (2025-11-14): the 上半 launch, V1_1;
  - `ea164ef7` (2025-11-18): the 上半 hotfix, still V1_1;
  - `23c99d5d` (2025-11-25): V1_2 and its levels; `21b1f240` (2025-12-05): 上半 final; `ee2cabff`: the last enemy change
    inside 上半;
  - `12e0a020` (2026-03-10): rewrote `act1autochess` into the 下半 schema, so master holds no pure 上半;
  - `1457587e` (2026-03-14): the 下半 launch, V2_1; `987b4cd5` (2026-03-17): a battle-only fix inside V2_1 (below);
    `86b6e749` (2026-03-27): V2_2; master: today's data (V2_2 with the April level and text fixes).

  PRTS [卫戍协议/帮助](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议/帮助) holds the skill-strategy history.
- **Decisions (the maintainer, 2026-10-22, two rounds):**
  - **Four versions, named by period** (the maintainer: 「去年年末的叫第一期，今年3月的第一次改动前叫第二期上半吧，我们现在的叫第二期
    下半（去年的我不记得了，要是中间也有改动就也分）」). The remake's 第一期 is the official `act1autochess`, 「卫戍协议：盟约」
    上半期活动; its 第二期 is `act2autochess`, the 下半期活动; each period's 上半 / 下半 are the official sub-versions
    V1_1 / V1_2 and V2_1 / V2_2. The registry ids and directory names stay the official ones; only the labels use the
    periods. PRTS uses 「第一期」 for something else, the 2024 卫戍协议 (`act1vautochess`, BWIKI 卫戍协议/1), which is
    not done.

    | label | official | id | window |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | 第一期上半 | `act1autochess` V1_1 (上半期, with the 11-18 hotfix) | `act1autochess@V1_1` | 2025-11-14 16:00 → 11-25 16:00 |
    | 第一期下半 | `act1autochess` V1_2 (上半期, final) | `act1autochess` | 2025-11-25 16:00 → 12-12 |
    | 第二期上半 | `act2autochess` V2_1 (下半期, until 更新#2) | `act2autochess@V2_1` | 2026-03-14 16:00 → 03-27 16:00 |
    | 第二期下半 | `act2autochess` V2_2 (下半期, today), the default | `act2autochess` | 2026-03-27 16:00 → 04-25 |

    **Wording.** A bare 上半 / 下半 in these docs and in code comments keeps the official activity sense: 上半 = 第一期
    (`act1autochess`), 下半 = 第二期 (`act2autochess`). A period's halves are always written with the period (第一期上半 …)
    or as the sub-version (V1_1 … V2_2).
  - **V1_1 includes the 11-18 hotfix** (`versionInfoDict` counts it as V1_1, and 7 of its 11 days ran on it).
  - **第一期下半 is V1_2's final state**, `21b1f240` (2025-12-05), as the table above names it. V1_2 opened on 11-25 with
    `23c99d5d`, and the official game ran without the 12-05 changes until then. They change play, not only texts:
    - activity_table: 莎草 (`chess_char_2_06`) gains the module `uniequip_002_papyrs` 小小的种子 as her default and backup
      module (elite ATK 412 → 450, DEF 150 → 173 with it), and 10 bond descriptions are reworded ("在场N人" → "在场N名不同
      【X】干员");
    - the character group (character / skill / uniequip / battle_equip; from 11-14 to 11-25 only the hotfix's two unread
      skill rows changed): 娜斯提
      (`char_4212_nasti`, with her 3 tokens) joins the owned 自选 pool (71 → 72), and the 自选 forms gain the modules
      `uniequip_003_ray` and Mon3tr's `uniequip_001_monstr` / `uniequip_002_monstr`;
    - the enemies at `ee2cabff` (12-05 17:15), which equal `21b1f240` for the autochess enemies;
    - battle templates, which the build does not read (§35.22.3 "Battle data inside a sub-version"): 灵巧's range check,
      回收利用's 地面 check and 迅捷作战粮's `_forceFlag`.

    Open for the maintainer, given 「要是中间也有改动就也分」: should 11-25 → 12-05 be a sub-version of its own? It would be
    one more profile (activity_table and the character group at `23c99d5d`, whose character, uniequip and battle_equip
    tables equal `8303c922`'s and whose skill_table equals `ea164ef7`'s, the rest at `23c99d5d`) and would differ from
    第一期下半 in chess, bonds and backups, and in battle by the three templates above.
  - **第二期上半 is V2_1 with the 03-17 fix** [ASSUMED], by analogy with V1_1 and its 11-18 hotfix. `987b4cd5`
    (2026-03-17, three days into V2_1) changed only `battle/buff_template_data.json`, among it 精准's ATK, so V2_1 ran on
    two battle data sets. The build does not read that file, so `data/versions/act2autochess-v2_1/` is the same either
    way; the sim follows the texts, which match the post-fix state (§35.22.3 "Battle data inside a sub-version"). Open
    for the maintainer: is 03-17 the 「第一次改动」 he meant, or 更新#2 on 03-27 as built?
  - **M0 ships in 0.3.2**, recorded here; its CHANGELOG line is under 0.3.2 工具.
  - A player's version is a **browser preference**; the server only enables versions and names a default. A room has
    **one version**, switched by unanimous vote like 重刷 (M1).
  - The remake's deliberate deviations and convenience features carry over to every version. Where an earlier version's
    mechanics differ, its text wins (M2, §35.22.7–§35.22.9). 上半's solo mode is timed (the official 300 s per round).
    `Match({ soloUnlimited: true })` opts out. The room control is the follow-up in §35.23.10 (default off). A lone
    human in co-op stays untimed as today, and co-op opens with 1–4 players.
- **Packaging (resolved for 0.3.2):** `data/versions/` is tracked in the repository, so the full package, the update zips
  and Docker carry it (`tools/package.mjs RUNTIME_DIRS` includes `data/`): 48 files, 15 014 606 bytes on disk (about
  15.0 MB; about 1.3 MB of a zip, `zip -r -X` as the packager runs it). Since M1 (DESIGN §35.23) a server that enables a
  version serves that directory and its match hosts read it. A version the server does not enable still answers 404;
  narrowing to 第二期下半 alone answers 404 for the whole tree (§35.23.11). Nothing set serves the enabled directories
  (`server/http/static.js isVersionData`, the on-disk path, so `/data/Versions/…` on a case-insensitive disk too). The
  build tag counts only the served version directories (`server/http/buildTag.js`), so rebuilding an unserved directory
  moves no open page.

**Milestones** (M0–M2 are built: M1 is §35.23, and M2's core rules and kits are §35.22.7–§35.22.9; M3 is the plan [ASSUMED]):

| | content | players see |
|---|---|---|
| **M0** (§35.22.1–§35.22.6) | registry, per-version build profiles and the three earlier builds; behaviour keyed by charId / effectId instead of the default data's ids; the default pinned by tests | nothing |
| M1 (§35.23) | one match process per version, the room's version and its vote, the lobby switch, per-version settings and logs; the static server serves `data/versions/` | the switch, on unless the server narrows the list (§35.23.11) |
| M2 (§35.22.7–§35.22.9) | the earlier versions' fidelity: the rule switches, the same-key-different-meaning audit, the blackboard gaps and the rest of §35.22.5, per-version research data, golden and bot runs. The core rules, the 19 charId kits, the review of the 31 borrowed kits, and the 自选 tier-kit borrows are done; the remaining audit gaps stay open | 第一期 and 第二期上半 playable |
| M3 | translations, the asset union, a version-differences page | polish |

#### 35.22.1 The registry and the version identity

`shared/versions.js` is pure and browser-safe. It defines `DEFAULT_VERSION = 'act2autochess'` and a frozen `VERSIONS`
table (the four versions of the decision above, in that order: the default first), with the helpers `versionById`,
`versionSlug` and `versionOfData`.

- An id is the official activity id (activity_table `AUTOCHESS_SEASON`). A sub-version adds `@<versionInfoDict id>`;
  the id without one is the season's final sub-version.
- Paths and file names use the slug: lowercase, with no `@` (`act1autochess-v1_1`, `act2autochess-v2_1`).
- Data, rooms and logs without a version mean the **code constant** `act2autochess`, never a server setting. A server
  that changes its default therefore never re-labels old logs or replays.
- A non-default build carries `config.version = { id, family, rules }` as the last key of `config.json`. The default
  build has no such key. `versionOfData(data)` reads that key.
- The labels (第一期上半 / 第一期下半 / 第二期上半 / 第二期下半) are msgids in the four UI packs; their translations are
  [ASSUMED]: zh-TW the same words, en "Season 1 · Part 1" … "Season 2 · Part 2", ja 「第1期・前半」 … 「第2期・後半」, ko
  「1기 · 전반」 … 「2기 · 후반」.
- There is no `data/versions/index.json`: M1 reads the registry.

#### 35.22.2 The default version is pinned

The switch must not move the default by one byte. The original cs is the exception: it hashes every served code file,
so any code change moves it, and only its formula is pinned. The guard was recorded on the untouched tree before any
other M0 change, in `test/versions-default-guard.test.js` and `test/fixtures/versions-default-guard.json`:

- **G1**: the sha256 of the 22 tracked default data files (top-level `data/*.json` and `data/i18n/*.json`, never
  `data/versions/`) and of the golden corpus `test/golden/*.json`. Hand checks with `git diff` must use the pathspec
  `':(glob)data/*.json'`. A plain `'data/*.json'` also matches below `data/` and lists every version file. G1 is the
  M0 merge check: it proved that the M0 commits moved neither, and it was **deleted when M1 started** (§35.23.8). Since
  then `test/golden.test.js` and the rebuild check of `test/data.test.js` guard both, as before M0. Until then an intended
  golden update or default data rebuild refreshes the fixture in the same commit
  (`SP_UPDATE_GUARD=1 node --test test/versions-default-guard.test.js`, named in `test/golden/README.md` and DATA.md).
  The fixture holds `db8e226d`'s hashes. When the branch M0 merges into has changed a pinned file since then (the 0.3.2
  integration branch did: §35.19 moved `data/assets.json`, `test/golden/fields.json` and `test/golden/matches.json`), G1
  fails on the merged tree although M0 moved nothing, and regenerating it blindly would hash the merged tree and prove
  nothing. The merge therefore, with `<m0>` the M0 branch tip, (1) confirms that
  `git diff $(git merge-base <base> <m0>) <m0> -- ':(glob)data/*.json' ':(glob)data/i18n/*.json' ':(glob)test/golden/*.json'`
  is empty (the pathspec names exactly the files G1 pins; M0 edits `test/golden/README.md`, which G1 does not), (2)
  runs `node tools/golden.mjs` on the merged tree (every scenario unchanged), and only then (3) regenerates
  the fixture with `SP_UPDATE_GUARD=1` in the merge commit, whose message says the base moved and by which sections.
- **G2**: the default `config.json` has no `version`. The gameplay file lists and the vanilla content cover exactly the 16
  top-level files, nothing under `data/versions/`.
- **G3**: the cs formula, through known-answer vectors of `shared/plugins/identity.js`, and `CONTENT_HASH_VERSION` 1.
- **G4**: a fresh co-op / NORMAL `room.state` deep-equals the fixture. The code, ids, name and `content.cs` are
  normalised; `content.cs` must equal the welcome's vanilla hash. The room carries no `version`. The welcome's `versions`
  list is pinned as `welcomeVersions` (each `cs` normalised: the default as `<vanilla cs>`, the others as `<cs:id>`). A
  server narrowed to 第二期下半 alone still omits `versions` (§35.23.11).

G2–G4 stay after M1.

`node tools/golden.mjs` reports every scenario unchanged: roster 49, bonds 46, fields 22, matches 21, standins 10 and diy
140. `test/versions-keying.test.js` checks the default data against `test/fixtures/versions-keying-baseline.json`. That
baseline was frozen on `db8e226d` before the keying change. It covers only what M0 changed: the item handler key of
every item, the battle-effect list, the meta wraps and the 18 `item:` entries of the default meta registry with their
hooks. It is never regenerated: an intended change of an item handler or item override edits its entry by hand. The
tests pin which handler key an item reaches, not the handler's body; a swapped body is caught by `test/golden.test.js`.

#### 35.22.3 Build profiles and the data of the earlier versions

`node tools/build-data.mjs --version <id>` builds a version into its directory. An unknown id or a missing value exits
with code 2. Each version has a profile `tools/versions/<slug>.mjs`, which names:

- the pinned commit of each file group;
- the field adapters (`tools/versions/adapters.mjs`);
- the research directory;
- which 下半-measured tables apply;
- `hiddenCore`, the rule switches and the expected counts.

The default profile changes nothing. DATA.md "Official versions (M0)" holds the full contract: pins, adapters, the
keyed build tables, the hidden inputs and the warning classes.

| | chess | visible non-DIY | bonds | bands | items | effects | garrisons | enemies | bytes (15 built files) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 第二期下半 (`data/`) | 266 | 112 | 23 | 40 | 115 | 361 | 249 | 249 | 5 810 393 |
| 第二期上半 V2_1 | 240 | 115 | 23 | 36 | 115 | 361 | 229 | 249 | 5 404 644 |
| 第一期下半 V1_2 | 200 | 95 | 18 | 30 | 89 | 281 | 174 | 197 | 4 852 025 |
| 第一期上半 V1_1 | 200 | 95 | 18 | 20 | 89 | 281 | 170 | 197 | 4 756 074 |

- The three builds pass validation with 0 errors. Each version directory holds 16 files: the 15 built ones and a byte
  copy of `tuning.json`. Offline rebuilds are byte-identical (`test/data-versions.test.js`).
- **Pins.** V1_2 reads `21b1f240`, with enemies at `ee2cabff`. V1_1 reads `8303c922`, with activity_table and the
  `character` group at `ea164ef7`, and has no enemy pin, so no V1_2 enemy record leaks in. V2_1 reads `1457587e` for
  everything: no file the build reads changed before `86b6e749` (the GitHub history of each path; range, uniequip,
  battle_equip and enemy_database last changed at `12e0a020`), and the levels changed next at `a24a8ad1` (2026-04-08).
  What V1_2 takes from the 12-05 commit is listed in the decisions above.
- **The levels of 第一期's halves differ.** V1_1's levels are the launch's. The V1_2 update `23c99d5d` changed only the
  three solo hidden-core levels `level_act1autochess_h08_01_s` / `_02_s` / `_03_s` (no other act1autochess level file
  has another GitHub blob sha between `8303c922` and `23c99d5d`): their routes end at the 传送 entrance (1,3)
  (`tile_telin`) instead of the protection point (2,2) (`tile_end`), as today. In `waves.json` that is
  `routes[].end` [2,2] (V1_1) against [1,3] (V1_2), 22 changed values; V1_2's levels at `21b1f240` equal `23c99d5d`.
- **Battle data inside a sub-version.** `battle/buff_template_data.json` (the client's battle templates) is not a build
  input, so its changes leave `data/versions/` as it is. Two of them fall inside a sub-version (a script comparing the
  file at both ends; the keys are M2 references):
  - `987b4cd5` (2026-03-17, "Data:26-03-17", the only file it changed besides the resource manifest), inside V2_1:
    `act1autochess_bond_eff_preci` drops the `AutoChessFilterCharacterBondIds [preciShip]` condition on its ATK
    multiplier, so 精准's ATK reaches every operator holding the buff, not only 精准 members;
    `act2autochess_bond_eff_kazimierz[ex]` `_noSource` false → true; `act2autochess_bond_eff_siracusa[ex][buff]` and
    `act1autochess_debuff_1` `_attackType` NORMAL → ADDITION; `act2autochess_equip_acarm117_global_buff` (天马之枪)
    `_noSourceDamage` → true; `enemy_actoxi_deadrattle` (假想敌：蚀裂's death cloud) a new branch for TRAP targets. The
    sim implements 精准 from its text, which is the post-fix state: tier 2 buffs RANGED non-members
    (`bonds/addon/battle.js`). Whether it matches the other flags is an M2 check.
  - `21b1f240` (2025-12-05), inside V1_2 (most of its 891 changed keys are other modes' or schema defaults):
    `act1autochess_bond_eff_skillful` (灵巧) `_checkTargetInRangeId` `x-1` → `x-4` (a 13-tile diamond → the 3×3 of its
    text's 周围8格); `act1autochess_band18_buff` (回收利用, `aceffect_band_38` of 休谟斯) a new `ON_BUFF_START`
    `CheckBuildableType MELEE` that ends the buff on a non-地面 owner, as its text says; `act1autochess_acarm051_buff`
    (迅捷作战粮) `_forceFlag` false → true.

  The file's GitHub history inside the two seasons has no other change within V1_1, V1_2 or V2_1: the hotfix `ea164ef7`
  did not touch it, `23c99d5d` and `86b6e749` open sub-versions, and `fdf34ff5` (12-12, the season's last day) changes
  no autochess key. V2_2's own changes (from 04-01) are today's data and outside M0.
- **V1_1 with the 11-18 hotfix.** Every change of `ea164ef7` the build reads is in activity_table. Among them:
  - leader HP ×0.75 on Funny / Normal and −5…−12.5% on Hard;
  - `modeFactorInfo.SINGLE` 1.0 → 1.25;
  - `borrowCount` 8 → 20;
  - the enemy effects 攻坚装备II / III (`aceffect_enemy_4` / `_5`: effects 279 → 281);
  - 战场 m06 weight 40 → 60; m07 weight 40 → 5 and Hard modes only (taken out of both Normal modes, whose stage pool
    drops from 6 to 5, as in V1_2);
  - 墨浪 / 枯朽萃聚使徒 special-enemy weight 10 → 0;
  - the leader weight of `boss_2` (假想敌：铳) 5 → 2;
  - 9 more enemies inactive in the Normal modes (one of them, 沉沙 `enemy_1207_sfji`, also in the Funny modes);
  - 拟态物质's `effectDesc` (`eff_acarm069` and the golden `eff_acgarm069`): the operator gained is the 初始干员, of the
    same bond. `effects.json` and the item descriptions carry it; the wording is a note for 拟态物质's M2 handler.

  The commit made the same wording change in skill_table, in the two `sktok_acarm069_1` / `_2` descriptions, so the
  `character` group is pinned there too; the build does not read those rows (the output is byte-identical with the
  launch tables), and character / uniequip / battle_equip are the same at both commits. Its client drag fix is not a
  build input. Before this decision V1_1 was the launch day (`8303c922` for everything): `bosses`, `config`, `effects`,
  `enemies`, `factions`, `items` and `stages` changed with the switch; the bb-audit and keying expectations did not.
- **V2_1.** Its table already lists the 终极 modes (`mode_single_abyss` / `mode_multi_abyss`, `startTime`
  1774598400 = the start of V2_2): the adapter `launchModes` drops every mode whose `startTime` is later than the
  earliest mode's, so `config.json` has the seven launch modes and no stage names an absent mode; the 功勋 levels follow
  the same rule (below). The bosses keep the official ABYSS pool of `1457587e` (`boss_1` 2 400 000, raised to 3 600 000
  by V2_2), which no mode reads. Its chess and item ids are today's for the same records: the 120 shop rows are a subset
  of today's 133.
- **The 功勋 levels at a launch.** Both launch tables list all 60 levels of `milestoneList`, but levels 36–60 carry the
  `availableTime` of the second sub-version (1764057600 = 2025-11-25 16:00 at `8303c922` / `ea164ef7`, 1774598400 =
  2026-03-27 16:00 at `1457587e`). The adapter `launchMilestones` keeps the levels whose `availableTime` is the earliest,
  so V1_1's and V2_1's `config.rewards.milestones` (the settlement's LV strip, `public/js/screens/settle.js`) stop at 35;
  V1_2 and the default keep 60.
- **Adapters for 上半.** `bondActiveType` converts 上半's bond activation (ALL with the deck → BOARD_AND_DECK; MANI → BOARD
  with `['1']`, as 下半's maniShip) [ASSUMED]. `selfBackupPreset` makes 盟约·辅助干员 a PRESET backup with its default
  skill and module, as 下半 writes it [ASSUMED]. V2_1 has 下半's schema and takes neither.
- **Keyed build tables.**
  - The shop-excluded items and the item rules are keyed by the normal item's effectId, and research 04 by trapId.
  - The `choices.json` pools are keyed by effectId / charId. An entry the version lacks is dropped: 上半 sells none of
    野鬃, 灰毫 or 远牙 as shop chess (远牙 is a 自选 operator there), so `pool_char_pinus`, which draws shop chess, is empty
    [ASSUMED]; V2_1 has every entry.
  - The operators of a hand-written chess pool follow the version's trait text where it names others (the profile's
    `pools`): 歌蕾蒂娅's `garrison_39` grants 「斯卡蒂、幽灵鲨或安哲拉」 in 第一期 and 「斯卡蒂、幽灵鲨或深巡」 since 下半, so both
    第一期 builds list 安哲拉 (`char_218_cuttle`, shop chess `chess_char_3_10`) in `pool_chess_glady` where the default
    lists 深巡 (`char_4137_udflow`, shop chess in 第一期 too, so the lookup by charId alone kept him). Every other pool
    reference of 第一期 (佩佩's 幸运硬币 / 萨尔贡浓茶 / 黄沙罗盘, 洛洛, 凯瑟琳, 维多利亚) agrees with its text, and V2_1's
    `garrison_39` is the default's. `test/data-versions.test.js` pins the three names per directory against the text.
  - `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS` (§21.29) is keyed by charId and applies only over an official TAKE_DAMAGE row. 上半 has no TANK
    row, so neither 上半 build carries a deviated skill (`expect.triggerDeviations` `'none'`). V2_1 has the row; its
    profile's `'present'` checks the deviations of the operators it has, as `'default'` does for every one: 雷蛇
    (`char_107_liskam`) joined with V2_2.
- **What 第二期上半 takes from the default build.** Same season, so by id or name [ASSUMED]: the research (its profile names
  `docs/research/` itself: the bond specs by bondId, the item kinds by trapId, the enemy and stage notes, the core numbers
  of research 01 — the enemy multipliers per mode and round from PRTS 下半 — all written on V2_2); the four 下半-measured
  tables: the 机变 events (`effectChoiceInfoDict`) and the shop rows (`trapShopChessDatas`) are byte-identical at
  `1457587e` and today, the bounty cards have the same ids and draft data (one text differs: 悬赏·折射II
  `enemyeffect_15_7`, 「深池重甲卫队队长」 in V2_1, 「卫士队长」 since 04-14), the stock (路标档案馆 BV1eLXXBqEgF,
  2026-03-29, V2_2) and the 22 matches of `test/fixtures/official-bounty-drafts.json` ("the end of March 2026", either
  sub-version) name only operators and items V2_1 has (`stockByName` / `itemByName` throw otherwise); `hiddenCore`
  350 / 1200; and the default's rules (`rules: {}`: every 下半 rule switch dates from 2026-03-14 — news 5114: untimed
  solo, bench equipment and full-bench swap, the shared boss; PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助: the deploy order). Its `constants.singleReconnectTime` is its own constData's 86400.
- **Left out for 上半.** The four tables measured on 下半 (the stock overrides, the shop draft, the tactic draft and the
  bounty drafts) do not apply, so those draws are uniform [ASSUMED]. Leaving out the stock table removes only the
  per-identity overrides: the tier copies stay the literals cited for 下半 (below). Every research-derived 上半 field keeps
  its 下半 value until a per-version research folder exists [ASSUMED]. PRTS dates the solo 70% / co-op 80% enemy
  multipliers from 11-18, which the hotfixed V1_1 now matches. The research overlays keyed by bondId and trapId also write
  下半's descriptive texts into 上半: `bonds.json` `spec` (上半's 炎 gets 下半's third tier and "hits up to 3 targets") and
  the items' `implFormula` / `howToPlay`. The sim reads none of them; M1's screens (S.W.E.E.P., the guide) must not show
  them as 上半 rules. The same holds for 第二期上半: its overlays are the default's, written on V2_2, and several contradict
  V2_1's own blackboard — 奥术法阵's `implFormula` names the 失能 (`silence`) V2_1's item lacks; 奇迹's `spec` has
  p = 0.18 + 0.003·L and 20 funds every 100 layers (V2_1: base 0.25, 10 every 50); 投资人's the third tier at L ≥ 100 (V2_1:
  70); 远见's "replaces the above" at 150 layers (the 3-27 rule; V2_1's 150-layer clause stands apart); and 阿戈尔's
  algorithm 「更靠左和靠上」 (V2_1's text: 「靠下和靠左」). M2 adds `docs/research/versions/act2autochess-v2_1/` overrides,
  or M1's screens leave these texts out on V2_1.
- **Carried over for 上半 [ASSUMED].** The bounty cards' `draft` / `draftPool` classification is not one of the four
  tables: `HIDDEN_BOUNTY_IDS` and the effect-id shapes of `bountyDraftPool`, read from 下半's PRTS table and screenshots,
  still sort 上半's 93 cards (excluded perfect 20, unseen 14, hidden 4; drafted boss 19, initial 18, hunter 18), and the
  match honours them. M2's research decides 上半's own pools. Two more build rules sourced only from 下半's PRTS record
  (「卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录」) apply to 上半 outside `buildConfig` and the four tables:
  - `DIY_EXTRA_PROTOTYPES` (`tools/build-data.mjs`): the tier-5 自选 slots also offer the six 4★ prototypes (先锋 and 特种
    left out), so both 上半 builds' `backups.json` `diy.prototypes` has 15 at tier 5 and 9 at tier 6, as the default.
    PRTS's 上半 page 卫戍协议：盟约 does not mention 4★ prototypes in 自选; M2 checks the rule against 上半.
  - `BAND_SWAP_LPR` 1: 鸭爵's swapped-in enemies cost 1 LP at the protection point (下半's record, §策略 鸭爵 备注). V1_2's
    `band_ducklord` carries the same `round_start_all_player_change_enemy_2` enemylist, and the build reads it from the
    effect (`aceffect_band_49`), not the band, so both 上半 builds' `enemies.json` give `enemy_2001_duckmi_2` /
    `enemy_2002_bearmi_2` `lpr` 1 (V1_1's table already carries the 鸭爵 effect, unused there: V1_1 has no 鸭爵 band).
- **Config.** 上半's `hiddenCore` 300 / 1000 comes from PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约, and `tuning.json` is the same for every
  version [ASSUMED]. The 上半 rule switches are inert in M0: `deployOrder: 'rowBottomUp'`, `soloTimed`, no bench
  equipment, no full-bench swap and no shared boss. 上半's constData has no `singleReconnectTime` (`12e0a020` added it),
  so both 上半 builds write `constants.singleReconnectTime: null` instead of the 下半 value. The lobby's own fallback then
  keeps a dropped solo run resumable for 24 h [ASSUMED] until 上半's solo exit rule is decided, together with the timed
  solo.
- **Config carried over unreviewed [ASSUMED].** `tools/build-data.mjs` writes many rule constants as literals (its
  `buildConfig` return block is the full set), and every version build copies them as measured on 下半 — for V2_1 the
  same season. Among them: `bans` (NORMAL / HARD 3 core + 4 addon; 下半 has 8 core bonds, 上半 6, so the same draw bans
  half of 上半's), the `hiddenCore` extras (`minTeamLpExclusive`, `checkedAfterRound`, and `difficulties`, which names
  ABYSS although neither 上半 nor V2_1 opens an ABYSS mode), `trophies`, the boss overtime and HP scale, `unite`,
  `finalAssault` and `bandDraft`; the economy literals (the tier and item copies `poolCopies` / `itemPoolCopies`, which
  every 上半 chess and item uses with the stock table left out, the golden and merge counts, the co-op pool multiplier,
  the reward offer, the freeze, the temp / equipment / arts-per-round sizes and the alternative income cap); `dp`
  {10, 1, 99}; the timer literals `spFirst` 30, `bandTurn` 50 (the owner's 下半 report of 2026-10-10) and
  `soloPrepTimeData` 300 (tied to 上半's timed solo, Q7); and the title stat rules. They equal the default, so the
  blackboard audit cannot list them (§35.22.5). DATA.md `config` lists the values.
- **Loader.** `server/data.js` provides `versionDataDir`, `getVersionData` (lazy, cached and frozen) and
  `resetVersionData`. Nothing at run time calls them in M0. `loadData(DATA_DIR)` reads the top level only.
- **`config.version` is the gate.** Data that carries it (a version's build) finds hand-written behaviour by charId and
  effectId (§35.22.4). The default data and the plugin content sets built on it carry none and keep every by-id lookup
  of before M0: a plugin `data.patch` that sets an official chess record's `charId` (a reskin, say) keeps that chess
  id's kit, and one that sets an official item's `effectId` keeps the item key's behaviour. The AI overlays use the same
  gate. Two changes reach those content sets, both fixes. (a) 蒸汽之心's hammer list (`items/battle.js`) is now read once
  per data object instead of once per process, so a Studio trial of a plugin content set (`server/studio/trialRunner.js`,
  which records the vanilla battle first) uses that set's 蒸汽之心 hammers and big-hammer buffs, as a match host does,
  rather than those of the first data the process loaded. (b) The map-character shape check of `auto_chess_change_map`
  (§35.22.5, 森蚺): a plugin band whose key names device aliases is no longer team support. The kits' group-list
  additions (安哲拉, 星源, §35.22.4) are gated on `config.version`, so a Forge pack or a §34.16 official operator outside
  the roster that fields either on the default data keeps the lists of before M0.

#### 35.22.4 Behaviour keyed by charId and effectId

上半 reuses the default data's ids for other things. 50 of its 100 shop chess ids name another operator than 下半:
`chess_char_1_02_a` is 讯使, not 角峰. 72 item ids carry another effect and 6 are absent in 下半. On a version's build
(`config.version`, support `isVersionData`) the hand-written behaviour of the id-reused tables, chess (kits, traits,
tokens) and items, is found by what the record *is*. Data without `config.version` — the default and the plugin content
sets on it — resolves exactly as before (§35.22.2, §35.22.3). V2_1 reuses no id for another record, so on it the new
lookups give the default's answers.

Band and bond ids still key behaviour by id on every data: `bands/meta.js` (`band_cathy`, `DUCK_BAND` `band_ducklord`),
`bands/battle.js AMEDIC_BAND`, `server/match/gamedata.js` (`band_bldsk`, `band_cannot`), `bonds/core.js INSTALLERS`
(`kjeragShip: installKjerag` …) and `memberOf(…, 'yanShip')` in `items/battle.js`. That is correct while a version
reuses those ids only for the same band or bond: every build does (checked: the 29 V1_2 / 19 V1_1 / 36 V2_1 shared band
ids keep the default's name and effectId, and the 18 / 23 shared bond ids their name and effectId; only 上半's
`emptyShip` activeCount and 炎's thresholds differ, both documented). `test/versions-keying.test.js` pins it per
directory for the code-keyed band ids and every shared bond id, so a later build (`SP_VERSION_DATA_DIRS`) that reuses
one of them for another entity fails there.

- **Kits** (SIM.md §7.2).
  - `KIT_CHAR_IDS` names the charId each tier kit was written for. The first line of every kit file names the same one.
  - `officialKitKey` uses the by-id kit only when it was written for the record's charId. Otherwise it takes that
    operator's tier kit (the one at the record's tier when the operator has two: 锡人 → `2_19`, 耶拉 → `3_20`, 华法琳 →
    `4_26`), then a 自选 `op-*` kit registered under the charId, then none (the generic kit).
  - Test fixtures and plugin `chess_x_` records keep the by-id lookup, and so do stand-ins and 自选 pieces
    (`KITS[charId]`).
  - A chess whose own kit was written for its operator but failed to load stays generic, as on the default data; it
    never takes the operator's other tier kit. A `char_x_` charId on an official id keeps the by-id kit. Both rules are
    tested through `officialKitKey`'s injectable registries (`{ kits, kitCharIds, tierKeys }`, tests only).
- **Items** (SIM.md §7.2, META.md §2.1).
  - `items/battle.js BY_EFFECT`, 蒸汽之心 (`eff_acarm079`) and its hammers, the `items/meta.js` overrides and 凯瑟琳's
    one-cell allowance (`eff_acarm072`, official normal records only; counted at the prep's pick and in her later offers)
    all go through the normal record's effectId. On the default data they keep the item key (`DEFAULT_ITEM_KEYS`,
    `MUTATION_CELL_ID`, each override's default key).
  - A meta override is bound by its effectId **and** the buff key it reads (`items/meta.js OVERRIDES`): the same
    effectId can carry another mechanic. 上半's 寻呼模块 (`eff_acarm075`) is one special refresh showing operators of one
    bond (`use_equip_add_goods_rule_with_refresh_shop`), not 下半's pick of one of three same-bond operators, so it binds
    nothing until M2 writes its mechanic. The battle handlers already return when their buff is missing.
  - Item keys stay the identity of combo partners, buff keys and fx labels, which the golden digests hash.
- **Traits and tokens.** 远牙's dollSwap trait is the one his own traits grant, found by `char_430_fartth`. 夕's
  generic-kit dragon follows `char_2015_dusk` on official chess.
- **`bonds/core.js reached()`.** On a version's build a threshold the record lacks counts as not reached. Before this
  change, 上半's 炎, which has no 9-member tier, would have summoned two 炎佑 at 6 members. Data without
  `config.version` keeps the old rule (reached), so a plugin or Forge core bond without the threshold plays as before.
- **AI and game texts.** The community overlay and the fixed `data/i18n` overlay are keyed by default ids, so they
  apply only to data without `config.version` (AI-STRATEGY.md §2). The client uses the same gate
  (`public/js/data.js versionFiles`): files whose config carries `version` take only their content set's own game texts
  (`setContent` `overlay`; a per-version overlay is M3), never the language folder's `data/i18n/<lang>.json`. The
  per-text staleness hash (`shared/i18nData.js textHash2`, 2 base-36 characters) cannot stand in for that gate: applied
  to 上半's files the default overlay changes 7 leaves on V1_2 and 6 on V1_1 in each of en, ja, ko and zh-TW (下半's
  战栗维式重锤 text on `chess_item_2_03_e_b`, `eff_acarm054`'s +40% HP, `garrison_69_b` without 上半's "若同一行有至少3名干员",
  `band_dusk` / `aceffect_band_45` with 下半's 【墨色真颜】 clause, `allybuff_select_2_2`'s name). V2_1 takes no overlay
  either until M3 (its changed texts — 陈's 高台 clause, 远见, 投资人, 奇迹 — would get today's translation). `localeName`
  still maps Chinese names, which mean the same operator in every version.

**Kit resolution on 上半** (96 normal shop records; V1_2 and V1_1 resolve identically):

| resolution | count | operators |
|---|---|---|
| own id, same operator | 46 | — |
| tier kit by charId | 19 | 幽灵鲨 1_04→2_07, 拉普兰德 1_07→2_16, 蛇屠箱 1_10→3_16, 角峰 2_03→1_02, 深巡 2_07→1_04, 宴 2_09→1_18, 锡人 2_13→2_19, 耶拉 3_14→3_20, 初雪 4_03→3_14, 山 4_08→5_17, 忍冬 4_10→3_18, 华法琳 4_11→4_26, 异客 5_02→6_05, 歌蕾蒂娅 5_06→4_12, 银灰 5_14→4_22, 瑰盐 5_17→4_08, 锏 6_02→6_19, 纯烬艾雅法拉 6_09→6_20, 泥岩 6_16→4_18 |
