The tier kits were written at their 下半 tier and the `op-*` kits for 自选; both are reviewed against 第一期's records in
§35.22.9. `officialKitKey` counts a charId kit that is not a tier key as `op`, so the pinned split is own 46, tier 19,
op 31 (the 12 and the 19), pending 0. `PENDING_KIT_CHARS` is empty.

**Kit resolution on 第二期上半**: all 116 normal records (the 115 shop chess and 盟约·辅助干员) take their own id's kit. The 8 operators V2_2 sent away
(红豆, 地灵, 崖心, 协律, 见行者, 巫恋, 蜜蜡, 瑰盐) are shop chess in V2_1 and hidden records today, with their kits; the 8
that V2_2 re-tiered (锡人, 耶拉, 白面鸮, 百炼嘉维尔, 魔王, 华法琳, 妮芙, 录武官) sit at their V2_1 tier under the old ids,
whose kits were written for them there.

**自选 and 补位.** Every 补位 stand-in resolves as on the default data: a stand-in kit, or a 预备干员 with the generic
kit. 上半's owned 自选 pool (72 operators in V1_2, 71 in V1_1: 娜斯提 came with the 12-05 data) holds 24 that 第二期下半
(today's data) sells as shop chess: 星熊, 莫斯提马, 塑心, 安洁莉娜, 空弦, 迷迭香, 焰尾, 瑕光, 卡涅利安, 伺夜, 远牙, 耀骑士临光, 焰影苇草, 百炼嘉维尔, 荒芜拉普兰德, 凛御银灰, 圣聆初雪,
溯光星源, 夕, 隐德来希, 号角, 玛恩纳, 仇白 and 妮芙. V2_1's (67 operators) holds 4: 银灰, 空弦, 卡涅利安 and 伺夜, shop
chess since V2_2. A 自选 piece keeps the by-charId lookup. Those 25 (the 24, plus 银灰, who is not in 第一期's pool) borrow
the tier kit under the charId (`ops/ver-diy.js`, §35.22.9), are in `KITTED_CHARS` and are not keys of `OPERATOR_KITS`.
`PENDING_DIY_KIT_CHARS` is empty. Shop chess of the same operator still take the tier key.

**Group lists.** Some kits name the members of a character_table group by charId, because no built record carries
`groupId` (the `raw.groupId` fallback of `isAbyssal` never fires). 安哲拉 (`char_218_cuttle`, groupId `abyssal`) is shop
chess in both 第一期 builds (`chess_char_3_10_a`, a charId kit, §35.22.9), and only 斯卡蒂's list named her. On a version's build
(`config.version`) M0 adds her to the 深海猎人 lists of 歌蕾蒂娅 (阿戈尔的波涛's regen and damage cut), 浊心斯卡蒂
(捕食习性's upper ATK step) and `shared/tier5.js` (归溟幽灵鲨's 深海猎人 HP, 乌尔比安's hunter kill and blood share), and
星源 (`char_135_halo`, shop chess in 第一期) to 缪尔赛思's 莱茵生命 list (`abyssalOf` / `rhineOf`). Data without
`config.version` keeps the lists of before M0. Neither operator is in today's data or in V2_1, so the official default
data could not move either way, but a Forge pack (`server/plugins/forgeImport.js`: a pair's unit is its charId when that
is an official operator, with no groupId) or a §34.16 official operator outside the roster can field them on the default
data, and there they count as before. 伊芙利特 and 淬羽赫默, also shop chess in 第一期, stay out of 缪尔赛思's list: both
are 自选 picks today, so adding them would change the default (§35.22.5). The other hand-written group lists are
complete on every build: `STUDENTS` (`op-headb2.js`, `op-poca.js`) matches character_table's teamId `student` and
`SUI` (`op-shu.js`) its groupId `sui` (the 第一期 tables lack 望 `char_2027_wang`).
`test/versions-keying.test.js` pins, per directory, the 深海猎人 members the lists see and the shop 莱茵生命 members
缪尔赛思's list misses (`GROUPS`; a directory without an entry fails), and that a plugin content set on the default data
fielding 安哲拉 or 星源 keeps the old lists.

**Items.** On 上半 44 records dispatch to 22 battle handlers, each the handler of its own effectId. The meta overrides
bind 天师古鼎 `chess_item_6_02_e`, 教鞭, “神秘顾客” (both of its overrides) and 画卷. 紧急调度券 (`eff_acarm115`) does not exist
in 上半, and 寻呼模块 `chess_item_5_03_e` carries another buff: neither binds anything. 蒸汽之心 is `chess_item_6_04_e`, and
its hammers are `chess_item_2_03_e`–`2_06_e`. On V2_1 everything resolves as on the default data: 54 records, 27
handlers, 82 with a battle part, the default's meta keys, hammers and cell. `test/versions-keying.test.js` pins these
counts, the kit splits and the bound keys for each directory (`EXPECT`); a directory without an entry fails.

**The `reached()` proof.** The change cannot alter the default data (and, gated on `config.version`, no data without it):

- Statically: every core bond's main buff carries `power_bond_char_cnt` (5 or 6), and 炎's `ex_bond_char_cnt` is 9.
- Dynamically: a temporary counter on the unchanged `reached()` saw 1349 calls over the 288 golden scenarios and 211
  calls over `test/content/*.test.js`, none with a missing threshold.

#### 35.22.5 The blackboard audit (the M2 work list)

`node tools/bb-audit.mjs --data <dir>…` (with `--json` and `--strict`) reports five kinds of gap for each version
directory, compared with the default data:

- the blackboard fields the version lacks that the code reads with a default, flagged when the default is not neutral;
- the buff keys the code names that the version lacks;
- the skill trigger rules of its chess, backups and tokens that the engine does not implement
  (`shared/plugins/skillSpec.js TRIGGER_RULES`);
- the keys the version carries that the default data lacks and no code under `server/` or `shared/` names, each with
  its records: the buff key and `bbStr.key` of bands, bonds, items and effects, and each garrison's `effectType`,
  `effectKey`, `battleRuneKey` and `bbStr.key`. The first three compare records by id or key, so they never see these.
  Such a mechanic is silently inert: `garrisons/meta.js` runs `H[effectType]` and `garrisons/battle.js`
  `INSTALLERS[key]` only when a handler exists, without a warning;
- the blackboard fields the version carries under a behaviour key and buff the default data also has, where the default
  lacks the field (the first class the other way round), each with the code's reads of that name. A neutral default for
  a field the version lacks is harmless only when the version lacks the mechanic, not when it carries the mechanic under
  another field: those clauses look neutral in the first class and are inert. The reads are context, not a filter: a
  name the code reads elsewhere (`silence` for enemies) does not show that the clause's handler reads it.

`kits/` is skipped for the field and buff-key classes, because kits read skill blackboards. The talents of
`enemies.json` and `tokens.json` are outside every class, so a field `server/sim/content/tokens.js` reads by name is
never compared; 炎佑's V1_1 gap below comes from a hand diff of every same-id enemy and token blackboard. Fields are
matched by name across tables, so the audit over-reports (for example `atk`) [ASSUMED]. The default data audited against itself has no
finding. No class sees a record whose text changed under the same keys and blackboard fields; those are listed by hand
below (same key, different meaning).

| | field gaps (non-neutral default) | absent code-named buff keys | unknown trigger rules | version-only keys no code names | version-only fields |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 第二期上半 V2_1 | 0 (0) | 3 | 0 | 2 | 0 |
| 第一期下半 V1_2 | 32 (9) | 43 | 1 | 14 | 12 |
| 第一期上半 V1_1 | 36 (9) | 45 | 1 | 14 | 12 |

**第二期上半: what V2_1 lacks or changes against today** (each an M2 item unless the data already carries the difference):

- **Lacks:** the 4 strategies of 更新#2 (铃兰 `band_lisa`, 杰西卡 `band_jesica`, 缪尔赛思 `band_mlyss`, 芬 `band_fang`); 13
  shop entries — 雷蛇, 伺夜, 空弦, 银灰, 卡涅利安 and the 8 re-tiered entries (`chess_char_2_19` 锡人, `3_20` 耶拉, `4_21`
  白面鸮, `4_23` 百炼嘉维尔, `4_25` 魔王, `4_26` 华法琳, `5_22` 妮芙, `5_23` 录武官); the 10 traits `garrison_151`–`160` (20 records)
  (their `SERVER_ADD_MULTIPLE_BOND`, absent code-named key); 伺夜's `token_10028_vigil_wolf`; the 终极 modes; the 9 自选
  operators released after 03-14, absent from the `1457587e` character_table: 予愿安洁莉娜 `char_1015_aglna2`, 怒潮凛冬
  `char_1051_headb2`, 凯尔希·思衡托 `char_1052_kalts2`, 维伊 `char_4226_veen`, 可露希尔 `char_4228_closur`, 谬因
  `char_4229_aphris`, 机械师 `char_4230_mcnist`, 克莱门莎 `char_4231_clemnt` and 珊比 `char_4235_thumpy` (`backups.json`
  `diy.operators` 82 against 87; `ownedPool` 67 against 72, as 72 − 9 + the 4 V2_1-only picks 银灰, 空弦, 卡涅利安 and 伺夜,
  which are pending and dropped by the kitted gate, §35.22.4; `excluded` 5 against 7, without 焰狐龙梓兰
  `char_1048_orchd2` and 结城理 `char_4217_makoto`; `test/data-versions.test.js` pins the three sets). 怒潮凛冬 is not in
  V2_1 at all. All of it follows from the data.
- **奥术法阵** (`eff_acarm048`) without its 失能 (`silence_attachment`, added by V2_2): absent code-named key, and the
  handler returns without its buff, so V2_1's item does what its text says.
- **远见** (`visiShip`, `bondeffect_visi`): V2_1's 80-layer and 150-layer discounts use `bond_layer_char_goods_price_bond_discount`
  and `bond_layer_char_goods_price_discount` (上半's key), the two version-only keys; the code reads V2_2's
  `bond_multi_layer_char_goods_price_bond_discount` (absent code-named key), so V2_1's discounts are inert.
- **陈 以己之长** (`band_chen`, `aceffect_band_39`): V2_1 turns the damage of 高台 operators into 弱点伤害, V2_2 of every
  operator. The keys and blackboard are the same, and `bands/battle.js act1autochess_band19_buff` applies V2_2's every
  operator: V2_2's meaning silently applies (same key, different meaning).
- **阿戈尔** (`bondeffect_egir`): V2_1's text has the swallow order 「靠下和靠左」, today's 「靠左和靠上」 (changed on 04-14,
  after V2_2 opened; `bonds/core.js` implements today's). M2 question: a text fix or a rule change?
- **Numbers the data carries** (the code reads them from the blackboard): 投资人's third tier at 70 layers (V2_2 100),
  奇迹's 50 layers → 10 funds and refresh chance base 0.25 (V2_2 100 → 20, 0.18), 商业包装方案 every 7 sold (8), 余's
  HP 32 (27), the bonds of 锡人, 调香师, 魔王 and 异客 (改盟约 on 3-27), and 30 traits with other numbers, owners or
  texts (19 of them with other texts).
- **Faithful V2_1 data that differs from today** (the data carries it and the code reads it, so no code change; today's
  data is V2_2 with the April fixes and later modules; `test/data-versions.test.js` pins each set):
  - 节日气球 `enemy_10040_cnvbln` does not count in the total (`notCountInTotal` true, no `seasonOverride`), so its leak
    costs no LP (`server/match/fields.js`, `server/sim/battle/spawns.js`); today it counts. 圆仔 `enemy_2085_skzjxd` / `_2`
    is NORMAL, today ELITE. Both come from `level_autochess_enemy_data`, whose `enemyDbRefs` grew from 2 to 4 on 04-14
    (`86da4cfa`).
  - 卢西恩's boss waves `act2autochess_h07_05` / `_h07_05_s`: route 8 (route 4 of `_s`) and extra route 3 patrol 3
    checkpoints; today's repeat each one into a 19-checkpoint loop (04-14, `86da4cfa`).
  - The modules added after 03-14: 13 elite records offer one module fewer — `uniequip_003` of 忍冬 `3_18_b`, 信仰搅拌机
    `4_01_b`, 缄默德克萨斯 `4_16_b`, 烛煌 `5_03_b`, 乌尔比安 `5_05_b`, 史尔特尔 `5_07_b`, 浊心斯卡蒂 `6_04_b`, 焰影苇草 `6_08_b`,
    妮芙 `6_10_b`, 仇白 `6_15_b` and 锏 `6_19_b`, and `uniequip_004` of 归溟幽灵鲨 `5_13_b` and 百炼嘉维尔 `5_18_b` — and the
    summons of `6_04_b` and `5_05_b` lose those modules' `byModule` entries (`tokens.json`); the `1457587e`
    `uniequip_table` lacks them (`char_1012_skadi2` has 001 / 002 there). Four 自选 forms lack one too: 菲亚梅塔
    `uniequip_004_phenxi`, 提丰 `uniequip_004_typhon`, 黍 `uniequip_003_shu` and 娜仁图亚 `uniequip_003_narant`
    (`backups.json` `units`, with their `moduleNames`).
  - 异客 `chess_char_6_05_b`'s RA-A module `uniequip_004_pasngr`: the `1457587e` battle_equip has only the rank-0 机理分析
    candidate (`pasngr_t_1[enhance].damage_scale` 1.2), so with that module picked 机理分析 deals ×1.2 at every potential;
    today's table adds the 潜能 5 candidate (×1.25, the base talent's value). `shared/loadoutRecord.js composeTalents`
    merges the module's blackboard over the base talent, so the difference shows only when a loadout picks this module
    (`uniequip_002_pasngr` is the default). Faithful data, the only numeric chess difference besides the missing modules.
  - The 自选 operators' powers and bonds come from the 03-14 character_table (`backups.json` `diy.operators`): 鸿雪
    `char_4055_bgsnow` (powers rhodes + ursus) and 真言 `char_4204_mantra` (rhodes + elite) are 盟约·辅助干员 (`emptyShip`),
    not 萨尔贡 (`sargonShip`; today rhodes + sargon and rhodes + elite + babel + sargon), and seven more have one power
    fewer: 凯尔希 `char_003_kalts` and 阿斯卡纶 `char_4132_ascln` and 逻各斯 `char_4133_logos` (babel), 斩业星熊
    `char_1044_hsgma2` (higashi), 赤刃明霄陈 `char_1050_chen3` (rhodes), 弑君者 `char_1502_crosly` and 电弧 `char_4195_radian`
    (ursus). The `1457587e` character_table gives 鸿雪 the subPower ursus and 真言 none.
  - Texts only, among them: 悬赏·折射II (`enemyeffect_15_7` and its bounty card, 卫队 / 卫士, 04-14); the term markup of six
    enemies' abilities (`enemy_2008_flking`, `9011`, `10028`–`10030`, `10144_xdelk_2`) and 节日爵士乐手
    `enemy_10034_cnvsax`'s ability format; the skill or talent texts of six operators (菲莱 `chess_char_3_06`, 阿罗玛
    `4_10`, 乌尔比安 `5_05`, 圣聆初雪 `6_02`, 忍冬 `3_18` and 焰影苇草 `6_08`, both forms: term markup, 「攻击力 30%」,
    「（+2）」 against 「（+2%）」); the skill or module texts of 自选 forms (淬羽赫默, 提丰, 老鲤, 艾丽妮, 斥罪, 莱伊 and 琴柳) and
    of two summons (Mon3tr, 望's stone).
- **Unchanged against today:** the skill trigger rows (today's adds 怒潮凛冬's, `char_1051_headb2`, 2026-04-08, an operator
  V2_1 does not have), the
  机变 events, the shop rows, the stages (apart from the 终极 modes in their mode lists), every other enemy and every
  other wave apart from the 终极 modes in its `usedBy` (33 waves list them today).

**第一期** — the non-neutral gaps; each would silently apply the 下半 meaning and is an M2 item:

- 突袭 `no_attack_duration`, read with 10 in `bonds/addon/battle.js`;
- 拉特兰桥夹 `max_trigger_cnt`, read with 3;
- steadShip `damage_scale`, read with 1;
- 炎's 9-member fields (`atk` and others), which `reached()` now handles.

The neutral gaps are harmless only where 第一期 lacks the mechanic. Three bond clauses carry it under fields 下半 does not
have (the version-only fields, the same 12 entries in both 上半 builds: each bond and its `bondeffect_*` mirror), so they
are silently inert, each an M2 item:

- 维多利亚's 6-member clause, `power_atk` 0.8 (「所有携带装备的【维多利亚】干员攻击力+80%」): `bonds/core.js installVictoria`
  reads 下半's `atk_normal_equip` / `atk_golden_equip`, a neutral gap with default 0;
- 精准's 3-member 失能, `silence` 2 s (「攻击使目标失去特殊能力2秒」): `bonds/addon/battle.js` applies only the DEF / RES
  penetration;
- 突袭's 30-layer clause, `power_respawn_time` 1 and `power_cost_scale` 0.5 (「<达到30层>所有干员的部署费用-50%」): the code
  reads 下半's `power_attack_speed`, a neutral gap.

The same class lists four garrison fields. 坚守's `garrison_69` (蛇屠箱's trait) carries 「若同一行有至少3名干员」 as
`check_count` 3 with `conditionkey` `character_same_row` under `SERVER_ADD_BOND`, and `garrisons/meta.js` runs
`rowCondition` for `SERVER_POOL_CHAR` and `SERVER_MOST_BOND` only, so its +3 / +6 layers come without three in a row: an M2
item. The other three are read: `garrison_44`'s row gate (`act1autochess_gar_event_useskill`, `garrisons/battle.js
conditionMet`) and `garrison_88`'s `character_target_inboard` under `SERVER_ONCE_GOLD` (`server/match/effectsMeta.js`, for
every garrison).

Besides those:

- V1_1 also lacks 协防干员's (emptyShip) `damage_scale_normal` / `damage_scale_extra`.
- 缪尔赛思's 莱茵生命 list (`kits/ops/chess_char_6_11-mlyss.js`: her talent's SP on their skill casts and deploys, and 开源节流's
  deploy-cost cut) leaves out 伊芙利特 (`chess_char_6_12_a`) and 淬羽赫默 (`chess_char_6_15_a`), shop chess in 第一期. Both are
  自选 picks today, as are 麦哲伦, 多萝西 and 娜斯提, which the list also leaves out on the default (淬羽赫默's own list in
  `op-slent2.js` counts every member). M2 question for the maintainer: do 自选 莱茵生命 members count for 缪尔赛思?
- V1_1's 炎佑 (`enemy_9012_acloon`, the 炎 bond summon) has `2.ep_damage_scale` 1.15, not `2.damage_scale` 1.2.
  `server/sim/content/tokens.js` reads the latter with 1, so its 元素脆弱 aura does nothing on V1_1 although the ability
  text still promises it. V1_1's own 炎 text (`bonds.json` yanShip) says 「周围敌人受到15%的元素脆弱」 (20% from 11-25),
  which matches 1.15: M2 decides whether to read `ep_damage_scale` as that 15% 元素脆弱 [ASSUMED] or as a scale of the
  gauge fill. Its base template is 6000 HP / 300 ATK, against 12000 / 600 from 11-25 (enemy_database changed at
  `23c99d5d`), and its skills' cooldown (祛恶之焰, `skills[].cooldown`) is 25 s, against 15 s from 11-25 and today:
  faithful data, read as is (`tokens.js yanyouKit` takes the cooldown from the record). The
  hand diff found no other same-id enemy or token blackboard key gap, apart from 上半's missing `1.attack@max_target`,
  which reads correctly as single-target.
- The absent buff keys include the `act2autochess_band12` / `band3` band buffs, the 叙拉古 / 卡西米尔 / 萨尔贡-share bond
  keys and the `act2autochess_equip_acarm*` keys, three of which make these items inert in 上半 until M2: 伪装服, 海沟实验体
  and 耶拉冈德之泪.
- Several 下半 prep keys are also absent. Among them is the key 寻呼模块's override reads; 上半 names its own,
  `use_equip_add_goods_rule_with_refresh_shop`, and the override binds nothing there (§35.22.4). M2 writes that
  mechanic.
- **森蚺's `auto_chess_change_map`.** Its key is Touch's, which the default data has, so no class reports it (same key,
  different meaning).
  上半's 森蚺 (`band_zumama`, `aceffect_band_43`, both builds) carries Touch's key with device aliases
  (`trap_1104_aclasert#1`…`#4`: one 双眼皮 on every field, the alias format of 机变 cards). It is a device switch, not a
  map character. `bands/battle.js isMapCharBuff` accepts only Touch's map-character shape, so 森蚺 reports no battle part
  and the AI playbook does not class it as team support. M2 builds the device switch (`server/sim/content/devices.js`
  already implements the 双眼皮). This shape check (map characters `char_*#n`) is not gated on `config.version`: it
  applies to every data set, so a plugin band on the default data whose `auto_chess_change_map` names device aliases is
  no longer team support either (it was at db8e226d). Such a band spawns nothing in battle, so the new class is the
  accurate one; with the hammer cache it is one of the two M0 changes that reach content sets on the default data
  (§35.22.3). V2_1 has Touch (`band_amedic`), as
  today.
- **协防干员's damage clause.** In V1_2, 上半's emptyShip has 下半's keys (`damage_resistance`, `damage_scale_normal`
  1.2, `damage_scale_extra` 1.4), but its text drops 下半's 【协防干员】 scope: "所有干员受到的物理和法术伤害-20%，造成伤害提升至
  120%，若为精锐状态则改为造成伤害提升至140%". `bonds/addon/battle.js` gives the 1.2 / 1.4 to emptyShip members only. If 上半
  meant every operator, the 下半 meaning applies silently. PRTS records the clause as a mid-season "盟约效果追加" without
  saying who receives it. M2 question: all operators or only members? (Its `activeCount` is 3 in 上半 against 2 in 下半;
  `bonds.json` `spec` still says "2 distinct", the research-overlay note of §35.22.3.)
- **The 6 上半-only enemies have no attack clip.** 墨浪 `enemy_1201_sfzhi`, 莱茵生命防卫科成员 `enemy_1249_lysdb`, 实验用动力装甲
  `enemy_1254_lypa`, 萨卡兹王庭军术师 `enemy_1275_dwlock`, 巫王乐团圆号手 `enemy_1373_ltnhor_2` and 残党乐团鼓手
  `enemy_1376_ltndru` (both builds) are absent from `data/enemies.json`, and `data/assets.json`, the manifest
  `enemyAttackAnim` reads, holds only 下半's models. They get no `attackAnim`, so the sim uses `ATTACK_PAUSE` instead of
  the clip timing (`attackStand` / `attackWindup`); 墨浪, 军术师 and 圆号手 are ranged (range 2 / 2.5 / 1.5). This changes
  sim behaviour, so it is M2 work, not M3's visual asset union. Adding their models edits `data/assets.json`, a default
  data file and a hidden input of every version build: rebuild every version directory (§35.22.6) (G1, which pinned
  them, was deleted when M1 started, §35.23.8). A non-default build warns once per enemy whose model the
  manifest lacks, so the list stays visible in `.cache/build-data-report.<slug>.json`. V2_1's enemies are today's
  records (with the differences listed above) and have their models.
- **Version-only keys** (the same 14 in both 上半 builds; each is an M2 item):
  - four 上半 traits with no handler: 煌's `SERVER_GAIN_CHAR` (`garrison_52_a` / `_b`, 售出时 she becomes 烛煌, who counts
    as both 炎 and 维多利亚), 蜜莓's `SERVER_ADD_BOND_ACTIVATED_RANDOM` (`garrison_58`), 伊芙利特's
    `SERVER_ADD_BOND_FRONT_ALL_LAYER` (`garrison_83`) and 极光's `act1autochess_gar_event_recoverhp_enemy_abflag_inrange`
    (`garrison_27`);
  - 华法琳 / 寒芒克洛丝's grant to the operator in front (`garrison_72` / `_73`, `battleRuneKey` `give_garrion_to_front`):
    上半 spells the field `give_garrion_id`, and `garrisons/battle.js` reads `give_garrison_id`, so the trait is never
    granted;
  - the 标准模拟 band clause `act1autochess_band31_buff` ("<标准模拟> +30% ATK/DEF/HP") on `band_bldsk`, `band_amiya`,
    `band_duyaoy` and `band_sarkazb`, bandIds 下半 also has;
  - 灵巧's 20-layer award `bond_layer_refresh_shop_award_equip` (the first refreshed equipment of a prep costs 1) and
    远见's 100-layer `bond_layer_char_goods_price_discount` (every operator in the shop costs 1 less);
  - six 上半 equipment keys: `sell_char_count_gain_equip_owner_original` (商业包装方案, `eff_acarm074`),
    `use_equip_give_char_to_player_most_bond` (信标, `eff_acarm109`), `use_equip_add_goods_rule_with_refresh_shop`
    (寻呼模块, above) and `act1autochess_equip_acarm055` / `078` / `102_global_buff`.
- **`bans`.** The per-match bond ban draws 3 of 上半's 6 core bonds, a 下半 count (§35.22.3, config carried over); very
  likely not 上半's rule.
- **`AUTO_STOP`.** 上半's 斩业星熊 (`char_1044_hsgma2`, a 自选 operator) S3 `skchr_hsgma2_3` triggers on `AUTO_STOP`, one
  of the 15 rows of 上半's skillTriggerDataList. The engine does not implement it (`server/sim/skills.js`), and
  `validateAll` accepts it. M2 builds it; until then 上半's 自选 must not offer 斩业星熊, whose S3 would fire under a rule
  the engine does not know. He is not in 上半's shop and reaches 上半 only through 自选, so leaving him out there is the
  cheaper fix (the local round-50 design notes, §2.9).

`test/versions-bb-audit.test.js` pins, on every version directory: the field gaps (上半: the 突袭 and 炎 findings; V2_1:
none), the unknown trigger rules (上半: exactly `AUTO_STOP`; V2_1: none), the version-only keys with their records, the
version-only fields (上半: the 12 above; V2_1: none), and V2_1's three absent code-named keys.

#### 35.22.6 Tests and what stays open

**Tests:**

- `test/versions-default-guard.test.js`: G1 (until M1, refreshed at the merge only after the checks of §35.22.2; deleted
  as M1 started) and G2–G4.
- `test/build.test.js`: a file under `data/versions/` does not move the build tag.
- `test/data-versions.test.js`:
  - the registry (with the four labels), the profiles (V1_1's hotfix pins, V2_1's launch pins, adapters, research,
    tables, rules and deviation check) and the `--version` CLI;
  - the committed directories (their counts written out, the 功勋 levels, no 终极 mode, no stage naming an absent mode,
    the deviations present in V2_1 without 雷蛇) and their offline rebuild;
  - V2_1's same-id records that differ from today's data (bands, effects, enemies, waves' routes, the chess records'
    module ids and whole modules, their skill / talent texts, tokens, the 自选 operators' powers and bonds, the 自选
    forms' module ids), as listed in §35.22.5;
  - the adapters (`launchModes` and `launchMilestones` included), the keyed pools and the loader;
  - the static server answering 404 for `data/versions/` in every spelling and 200 for the default data, nested
    `data/i18n/<lang>.json` included, and for a sibling whose name only starts with `versions`.
- `test/content/kits_ver_m2.test.js`: the 19 charId kits, the 25 borrowed 自选 kits, 泥岩's version SP and 异客's V2_1
  module (§35.22.9). The default data still runs 角峰 at `chess_char_1_02`.
- `test/versions-keying.test.js`, on `data/`, every `data/versions/*/` and `SP_VERSION_DATA_DIRS`. It checks:
  - every chess resolves to its own charId's kit or is pending;
  - every 自选 pick (owned pool and prototypes, each slot) and 补位 stand-in resolves to its charId's kit, or is a 预备干员
    (generic) or a pending 自选 pick that the kitted gate drops;
  - `auto_chess_change_map`: Touch (下半: the default and V2_1) has a battle part and is team support, 上半's 森蚺 neither;
  - every item runs its own effectId's handler, and each meta override binds only keys carrying its effectId and buff;
  - each directory's pinned counts: kit split, dispatched items and handlers, battle-effect items, 蒸汽之心's hammers
    (the version directory after the default exercises the hammer cache reset), bound meta keys and 突变细胞;
  - 凯瑟琳's cell at the prep's pick and in her later offers (the band's offer leaves out that directory's cell once
    taken, never the default id where 上半 gives it to another item; V1_1 has no 凯瑟琳 band); the official-record rules
    of 夕, 远牙 and 凯瑟琳 (a plugin record never counts);
  - the default data matches the baseline, and a plugin patch of an official `charId` / `effectId` on it keeps the old
    lookups;
  - the same patch keeps 凯瑟琳's cell (`isMutationCell` / `mutationCellIds`), 蒸汽之心's hammers, 远牙's granted trait and
    夕's dragon by id; `officialKitKey`'s failed-load and `char_x_` rules;
  - each code-keyed band id and every shared bond id keeps the default's name and effectId;
  - the kits' group lists: on a version's build the four 深海猎人 lists name the same six operators, on data without
    `config.version` the lists of before M0 (a plugin chess with 安哲拉's or 星源's charId is not counted there), and per
    directory the members they see and the shop 莱茵生命 members 缪尔赛思's list misses (§35.22.4);
  - the client store (`public/js/data.js`) applies no `data/i18n` overlay to a version directory, served or as a content
    set without its own texts, and does apply a set's own texts;
  - 炎, 远牙, 夕 and the AI overlays (each of these fails on the code before M0).
- `test/versions-bb-audit.test.js`.
- `test/docs-consistency.test.js` §21.29: the charId-keyed `TRIGGER_DEVIATIONS`.

The per-version rebuild tests need the pinned caches `.cache/gamedata@<sha>/` and skip without them.

**[ASSUMED] in M0** (each listed above where it applies):

- The labels' translations.
- The 下半 rule constants of `config.json` (`bans`, the economy literals with the tier copies, `dp`, the timer literals
  `spFirst` / `bandTurn` / `soloPrepTimeData`, the title rules and the rest, §35.22.3).
- The two 上半 adapters.
- The left-out 下半 tables and the research fallback for 上半; V2_1's research, tables, `hiddenCore` and rules taken from
  the default by id.
- Pool entries dropped.
- The shop-excluded items and item rules held for 上半.
- One `tuning.json`.
- hiddenCore 300 / 1000.
- The first tier kit in `KIT_FILES` order when none matches the record's tier.
- The borrowed kits. Retired by §35.22.9: the 31 were compared, and the one new key (泥岩's `sp`) is read from the record.
- The 25 自选 operators with a tier kit only (24 in 第一期, 4 in V2_1, 3 of them in both) left out of those versions' 自选.
  Retired by §35.22.9: they borrow the tier kit and are in `KITTED_CHARS`.
- `constants.singleReconnectTime` null for 上半, with the lobby's 24 h fallback.
- Official records only for 夕 / 远牙 / 凯瑟琳.
- A missing threshold is not reached for every core bond, on a version's build.
- The 下半 bounty draft classification and research texts (`spec`, `implFormula`, `howToPlay`) carried into 上半; the
  V2_2 research texts carried into V2_1.
- 下半's tier-5 4★ prototype extension of 自选 (`DIY_EXTRA_PROTOTYPES`) and 鸭爵's swapped enemies costing 1 LP
  (`BAND_SWAP_LPR`), both from 下半's PRTS record, carried into 上半 (§35.22.3).
- `config.version` marks non-default data.
- The audit's name matching.

A version build also reads default-side inputs: the committed `data/assets.json`, `data/tuning.json` (byte-copied) and
`docs/research/*.json` (the default's research, which V2_1 reads by id and 上半 uses as its fallback). A change to any of them needs every version rebuilt. The
rebuild test's failure message says so; outside a machine with the pinned caches only the `tuning.json` copy is checked.

**Open (for the maintainer):**

- whether 11-25 → 12-05 becomes a sub-version of 第一期 of its own (the decisions above);
- whether 「今年3月的第一次改动」 means the 03-17 battle fix `987b4cd5` or 更新#2 on 03-27 (as built); with the first,
  第二期上半 would be its first three days and the build stays the same (§35.22.3 "Battle data inside a sub-version").

**Open (for M1)** — done in M1, §35.23: settings by operator (§35.23.6), records by version and levels by season
(§35.23.7), `data/versions/` served for enabled versions and counted in the build tag (§35.23.1). As recorded before M1:

- **Browser settings keyed by chess id.** The per-browser loadout (`public/js/ui/loadoutModel.js`,
  `{ [baseChessId]: { skill?, module? } }` in `sp.pref.loadout`) and ownership (`public/js/ui/ownershipModel.js`,
  `notOwned`, a list of base chess ids), and the `room.loadout` / `room.ownership` payloads that carry them
  (`shared/protocol.js checkLoadout` checks each entry against the room data's record of that chess id), are keyed by
  chess id. In a 上半 room `chess_char_1_02` is 讯使, not 角峰: a 下半 choice of 角峰's skill 1 would pass and select 讯使's
  second skill, and 角峰 marked not owned would field 讯使's stand-in. M1 re-keys them by charId, migrating old keys
  through 下半's mapping, before a version room accepts them (the local round-50 design notes, §2.8). Nothing serves a
  version room in M0, so nothing breaks today.
- **Device match records.** A record of `public/js/ui/stats.js` carries no version. M1 records it (a record without one
  counts as 第二期下半), re-views a record (`public/js/screens/stats.js`, `ResultView`) with its own version's data (chess
  and item ids are season-local: a 第一期 record shown with today's data names other operators and items), and counts
  the 功勋 LV per season: `screens/settle.js levelProgress` sums every unmodded, non-test record today, while each season
  has its own 卫戍认证 (`act1autochess_token_chess` / `act2autochess_token_chess`) and V1_1 / V2_1 stop at 35 levels
  (the local round-50 design notes, §2.8, row 统计).
- **Serving `data/versions/`.** M1 lifts the 404 of `server/http/static.js isVersionData` for the versions a server
  enables, and adds them to the build tag (`server/http/buildTag.js UNSERVED_BUILD_DIRS`).

#### 35.22.7 Historical core rules (M2)

The core can execute the three historical data sets in an isolated process/worker. The lobby, the vote and the version
hosts are §35.23; the kits are §35.22.9. Sources: the pinned official tables in `tools/versions/`,
[PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议/帮助), the historical inventory in §35.22.5, and the maintainer's
2026-10-22 decisions (conveniences/deviations carry; differing mechanics follow their own text).

- `config.version.rules` now controls bottom-row-first deployment (left to right within a row), first-season bench
  equipment/full-bench swaps, and suppression of the later two-player boss mirror branch. The existing team boss pool
  is retained. Historical Match construction rejects unavailable modes, including ABYSS.
- First-season solo preparation uses the table's **300 real seconds**, and its special draft **150 seconds**.
  `Match` option `soloUnlimited: true` opts into unlimited preparation/drafts; the default is false. The room's 「不限时」
  (default off) is §35.23.10: until that switch is on, a 第一期 solo keeps the official 300 s preparation and 150 s 机变.
  Co-op still supports 1–4 players, with the lone-human unlimited convenience; reconnection,
  deliberate trigger overrides, stand-ins, DIY and other maintainer-approved conveniences carry over.
- `AUTO_STOP` closes an active skill manually after half its duration when an enemy is in the running range, respecting
  operation cooldown/silence. 斩业星熊 S3's existing manual-close kit then enters 临死模式. The trigger enum/schema and
  the version audit recognize it. First-season defender/vanguard trigger tables remain their own data.
- Historical Precision affects high-ground operators and applies its silence; Skillful always reaches eight
  neighbours and prices the first manual refresh's equipment at 1 after its threshold; Raid has no modern idle
  trigger and uses its cost/respawn milestone. `costMul` composes multiplicatively and is charged on paid deployments.
  V1_2's auxiliary damage clause applies to all operators, V1_1 has no damage bonus. Victoria reads `power_atk` once
  for an equipped member. Historical Steadfast shares damage without emitting later retaliation hits or fragility.
- Historical Chen affects high-ground operators only (also V2_1); the four first-season strategies' +30% clause is
  restricted to FUNNY (标准模拟). Historical 大帝 applies one redeploy reduction. 森蚺's map-device aliases activate
  turrets for the team in preparation and in standalone battle specs. Egir uses bottom then left in historical data;
  the season-one unspecified order follows its deployment order **[ASSUMED]**.
- Added the old sold-煌 transformation, 蜜莓 repeated random active-bond grants, 伊芙利特's facing ray, 极光's healing,
  front trait grants using `give_garrion_id`, and the 坚守 row gate. `garrison_53` includes 奇迹 as its own text says.
  Old 远见 keys normalize into the existing latched discount handler (including V2_1's 80/150 thresholds).
- Historical 伪装服 grants camouflage on deployment; 海沟实验体 expands the wearer's Egir devour to four neighbours,
  with the heavy-blade HP combination; 耶拉冈德之泪 applies cold around kills, twice with its partner. Historical
  packaging returns the carrier's initial copy; beacon sends the same-quality carrier to a qualifying teammate or
  returns it; pager performs a same-bond shop refresh. 拉特兰桥夹 has no unstated modern cap. V1_1 炎佑 reads
  `2.ep_damage_scale` as the text's 15% elemental vulnerability **[ASSUMED]**, rather than gauge gain.

Each version now has its own bond/item explanatory research; this removes misleading V2_2 numbers and scopes from
historical `spec`/`implFormula` fields. First-season enemy research uses the **11-18 blackboards**, not the rounded
announcement: solo ATK ×0.7 / HP ×0.75; co-op FUNNY/NORMAL ×0.8 / ×0.8; DEF unchanged. **[ASSUMED]** co-op HARD has
neutral multipliers and no additional per-round/hidden stacks until historical measurements are available.

The first-season bounty classifier has 18 initial / 19 boss / 18 hunter candidates. It omits the later measured
bounty groups, shop slots, tactic weights and identity stock overrides. Draws within available pools are uniform;
all first-season schedules now explicitly carry `assumed: true`. **[ASSUMED]** the round-to-family weights, candidate
shape/exclusion rules, tier stock counts, bond ban counts (3 core / 4 addon), DP, title rules, generic terrain/reward
research and stable item classifications/ranges carry over where no historical evidence settles them. V2_1 retains
the second-season measured draft structures **[ASSUMED]**; its own pinned cards, module/trigger changes, inflation
thresholds, balloon leak accounting and boss routes remain the official data described in §35.22.5.

Further explicit uncertainties/limits:

- 极光's “数次” is three healing pulses, immediately then at 1 s intervals **[ASSUMED]**.
- 海沟实验体's inherited HP is the victims' base max HP, as with the existing ATK/block inheritance **[ASSUMED]**.
- 寻呼模块 refresh replaces frozen/equipment slots too, draws from same-bond operators up to the current shop tier,
  and does not count as a manual refresh **[ASSUMED]**.
- `garrison_06`'s bench-leftmost 流形 text is an unreferenced official template: neither built historical garrisons nor
  the chess records carry it. The actual 缪尔赛思 kit and 莱茵生命 list belong to the separate kits run. No inert trait
  is attached to an operator by guessing.
- The six first-season enemies listed in §35.22.5 still lack exact attack clips. Their original skeletons are absent
  from the local cache; downloading them failed (`Could not resolve host: raw.githubusercontent.com`). The existing
  `ATTACK_PAUSE` / fallback windup remains **[ASSUMED]** and the build warns for each. The `_2` variants of three
  enemies are not substituted as unverified timing evidence; default `data/assets.json` stays unchanged.

#### 35.22.8 Version tools and audit evidence (M2)

`tools/golden.mjs --version <id>` and `tools/botbench.mjs --version <id>` load the version before importing simulation
content. Worker threads repeat that bootstrap; there is no mutable cross-version host. Non-default BattleSpecs and
botbench records carry `version`; specs reject an explicit identity that differs from their DataSource. The omitted
field retains the legacy format, and the default serialized shape stays unchanged. Match combat and rehearsal pass
the identity through. Goldens live under `test/golden/<slug>/`; their scenarios use only supported mode IDs and match
configurations. Run `--update --twice` to build and compare a full corpus with a second reverse-order pass.

`node tools/bb-audit.mjs --strict` now defaults to all three historical directories. It intentionally still exits 1:
structural differences are not suppressed. The exhaustive, test-checked
[review](../../docs/research/versions/blackboard-review.json) lists a reason for **every** remaining entry:

| Version | field gaps | absent modern keys | unknown triggers | unhandled historical keys | historical fields |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| V2_1 | 0 | 3 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| V1_2 | 32 | 43 | 0 | 0 | 12 |
| V1_1 | 36 | 45 | 0 | 0 | 12 |

`test/versions-core.test.js` exercises rules, identity rejection and real battle/preparation handlers.
`test/versions-bb-audit.test.js` checks all historical keys are named, the trigger set is implemented and every surviving
diagnostic matches the reviewed inventory. `test/versions-golden.test.js` checks version corpus identity/error counts
and repeats representative scenarios through worker threads. Default data hashes and default golden files are not
updated. New historical corpora have no earlier baseline; all scenario IDs are recorded in their six family files.
Validation: affected tests **118 passed / 0 failed**; data/rebuild and default guards excluding the three socket tests
**22 passed / 0 failed**; version corpus checks **6 passed / 0 failed**. The default **288** golden scenarios match;
V1_1 **218**, V1_2 **220**, V2_1 **269** scenarios pass two in-process runs with reversed order. Historical match
families were rerun after the AI classification change. Full CI was attempted with `--keep-going`: setup passed,
recorded server tests failed with `listen EPERM`, then the process terminated with exit **137** before a final unit
summary. The remaining CI steps were run separately: lint (0 errors), imports and types passed; smoke also failed
with `listen EPERM`. This is not a full CI pass. Detailed logs accompany the round51 M2 handoff report.

#### 35.22.9 The version kits (M2)

The 19 第一期 shop operators who had no kit now have one, keyed by charId (`server/sim/content/kits/ops/ver-<codename>.js`,
`VERSION_KIT_FILES`). They are not shop-chess files: those chess ids belong to other operators today (`chess_char_1_02_a`
stays 角峰 on the default data and is 讯使 on 第一期). Each skill id is under `skills`. `test/content/kits_ver_m2.test.js`
checks, on `data/versions/act1autochess`, both forms and every skill index (`skillSpecSource` `skills`, a duration taken
from the record, a blackboard number on a spec or on the cast) and that the shop skill starts.

The 25 自选 picks whose hand-written kit is a tier kit borrow it (`ops/ver-diy.js`, `shared/borrow.js`). Each skill is
rebuilt with that skill's own blackboard: the part whose shop-default skill is the id, else the other parts, else the
generic spec so `selectSkillSpec` still reports `skills`. The body is one call of the owner (the part of the selected
skill, else the piece's tier, else the first). 百炼嘉维尔's S2 comes from the tier-5 file and S1/S3 from the tier-4 file;
妮芙 uses only the tier-5 file. The charIds are `DIY_BORROW_CHARS`: the 24 of 第一期's pool, plus 银灰, who is in V2_1's
pool and not in 第一期's. They join `KITTED_CHARS` and are not keys of `OPERATOR_KITS`, so the default `diy.ownedPool`
and the golden 自选 scenarios are unchanged. A shop chess of the same operator on a versioned build of today's data
keeps the tier key (星熊 `chess_char_4_17_a`).

**The 31 borrowed kits**, compared at the same status (第一期's shop record against today's chess of that charId, or
against the 自选 form of that status). The kits read the record's blackboard and do not override its trigger, so a
different number or a different resolved rule needs no branch:

- 蛇屠箱's talent `def` is 0.06 on 第一期 and 0.12 today; 锏's `atk_scale` 1.7 against 1.75; 纯烬艾雅法拉's talent
  `max_hp` 0.08 against 0.1; 泥岩's `hp_ratio` 0.24 against 0.22.
- Triggers: 蛇屠箱 and 角峰 S1/S2 are `DEFAULT` on 第一期 and `TAKE_DAMAGE` today; 深巡 S1 the same, S2 staying
  `ACTIVE_RANGE`; 忍冬 S2/S3 are `SP_FULL` on 第一期 and `SKILL_RANGE` / `ACTIVE_RANGE` today; 泥岩 S1 is `DEFAULT` and
  S3 `SKILL_RANGE` on 第一期, both `TAKE_DAMAGE` today; 菲亚梅塔 S1 is `ACTIVE_RANGE` on 第一期 and `DEFAULT` today; 林
  S3 is `SEARCH` on 第一期 and `CUSTOM_RANGE` today.
- 泥岩's elite 沃土予身 adds `sp: 1` per broken layer (the 第一期 elite blackboard and text). Today's elite has no `sp`.
  The kit grants that SP only when the key is present (`num` of a missing key is 0), so the default data does not move.
- 异客's elite 孤卒 on 第一期 is attack only (`atk` 0.1; the text has no 技力回复). Today's elite adds
  `sp_recovery_per_sec` 0.25. A missing key stays 0, so 第一期 does not gain it.
- 左乐's elite 秉烛照影 on 第一期 carries `hp_ratio` and `damage_resistance`; the kit applies that 庇护 only when
  `hp_ratio` is present. Today's same-status form has neither key.
- 提丰's elite 锐如兽牙 on 第一期 carries `atk_scale` and `value`; the kit applies that module part only when the module
  is on. 娜仁图亚, 澄闪, 死芒, 伊芙利特 and 淬羽赫默's elite shop status is equip level 2, which no 自选 form stores
  (the forms are 1 and 3); their normal form, the same skill rank, matches today's blackboard.

**V2_1.** Every chess id V2_1 shares with today has the same skill blackboard and the same talent blackboard (232
records). 异客's RA-A difference is the module overlay already in §35.22.5: `uniequip_004_pasngr` sets
`pasngr_t_1[enhance].damage_scale` to 1.2, and `composeTalents` merges it over the base 1.25 when that module is picked.
The kit reads the merged blackboard. A true attack of 100 deals 120 on V2_1 and 125 at today's full potential. The eight
operators V2_2 re-tiered sit on the ids their kits were written for, and those ids match today. No kit branch.

海蒂's 私人信使 (talent index 1, `sp_recovery_per_sec`) is 破碎日冕 only and is not applied. The default golden corpus is
unchanged: today's 泥岩 record has no `sp` key.

- 讯使 S1: the built trigger is `DEFAULT`; an AUTO「立即获得部署费用」fires when SP is full (the same reading as 德克萨斯
  冲锋号令).
- 万顷: the heal pulse is once per elapsed second. The text says 每秒 and the blackboard has no heal interval.
- 亚叶 S2 fires only at an enemy; `damage_scale` 0.5 is the fraction of a `'terrain'`-tagged hit she keeps; 地面 is a

### 35.23 Official versions: the version switch (M1)

- **Requested:** the maintainer's words for the switch (2026-10-22): a global version button in the lobby, every affected
  place switching to that version; the room keeps a version switch for the host, and a host switch goes through a vote
  exactly like 重刷 (reroll), better ideas allowed; and (2026-10-23) everything doable in 0.3.2 lands in 0.3.2. This
  section records M1 of §35.22's milestones. The switch was first behind a server option that was off by default; nothing
  set now enables every version (§35.23.11). The earlier
  versions' own rules were written as a later milestone; on this branch they are already in (§35.22.7–§35.22.9), so a
  version room plays those rules with that version's data. Where §35.22.5 still lists an open gap, that gap stays.
- **Official:** the versions and their data are §35.22's. The vote copies the remake's 重刷本局 vote
  (`server/match/match/setupVote.js`, `public/js/ui/setupReroll.js`, both unchanged); the official game has no version
  vote, so its rules are the maintainer's.
- **Decisions (the maintainer, 2026-10-22; the local round-50 design notes, both 维护者决定 sections):**
  - The lobby switch is **each player's own** (a browser preference, Q13 = a); the server only enables versions and names
    a default.
  - **The room's version follows the room:** joining another version's room loads that version, the room shows its
    version tag, and leaving restores the player's preference.
  - A version switch happens **only before the match**, by **unanimous vote of the seated humans**: the host proposes, a
    lone human switches at once, any decline cancels, spectators and AI seats do not vote.
  - **第二期下半 rooms and data stay byte-identical:** an omitted version means the code constant `act2autochess`
    (§35.22.1). A room of that version still sends no `version` field. The welcome lists the enabled versions (§35.23.11).
    G2–G4 (§35.22.2) still pass; G4 pins that room and the welcome list.
  - The reroll's own rules are not changed here. GitHub #854 / #858 and PR #860 propose changing who may start the 重刷
    vote; the version vote keeps the design's rule (the host proposes), so an outcome of that triage is mirrored in
    `server/lobby.js proposeVersion` and `public/js/ui/versionVote.js` in a change of its own.

#### 35.23.1 The server offers versions (configuration and the service)

- `server/http/config.js versionsFrom`: the startServer option `versions: { enabled, default, maxHosts }` wins over
  `SP_VERSIONS` (`all` or a comma list of registry ids) / `SP_VERSION_DEFAULT` / `SP_MAX_VERSION_HOSTS`. Nothing set
  enables every registry id (§35.23.11); `SP_VERSIONS=act2autochess` is act2autochess alone, the switch off. Unknown ids
  are warned about and dropped (none left: act2autochess); a default
  that is not enabled is warned about and replaced by the first enabled version in registry order; maxHosts defaults to
  the number of enabled non-default versions (at least 1 when there are any). One boot line:
  `[versions] enabled: act2autochess (第二期下半, default), …`. The setup is in docs/DEPLOY.md §11.
- `server/versions.js createVersionSets`: at boot, each enabled non-default version's
  `cs = contentHashOf({ app, sim, data })`, the vanilla code with the sha256 of its directory's gameplay files
  (`server/plugins/identity.js versionContentOf`, bytes only, nothing parsed), and its config.json modes (`offered()`:
  第一期 and 第二期上半 have no 终极). A version whose directory is incomplete is warned about and stays disabled. Data,
  bounds snapshot (`officialBoundsOf`, never the shared cache) and manifest load on first use; a load that throws or
  leaves a data file out is remembered (logged once, null from then on, no manifest — review fix: every room message of
  that version re-read the 4.6–5.2 MB directory). Such a version is no longer offered (second review): `available(id)`
  is false, so `room.create` and `room.proposeVersion` answer VERSION_UNSUPPORTED ('the data of this version is not
  available') instead of building a room no page can load, and the welcome of later connections leaves it out. Third
  review: when that version is the configured default (`SP_VERSION_DEFAULT`), the lobby's default falls back to
  第二期下半 (else the first enabled version that loads) and the welcome marks that one `default` — every page used to
  send `room.create` without a version and get VERSION_UNSUPPORTED, and settings outside a room were checked against an
  empty data set. `/healthz` keeps the configured default. It is
  also the match hosts' contents interface (`get(cs)`, `dataBytes(cs)`).
- HTTP: `/cs/<cs16>/index.json` of a version has the vanilla manifest's shape with `version`, data URLs under
  `/data/versions/<slug>/` and `bounds.official`; `/cs/<cs16>/bounds/official.json` is its snapshot. `/data/versions/<slug>/`
  is served for enabled versions only (the real path decides; 404 in every other spelling, for a version the server does
  not enable, and for the whole tree when the server is narrowed to 第二期下半 alone, where §35.22's `isVersionData`
  refusal still holds — §35.23.11). The vanilla manifest is byte-identical. The build tag counts only the
  served version directories, and `/healthz` gains `versions` only when a non-default version is enabled.
- welcome `versions?: [{ id, cs, family, label, roomModes, default? }]`, sent only when more than act2autochess is
  enabled (or the only one enabled is another version). Protocol: docs/design/network.md §8.5; data: DATA.md "Serving the
  versions (M1)".

#### 35.23.2 Rooms of a version, and the vote

- `Room.version`; `needsContent = modded || version !== act2autochess` replaces `modded` wherever the pages must load
  content (the seat / spectator `content`, ready, start, `room.contentReady`, the disconnect reset). `modded` (the 非官方
  mark) is unchanged: a version room's content is `{ cs, vanilla: true, version, plugins }`, official, with cosmetic
  plugins only.
- `room.create { version? }` (omitted: the server's default): VERSION_UNKNOWN / VERSION_UNSUPPORTED; a difficulty the
  version lacks is lowered (ABYSS → HARD); a fixed plugin policy (rooms: fixed) that cannot play the version answers
  VERSION_UNSUPPORTED before the room exists, otherwise default plugins that cannot apply are warned about once and left
  off. They are resolved before the creator leaves the old room (a refusal keeps them there); a content set that room
  alone holds is passed as `replacing`, so it counts as freed against `limits.maxSets` as when the room went first
  (fourth review: a default set the server had not kept answered PLUGIN_BUSY there, and the room started without it).
  `room.setDifficulty` / `setMode` accept the version's modes only (BAD_TARGET 'not a mode of this version').
- The vote, modelled on 重刷: `room.proposeVersion` (the host; the refusal order of network.md §8.5),
  `room.versionVote`, `room.cancelVersionVote`. The voters are the seated humans at the proposal, the host counted as
  agreed; a lone human switches at once. It expires after 60 s [ASSUMED], a new proposal waits 300 ms, and 5 s pass after
  a declined or expired vote before the next [ASSUMED]. A human taking a seat, a seat freed, a host change, a voter
  dropping or a match start voids it (toast 同盟成员有变动…, to the members who saw the vote: a joiner is seated only after
  the void — second review); spectators and AI seats do not. While it is open, `room.start`
  / `setPlugins` / `setMode` answer VERSION_VOTE. `room.state.versionVote.remainingMs` follows the lobby clock (all four
  `toState` callers, `sendState` included, pass `this.now()`).
- `applyVersion`: the version, the difficulty lowered, the version's base with the room's cosmetic plugins resolved again
  (a dry run at the proposal refuses VERSION_UNSUPPORTED; when they stop resolving before the vote ends, the switch keeps
  the base without them and warns once), every page loading again, the other seats un-readied, the whole replay dropped,
  every human seat's loadout / 干员持有 / 自选 cleared, a seat card the version lacks dropped.
- **Settings and chess ids** (the design's Critic): chess ids are season-local (`chess_char_1_02` is 角峰 in 第二期, 讯使 in
  第一期). In a `needsContent` room a loadout / 干员持有 / 自选 sent before the seat's page reported the room's content ready
  is stamped `unverified` [ASSUMED], and a version room's match drops an unverified (or unknown-data) loadout / 干员持有
  list whole. A setting checked for another data set keeps only the chess ids whose charId is the same in both. Outside a
  room the server's default version's data and cs apply (`server/lobby.js dataFor` / `csOf`).
- Gameplay plugins: `roomSets.resolve({ gameplay: false })` refuses a content / script / trusted plugin before anything is
  built (VERSION_UNSUPPORTED), and the lobby refuses a modded result in a version room as well. Cosmetic plugins carry
  over. docs/PLUGINS.md says so.

#### 35.23.3 Version matches run in version hosts

- `server/match/host/` kind `'version'`: one child process per version cs, at most maxHosts, apart from the plugin hosts;
  log prefix `[versions]` with the version id as label; stop reasons `version-crash` / `version-hang` / `version-start`
  shown as 本局模拟出错，已结束 / 本局模拟长时间无响应，已结束 / 该版本的模拟无法启动; a full pool answers INTERNAL
  'version hosts busy'. The host installs the version's bounds snapshot and refuses a match whose `opts.version` is not
  its data's (`entry.js`). Memory per host is estimated at 100+ MB from the 117 MB measured for plugin hosts [ASSUMED].
- The lobby passes `version` and `requireCs: true` (bot rehearsals are never delegated to a browser), never `kitted`. A
  version room without a version host refuses to start (INTERNAL) unless a test supplied its own match class.
- `Match.version`; the test journal's `init.options.version`; the external AI's `context.gameVersion` and the knowledge
  pack's `gameVersion` (non-default only, so the default pack's hash is unchanged; docs/EXTERNAL_AI.md). A version journal
  replays in a process whose data singleton is that version's (`test/helpers/versionReplay.mjs`).
- `soloResumeWindowMs` reads the room's version data: 第一期 lacks `constants.singleReconnectTime` (M2's timed solo did not
  add the constant), so its solo resume window stays the lobby's 24 h fallback. That window is a carried-over convenience
  [ASSUMED], not official 第一期 data; the code path is unchanged (§35.23.10).
- The first room of a version parses its 4.6–5.2 MB of data on the event loop; only the config modes are read at boot.

#### 35.23.4 The page shows one version

- `public/js/versions.js`: the lobby's version is this browser's choice (`sp.pref.lobby.version`) when welcome lists it,
  else the server's default (welcome.versions `default`), else the code constant. A room plays its own version
  (`room.state.version`, absent = 第二期下半) whatever the choice. A result shown after its room is gone (a kick, a grace
  timeout, a settlement left open, also on a reloaded page that never saw the room) keeps the version its frames name:
  `m.public` / `m.result` carry `version` in a non-default match only (the default's frames unchanged), and the record
  takes it too (review fix: a replay to a reloaded page was recorded as 第二期下半 and drawn with the lobby's data). The
  difficulties a version
  offers and the fallback for one it lacks re-implement the server's rule (the highest offered below it, else the lowest),
  because the page imports no server code. Without welcome.versions (a server narrowed to 第二期下半 alone) none of this
  runs. G4 pins a default server's welcome.versions (§35.23.11).
- `public/js/content.js loadVersionSet(cs, id)` checks a version set as the protocol says: the manifest (`vanilla: true`,
  its `version` the one expected, no plugins, no settings, a composition that hashes to the cs), every data file under
  `/data/versions/<slug>/` (no `..`) and the bounds snapshot, each by size and SHA-256 of its bytes. The room is told
  (`room.contentReady`) only for its own content, never from the lobby, and again whenever the page's seat or spectator
  entry shows another state (a reconnect: the Critic's stuck-loading case).
- The set on screen stays until the next one is checked, so a 第一期 lobby entering a 第二期上半 room never shows 第二期下半
  in between; the last two checked sets are kept, so a lobby → room → lobby trip downloads nothing twice. A failed set is
  reported once, answered `{ ok: false }`, and the page falls back to the server's files rather than show another
  version's operators under its name; the chip offers 重试. A gameplay plugin room keeps its 0.3.0 path (`kind: 'plugins'`).
- The battle runner simulates a version set on the page's own Battle (a version set is data only), so 作战统计 works in a
  version room. `loadBrowserSim` hands the content modules the set's version marker (`raw.config = { version }`; the
  default data and plugin sets still carry no `config`): without it every M0 version branch took the default path on the
  page (another operator's kit for 50 reused chess ids of 第一期下半, another item's behaviour for 72 reused item ids, a
  bond tier the version lacks), while the version host ran the version's — and with client combat on and verification
  off by default, the page's result was the field's (second review, must-fix; test/version-browser-sim.test.js checks
  every kit and item dispatch against the host's). The game screen waits for the version's data before it draws a match; `main.js warmGameData` fetches only
  the art list in a version room; `diag.js` names the version; the plugin panel shows only a `kind: 'plugins'` download.
- In a language other than Simplified Chinese a version's game texts stay Chinese (`data.js versionFiles`, M0) until M3;
  the picker and docs/PLAYING.md §15 say so.

#### 35.23.5 The lobby switch and the room's version

- `public/js/ui/versionPicker.js`: the chip at the lobby's top right, before 恢复本机对局 (a server that enables one version
  only, not 第二期下半, shows it as a read-only tag), with 载入中 n% / 校验失败 + 重试 while the version loads. The picker
  (the project's Modal) lists each enabled version with its official period (盟约·上半 / 下半, V1_1 … V2_2, the dates of
  DATA.md's registry table), 当前 / 服务器默认, and 预览. The note was 「部分规则仍按第二期下半，正在逐步复刻」 [ASSUMED];
  §35.23.10 replaces it. In the lobby a pick is this browser's choice at once; in a room the host's pick sends
  `room.proposeVersion`, read-only for everybody else (由创建者发起).
- The title shows a non-default version (「· 第一期下半」) [ASSUMED placement]. 选择模拟协议 lists the version's difficulties
  and selects the fallback when the remembered one is not offered (`sp.pref.lobby.difficulty` changes only on a pick); its
  cards wait while the version loads. `room.create` carries `version` on a server that enables versions.
- The room: `needsContent` gates 准备就绪 / 开始模拟, the seat tags (版本数据载入中 / 就绪 / 校验失败) and the status line
  (「等待所有博士载入「{version}」的数据」). The version tag sits beside the mode in the top bar and beside the 非官方 mark in
  the briefing, the settlement and the result [ASSUMED placement]. The version pill beside the difficulty (and ⚙ 同盟设置's
  版本 row) opens the picker; it is off during a vote, with gameplay plugins (「请先关闭改变玩法的插件」) and offline. A RATE
  answer to a proposal right after a switch is retried once after 1.2 s [ASSUMED].
- `public/js/ui/versionVote.js`, modelled on `ui/setupReroll.js`: the strip between the 观战席 and the footer
  (「切换到「{version}」投票：{agreed}/{total}」, the countdown from `remainingMs`, the voters' chips, 同意切换 / 拒绝切换 for a
  voter, 取消投票 for the host). On a short screen it floats over the seats. The settlement screen shows it too, so voters
  still on the settlement can vote. While 干员调配 or 统计数据 covers the room (fixed overlays; a guest edits the loadout
  there before readying), a voter or the host sees the strip floating above them (`CoveredVersionVote`, z 56; second
  review: the vote ran out unseen and held the host in the retry pause). Third review: both overlays are aria-modal
  dialogs, so the floating strip is its own `role="alertdialog"` and the strip it covers is `aria-hidden` + `inert`
  meanwhile (one live region); a floating strip (over an overlay or the settlement) is never taller than the screen —
  on a short screen its text scrolls and its buttons stay in view (English at 特大 on a 640×360 phone ran its title off
  the top); an open 干员调配 / 干员持有 follows a switch (its roster memo is keyed by `data.contentId()`, as are the
  in-match lookups, 统计数据's and the strategy draft's: it kept the old version's operators beside the new version's
  skills for the same chess ids). Fourth review: the room's own strip on a short screen keeps its buttons in view as the
  floating one does (English at 特大: a tap on 同意切换 missed it, the strip scrolled as one box); the floating alertdialog
  takes the focus when it appears — the strip itself, not a button, so a key pressed in the roster's search field never
  votes — and gives it back when it goes; the countdown is `aria-hidden` (it ticked inside the live region about 60
  times a vote).
- The version tag (`VersionTag`) holds its name in its own box (`.vertag__label`: a bare text in the inline-flex tag was
  cut with no ellipsis), and on a short screen shows the whole name, on two lines if it must (fourth review: English
  「Season 1 · P」 at md and 「Season 1」 at 特大 on a phone read the same for both halves).
- 选择模拟协议 says 版本数据校验失败 with 重试 when the lobby version's set failed (it kept saying 正在载入版本数据…, the
  chip's 重试 hidden under the modal).
- A switch toasts 「同盟已切换到「{version}」」 (never on entering a room) and closes a result of the old version still on
  screen. A room whose version is not the lobby's shows 「本房间为「{room}」，离开后恢复你的大厅版本「{mine}」」 (on a short
  screen in the pill's tooltip [ASSUMED]). The invite names the version. The plugin panel disables the gameplay switches
  in a version room (「改变玩法的插件只能在第二期下半使用」).

#### 35.23.6 Settings by operator

- The loadout and the 干员持有 list are stored by charId and shared by every version: `sp.pref.loadout` =
  `{ v: 2, byChar, ops }`, `sp.pref.ownership` = `{ v: 2, notOwnedChars }`. The screens keep chess ids through views of the
  data on screen (`charKeyMap`), rebuilt when that data changes; an edit is written back through the map of the view it
  was made on, and only what the edit changed (`rebaseByChar` / `rebaseChars`), so an edit on a stale view (the overlay
  open across a switch) is never filed under another version's operator. A v1 store migrates through 第二期下半's
  `/data/chess.json`, fetched directly; nothing is sent before the migration settles. 自选 is per version (`sp.pref.diy`
  for 第二期下半, unchanged; `sp.pref.diy.<slug>` else). 导出 writes v2, 导入 reads v1 and v2. Review fixes: the v2 stores
  also carry the v1 fields (`entries`, `notOwned`, by 第二期下半's chess ids) for an older build on the same origin; and a
  v1 mark on 第二期下半's two hidden droppable chess (chess_char_5_18_a, chess_char_6_10_a) is dropped by the migration and
  a v1 import instead of marking the visible chess of the same operator.
- Every sync (loadout, 干员持有, 自选, skins, card) waits for the data the server checks against (`canSendSettings`): never
  while the page's content loads; in a room once the room's content is on the page; outside a room only while the page
  shows the server's default version. A room entered after the lobby could not send them sends them then, not at every
  room entry (five sends at once spent the server's heavy-message budget, and the solo briefing's last edit before
  准备就绪 missed its match: test/ui/loadout.e2e.test.js). At the merge the lanes disagreed on a server whose default is
  not act2autochess (the page waited for 第二期下半's own files, the server checks the default version's): the
  integration commit keys the outside-room gate on welcome.versions' default entry and its cs. The wire keys stay the
  chess ids of the room's data. Review fix: skins and the card are sent on a content change only when their payload
  changed; the payload follows the data on screen (the skins filtered to its operators, which is what the server keeps
  on the session and the seat, `server/lobby.js skins`; the card '' when the data lacks it), so a 第一期-only operator's
  skin still reaches a 第一期 room (second review: the unfiltered map was not sent again, and the server had dropped
  that operator in the 第二期下半 lobby; test/ui/skins-sync.test.js); before, a deep-link join and a vote right after a rejoin spent the heavy-message budget
  (burst 6) and some settings waited 1.5 s for the RATE retry. A deep-link join still sends four from the lobby and
  three in the room, so one may still be retried.

#### 35.23.7 Statistics

Records of another version carry `version` (a registry id, never 第二期下半; not part of the record id; `STATS_VERSION` 3,
`MIGRATIONS[2]` rewrites nothing). 统计数据 has a version filter (the version shown by default, every version with
records, 全部版本), history rows carry the tag, and another version's settlement re-views as 「切换到「X」查看」 outside a room:
the page shows that version while the replay is open (the store's `replayVersion`), and the lobby preference stays
(review fix: the row used to change the lobby's version for good). Second review: a version this server does not offer
(welcome.versions; a server narrowed to 第二期下半 offers none — §35.23.11) is not offered as a switch (「这台服务器没有提供「X」，不能回看该局结算」), and
the replay's wait always keeps 返回统计 — with 版本数据校验失败 + 重试 when the version set failed, or the room's line
when the room now plays another version. A row opened in a room sets `replayVersion` to the record's version too (the
room's version comes first, so nothing changes while seated): a room left while the replay is open (a kick, the room
closed) never moves the page to the lobby's version, and the settlement stays drawn (third review: the version used to
be corrected one render later, after the page had already started loading the lobby's set and the settlement had
unmounted behind the wait). 卫戍成就's peak (in 统计数据's 总览 and in 成就统计) draws its lineup and gear only with the data
of its own version (the loaded config, also while another version downloads): a peak of another version (the filter on
another version or 全部版本, or 成就统计's other half of the
season) keeps its alliance and layers and says 「切换到「X」查看」 over the empty slots (fourth review: 第一期下半 reuses 100
chess ids and 72 item ids for other operators and items — `chess_char_1_02` 讯使 was drawn as 第二期下半's 角峰). Bonds
and bands keep their names: no id of theirs means another alliance in another version (`band_zumama`, 第一期 only, shows
its id as before). A quit record's version is the room's, else its
frames' (`m.public` `version`; absent = 第二期下半): the store's `matchVersion` (the last room seen) is gone.
The levels, the lobby card and 成就统计 count one season (第一期 = both act1autochess versions, 第二期 = both act2autochess),
each season having its own 卫戍认证. The guide shows 「玩法说明的图片来自第二期下半，「{version}」中部分规则不同」 for another
version [ASSUMED, until M3's notes page].

#### 35.23.8 The default guard

G1 (the default data and golden hashes) was the M0 merge check and was deleted as M1 started (§35.22.2);
`test/data.test.js` and `test/golden.test.js` guard both, as before M0. G2–G4 stay. The fixture's `kat` and `roomState`
stay byte-identical; G4 also pins `welcomeVersions` (§35.23.11). On the merged tree `node tools/golden.mjs` reports every scenario unchanged: roster 49, bonds 46, fields
22, matches 21, standins 10, diy 140.

  runs; the real version service), `test/versions-service.test.js`, `test/http-versions-config.test.js`,

  server limited to 第二期下半 (`SP_VERSIONS=act2autochess`) a record of another version is not clickable. Third review added: agreeing from the floating strip
  over 干员调配 ends with its count on the new version's (95, and 112 back); the strip is an alertdialog and the room's
  under it aria-hidden; no wait is drawn over the replay after the kick (a MutationObserver); versions-fit measures the
  floating strip over 干员调配, the settlement's strip and 选择模拟协议's 版本数据校验失败 + 重试 row in the whole matrix;
  `test/ui/lobby-hub.test.js` draws the failed row and the replay's wait (`ReplayWait`: the unoffered line, the room's
  line, 重试 outside a room only, 返回统计 always); `test/match/version-host.test.js` (d) checks that `replayTestMatch`
  refuses a 第一期下半 journal in a 第二期下半 process (either data passed in: it mirrors the version host's check instead
  of re-simulating on other kits); `test/lobby-versions.test.js` and `test/versions-service.test.js` cover a configured
  default whose data fails to load. Fourth review added: versions-fit checks the whole name of the top-bar, history,
  briefing and settlement tags (their label cut sideways fails) and that each vote button is in view inside its strip and hit by a
  tap on its centre (the room's strip as well as the floating ones); versions.e2e checks the countdown's `aria-hidden`
