  `test/lobby-plugins.test.js` creates a room while the server is at `maxSets` with a set the creator's old room alone
  holds (also without sockets in `test/lobby-versions.test.js`, using the real registry and content services);
  `test/versions-service.test.js` a failed configured default when 第二期下半 is not enabled (the first version
  that loads is the welcome's default).
- Integration (`test/ui/versions-flow.e2e.test.js`, an in-process server with every version, `versionVoteMs` 4 s and fast
  match hosts; `startServer` hands `versionVoteMs` / `versionRetryMs` to the lobby for it): the vote's rules in the browser
  (a spectator is no voter and gets no 同意 button, an expired vote, a human taking a seat voids one while a spectator
  leaving does not, the host cancels, three seated humans agree and every page switches) and the cross-lane settings
  race: 角峰's skill edited while a vote for 第一期下半 is open is sent for 第二期下半 (`chess_char_1_02_a`), sent again
  after the switch only once `room.contentReady` named the version's cs, on his 第一期下半 chess `chess_char_2_03_a`, and
  the match's briefing fights with it there and with nothing of his on 讯使's `chess_char_1_02_a`. That match, with two AI
  teammates under AI 托管, runs in a version host (`/healthz` versions: a worker) for 14 rounds to its settlement, which
  shows the version's tag; the device record names `act1autochess`, and the guest leaving returns to 第二期下半.
- Manual checks on the merged tree (the M1 default, since replaced by §35.23.11): a server with nothing set then had a
  welcome, `room.state` and `/healthz` with the same keys as on the base (no `versions`, no `version`), `/data/versions/…`
  answered 404 and the boot line read
  `[versions] enabled: act2autochess (第二期下半, default)`; with `SP_VERSIONS=act1autochess SP_VERSION_DEFAULT=act1autochess`
  the welcome lists one entry, the chip is a read-only tag, a created room carries `version` (终极 lowered to 绝境), and
  the lobby's loadout reaches the server by 第一期下半's chess ids (the integration's gate fix, §35.23.6); with every
  version a gameplay plugin is refused in a 第一期下半 room (VERSION_UNSUPPORTED) and accepted in a 第二期下半 one, and the
  log names the version host `[versions] match host [act1autochess] started … ready`, with no `[plugins]` line for it.

**[ASSUMED] in M1:**

- The 60 s vote expiry and the 5 s guard after a declined or expired vote.
- Settings stamped as verified only after the seat's content is ready.
- 24 h solo resume for 第一期 (no `singleReconnectTime`; M2 did not add the constant). Kept as a carried-over convenience
  [ASSUMED], not official 第一期 data; the code path is unchanged (§35.23.10).
- A version host's memory (100+ MB, from the plugin hosts' 117 MB).
- The picker's 预览 line (since §35.23.10: bounty draws, some numbers and six enemies' attack wind-ups are still
  estimates); the title's version line and the tags' placement; the banner moving into the pill's tooltip on
  a short screen.
- The 1.2 s retry of a RATE-refused proposal.
- The guide's note.

**Left for later:** the briefing header's tag is not in the text-fit matrix (a briefing needs a running match; the
settlement's tag is covered). M2's core rules and kits are in (§35.22.7–§35.22.9). The 「不限时」 room control, the
briefing and the lobby card, and the picker's note are §35.23.10. M3 (translations, the asset
union, a differences page) stays as §35.22 plans it.

#### 35.23.10 Timed solo's 「不限时」, and what the screens say

Maintainer decision (上半独立模式按官方每轮 300 秒，另给「不限时」选项，默认关). The match already opted out through
`Match` `opts.soloUnlimited` (§35.22.7). This wires that flag from the room, and makes the solo texts follow the version.

- **Room.** `room.setSoloUnlimited { on }` is host-only, before the match, and only for a solo room of a version whose
  `config.version.rules.soloTimed` is true (第一期上半 and 第一期下半). Otherwise `BAD_TARGET`. A spectator is
  `SPECTATOR`, anyone else `NOT_HOST`, a running match `ROOM_STARTED`. `on: false` clears the flag. `room.state` carries
  `soloUnlimited: true` only while the flag is on, so a default room's `room.state` stays identical. `room.create` accepts
  an optional boolean and stores `true` only for that same solo; a co-op room or an untimed version ignores a stray
  `true`. A version switch clears the flag unless the room stays a timed solo (switching between the two 第一期 versions
  keeps it). `startMatch` passes `soloUnlimited: true` only while the flag is on; `wireOptions` and the test journal copy
  the key only when it is present. Lone-human co-op stays untimed (`soloUntimed` still treats a single human in co-op as
  untimed). Default 第二期下半 solo stays untimed, and its match opts do not grow a key.
- **Screens.** The switch is the solo room's ⚙ 模拟设置 (co-op keeps ⚙ 同盟设置). 选择模拟协议 shows it only while the
  lobby is on a timed solo and that version's data is on screen; the choice is `sp.pref.lobby.soloUnlimited` (default
  off) and is what `room.create` sends. The room gear is the authority and writes the same pref. The lobby's 独立模拟
  card and the solo briefing follow the version: a timed solo says 休整 300 秒 and 机变 150 秒, or the existing 不限时
  lines when the option is on; 第二期 says 不限时, and a phone still shows 「1 名博士」. While the version's data is not
  on screen, the card and the briefing state no time rule and the gear stays hidden.
- **Picker.** 「部分规则仍按第二期下半，正在逐步复刻」 is replaced by 「核心规则已按该版本。悬赏抽取、部分数值和六名敌人的攻击前摇仍是推算」
  [ASSUMED]. What that line names is still estimated after M2 (§35.22.5–§35.22.9): first-season bounty drafts carried
  with `assumed: true`, some numbers that equal 下半 because no source gave another, and six first-season enemies whose
  attack clips fall back to `ATTACK_PAUSE`. 预览 and the Chinese-texts line stay.
- **24 h resume, unchanged.** 第一期's constData has no `singleReconnectTime` (`12e0a020` added it later; both 上半 builds
  store `null`). `soloResumeWindowMs` still falls back to `SOLO_RECONNECT_FALLBACK_SEC` (86400). That window is a
  carried-over convenience [ASSUMED], not official 第一期 data and not "because nothing in a solo run is timed" — a
  第一期 solo is timed unless 「不限时」 is on. The constant and the fallback are not changed.

Tests: `test/lobby-solo-unlimited.test.js`, `test/ui/solo-unlimited.test.js`. Golden of the default version is unchanged.

#### 35.23.11 Every official version is enabled unless the list is narrowed

- **Seen:** the maintainer tried the 0.3.2 package and could not find the version switch. `versionsFrom` enabled only
  `act2autochess` when neither the `versions` option nor `SP_VERSIONS` was set, so the welcome carried no `versions` and
  the lobby hid the button. The M2 done-criterion in the local round-50 design notes was that the server enables 上半 by
  default.
- **Now:** nothing set enables every registry id, in registry order (第二期下半, 第二期上半, 第一期下半, 第一期上半). The
  default version stays the code constant `act2autochess`. `SP_VERSIONS` or `versions.enabled` still narrows the list:
  `SP_VERSIONS=act2autochess` is 第二期下半 alone and the switch stays off. `npm start`, `scripts/start.sh` and
  `scripts/start-windows.bat` set no flag. Version match hosts stay lazy and bounded: the pool exists when a non-default
  version is enabled, a process is forked on the first match of that version, never at boot, and `SP_MAX_VERSION_HOSTS`
  still caps how many (default: one per enabled non-default version, at least 1 when there is one). An idle server
  reports `hosts.workers` 0 and `hosts.matches` 0. A room of `act2autochess` still omits `version` and `versionVote`
  from `room.state`. G4 pins that room and the welcome's `versions` list (each `cs` normalised, so a sim change does not
  move the fixture).
- **Golden:** unchanged. The default version's battle results are not part of this change.
- **Tests:** `test/http-versions-config.test.js`, `test/versions-default-guard.test.js`, `test/lobby.test.js` (healthz),
  `test/data-versions.test.js`, `test/ui/versions.e2e.test.js` (the narrow-server case is `SP_VERSIONS=act2autochess`).

#### 35.23.12 Browser checks after enabling every version by default

- **Seen:** the browser checks requested after §35.23.11 reproduced three failures. None required restoring the
  single-version default:
  - `lobby-hub`'s room-settings assertion still expected only 「AI 队友最后选择」. §35.100 also offers the host's
    default-off 「允许观战者接替 AI」; the assertion now checks both, as does the same stale guest assertion in
    `room-settings.e2e.test.js` found by the full sweep. These were stale assertions about §35.100,
    independent of the version default.
  - The hub's text matrix found 21 clipped version labels: English at 中 / 大 / 特大 on the four phones and some
    small desktop windows, Japanese at 特大. The chip's fixed rem width caps did not follow the enlarged text.
    They are removed: the top bar reserves the label's natural width. **[ASSUMED]** presentation policy: show both
    season and part in full rather than abbreviating the part. The matrix keeps its text checks and also checks the
    switch's bounds, overlap with the tools and centre hit target; 小 is checked at 界面大小 75 % as well as 100 %.
  - `versions-flow` reached the version match's settlement and restored the guest's lobby, then failed its zero-error
    assertion. A temporary observer trace identified the bond strip's resize callback during prep: updating arrow
    state could re-render its observed slots during observer delivery. Resize updates are now coalesced into the
    next animation frame, unchanged edge states do not re-render, and unmount cancels pending work. The errors were
    also reproduced with that test's explicit `versions.enabled: 'all'`; this was not a single-version assumption.
    The bond-scroll browser check captures native observer errors and uses the shared Chrome discovery helper.
- **Further sweep:** `room-fit`'s generic text check had never seen the compact version pill on a default server.
  Its ellipsis is an existing policy (explicitly permitted by `versions-fit`), unlike the lobby chip's full label.
  The exception is now local to `room-fit`'s calls into the shared text checker and applies only to that pill's label;
  its full accessible name and footer bounds are checked in every room view, its centre hit target while the room is
  interactive (not behind the synthetic trusted-plugin consent modal). The product's compact
  room control is unchanged. This is the single-version-default test assumption exposed by §35.23.11.
- `plugins-settings.e2e.test.js` observed the guest on the new composition (0.4) while the host was still on the
  vanilla value (0.15). Each browser checks and installs its content independently; the test now waits for both
  clients before asserting the shared setting, rather than using guest readiness as a barrier for the host.
- **Unrelated baseline failures left unchanged:** `bond-timers.e2e.test.js`'s first settings case times out waiting
  for `.dbond-times`, `playtest2.e2e.test.js`'s detail-card bond case times out waiting for `.bpop`, and
  `playtest5-ui.e2e.test.js`'s first HUD-clearance case reports the 844×390 back-row tile 5 only 98 % free. All reproduce
  with the original `bondStrip.js` callback and `SP_VERSIONS=act2autochess` as well as with the new default / resize
  fix; the detail-card and HUD cases were isolated with `--test-name-pattern='9: the detail card'` and
  `--test-name-pattern='9: bench, temp'` respectively. Those tests and their timer, popup and camera behavior are
  outside this default-version regression change.
- **Sources:** §35.23.11, §35.100 and the real Chrome failures / observer trace above. No official gameplay rule or
  new gameplay assumption; the default data and all golden scenarios are unchanged. The stale off-by-default sentence
  in the DESIGN introduction now agrees with §35.23.11. Normative presentation: design/client.md "Official versions".
- **Verification:** every `test/ui/*.e2e.test.js` file was invoked individually with `SP_E2E=1`: 89 files, latest
  results 85 pass / 3 baseline failures above / 1 skipped (`real.e2e.test.js` requires `SP_REAL_E2E=1`). The explicit
  Chrome-path AI-handover run passes too. Full corrected runs: lobby-hub 15 pass, room-fit 10 pass, versions-flow
  2 pass, versions-fit 2 pass, room-settings 1 pass, plugins-settings 1 pass, bond-scroll 1 pass; all 0 fail.
  `npm run ci -- --keep-going`: 10,146 pass / 1 fail / 27 skip; the sole failure was the heavy-boss timing check
  (0.636 ms/tick under load, limit 0.5), and `node --test test/sim/robustness.test.js` passed all 35 when run alone.
  Setup, smoke, guards, lint, imports and typecheck passed. All golden scenarios match; no golden files moved.
  Final targeted Node/docs checks: 83 pass / 0 fail. All four translation packs: 2334/2334 messages, 0 errors;
  targeted lint and `git diff --check` pass.

### 35.24 Strategy grants at the prep start (#851)

- **Seen:** GitHub #851: with 老鲤, the R3 tier-II and tier-IV operators were already in the hand when the R3 机变 draft
  opened. Repro (同盟 NORMAL, seed 5): `ROUND_START` R3 holds both grants, then `SP_DRAFT`.
- **Official:** 老鲤 `aceffect_band_46` 「第3回合进入休整期时，获得2、4阶随机干员各1名」 (key
  `prep_start_gain_chess_from_pool_in_round`); 松桐 `aceffect_band_56` 「偶数回合的休整期开始时，随机获得调度中心的一名干员」;
  娜仁图亚 `aceffect_band_60` 「偶数回合进入休整期时」; 小贾斯汀 / 杰西卡 「在第…回合进入休整期时」; the other
  `preparation_start_gain_chess_from_round` texts give no time (昆图斯 「第3回合获得」, 梓兰 「第10回合获得」, 变形者集群 「每5回合
  获得」, 芬 「第8、10、12、14回合时」, Pith / 夕 「第1回合获得」, 马克维茨 「初始获得」) or 「在第1回合开始时」 (缪尔赛思); 杜宾 「每2个回合
  获得」 (`preparation_start_gain_chess_every_n_round`). research 01 §4.1 puts the 机变 choice (step 4) before the
  「进入休整期时」 effects (step 6), and its appendix A1 quotes the official 「进入此阶段时，触发<进入休整期时>的效果」 for the
  休整期.
- **Cause:** `bands/meta.js` ran these five keys on `onRoundStart`, before the `ROUND_START` delay and the 机变 draft; its
  header mapped 「进入休整期时」 to the round start, citing a step of research 01 that comes after the draft.
- **Now:** `prep_start_gain_chess_from_pool_in_round` (老鲤), `round_start_gain_char_chess_in_shop_every_n_round` (松桐),
  `preparation_start_add_special_goods_every_n_round` (娜仁图亚), `preparation_start_gain_chess_from_round` and
  `preparation_start_gain_chess_every_n_round` (杜宾) run on `onPrepStart` (`prep.js enterPrep`: after the 机变 draft and
  the prep offer window, after the deferred item merges, before the AI seats' prep is scheduled, so 托管 and the bots see
  the grants). A 机变 round (`config.json` `spRounds`: R3 in every 同盟 difficulty and in single HARD / ABYSS, R6 in 同盟 and
  single NORMAL) meets 老鲤 / 昆图斯 at R3 and 杜宾 / 松桐 / 娜仁图亚 at R6; elsewhere only the order around the round start changes
  (none of these grants depends on anything between). 余 「第8回合开始时」, 铃兰 「每个回合开始时」 and 哈洛德's
  `gain_bond_char_per_round` (「从第4回合开始每2回合获得」) stay on the round start. Normative: META.md §2.2 / §2.3,
  design/match.md §6.1.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the whole key moves, so 缪尔赛思's 「在第1回合开始时」 and the texts with no time follow their key's
  `preparation_start` (R1 is no 机变 round: no visible change). The operators' traits with `SERVER_PREP_START`
  (「<进入休整期时>」) still ran on `onRoundStart`, before a 机变 draft (META.md §2.3 at the time); not part of this report,
  unchanged here — moved to `onPrepStart` in §35.53.
- **Tests:** new `test/match/prep-start-grants.test.js` (同盟 NORMAL: nothing granted at `ROUND_START` / `SP_DRAFT` of R3,
  both at `PREP` R3, once; single NORMAL: both at the R3 prep start; both cases fail on the old code);
  `test/content/bands.test.js` (a `prepStart` helper for the five keys; 老鲤's round start alone grants nothing);
  `catherine-cell.test.js` (昆图斯's R3 cell) and `community-round30.test.js` (变形者集群) dispatch `onPrepStart`;
  `choices.test.js` 战术决策 E2E leaves a strategy's prep-start item out of the 列装 count (the AI's 昆图斯 突变细胞 now comes
  after the R3 draft); `feedback1-offers.test.js` (娜仁图亚) and `reviewLimits.test.js` (梓兰's cap) call the prep start.

Golden: no change (the `matches` family, the one with these strategies' prep hooks: 21 scenarios match).

### 35.25 Script probe ceiling in the executor tests (#847)

- **Seen:** GitHub #847: `trial uses the actual sandbox executor, pinned mode and runtime kit` (`test/studio/review.test.js`)
  failed once with `PLUGIN_SCRIPT_CHECK` 「script setup exceeded 5000 ms」 on a fully loaded Windows runner (run
  39612686697, job 119265417222, the older image; 1 of 66 sampled Windows jobs).
- **Cause:** the trial child composes the content set, whose registration probe (`server/plugins/scriptPreflight.js`)
  has the lobby's 5 s default ceiling (owner-accepted 2026-10-14); a child that starts slowly under load passes it. The
  two executor tests check the executor and the pinned mode, not the probe's timing, yet forked their trial child bare.
- **Now:** both executor tests (sandbox, trusted) fork their trial child with `SP_SCRIPT_PROBE_MS=15000`, as the probe-
  timeout tests' `loopBench` already gives its ceiling. The timeout message ends with 「; a slow or busy server can raise
  SP_SCRIPT_PROBE_MS」 (the tests match only `${ms} ms.*SIGKILL`). Unchanged: the lobby default (5 s), the 500–60000 range,
  the failure cache, the shared SIGKILL deadline (PLUGINS.md), no retry. Normative: PLUGINS.md (the message).
- **Tests:** the two executor tests. Locally (macOS, this worktree) the sandbox case fails before and after the change with
  `ERR_ACCESS_DENIED` (「Use --allow-fs-read」) from the sandbox child, an environment issue of this machine unrelated to
  the ceiling; the trusted case passes.

Golden: not concerned.

### 35.26 One 鸭爵 draw per round (#839)

- **Seen:** GitHub #839 「四人鸭爵不同步」: in 同盟 each player's 鸭爵 swaps differed (count, units, enemies); the reporter
  remembers the official swaps as identical and 「总在下路」. Repro (40 seeds × R5–R13, 4 players): the four counts equal
  in 50 of 360 rounds; 169 of 1,266 swaps (13 %) on an upper-lane route (start row 12).
- **Official:** `aceffect_band_49` key `round_start_all_player_change_enemy_2` (min 0, max 2, round 5, minweight 0.6,
  maxweight 0.99, enemylist `enemy_2002_bearmi_2` / `enemy_2034_sythef_2` / `enemy_2085_skzjxd_2` /
  `enemy_2001_duckmi_2`); PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半/PRTS盟约记录 鸭爵 备注 「每回合将有0~2名敌人被替换为上述敌人之一」. The
  client sends only the wave's own actions to the server (`ShouldActionUpToServer`: not the leader); bounty units are
  inserted per player afterwards (`_InsertSpActionToNormal`, waves.js withBounties).
- **Cause:** `duckReplace` drew each player's swaps separately from the shared `m.rngMeta`, over the player's own list —
  bounty units included in the 60–99 % share, the host action's own units re-timed around them. A shared stream alone
  would not have fixed it: with different bounties the picks still differed in 81 of 180 rounds (triage check).
- **Now:** `bands/meta.js duckPicks` draws once per round from the round's own wave (`ev.waveSpawns`, a copy of the wave
  before the player's bounties, now on the normal and the boss / hidden `onBattleStart` events) with a stream of its own,
  `createRng(ctx.seedFor('duck:<round>'))` (`effectsMeta.js makeCtx seedFor`: a uint32 from the match seed, the same for
  every player, dispatch and preview; a read in the preview context). A pick is (action, k-th unit of that action);
  `applyDuckPicks` swaps the k-th own unit of that action in each player's list, counting over the runs a bounty split
  the action into, at that player's own time and route. Every player now has the same count, the same units and the same
  鸭爵 enemies (the same 40 seeds: equal counts in 360 of 360 rounds). The leader and its parts are left out of the
  share as well. The stats preview and the bot rehearsals see the live swaps and no longer draw from the meta stream.
  Normative: META.md §2.2 (`waveSpawns`) / §2.4 (`seedFor`), PLAYING.md (鸭爵).
- **[ASSUMED]:** the same enemy for every player (the report says the same enemy is swapped; nothing says whether the
  replacement kind is shared). The lane is unchanged: the 60–99 % time share stays the candidate rule, no bottom-lane
  restriction; the report has no recording, and 168 of 1,336 swaps (13 %) still fall on an upper-lane route over the
  same 40 seeds. A recording or the maintainer's recall that the upper lane is never used would restrict the candidates
  to the bottom spawn row. The leader and its parts out of the share (the client never sends the leader's action to the
  server; it also keeps a pair field's halves alike). On a pair boss field both halves use the same round stream (no
  side salt): halves that line up pick mirrored units; a half of three or fewer units has no unit inside the share.
- **Tests:** new `test/match/ducklord-shared-draw.test.js` (4 players with 0–3 bounties each, 60 draws: identical swaps,
  the same own enemy count, the preview equal and drawing nothing; 0, 1 and 2 swaps occur; some swaps re-timed per
  player; `applyDuckPicks` across split runs). Adjusted (another draw = another match seed now): `bands.test.js` 鸭爵,
  `feedback1-ducklord.test.js` (`optsWith`; the pair-field test runs up to 24 seeds for both halves of both leader
  rounds), `feedback5-cb4-pins.test.js` item 35.

Golden: the four matches whose AI holds 鸭爵 move, every other scenario keeps its values (`solo-FUNNY-2`, `solo-NORMAL-2`,
`solo-HARD-2`, `solo-ABYSS-2`; regenerated with `node tools/golden.mjs --update --family matches --only <IDs>`). First
consequences: the meta stream draws 39 / 84 / 84 / 91 → 2 / 4 / 17 / 4 numbers; the first swaps differ, so the duck
bounties paid change (`solo-NORMAL-2` / `solo-ABYSS-2` R5 pending 1 → 0, `solo-FUNNY-2` R6 2 → 1) and the AI's later
shops and boards follow. Results: `solo-HARD-2` victory → defeat (R14 boss pool left 0 → 127,370, team LP 29 → 0),
`solo-NORMAL-2` team LP 29 → 14, `solo-FUNNY-2` 27 → 29, `solo-ABYSS-2` boss pool left 2,175,584 → 2,590,453. The
G1 fixture (`test/fixtures/versions-default-guard.json`, the golden corpus hash) is refreshed with `SP_UPDATE_GUARD=1`
for this golden update; no data file moved.

### 35.27 Hidden Core pairs drawn anew (#845)

- **Seen:** GitHub #845 「第14和第15波boss分组必定相同」: the Hidden Core kept the Final Assault's partners and sides. The
  reporter cites the tournament recording BV1VCXyBjEN1 p1 (「【DAY1】无敌老队·团队竞速」, 2026-03-29, 4-player 同盟) at
  40–45 min: R14 1-4 / 2-3, R15 1-2 / 3-4.
- **Official (checked on the recording, 2026-10-23):** watched in the built-in browser (the site's unauthenticated 360p
  stream; no file downloaded, no frame kept in the repository). In boss rounds the players' column (the four 名片
  portraits, the same fixed order on every screen) frames the viewer's own pair in green; normal rounds frame none (R13 at
  38:20). R14 prep (回合 14, 盐风主教昆图斯): on 伊颜轩's screen (41:00) portraits 2 and 3 are green, on Disillusion号角鸽's
  (42:40) 1 and 4 — complementary pairs. R15 prep (回合 ??): on 久城kazuya's screen (44:00) 1 and 2 are green, on 金乌's
  (44:40) 1 and 2 as well. So R14 paired Disillusion号角鸽 (4) with 1 and 伊颜轩 (3) with 2, and R15 paired 1 with 2 and the
  other two together: the reporter's 1-4 / 2-3 → 1-2 / 3-4, an independent redraw. The halves (which side each player
  defends) were not compared across the two rounds.
- **Cause:** a deliberate 0.2.5 choice (§30.12: the maintainer kept the partners for the hidden boss), not official
  data; `pairPlayers` was called with `previous` = the Final Assault's pairs and the same seed for both rounds.
- **Now:** `phases.js _planBossWaves` draws the Hidden Core's pairs and sides anew (`previous` null) with a seed of its
  own, `deriveSeed(seed, 'boss-pairs:15')` (`finalAssault.js bossPairSalt`); the Final Assault keeps `'boss-pairs'`, so
  its draw is unchanged. The plan is still made at the R15 round start, before the prep preview and the board checks,
  and `startFinalAssault` keeps it. Only the round's first plan draws anew (`bossPairsRound`): a departure in the R15
  round start, 机变 draft or prep re-plans (`platform.js _quit`) with `previous` = that draw, so the intact pair stays and
  only the orphan is re-paired, as in the Final Assault (review of this section: the re-plan drew everything anew, and
  all eight probe seeds broke the pair the departed player was not in). A player who changes half keeps the whole
  board: boards are player-local and mirrored per side (`bossFieldPlacement`), and the two halves' deploy maps are
  identical tile for tile (triage check: bossL / bossR 20 tiles, no difference). The client camera, placement and green
  partner frames already read `m.public.bossPairs` per round. This supersedes §30.12's retained partners. Normative:
  META.md (Final Assault / Hidden Core), PLAYING.md (最终攻势).
- **[ASSUMED]:** the sides are drawn anew with the pairs (the recording shows the new pairs; the halves were not
  compared); the reading of the green portrait frame as the viewer's pair rests on the pattern above (boss rounds
  only, complementary in R14, identical for two players in R15) and on our own client's partner frames modelled on it.
- **Tests:** `finalAssault.test.js` (six seeds: the Final Assault's draw as before, the Hidden Core's its own draw used
  by the R15 prep preview, the side checks and the fields; every board kept whole on its R15 half; some seed changes the
  pairs or sides); eight seeds where a player of the first pair leaves in the R15 prep: the other pair stays whole, the
  orphan alone, the preview and the fields following (failed on the first version, every seed).

Golden: one scenario moves, `coop2-ABYSS-10-boosted` (2 AI players): its Hidden Core swaps the two players' halves
(R14 ai_1 left / ai_0 right, R15 ai_0 left / ai_1 right). Hidden Core results: team LP 800 → 723, boss damage ai_0
6,144,725 → 6,761,316 / ai_1 7,746,032 → 7,123,077. Every other scenario keeps its values (`solo-HARD-9-boosted` is one
player). G1 fixture refreshed with `SP_UPDATE_GUARD=1`.

### 35.28 新约能天使's 火力电台 by the PRTS notes

- **Seen:** while reviewing PR #861 (the official TalentBomb visual for 火力电台), three differences from PRTS in the sim
  (round 51 triage, decision D6; the maintainer's 2026-10-23 rule puts it in 0.3.2).
- **Official:** talent text 「每当有友方干员的弹药被消耗就会回复自身6%生命值，并有25%概率立即对该干员攻击范围的敌人召唤一次
  轰炸」 (`hp_ratio` 0.06, `prob` 0.25, `aoe_atk_scale` 1.65 at 潜能 ≥ 4). [PRTS 新约能天使](https://prts.wiki/w/新约能天使) 天赋
  备注 (read 2026-10-22): per consumption the heal and the chance are handled once per bullet spent, 「每轮轰炸仅选取一次目标，
  每次成功的概率判定都会增加一次本轮的轰炸」; 「轰炸半径1.3，单轮多次轰炸之间间隔0.1s，造成预计算的物理普通伤害」; the target
  is chosen by her among the enemies of the consumer's range (「始终使用默认索敌逻辑」, 「不受对方的攻击距离增减、视野、索敌逻辑
  影响」); 「轰炸为附着型弹道」. The prefab's TalentBomb (fx/index.json) is a MultiRangedAttack with `triggerDelta` 0.1.
- **Cause:** the kit rolled every bullet on its own: each success chose its own target with the consumer's sort (its
  blocker first) among the consumer's current range and hit radius 1 (`AIRSTRIKE_RADIUS`, [ASSUMED]) at once.
- **Now:** `kits/ops/chess_char_6_13-angel2.js` 火力电台: the bullets one action spends in a tick (the engine's first and a
  kit's further ones — 新约能天使 S3's 5, a stopped S1 volley) are one round; each heals and rolls, the first success chooses
  the round's target — her default order (`sortEnemyTargets` with her as the attacker) among the enemies of the
  consumer's own range (its own grid with no 攻击距离 change, `absoluteRangeKeys(u.rangeGrid, …, 0)`: 「不受对方的攻击距离
  增减」 — the review of this section found `baseRangeKeys` carrying the permanent 攻击距离 +N extension of talents,
  modules and bonds, `s.baseRangeExtend`; [ASSUMED] that the note excludes those too; a grid that the unit's module or
  kit puts in place of its own, `u.rangeGrid` — tier4 `applyModuleRange`, op-aphris's path —, is that unit's own grid
  and counts; tier5 `moduleRangeUp` without a grid uses `rangeExtend` and is excluded too: none of today's ammunition
  consumers has a module grid) — and computes the damage; each success adds a bombing
  0.1 s after the previous one (`BOMB_GAP`), radius 1.3 (`BOMB_RADIUS`) around the target where it stands then (the
  bomb follows it). S3's 投递坐标 splash keeps radius 1 [ASSUMED]. The `airstrike` fx carries `talent: true` and r 1.3.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a bombing whose target has died lands where it last stood; her 视野遮挡 rule (a sight-blocking device
  between her and the target) is not modelled (no stage here has one).
- **Tests:** `kits_t6.test.js` 火力电台 (one action spending 3 bullets, every chance a success: three bombings of the one
  target 0.1 s apart, the enemy 1.22 tiles away hit by each, the same damage; fails on the old code); the review added an
  ATK buff after a round's first bombing (the later two keep the first amount: computed once per round) and a consumer
  with a permanent 攻击距离 +1 whose only enemy stands in the extension (no bombing; fails with `baseRangeKeys`).

Golden: the twelve scenarios with 新约能天使 move, every other keeps its values (regenerated with `node tools/golden.mjs
--update --family roster,fields,matches --only <IDs>`): `roster-017` / `-019` / `-036` / `-037` / `-045` / `-047`, the pair
fields `boss-boss_1-pair` / `_3` / `_5` / `_7` and `hidden-boss_9-pair`, and `solo-NORMAL-2` (ai_0 damage 2,413,795 →
2,416,606). First consequences: fewer, later and wider bombings — `roster-017` two more hits; `roster-036` 新约能天使's
damage 33,053 → 35,712; the pair fields' leader HP left 43,995 → 71,949 (`boss_1`), 3,377,068 → 3,534,248 (`hidden-boss_9`),
`boss_5` ends 104.40 → 99.57 s, `boss_3` 170.27 → 167.57 s. G1 fixture refreshed.

### 35.29 The other leaders' attack-class skills take the attack's turn

§35.19 left two lists for later: the other leaders' attack-class skills, still cast on their own timers beside the
normal attack, and the prefabs whose numbered clips do not follow their skill slots. The attack's turn is done here,
the slot clips in §35.48; the bounty leaders and the normal enemies with such skills followed in §35.52.

- **Official:** every leader and part of the ten boss records and their escorts was checked in the prefab data
  (`public/assets/local/fx/index.json` units.<key>.skills, from the client's `battle/enm_pfb_*.ab`) for a skill whose
  ability is a MeleeAttack / RangedAttack / Multi*Attack — the class of a normal attack:
  - 卢西恩 `aoe` (MeleeAttack `Skill_2`, 1.8 s) and 不祥幻影 `aoe` (MeleeAttack `Skill`, 1.8 s); 卢西恩's `blink` is an
    ActiveBuffAbility;
  - 假想敌：胄 (both records) `1` 灭顶之灾 (RangedAttack `Skill_1`, 2.333 s, priority 1); `2` 死亡集群 is an
    AnimatedActionToOwnerAbility, `3` (隐秘核心) an EmptyAnimatedAbility;
  - 假想敌：管 (both) `1` 裂管之奏 (MultiRangedAttack `Skill`, 4.167 s) and 假想敌：弦 `1` 断弦之奏 (RangedAttack `Skill`,
    3.333 s) — their normal attack is an AbilitySelectableGroup (the strikes on 余音, the kits' own loop);
  - 阿利斯泰尔 `equip` / `equipA` 王权号令 (RangedAttack `Skill_1`, 3.667 s, priority 1, SP 10 in the levels) and `Suicide` 斥退
    (MeleeAttack `Skill_3`, 5.667 s, priority 5); `store` / `storeA` 莫非王土 are AbilitySelectableGroups of
    AnimatedActionToTargetAbility;
  - “萨米的意志” `Lasso` 自然涌动 (RangedAttack, no `_animKey`) and `Doom` (MeleeAttack `Skill_02`, 2.667 s) — its normal
    attack 冰凌 is a ProjectileToTileAbility (the kit's own loop);
  - not changed: 假想敌：铳 `3` (RangedAttack with no clip, cooldown 10,000,000: the 盲信之誓 chains the kit runs on its
    interval), `1` (ActiveBuffAbility), `2` (AnimatedActionToTargetAbility); 碎铳之簧 b / c (RangedAttack `Skill_b`,
    MultiRangedAttack `Skill_c`) are SP attacks — every (spCost + 1)-th attack —, already in the attack's turn; “帝国的甲胄”
    `MultiCombat` already arms its next attack and `Appear` is its spawn; 昆图斯 §35.19; 炎佑 is an ally summon here; the
    other escorts (堂皇 `InvisibleShield`, 复核洋流使者, 萨科塔昂首 `Roar`, 再生, 淤困) have no attack-class skill.
  - PRTS's skill rules (盐风主教昆图斯 page, §35.19): 「大多数技能自动触发会占用攻击间隔」, 「优先级相同则随机触发其中之一」,
    「同一技能的冷却时间于该技能结束时开始计时」.
- **Now (sim):** `enemies.js attackTurnSkill` holds the attack-turn choice of §35.19 (ready, castable, its condition holding;
  the highest priority — an ability's `pr`, else the data's —, equal ones at random) and casts; `attackSlotSkill` calls
  it at a swing's damage frame, and the kits whose leader attacks through its own loop call it when that loop's attack
  is due and has a target (管 / 弦 on an echo of their form, 萨米 with a column to strike), skipping the attack when it
  casts. `bosses.js ATTACK_SKILL_CLIP` gives each skill's clip (the cooldown counts from its end); the skills above are
  marked `atk`. 阿利斯泰尔's 王权号令 becomes an attack-class ability that is ready while its SP (`kitLion`'s own counter,
  `ENEMY_SP_PER_SEC` [ASSUMED]) is full and a target is in range: cast in the next attack's turn, the SP restarting at the
  cast — before, it came the moment the SP was full, on top of the attacks. 莫非王土 keeps its cycle. No target, no
  turn, no skill.
- **The aoe's own trigger (review of this section):** 卢西恩 and 不祥幻影 are MELEE with range 0, so their swing only
  ever has their blocker as its target; cast only at a swing's damage frame, an unblocked one — 卢西恩 most of the time,
  his blink takes him past his blocker — never cast its aoe, against PRTS's trigger (不祥幻影 「仅在半径2范围内存在我方
  单位时触发」; 卢西恩 技能 「需要目标」, a radius-2 selector). PRTS's 「可以攻击时不进行攻击，改为触发技能」 is read as the
  attack being ready: a skill with a trigger selector of its own (`own: true` — the aoe's `targetsNear` radius 2) also
  takes the turn of an attack whose cooldown has run out with no swing started (`enemies.js` onTick,
  `attackTurnSkill(b, e, ownOnly)`), the attack cooldown restarting as after a strike; blocked, it still comes at the
  swing's damage frame. [ASSUMED] the effect at once (no swing, no wind-up) and the normal attack's refusals kept
  (恐惧 / 缴械 / 战栗 while blocked). The skills that use the attack's own targets keep the swing alone.
- **Now (client):** the slot clips of the §35.19 list are §35.48. Both 0.3.2 backlog lanes fixed that table; the
  integration kept the client lane's: the same clips for 泥岩, “自在”, 斩胄之剑 / 破胄之锤 and 锏's circles, but 杰斯顿 and
  碎铳之簧 as `strike` slots (their casts come inside an attack, with no skill window, so a plain slot clip would never
  have shown) and 锏's blinks on the attack role. This lane's alternatives were dropped: the blinks on the
  `A_` / `B_Skill1_Begin` → `_End` pair [ASSUMED: by elimination, the one clip set no other slot names] and 杰斯顿's second
  form without a `skill` role (the strike plays `C2_Skill` without it). Normative: SIM.md §1.2.
- **[ASSUMED]:** as in §35.19 (the choice at the damage frame, the attack cooldown restarting at the skill, the effect at
  the attack's damage frame, the clip kept under a slow). 萨米's 自然涌动 names no clip: its cooldown counts from the cast.
  阿利斯泰尔's SP accrues as before (1/s, `ENEMY_SP_PER_SEC`), now from the cast.
- **Not in this section:** the non-leader enemies and bounty leaders with an attack-class skill on a cooldown (高准度
  伦蒂尼姆城防自行炮, 萨卡兹王庭军精锐术师, 重弩突袭者, W, 鼠王, 腐败骑士 / 凋零骑士, “自在”, 锏, 迷路的巨像, 陷落雪祀,
  假想敌：黑云, 节日爵士乐手, 扎罗) take the attack's turn since §35.52 (the maintainer's 2026-10-23 rule: nothing doable
  deferred); the SP attacks (粉碎攻坚手, 澪, 杰斯顿 …) are already the attack itself.
- **Tests:** new `test/content/boss-attack-slot.test.js` (ATTACK_SKILL_CLIP = the clip of each attack-class ability, with
  the fx index when extracted; 不祥幻影 / 卢西恩 aoe, 胄's 灭顶之灾 with 死亡集群 on its timer, 管 / 弦, 阿利斯泰尔's 王权号令
  only once its SP is full and an attack between two, 斥退, 萨米's 自然涌动 — each one action per attack interval, no cast
  in an attack's tick; no target, no aoe); `test/render/forms.test.js` (锏's second form without a skill role; the
  clip tests are §35.48's). Adjusted `enemies_bosses.test.js` (the shell, 裂管之奏 / 断弦之奏, the aoe, 王权号令, 斥退, 自然涌动 / Doom at
  the first turn after their (initial) cooldown; the leaders attack instead of `noAttack`; the phantom walks into its
  blocker); `community-round30.test.js`, `feedback1b-displacement.test.js` and `feedback1f-liftoff.test.js` (胄 attacks
  — harmlessly where its rays would kill the operator the shell needs —; the airborne check of the aoe on 不祥幻影, blocked
  once 角峰 lands); `feedback2-stealth-splash.test.js` #32.6 (红豆 blocks 卢西恩, its blink held back, so it has turns).
  The review added: an unblocked 不祥幻影 / 卢西恩 casts its aoe with an operator within radius 2 (its own trigger; the
  attack cooldown restarting at the cast), none out of it; 阿利斯泰尔's 王权号令 keeps the turn an attack when its target
  is attacked only through the collider's reach (√13 ≈ 3.61 tiles: the decree selects within 3.4 — without `cond`'s
  target check the turn would be spent on nothing, the SP kept).

Golden: the scenarios with 胄 (boss_1 / boss_8), 管 (boss_3 / boss_10), 卢西恩 (boss_5), 阿利斯泰尔 (boss_6), 萨米 (boss_7)
or 不祥幻影 move; 铳 and 昆图斯 keep theirs (regenerated with `node tools/golden.mjs --update --family
roster,fields,matches`; the others recompute to the same values). `roster-006`, `roster-048` (不祥幻影 among the extra

922,115; boss_6 pair 6,486,991 → 6,270,418; boss_5 pair cleared at 104.8 s (was 99.57); hidden boss_8 pair 12,014,148 →

### 35.30 Settlement penetration order (PRs #796 and #798, @Siornya)

**PR #796 (refactor 01, the attack resolution API).** Before the penetration change, #796 makes the new
`server/sim/attack.js` the one public settlement entry for content and engine callers: it re-exports the helpers
(`hasHp`, `isHpLoss`, `makeDamageInfo`, `mitigate`, `periodicDamage`, `burstLocked`, `elementView`, `palsyBuff`,
`applyRedirectCut`, `redirectShare`) that content, bots, professions, battle events / status and tests imported from
`damage.js`, and switches those 64 files' imports to it. The legacy pipeline stays behind it, so nothing behaves
differently: a clean merge (79145a9eb), 0 of 288 golden scenarios moved. It adds `server/sim/AGENTS.md`, a short guide
for authors (call the operations on `battle`, import helpers from attack.js, never internal paths), and two AGENTS.md
lines (read that guide before editing the sim; two-space indentation, no tabs). The object-request operations behind the
entry (`dealDamage`, `dealHPLose`, `heal`, `dealElementDamage`, `healElement` with `source` / `credit`) arrive with #802
(§35.32), and the guide was rewritten for them there; #856 writes the contract into the docs (§35.35). Credited at the
fourth review of the batch (§35.47): until then the index and §35.32 pointed here for #796, but this section described
only #798.

**PR #798.** Fixed DEF/RES penetration is subtracted and clamped at zero before percentage penetration. The former order
could over-penetrate when both were present. Source: [PRTS 伤害公式](https://prts.wiki/w/游戏数据基础#伤害公式).
Core and Irene regressions cover the arithmetic.

**Golden.** 17 of 288 scenarios move, the same 17 the PR lists (roster 5, fields 4, matches 4, standins 2, diy 2); the
versions G1 fixture of §35.22.2 was refreshed with the merge (213d2ce61). Added at the merge review: each one traced on
the merged tree with a probe on `mitigate` reporting the first instance whose result differs between the two orders
(every earlier instance is the same, so it is the first divergence). Every one is a hit that combines fixed and
percentage penetration of the same kind:

| Fixed penetration | Percentage penetration | Scenarios (first differing hit) |
|---|---|---|
| 艾丽妮 (自选) trait, DEF −70 | her T1 审判之火 charge, 50 % DEF | diy-005 (8.7 s, on 粉碎攻坚组长, 210 → 175), diy-006 (20.8 s) |
| 圣聆初雪 (elite) S3 群山俯首, RES −10 | 精准号 bond, 30 % RES | boss-boss_3-pair (3 s, on 假想敌：管), boss-boss_5-pair, hidden-boss_9-pair, unite-1, roster-018 |
| 迷迭香 talent, DEF −175 | 精准号 30 % DEF (roster-045: and the 锐利 choice, 60 % in all) | coop2-ABYSS-10-boosted, coop2-NORMAL-14-standins, roster-037, roster-045 |
| 迷迭香 talent, DEF −175 | the 锐利 choice (`allybuff_select_19`, 30 % DEF) | roster-017 |
| 送葬人 talent, DEF −175 (elite) / −95 | 精准号 30 % DEF | coop4-FUNNY-6, solo-ABYSS-2 |
| 送葬人 talent, DEF −95 | the 锐利 choice, 30 % DEF | roster-003 |
| Pith (焰影苇草's 补位) talent, RES −12 | 精准号 30 % RES | standin-04, standin-09 |

### 35.31 Q / X prefer the selected operator, otherwise use hover (PR #859, @FelixSion28)

The report in PR #859 describes selecting operator A and moving the pointer over B, then selling B with X.
Q / X now prefer the current selected own operator, matching the underframe buttons. When nothing
is selected they retain the live pointer hit test from §32.14.22. Q retreats a board operator; X sells a
board, bench or temporary-bench operator. No selection or hovered operator means no action. Selecting
an item or summon blocks both shortcuts without falling back to another hovered operator. Removing
the selected piece or leaving editable prep invalidates the selection. Pointer movement cannot change
a selected target; without a selection, leaving the window, losing focus or touch input clears hover.
Dragging, facing, held pointer buttons, the pen, pending actions, dialogs, text input, auto-repeat,
readiness, spectating and autopilot keep their existing guards. W continues buying the hovered shop card.
Settings labels and every language pack describe selection priority.

Decision: the maintainer accepted PR #859 on 2026-10-23 for convenience (「按他说的来吧，感觉是方便一些」), including the
PR's behaviour for a right-click or long-press on another own operator while one is selected. The triage had read
game.js's pieceClick alone (it returns before `setSel` on a right-click) and expected the selection to stay; in the
browser the press on the field clears the selection first, as any press there does, and the release only opens the
other operator's card (no underframe). Q / X then follow the hover as with no selection, so what they act on is
what the card and the pointer show. The PR's 「维护者确认的交互调整」 (DEV-20261022-02) had no earlier record; this
dated decision is the source.

This client input change supersedes §32.14.22 for Q / X (0.3.0 chose hover only, so that a forgotten selection
could not be sold by a stray key); it adds no battle rule or [ASSUMED] gameplay detail. Reference: PLAYING §11;
current contract: design/client.md. Browser regression: test/ui/hover-hotkeys.e2e.test.js covers the selected
target against a different hovered operator in the simplified, 2D and 3D boards, the right-click case above
(added at the merge review: the old selection is not sold), hover without selection, repeated requests and blocked actions. Golden unchanged.

### 35.32 Ordered settlement pipeline and dependent consumers (PR #802, @Siornya)

The third part of @Siornya's settlement series (#796 and #798 are §35.30; #844, #855 and #856 follow in §35.33–§35.35)
replaces the v0.3.1 damage pipeline with the kernel under `server/sim/attack/`, entered only through
`server/sim/attack.js`: `dealDamage`, `dealElementDamage`, `dealHPLose`, `heal` and `healElement` take object requests
with the actual `source` and an independent `credit` (statistics and kills; `source: null, credit: owner` is attributed
无来源 damage); the positional Battle calls and the `loseHp` / `reduceElement` aliases stay and run the same kernel.
The stages follow PRTS [伤判效果](https://prts.wiki/w/伤判效果), [伤害分类](https://prts.wiki/w/伤害分类) and
[事件](https://prts.wiki/w/事件): `hit` → the source's calculation (`damageCalculate`; not for `calculation:
'precomputed'` or `'resolved'` requests) → the target's (`damageCalculateTarget`, the arts attack addition) → the DEF /
RES formula with fixed before percentage penetration (§35.30) and the source's multipliers → `damageOutput` →
`damageBeforeApply` (source) → `damageApplying` (target) → 限伤 → the target's injury effects in priority, then
acquisition order (damage-taken multipliers such as 脆弱 / 庇护, fixed reductions — the strongest per HP type —,
barriers and count shields, held dodge / block effects; a cancellation stops the later defences while observers still
run) → `hpDamage` and transfer reservations → the HP step (special HP maps only the HP delta) → `hpChanged`,
`modifierApplied`, `damageApplied`, the legacy `damaged`, fatal / kill, `onApplied`. Requests carry the native
`attackType` (NONE / NORMAL / SPLASH / BUFF / ADDITION, default NORMAL) and `applyWay`; `isAttack` is the attack
behaviour only. Current contract: SIM §4 and design/engine.md §5.5 (the damage line and "Settlement consumers and
evidence"); short examples: `server/sim/AGENTS.md`.

**Behaviour changes.** v0.3.1, now and the source of each; "templates" are the official `buff_template_data` at
[ArknightsGameResource 9bf23966](https://github.com/yuanyan3060/ArknightsGameResource/blob/9bf239661e37766fba585806fb9fa67e79bb679e/gamedata/battle/buff_template_data.json)
(the PR's pinned revision; the templates cited here were read again for this merge).

| Mechanism | v0.3.1 | 0.3.2 | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Injury order | one aggregate dodge roll first, count shields before value barriers, some content cancelling in `hit` | every effect on its buff, priority then acquisition order; a shield acquired before a dodge is spent first and the reverse | PRTS 伤判效果 (事件优先级 > 获取顺序, 例1 赤冬 / 深海色, 例2 泥岩 / 铃兰) |
| After a cancellation | everything stopped, or one late notification | later defences spend no count and roll nothing; 反伤 and other observers still run at their position | PRTS 伤判效果 ("绝大部分效果（例如反伤、受伤计数等）仍可处理") |
| Dodge, block, miss | one cancel flag and `canDodge` | separate outcomes; skipping a dodge and rolling one that cannot cancel are distinct (`dodgeMode`) | PRTS 伤判效果 (无视闪避), 骋风 |
| Derived damage | riders re-ran the source's calculation | `calculation` base / precomputed (target calculation and DEF / RES kept) / resolved | PRTS 伤害分类; AdvancedApplyDamage `_emitSourceOnCalculateDamage` |
| Special HP | a hit-count unit turned the damage into 1 before barriers and riders | the damage is computed normally, the HP step removes one point; `damaged`, the damage number and riders see the damage | PRTS 游戏数据基础 (特殊生命值机制), 假想敌：再生 |
| 青瓷茶器 | non-physical types (element too) spent its count | only arts and true spend it; the damage value stays | PRTS 青瓷茶器 |
| Fixed reductions | emulated through DEF / RES or a multiplier (source multipliers doubled them) | subtracted at the effect's injury position; same-named ones take the strongest per type | PRTS 术语释义, 浊心 / 海沟实验体 notes |
| Burst class | by the current side | by entity class: an enemy-class ally keeps the enemy burst and the leader capacity | PRTS 元素 |
| Element-burst damage on operators (灼燃 1200 arts, 侵蚀 800 phys, 凋亡 100 arts/s; the legacy necrosis tick) | not dodgeable (`canDodge: false`) | ordinary 无来源 damage: a phys / arts dodge (held dodge effects and injury dodges such as 老鲤 S3, 赤刃明霄陈, 蒂比 too) cancels the HP damage; the burst status and cooldown stay. Found at the merge review: the merge listed no row; necrosis, which kept `dodgeMode: 'ignore'`, follows since then (no official template of its own) | templates `ep_break_fire_char` (MAGICAL), `ep_break_water_char` (PHYSICAL), the dark char's `periodic_magic_damage` (MAGICAL BUFF), `ep_break_anger_char` (PURE): NoSourceDamage, `_ignoreCancelReasonMask` NONE |
| 星熊 | armour: phys / arts with an enemy source; thorns on `damaged`, counter / reflect tags skipped | armour: an unqualified damage block (any type, any source); thorns observe every enemy-source instance, the blocked ones too | PRTS 星熊 (伤害抵挡); templates `hsguma_e_002_t` (BlockDamage, no type filter), `inverse_damage` |
| 拉普兰德 S1 | early cancel reported as a block | a held physical dodge | PRTS 拉普兰德 (修正: 闪避) |
| 雷蛇 S1, 凯尔希 S1 | independent early cancels | held ordinary blocks | PRTS 雷蛇, 凯尔希, 伤判效果 |
| 圣约送葬人 S2 | needed an attack; melee guessed from profession / distance | +2000 injury dodge on the real enemy source and the instance's route | PRTS 圣约送葬人; `excu2_s_2` |
| 赤刃明霄陈 | aggregate dodge rolled twice; absorbed hits reset the timer | −1000 held one-use dodge, spent by its own attempt; successful damage resets the timer | PRTS 赤刃明霄陈 |
| 薇薇安娜 | early melee guess; refills rebuilt the buff | enemy source + instance route block; refills keep the order | PRTS 薇薇安娜 |
| 余 | S1 on late notifications; talent arts before the burn; S3 wall cancelled ranged attacks in `hit` | S1 a held enemy-damage observer; talent burns first, then precomputed BUFF arts; the S3 wall stands half a tile ahead, 0.3 wide, and removes enemy projectiles that cross it | PRTS 余 (S1, 礼尚往来, S3 备注); `yu_s_1[inverse_damage]` |
| 泡泡 | counter and 尖刺盾 on the late `damaged`, 尖刺盾 on unselectable enemies too | S2 counters the source at injury; 尖刺盾 at injury, after a selection check | PRTS 泡泡; `bubble_s_2`, `bubble_t_1` |
| 斥罪 | late thorns; the empty barrier rebuilt; counter tags excluded | thorns before her −1000 barrier (the breaking hit reflects); the empty barrier keeps its instance and position | PRTS 斥罪; `judge_t_2`; 伤判效果 (−1000) |
| 年 | S2 late; module from broad notifications | S2 at injury on its source; the module reacts to its own layer loss | PRTS 年; `inverse_damage[magic]` |
| 斩业星熊 | S1 / AST-X riders re-ran calculation, module on late notifications | S1 NORMAL / NONE precomputed; AST-X ADDITION output and NORMAL take-damage counter only in their windows | PRTS 斩业星熊; `hsgma2_s_1`, `hsgma2_e_003_tr_output` / `_take` |
| 刺玫 S2 | counter on the protégé's late `damaged` | the protégé holds an injury observer: the counter (RANGED precomputed arts, hers) and her trait heal resolve before the protégé loses HP | PRTS 刺玫; `vendla_s_2` (ON_TAKE_DAMAGE), `vendla_tr` |
| 玛恩纳 | thorns and S3 from late notifications | thorns: true NORMAL / NONE precomputed; S3: an enemy-held 卡西米尔 NORMAL / SPLASH injury observer dealing true ADDITION | PRTS 玛恩纳; `mlynar_t_2[inverse]`, `mlynar_s_3[aura]` |
| 菲莱 | late trigger, counter tags excluded, immediate area hit | any injury (cooldown reserved); a delayed child on ground targets; precomputed arts, then apoptosis; S1 heals each gauge by the maximum | PRTS 菲莱; `philae_s_2`, `philae_s_2[ep]` |
| 信仰搅拌机 | S3 counter / ammo on the late `damaged` | S3 counter at its injury stage; the talent on successful damage | `rmixer_sk3` (ON_TAKE_DAMAGE), `rmixer_t1` (ON_AFTER_OUTPUT_DAMAGE) |
| 纯烬艾雅法拉 S2 | recasts stacked new pools | a recast from one source resets its pool and duration; absorption at −2000 | PRTS 纯烬艾雅法拉 |
| 武者, 左乐 | heals / SP from late success; barriers re-created | source calculation and output stages; output precedes the target's dodge / barrier | PRTS 左乐; `zuole` / `helage_trait` / `utage_trait` (ON_OUTPUT_DAMAGE) |
| 收割者 | one heal per normal attack, other damage on late notifications | output admission up to the block count per 0.05 s window, heals queued 0.12 s apart; 隐德来希 filters BUFF | PRTS 隐德来希, 圣约送葬人; `excu2_trait[heal_fake]` / `[heal]` |
| 咒愈师 | true HP loss could heal, 元素伤害 could not | successful ordinary damage heals by the damage amount, not the special-HP HP removed | PRTS 缇缇; `vendla_tr` / `reed2_tr` / `titi_tr` (AssignDamageValueToBlackboard, `_assignRealDelta` false) |
| 天马之枪 | 30 % of the carrier's ATK, sourced, after the parent | 30 % of the parent's scaled input, source-less true ADDITION credited to the carrier, before the parent's injury | PRTS 下半盟约记录 天马之枪 备注 |
| 卡西米尔 (6) | on attacks, sourced | on native NORMAL damage (not the SPLASH part), a source-less true ADDITION credited to the member | PRTS 盟约记录; 「未阻挡敌人时攻击附带」 |
| 蒂比 | S2 cancelled early; dodges reset the talent | S2 after output, before ordinary injury; the talent counts from deployment and grants a held one-use dodge; S2 dodges keep its progress | PRTS 蒂比; `tippi_t` / `timer` / `evade`, `tippi_s_2` |
| 焰尾 | skill dodge rolled in an early listener | probability and held dodges in one ordered chain | PRTS 焰尾, 伤判效果 |
| Enemy arts barriers | final damage estimated and rescaled in `hitIn` | absorb what reaches their position; the breaking hit's bonus is removed after the instance | PRTS 伤判效果 (例2) |
| Count shields (莱茵生命防卫科高级成员, 假想敌：再生's aura) | a private counter cancelling in `hit` | a held phys / arts count shield in the ordinary order: a fully absorbed hit reports `damaged` 0, the unit shows the shield flag | PRTS 莱茵生命防卫科高级成员, 假想敌：再生; `enemy_lysdb[damage_block_once]` (DEFAULT, ON_TAKE_DAMAGE) |
| 浊心斯卡蒂 S1 share | the pre-share amount reverse-derived; she took ordinary true damage | a share reserved after the patient's barriers; she takes it as HP loss (no second injury pass) | PRTS 浊心斯卡蒂 (S1, priority −2000) |
| 坚守 share | a `hit`-stage redirect cut | the same parallel reservation after barriers; members take resolved true damage | §29.2 (order [ASSUMED]) |

[ASSUMED], as marked in the code: 限伤's position (one pre-injury check, research 12 S43); the Stead / barrier order
and mixed parallel sharing (PRTS settles 浊心斯卡蒂's priority, not its combination with 坚守); initial acquisition and
reapplication ties; some inherited cancellation masks; dispatching literal zero damage; the exact 30 Hz deadlines (the
收割者 queue, 蒂比's timer); 余's wall sampled at sim-tick positions; the item riders still on the late `damaged`
(`items/battle.js onDamageEnemies`, research 12 S59); a resolved request's amount as 天马之枪's input. The re-entrant
buff removal of §35.5 is kept.

**Red numbers.** The 1.5× test now compares the final damage — after barriers, fixed reductions and `hpDamage`, the
number the client shows — with the expected damage; v0.3.1 tested the amount before barriers. PRTS 作战机制
「伤害红字」 leaves 屏障吸收 and the other 后置增益 out of the expected damage and shows the final damage, which can
differ from the HP change; on a special-HP target the number is the computed damage. A yellow MISS uses the amount
left at the dodging effect's position.

|---|---|---|---|
| 锡人 (both kits) | the alchemy damage kept 锡人 as its source and could not be dodged; the talent boosted 'dot'-tagged damage early and re-applied itself | cached ATK, source-less precomputed BUFF arts credited to 锡人, ordinarily dodgeable; the talent (持续伤害 ×damage_scale) is an injury effect on BUFF damage, the strongest copy only; a landed unit keeps its coverage after 锡人 leaves | PRTS 锡人; `tinman_t_2`, `tinman_s_1[dot]`, `tinman_s_2[buff]` (NoSourceDamage, cached value, filters) |
| 逻各斯 | calculation effects inferred from attacks or broad notifications; HP loss could feed the damage rider; S1's inherited-HP follow-up before the talent | the source mark, the target's arts addition and the successful-arts rider each at their stage; special HP keeps the damage base; S1 checks the executed target's death, then the talent, then the follow-up | PRTS 逻各斯; `logos_t_2` / `core`, `logos_s_1` (CheckUnitAlive → IfNot → TriggerBuffsByKeys) |

| 棘刺 | the fixed poison re-ran the source calculation and could not be dodged; S2 guessed the type from tags; Delta waited for successful damage | the poison is precomputed and ordinarily dodgeable; S2 filters the native type and counters after 0.5 s; Delta derives the gauge from source output | PRTS 棘刺; `thorns` templates |
| 仇白 | the talent checked control and added damage at once; the first-hit mark survived a redeploy | control checked after the authored 0.1 s, then a precomputed ADDITION; marks are per deployment | PRTS 仇白; the talent's trigger / delay nodes |
| 弱点伤害 of the 混乱 trait (宴, 流星, 伺夜) | decided in `hit`, the comparison including the source's phys / arts dealt and the target's taken multipliers | the theoretical phys and arts damage from ATK, scales, the arts addition, penetration and DEF / RES only, at each source calculation of phys / arts damage | PRTS 术语释义 弱点伤害; 下半盟约记录 (备注: precomputed and source-less damage unaffected) |

### 35.34 碎铳之簧: defence, modifier transfer and erosion (PR #855, @Siornya)

The fifth part moves the Hidden Core's 碎铳之簧 (9018 / 9019 / 9020) onto the pipeline of §35.32 and removes the
transitional enemy helpers (`expectedFinal`, `absorbArts`). v0.3.1 behaviour, the new one and the source; templates as
in §35.32:

| Mechanism | v0.3.1 | 0.3.2 | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Defence | a private barrier pool / hit counter rescaling or cancelling in `hitIn` | the shared ordered barrier (9018, arts) and count shield (9020) of the injury chain; 9019's element shield cuts phys / arts to ×damage_scale there | `actrpa/b/c_t_1` |
| Blocking | unblockable only while shielded | always unblockable, also while the shield regenerates | PRTS 碎铳之簧: 能力修正 「无论护盾是否生效，自身始终无法被阻挡」 (原文 「护盾生效时…」, 原因=6: 描述与游戏实际表现不符合); the talents list 不可阻挡 unconditionally |
| 9018's counter | any physical damage type; a sourced counter, erosion even when dodged | NORMAL physical damage only, observed even after an earlier cancellation; a 无来源 phys ADDITION credited to the spring; erosion only when the counter lands | PRTS 碎铳之簧 (物理普通伤害); `actrpa_t_1` (filter, `_noSource`) |
| What passes on | the late `damaged` amount (HP losses included; heals and element never) | 9018: every applied modifier — damage, ordinary heals, element (not HP loss, regeneration or restoration) — at ON_APPLIED_MODIFIER; 9019 / 9020: the damage at their injury stage | PRTS 碎铳之簧 (「被施加调整值后（调整值包括伤害、治疗、元素损伤等）」 for the arts variant, 「受到伤害时」 for the others); `actrpa/b/c_passive` (ON_APPLIED_MODIFIER → AssignModifierValueIntoBlackboard → CreateBuffUseAbilitySelector `BossSelector`) |
| Which gun | the nearest | the highest-hatred living 铳 | PRTS 碎铳之簧 (「仇恨值最高的1名假想敌：铳」) |

[ASSUMED], marked in the code: the hatred order is the engine's targeting order (taunt, then the latest arrival) applied
to the guns, ties included — the BossSelector's logic is not in our data; 9020's count shield takes every HP type (the
native `filterDamageType: false`; PRTS's detailed row says phys / arts); the order of the initial passive and defence
buffs (the prefab's is not in our data); the equal split to the other springs (the 隐秘核心 handbook; PRTS names only the
gun). Erosion follows the counter's success even on a special-HP target whose HP does not drop (`onApplied`).

Review fixes: the [ASSUMED] mark on the hatred choice where the gun is picked, bosses.js's 碎铳之簧 header and
PART_TRANSFER comment (they still said "unblockable while shielded" and "every damage taken"), SIM §5's re-entrancy

shield drops — are regressions of the PR (`test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js`).
