### 35.36 Open operator lists follow the room's content (PR #852, @Xinglan233)

- **Seen:** PR #852. A guest kept 干员调配 / 干员持有 open while the host switched the room's gameplay plugins; the
  open list kept the names and slots of the previous content set until the language changed.
- **Cause:** the two roster memos (`loadout.js`, `ownership.js`) were keyed on readiness and the locale only.
- **Now:** both memos also key on `data.contentId()`, so an open list re-reads the chess table when the room's checked
  content set changes (vanilla → content, content → another content set, content → vanilla).
- **Review fixes:** the PR's browser test created the room through the 0.3.1 lobby (`.diff-card` 标准模拟, then
  `.create-box` 创建同盟). Since §35.18 (PR #812) 创建同盟 enters the room at once, so the test now uses
  `Client.createCoop()`, and the guest opens 同盟模拟 before typing the key. For versions M1: the room's version must
  change `contentId()` or join these memo deps, or the same staleness returns (noted at `data.js` contentId).
- **[ASSUMED]:** none; client only.
- **Tests:** `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/loadout-content.e2e.test.js` — 5/5 on the merged tree; with the two memo
  changes reverted the three content subtests fail (vanilla → content, content → content, content → vanilla). Two
  earlier runs during a load spike (load average ≈ 46) failed at the host's third switch, and four later runs passed;
  recorded as load-related. Golden unchanged.

### 35.37 Imported-art index updates serialised across tabs (PR #853, @Xinglan233)

- **Seen:** PR #853. Two same-origin tabs finishing an import together each read the same `/sp-imported-index.json`
  and overwrote the other's additions: the cached-file count came out short, and 清理旧版本素材 missed the unlisted
  entries. A prune could also overwrite an import's index or bring back deleted paths.
- **Now:** `addImportedPaths` and `pruneImportedPaths` hold one exclusive Web Lock (`sp-imported-assets-v1:index`)
  from reading the index to writing its replacement; the cache name and the JSON format are unchanged. Neither path
  calls the other, and the lock is released when the callback rejects.
- **Review fixes:** the PR put this history into the released `docs/history/0.3.1.md`; it moved here. The PR cited
  "supported browser minimums (Chrome/Edge 90, Firefox 108, Safari 16.4)", which the project does not document
  (DEPLOY.md: Firefox and Safari have not been specifically tested). ASSETS.md, design/client.md and the
  `assetCache.js` comment now say only that a browser without `navigator.locks` keeps the previous sequential
  behaviour, without cross-tab serialization.
- **Limits:** tabs still running older code do not take the lock; cache clearing, an import's asset writes and
  orphans from before the fix are not covered. Web Locks and CacheStorage are both secure-context only, so plain-http
  LAN pages (no import) are unchanged.
- **Tests:** `test/ui/asset-import.test.js` (24/24) and `SP_E2E=1 node --test
  test/ui/asset-index-concurrency.browser.test.js` (two pages on real CacheStorage, 5/5). Golden unchanged.

### 35.38 Skill-mode sounds from plain mode banks (PR #848, @Convey123)

- **Seen:** PR #848. 宴 (char_337_utage) S2's hits played her normal impact. The official bank
  `battle.ON_ABILITY_HIT.char_337_utage.attack.2` is `p_imp_darksword_n`, and the local fx index links
  `skchr_utage_2` to mode 2; `assignSkillModeSfx` dropped every bank without a uniform `_d` / `_h` / `_s` letter.
- **Now:** a numbered mode bank (mode > 0) whose files are all plain is that mode's sound when it plays a file the
  unit's normal attack never does, judged per kind (swing / impact). Without the local fx mode links nothing changes.
- **Review fixes** (the triage scanned the official banks with the PR as written: 36 slot changes on 25 operators,
  including 8 slots on 6 operators that replaced an existing assignment; the merge commit's "7" undercounted them):
  - A plain bank only fills a skill no lettered bank fills. As written, ON_ABILITY_START cast / ready banks took
    the slot from the lettered ON swing: 假日威龙陈 S1–S3 (`p_atk_watergun_d/h/s` → `…_cast`), 刻俄柏 S3,
    断崖 S2 (`p_atk_knifethrow_h` → `…_cast`), 赫德雷 S3 (`p_atk_hdvysword_s` → `…_sready`), 折桠 S2 and
    涤火杰西卡 S3. All keep their 0.3.1 sounds.
  - The normal files include every mode 0 bank (`attack.0`, `combat.0.1`, …), not only `''` / `.0`. 仇白's S2
    mode replays her normal `combat.0.1` stab, which the PR counted as a new S2 swing.
  - A plain bank speaks only for the mode the skill links, never as a phase fill. 乌尔比安's `S3_Respawn_A`
    swing / impact is not her S3 mode's sound; that mode plays the normal `_n` files.
  - `ownPlain` requires every file to be plain (`paths.every`), so a mixed bank stays skipped as ASSETS.md says. No
    such bank exists in the current data.
  - The widened normal set adds one slot the PR missed: 弑君者 S3's impact `p_imp_asndgrsmk` (her normal attack
    lives only in `attack.0.1` / `attack.0.2`).
- **Reach** after the review (26 slots on 19 operators, recounted at the merge review from the official banks — the merge
  commit and the first version of this section said 29 on 22; official bank, operator's mode link):
  寒芒克洛丝 S1 swing `p_atk_krossbow_n`; 涤火杰西卡 S2 swing `p_atk_carbine_n`; 雷蛇 S2 swing `p_atk_lightning_n`;
  拉普兰德 S2 swing / impact `katanawave_n`; 弑君者 S3 impact `p_imp_asndgrsmk`; 银灰 S2 and S3 impact
  `p_imp_sword_n`; 赫拉格 S3 swing `p_skill_huirenzhan`; 余 S1 swing / impact `frbytrtn_p`; 塑心 S2 swing
  `p_atk_shphrt_s_1–3`; 傀影 S2 swing / impact `machete_fire`; 送葬人 S2 swing `p_atk_shotgun_double`; 空弦 S3
  swing `p_atk_archet_s_cast`; 宴 S2 impact `p_imp_darksword_n`; 嵯峨 S3 impact `p_imp_ninjasword_h_1`; 伊内丝
  S2 / S3 swing and impact (`insasn_h_2` / `insasn_h_1`, `insasn_s_2` / `insasn_s_1`); 森蚺 S3 impact
  `p_atk_robotsaw_n`; 伺夜 S3 swing `p_atk_vwgun_h_1`; 死芒 S3 swing `p_atk_prlfrhlrdrls`; 领主·Sharp S1 impact
  `p_imp_prfsvlr_n`. Enemies have no fx mode links and get nothing.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a plain mode bank is the mode's own sound when its file differs from every normal file (no official
  statement; the official banks are only the event → file tables); lettered banks win over plain ones for the same
  skill.
- **Manifest:** the merge left `data/assets.json` as it was (this machine's asset folder lacked 23 of the 25 new files,
  and an offline rebuild would have dropped 48 entries whose files were missing too), so the shipped game still played
  宴's normal impact. At the merge review an online `node tools/fetch-assets.mjs` downloaded the 20 missing mode files
  and rewrote the manifest: exactly the 26 `attacks` / `hits` slots above on 19 operators change (with their pool
  entries), and `char_337_utage` gets `hits["1"]` = `p_imp_darksword_n` from `battle.ON_ABILITY_HIT.char_337_utage.attack.2`.
- **Normal attacks that played a skill's sound** (found by that regeneration: `test/feedback7-chen3-s3-sfx.test.js` saw
  弑君者's new S3 slot equal to her normal impact). `pickUnitSfx` ordered numbered banks by depth, so a shallower skill
  bank beat the default mode's `attack.0.N`: 弑君者's normal swing / impact were her S2 `attack.4` `p_atk_asndgrbm` and
  S3 `attack.3` `p_imp_asndgrsmk` (now `attack.0.1` `p_atk_asndgr_n` / `p_imp_asndgr_n`), 莱伊's normal swing his
  `attack.3` `p_atk_sndshotgun_sreload` (now `attack.0.3` `p_atk_sndshotgun_nreload`); 望's and 灰毫's swing keep their
  file but now come from their mode-0 bank. An operator's mode-0 banks (`attack.0`, `attack.0.N`; START before ON) now come
  before any other number, as `assignSkillModeSfx` already counts them as the normal attack; enemies keep the old order.
  The second rebuild (3 more files) changes only those four operators besides the 26 slots; its shrink guard listed the 20
  pool entries of the replaced banks, written with `--allow-shrink`. Nothing else in the manifest moves (hash, file count
  and bytes aside). `test/feedback6-leizi2-attack-sfx.test.js`'s "every attacks entry is a skill-mode file" accepts a mode's
  own plain file as the impact test already did, and `test/feedback5-operator-sfx.test.js` pins the mode-0 order.
- **Tests:** `test/skill-mode-map.test.js` adds the four review rules (each fails on the PR's `audio.mjs`) next to
  the PR's 宴 / 银灰 cases; `test/feedback7-chen3-s3-sfx.test.js` passes on the committed manifest. Golden unchanged
  (assets only).

### 35.39 蕾缪安 S3's crosshair held until its shell lands (PR #862, @evenifyouforget)

- **Seen:** PR #862. With 官方特效 on, each 蕾缪安 (Lemuen) S3 lock crosshair vanished about 1 s after the lock,
  long before the shell fell.
- **Cause:** the lock binding used `hold: 'fx'`, which read the kit's `hold: 1` flag as a 1 s duration.
- **Now:** `hold: 'lock'` (`SkillFx._lockOn` / `_lockShell` / `_syncLocks`) follows the procedural reticle's rules
  in render/fx/locks.js. The crosshair follows the locked enemy and stays at its last spot once the enemy is gone; it
  stays while her skill runs; a `bombardShell` claims her nearest free lock within 3, and that shell's `bombard` ends
  it; otherwise it ends 5 s after the last sign of her S3. When she is knocked out, locks with no shell yet end, as
  the kit drops unfired shells. Display only: the lock marks leave `this.marks` without a gap in the bombard binding's
  `during` (the first shell lands 0.3 s after the skill, within `COVER_AFTER_S` 1 s).
- **Review fixes:** a tier switch (or the effects turning off) now ends the held locks next to `tloops` / `aloops`;
  before, up to 48 stale entries waited for the next active frame. design/vfx-format.md describes `hold: 'lock'`
  instead of "1.5 s without a hold".
- **[ASSUMED]:** `LOCK_T` 5 s and `MAX_LOCKS` 48 carried over from locks.js, and `LOCK_SHELL_NEAR` 3 for matching
  shells to locks. Like locks.js, the crosshair follows a hidden (隐匿) enemy's view, while the kit's mark freezes.
- **Tests:** `test/render/vfx-procedural.test.js` (the PR's two cases and the tier-switch case, which fails without
  the reset), `test/render/vfx-talents.test.js`; 29/29. Golden unchanged.

### 35.40 zipFolder reports the failing tool (#840, PR #841, @zasx77771)

- **Seen:** #840. On the Windows runner image 20261018.275.1 `the zip reader` failed with "zipping failed: install
  `zip` (or a bsdtar `tar`)" although the image has a bsdtar; the message dropped the tool's own words, and the
  tests decided "a zip tool exists" from `zip -v` / `tar --version`, not from what the tool can write.
- **Now (PR #841):** `zipFolder` tries Info-ZIP `zip`, then bsdtar `tar --format zip --options
  zip:hdrcharset=UTF-8`, removes a half-written zip before each attempt, needs exit 0 and an existing file, pipes
  stdio, and names each failed attempt with its errno or exit status and the first stderr line. The tests probe what
  the machine's zipper really writes.
- **Review fixes:** the probe has three outcomes: the name is in the bytes; `zipFolder` threw (its error kept); or a
  tool succeeded but the name is missing — the silent `??` loss #311 fixed. Only "threw" falls back to the ASCII
  sample `a.png`, and then the new test "zipFolder writes a UTF-8 name on this machine" is skipped with zipFolder's
  error as the reason, so a CI log shows tar's own words; "missing" fails that test. A signal kill reports
  `signal SIGxxx` instead of `exit null`; when every attempt is ENOENT the old install hint is appended. The two
  comments are corrected (the packing side does not "refuse loudly" when a tool exits 0, and #840 was the runner
  image, not some runners).
- **[ASSUMED]:** none; tooling only.
- **Tests:** `test/update-package.test.js` on macOS (Info-ZIP and bsdtar): 13 pass, 1 skipped (the failing-zipper
  case, which needs a machine without a working zipper); the new ENOENT case runs everywhere. A tar-only PATH writes
  the UTF-8 name. The next Windows run on the new image decides #840's cause; its log now carries zipFolder's error.
  Golden unchanged.

### 35.41 Any seated player can start or cancel 重刷本局 (#854, #858, PR #860, @zasx77771)

- **Seen:** #854 / #858. Since PR #408 (@Siornya, §28.21) only the host could start 重刷本局 in 确认本局信息, so a
  guest who wanted another opening had to ask the host.
- **Decision:** the maintainer accepted #860 on 2026-10-23 (#858 option 1).
- **Now (PR #860):** the lobby no longer refuses non-hosts for `room.rerollSetup` / `room.cancelReroll`; spectators
  and absent seats are still refused. The global 300 ms guard is replaced by a 30 s start cooldown per seat
  (`_rerollStartAt`), cleared after a completed reroll and when the strategy draft (BAND_DRAFT) begins, deleted for a leaving seat, and kept
  after a cancel or a rejection (with one human every start completes at once and clears it, so there the throttle is
  the connection's heavy bucket, added at the fourth review: §35.47). The vote stays unanimous among the humans; bots
  and spectators do not vote; there is
  no vote timeout. The client shows 重刷本局 and 取消投票 to every seated player.
- **Review fixes:** PLAYING.md still said only the host may start the vote. The `setupVote.js` header had dropped
  the existing [ASSUMED] list (unanimous human vote, paused / resumed timer, independent reroll stream); it now keeps
  it next to the new rule. A start refused by the cooldown showed the generic 「操作过于频繁」; the refusal's detail
  `setup reroll cooldown` now shows 「每人 30 秒内只能发起一次重刷投票」 (five language packs), and other RATE
  refusals keep the generic text. design/match.md §28.21 names the detail.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the 30 s per-seat cooldown and its reset rules (remake convenience; the official mode has no
  reroll). The room version vote planned for versions M1 stays host-started and host-cancelled.
- **Tests:** `test/match/setup-reroll.test.js`, `test/match/setup-reroll-ws.test.js` (now also the cooldown's wire
  detail and toast text), `test/lobby.test.js` — 85/85; `SP_E2E=1 node --test test/ui/followup-023.e2e.test.js`
  6/6 in two of three runs, the first failing once while the machine's load average was about 160. `node
  tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict`: 0 missing. Golden unchanged.

### 35.42 A t() msgid must be a literal (#849, PR #850, @zasx77771)

- **Seen:** #849. `tools/i18n.mjs extract` only sees a string written at the call. In `t(flag ? '甲' : '乙')`
  neither string is extracted or listed as unwrapped, so the key never reaches a pack and every language falls back
  to Chinese without any check noticing.
- **Now (PR #850):** I18N.md §1 states the rule, names the silent cases and shows the allowed ternary around the
  call.
- **Our follow-up:** the text's `t(name)` is only nearly silent (a Chinese literal not marked with `N_` is listed as
  unwrapped); I18N.md says so and adds the `TABLE[k] ? t(TABLE[k]) : t('其他')` form. The two computed call sites
  the triage found are rewritten: `loadout.js`'s 导出 / 导入自选编队 title (a ternary inside `t()`) and
  `pluginPanel.js`'s class badge (`t(CLASS_NAMES[p.class] || '插件')`). Their msgids were already in every pack,
  so nothing changes on screen; `check --all --strict` now counts the two titles (2243 → 2245 msgids, all
  translated). An extractor warning for a non-literal argument stays a separate change.
- **Tests:** `node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict` (0 missing), `test/docs-paths.test.js`,
  `test/docs-consistency.test.js`. Golden unchanged.

### 35.43 界面大小 in 异形屏适配 (#837, PR #838 reworked, @zasx77771)

- **Seen:** #837 / PR #838. On a phone in landscape below 432 CSS px of height the 40 px root floor is larger than the
  design scale (33.9 px at 756×366), so the HUD bands and the shop bar take room from the board. PR #838 added an
  automatic factor (`fit / 40` from `visualViewport.height`, down to 0.66), a 75–150 % slider and a shop-bar zoom.
- **Decision:** the maintainer asked on 2026-10-23 whether the existing 异形屏适配 could do this. It cannot: it moves
  the edges and the 40 px root floor stays. So a manual 「界面大小」 row goes inside that section: default 100 %, no
  automatic shrink, the text and touch floors kept, every 0.3.2 screen covered, including §35.18's lobby, room and
  briefing.
- **Now:** 设置 → 异形屏适配 ends with 界面大小, 75–100 % in 5 % steps with −/+ buttons, stored with the edge offsets
  (`sp.pref.screenFit` `ui`, this device only; a value stored before reads as 100 %). 重置 returns it to 100 % with the
  edges. Under 100 % `<html>` gets `--sp-ui`; theme.css and devices.css multiply both layout roots (the plain one and
  the 异形屏适配 one) by it, so every rem, the HUD bands the prep cameras keep clear and the board's room follow. All 498
  `max(<rem>, <N>px)` floors outside the readability properties carry it, converted by one rule. A size floor (width,
  height, min-*, flex, grid tracks, custom properties; 360) becomes `max(<rem>, min(N, 32)px, calc(Npx * var(--sp-ui,
  1)))`: a touch target ≥ 32 px stays ≥ 32 px, and a smaller floor never shrinks. Any other floor (138) becomes
  `calc(Npx * var(--sp-ui, 1))`. The briefing tooltip's `min(240px, …)` cap scales too. At 100 % no property is set,
  and every rule computes exactly as before. (The merge review simplified and corrected the rule; see the review below.)
  The `<html>` root is `clamp(40px, …, 240px) × --sp-ui`: the 40 px clamp stays in the formula, but the root itself goes
  below it (30 px at 75 %), contrary to the implementation commit's "the 40 px root floor stays".
- **Readability:** the 269 font-size floors (and line-height) stay px, and 中 / 大 / 特大 keep their 64 / 76 / 88 px
  `--t` floors. 小's `--t` is 1rem with no floor, so it follows the layout. A first version floored 小 at the unscaled
  40 px root; text then stayed while its boxes shrank, and the text-fit checker found clipped labels. A player who
  wants smaller boxes and larger text sets 文字大小 中 or above, which keeps its floors. (Superseded at the merge review:
  界面大小 applies at 小 only, see below.)
- **Measured** (claude/032-int's `test/e2e/text-fit.mjs`, run from a scratch driver kept with its results in the
  untracked `handoff/round51/uisize/`: the lobby hub, a co-op room with an AI teammate, a solo room and a solo briefing;
  640×360, 667×375, 740×360 and 844×390 as touch phones; five languages × four 文字大小; 80 cases per screen). The run was
  on claude/032-prs before claude/032-int's later text-fit fixes were merged into it, so 100 % already had problems (hub
  380, room 160, solo 251), all at 中 and above except 16 on the hub at 小. At 小, 85 % and 75 % add
  none on the hub and in both rooms. At 中 / 大 / 特大 the kept text floors outgrow their shrinking boxes: 110 / 158 /
  206 new problems at 85 % and 180 / 186 / 360 at 75 % (each count over the three screens' 60 cases). The briefing's
  enemies are drawn per match, so its counts vary run to run (318–329 at 100 %). In the game at 756×366 the root goes
  40 → 34 → 30 px. The ready button keeps 32 px (34 at 100 %), the shop bar is 152 → 134 → 123 px tall, and the HUD
  text checker finds no problem at any of the three sizes. Not measured then: the lobby's two dialogs 选择同盟方式 and
  选择模拟协议. At 小 they did have new problems in English and Korean (cut difficulty names at 75 %, Korean words broken
  at 85 / 75 %); §35.47 fixes them and gives their numbers.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the range 75–100 % and the 5 % step — pending the maintainer, who asked for 75–150 % through #838's
  review lens: above 100 % is left out because a scratch run at 125 / 150 % on the same phones overlapped the screens (the
  hub's 成就统计 under the mode cards at 150 %, the co-op room 0 → 8 → 19 problems at 小), and larger text is what 文字大小
  is for; offering it on desktops only (capped by the viewport height) is the alternative to ask about. 小 follows
  界面大小 — pending the maintainer: the brief said the text floors stay, but 小 has none, so its body text goes from
  7.2 to ≈ 5.4 CSS px at 75 % on a phone (raising UI_MIN or flooring 小 at the unscaled root are the alternatives; the
  first version's 40 px floor clipped labels in the shrinking boxes). The 32 px touch minimum (as the triage asked).
  The label 「界面大小」, micro `UI SIZE`, and the hints.
- **Not taken from #838:** the automatic factor and its `visualViewport` reading (the soft keyboard would shrink the UI),
  the shop-bar zoom (`--sp-shop`), the slider above 100 %, and its `HUD_REM.shopBarBorderPx` 3 → 5 change (a separate
  geometry fix, not needed for this). Its idea of one factor carried by the root and every px floor is kept.
- **Before release:** claude/032-int's later lobby / room work (§35.18) adds about 14 `max(rem, px)` floors in lobby.css /
  room.css without the factor. When that branch merges, `test/ui/ui-size.test.js` names them: they take the same forms
  (bare px up to 32, `max(rem, 32px, calc(Npx * var(--sp-ui, 1)))` above, `calc(…)` for the others, mirrors copied), and
  the text-fit sweep is re-run with the in-tree tool on the lobby, rooms and briefing; this note is then replaced with
  the release's numbers.
- **Tests:** `test/ui/ui-size.test.js`: the stored size, `applyFit`, both roots, the text floors, and the guard (every
  floor carries the factor or is an exempt readability property; a scaled size floor keeps `min(N, 32)` px; no
  font-size floor scales). `test/helpers/uiSize.js` `unscaled` lets the CSS-pinning tests (combat-metrics-mobile,
  loadout-rows, loadout-stats, lobby-hub, playtest3, playtest5-ui) keep checking design px. `test/ui/screen-fit.test.js`;
  `SP_E2E=1` `test/ui/screen-fit.e2e.test.js` (a stored 75 % at 844×390: root 30 px, 小's text 30 px, 中's 64 px floor,
  ready button ≥ 32 px, the board filling its host; − steps 5 % live, 重置 back to no property; 4/4) and
  `test/ui/text-scale.e2e.test.js` 2/2; the UI unit suite 1131/1131. Golden unchanged (client only).
- **Merge review (2026-10-23):**
  - Only at 文字大小 小. The implementer's own measurement found 110 / 158 / 206 new problems at 85 % and 180 / 186 / 360
    at 75 % at 中 / 大 / 特大 (the kept 64 / 76 / 88 px floors in shrinking boxes; at 844×390, 中, 75 % 待命中 filled the P1
    card edge to edge and the zh 界面大小 label wrapped to 46 px). `effectiveUi` keeps 100 % at 中 and above, the row is
    disabled with 「文字大小为「中」及以上时不可缩小界面」, and the stored size applies again at 小 (`settingsStore` change).
  - The slider applies on release (`change`); `input` moves only the reading. Live, the modal resized the track under
    the pointer (a drag at 1280×720 fired 95 100 95 90 85). The −/+ buttons still apply at once.
  - Mirrors copy their size floor: the narrow-touch `.dpanel` bottom used `max(.56rem, calc(34px × ui))` for the ⚙ row
    whose height is `max(.56rem, 32px, calc(34px × ui))`, so at 90 % and below the card ended up to 6.5 px over the ⚙ row
    (756×366, 75 %); the 交流 term and sweep's header band (`.sw-sec` background vs `.sw-sec__head`) had the same drift,
    as did the lobby `.pcard` padding beside its 1.3rem / 70 px stamp. A probe at 756×366 now shows the card 3–4 px above
    the ⚙ row at every size and the band equal to the header. A new guard test flags a non-size value that repeats a
    scaled size floor of its file without the 32 px.
  - A size floor of 32 px or less is bare px again (`max(.5rem, 30px)`): its third term `calc(30px × ui)` could never
    win with the factor ≤ 1 (249 floors in nine stylesheets); the guard requires the form.
  - The host test dock reads the real root (`getComputedStyle(<html>).fontSize`, as fieldHost.js / settle.js) instead of
    the unscaled clamp: at 1280×720 and 75 % it placed itself for a 66.7 px root (x ≈ 192 instead of 144).
  - Tests: `test/ui/ui-size.test.js` (the gate, the three guards), `SP_E2E=1 test/ui/screen-fit.e2e.test.js` 5/5 (中 turns
    a stored 75 % off and 小 back on; the disabled row; drag then release), `test/test-mode.test.js` (the dock root).

### 35.44 Merge review of the PR batch (2026-10-23)

A review of claude/032-prs (#796–#856, #848, #852, #853, #859, #860, #862, #841, #850, #838) after the merges above. What
it changed, and where each change is recorded:

- **Element-burst damage on operators can be dodged** (§35.32's table, design/engine.md §5.5, CHANGELOG): #802 made the
  burst's HP damage ordinary 无来源 damage (the native `ep_break_*` NoSourceDamage has no cancellation mask; read again in
  ArknightsGameResource 9bf23966: fire / water / anger char templates and the dark char's `periodic_magic_damage`), but
  nothing recorded it against v0.3.1's `canDodge: false`. A dodge buff now skips 灼燃's 1200, 侵蚀's 800 and 凋亡's ticks,
  as do injury dodges with no enemy source (老鲤 S3, 赤刃明霄陈, 蒂比); the status and cooldown stay. The legacy necrosis
  tick, which kept `dodgeMode: 'ignore'`, follows the same rule now (`test/sim/attack-fidelity.test.js`). No moved golden
  scenario of §35.32 has it as its first divergence, and the necrosis change moves none.
- **§35.30's golden** lists the 17 scenarios #798 moved with the hit that first differs (a probe on `mitigate` at the
  merge commit).
- **Fixed reductions of different names stack** (attack/effects.js): the code took one strongest `damageReduction`
  across all buffs while its comment (and PRTS 伤害减免) says same-named ones take the strongest. Reductions group by
  `reductionGroup`, else the buff key; 浊心斯卡蒂's aura, whose key carries her unit id, names its group so two of them
  still keep only the stronger. No behaviour change in today's content (she is the only user; golden unchanged).
- **#848 reaches the game** (§35.38): 26 slots on 19 operators (not 29 on 22; the PR replaced 8 slots on 6 operators, not
  7), the manifest regenerated online, and the normal-attack pick fixed for 弑君者 / 莱伊 (and 望 / 灰毫's pools).
- **界面大小** (§35.43): at 文字大小 小 only, applied on release, mirrored floors, bare small floors, the test dock's root;
  the range and 小's shrinking text are [ASSUMED] pending the maintainer.
- **重刷本局's cooldown text** names the server's value: `SETUP_REROLL_COOLDOWN_MS` (shared/constants.js, 30 s) feeds
  `t('每人 {s} 秒内只能发起一次重刷投票', { s })` (five packs) instead of a 30 written into each language.
- **PR #859's right-click case** (§35.31, whose heading now credits @FelixSion28): a separate browser case per board
  mode selects A, right-clicks B, hovers B and presses Q — the retreat request names B and A stays on the board.
- **The open-roster browser test** (§35.36) waits up to 90 s per step: it once failed at ~50 s under a parallel sweep
  (load ~40) and passes in ~7 s alone.

Tests: the touched unit files (`attack-effects`, `attack-fidelity`, `test-mode`, `ui-size`, `screen-fit`,
`text-scale`, the sfx / asset files, `setup-reroll-ws`), `SP_E2E=1` `screen-fit` 5/5, `hover-hotkeys` 6/6,
`loadout-content` 5/5 (one file at a time), `node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict` (2248 msgids, none missing), the
full golden comparison (288 match; nothing moved). The versions G1 fixture is refreshed for `data/assets.json` alone (the
regenerated manifest; every other default data file byte-identical), and `test/audio-pools-data.test.js` counts 978
references / 967 pools (952 / 941 + #848's slots and the four mode-0 picks). One complete CI run (`node tools/ci.mjs
--keep-going`, 9 567 tests): setup, smoke, lint, imports and typecheck pass; the test step's only failures were those two
expected counts, fixed and rerun after it.

### 35.45 Second review of the PR batch (2026-10-23)

A second review of claude/032-prs after §35.44 found two documentation gaps and one 界面大小 bug:

- **The injury fields are documented for content and plugin authors** (SIM §3, design/engine.md §5.5): engine.md still
  said fixed reductions take "the strongest per HP type", while §35.44 groups them by name. It now says the strongest
  per name and HP type, differently named ones stacking (name = `reductionGroup`, else the buff key). SIM §3's
  `addBuff` signature lists the fields the series added — `damageReduction`, `reductionGroup`, `injury`,
  `observeInjury`, `injuryPriority`, `keepEmptyShield` — with one line each, and warns that a per-unit key
  (`key:${unit.id}`) stacks per source unless the buffs share a `reductionGroup` (浊心斯卡蒂's aura). The additive
  mod-key list gains `artsAttackAddition` (法术攻击加算, in ADD_KEYS since #802). `test/sim/attack-effects.test.js`
  spells out the per-unit-key pattern 浊心斯卡蒂's aura uses: two keys sharing a group that names no key keep only the
  stronger. The existing cases already failed with `reductionGroup` dropped (the 'same' group of keys `one` / `two`;
  `'c'` joining `'a'`), so the new case adds the aura's shape, not a missing guard (corrected in §35.46).
- **attack/element.js's header** no longer says 元素伤害 has "no dodge": since #802 `makeDamageInfo` makes every HP type
  dodgeable by default, so an injury dodge that names every type (圣约送葬人 S2, PRTS) cancels an enemy's elemental hit;
  only the probability dodges `dodgePhys` / `dodgeArts` stay phys / arts. Comment only.
- **界面大小's reading could stick** (screenFit.js): a drag only set a preview, cleared by `change`. Chrome fires no
  `change` when a drag ends on its start value, and a touch drag can turn into a scroll; the row then kept the old
  number while −, + and 重置 changed the real size (reproduced with a real mouse drag 85 → 75 → 85 in headless Chrome:
  − set `--sp-ui` 0.8, the row read 85 %). The preview now records the stored size it was taken from and is ignored
  once that changes; `pointerup` applies the value (as `change` does), `pointercancel` and blur drop it, and −, + and
  重置 clear it. New browser case in `test/ui/screen-fit.e2e.test.js` (`page.mouse` drag away and back, −, a drag to
  another value, 重置; it fails on the previous screenFit.js).
- **testDock.js `remOf`** loses the `ui` parameter §35.43 added: no production caller passed it (the dock reads the
  measured root, `size.rem`), so its assertion covered a branch the dock never took; `test/test-mode.test.js` keeps the
  measured-root checks.

Tests: `attack-effects` 26/26, `test-mode` 42/42, `ui-size` + `screen-fit` 17/17, `SP_E2E=1` `screen-fit` 6/6; the
full golden comparison (288 match; nothing moved: comments, docs, client and tests only). One complete CI run
(`node tools/ci.mjs --keep-going`, 9 567 tests): setup, smoke, lint, imports and typecheck pass; the test step's only
failure was the load-bound boss-field speed test (1.24 ms/tick under the parallel run), which passes alone.

### 35.46 Third review of the PR batch (2026-10-23)

A third review of claude/032-prs after §35.45 found one false statement, one browser case that pinned less than it
claimed, and several nits:

- **§35.45's reason for the new `reductionGroup` case was wrong** (as was the e44ae2c1 commit message): it said the
  earlier case still passed with `reductionGroup` dropped. With `const name = buff.key` in attack/effects.js, the tests
  from before that commit already fail twice — 'same-named flat reduction …' (keys `one` / `two` in group `'same'`
  stack: phys 0 instead of 25) and 'differently named flat reductions stack …' (`'c'` stacks with `'a'` and `'b'`:
  10 instead of 40). §35.45 now says the new case only spells out the aura's per-unit-key shape.
- **界面大小's browser coverage**: the §35.45 case passed with the `from` check, `onPointerUp`, or both removed — it
  needed only −'s preview reset. A new case in `test/ui/screen-fit.e2e.test.js` drives each way out of a drag on its
  own: `input` then a bare `pointerup` (no `change`) applies 90 %; `input` then `pointercancel` brings the row back to
  the stored size; a reading left with no release yields to a size stored through `updateFit` (the `from` check); a
  `pointerup` on the stored value saves nothing. Each of the four mechanisms removed in a scratch copy fails it.
- **setUi applies only a new size**: every `pointerup` used to save the preference and send a window `resize` (a board /
  HUD re-measure), so a plain click on the thumb re-measured for nothing and a drag applied twice (`pointerup`, then
  `change`). It now clears the reading always and calls `updateFit` only when `sanitizeUi(v)` differs from the stored
  size. screenFit.js's header says the size applies on `pointerup` or `change` and names the reading-drop rule, as
  design/client.md does.
- **SIM §3's `injury(event)` line** says what `dodge(p)` / `block(p)` return: true when the attempt succeeds, which is
  not always a cancellation — `dodge()` is skipped (false) on an undodgeable instance (`dodgeMode: 'ignore'`, i.e.
  `canDodge: false`), and under a cancellation mask (`dodgeMode: 'uncancellable'`, `ignoreCancelReasons`) either returns
  true without cancelling; `observeInjury`'s `cancelled` / `outcome` or the dodge / block hook's `ctx.cancelled` tell.
  圣约送葬人 S2 (ammo) and 赤刃明霄陈 / 蒂比 (held buff) act on the return value.
- **Comments and formatting**: enemies.js's special-types note and enemies/helpers.js's `hitCount` say 茶器 counts only
  arts and true instances (phys and 元素伤害 remove 0, §35.32's 青瓷茶器 row; health.js), not "phys removes 0";
  浊心斯卡蒂's 新生代 reduction block is indented as the rest of `install()` (two spaces, AGENTS.md). The CHANGELOG 插件
  line states the end state of `damageReduction` instead of 「改为」 (the field does not exist in v0.3.1).

Tests: `SP_E2E=1` `screen-fit` 7/7, `ui-size` + `screen-fit`, `attack-effects`, `attack-hit-count`; the full golden
comparison (nothing moved: comments, docs, client and tests only). One complete CI run (`node tools/ci.mjs
--keep-going`, 9 567 tests): setup, smoke, lint, imports and typecheck pass; the test step's only failure was again the
load-bound boss-field speed test (best 0.58 ms/tick under the parallel run), and `test/sim/robustness.test.js` passes
alone (35/35).

### 35.47 Fourth review of the PR batch (2026-10-23)

A fourth review of claude/032-prs after §35.46 found one must-fix in 界面大小, two should-fixes (a missing credit and a
lobby dialog cut at 75 %) and four nits:

- **A click on the disabled 界面大小 row overwrote the stored size** (must-fix). At 文字大小 中 and above the range is
  disabled and shows 100, but Chrome still sends it `pointerdown` / `pointerup`. The `pointerup` handler from §35.45
  called `setUi(100)`, and §35.46's "only a new size" check compared 100 with the stored 85 and saved it, so the 85 %
  was gone at 小 — against the rule (screenFit.js header, §35.44) that the stored size is kept and applies again at 小.
  `setUi` now does nothing while the row is not allowed. The browser case "界面大小 at 文字大小 中 …" clicks the disabled
  range with `page.mouse.click` and checks that the stored 85 survives and applies again at 小; it fails without the
  check.
- **PR #796 was not credited** (should-fix). The index and §35.32 said §35.30 covers it, but §35.30 described only #798,
  and the CHANGELOG had no #796 line. §35.30 now has a #796 paragraph (the public entry `server/sim/attack.js`, the 64
  files switched to it, no behaviour change: a clean merge, 0 golden moves; `server/sim/AGENTS.md` and the AGENTS.md
  lines), its title names both PRs, and a CHANGELOG 插件 line credits @Siornya for #796 and for #856's settlement
  contract (§35.35).
- **选择模拟协议 at 75 % on short phones** (should-fix). In the ≤ 520 px block the right column was `min(7.4rem, 50vw)`
  (选择同盟方式: `54vw`), which shrinks with --sp-ui (296 → 222 px at 75 %), while the card names' 16 px floor (a
  clamp(), which 界面大小 leaves alone), the brief's 11 px floor and the px paddings and stamp space stay. In English the
  unselected 'Perilous Simulation' and 'Ultimate Simulation' ended in '…' at 75 % on 640×360, 667×375, 740×360 and
  844×390 (touch, 小); in Korean the brief broke 시뮬레이|션 at 75 % and 选择同盟方式 broke 가|능 at 85 %. Both columns
  and the protocol brief's `max-width` now divide their rem by --sp-ui (`min(calc(7.4rem / var(--sp-ui, 1)), 50vw)`,
  `min(calc(6.4rem / var(--sp-ui, 1)), 100%)`), so they keep their unscaled widths. The same scratch sweep (round 51's
  text-fit checker on the real lobby: hub → 同盟模拟 → 创建同盟 → the difficulty pill; the four phones × zh / zh-TW / ja /
  en / ko × 100 / 85 / 75 %, 小) now finds 0 problems in both dialogs and in the room in every case. The hub's two
  English and Korean entry-name overflows ('Solo Simulation', 'Team Simulation' past `.mode-card` at 844×390) are the
  same at 100 % and are not from 界面大小. New browser case in `test/ui/screen-fit.e2e.test.js` (640×360, en and ko,
  75 %: both columns 296 px, the brief `min(256px, 100%)`, no unselected name cut; fails with the old column), and
  lobby-hub.test.js pins the three rules. §35.43 records that its measurement left these dialogs out.
- **A held dodge with several stacks** (nit, plugins only): it rolled once per stack until a roll cancelled, so under a
  dodge cancellation mask (`dodgeMode: 'uncancellable'` or `ignoreCancelReasons: ['dodge']`), where a success cancels
  nothing, each further success repeated `onDodge` — another MISS and another 'dodge' hook for one hit that lands
  (阿斯卡纶 S3 heals on each, 焰尾 / 杰西卡 S3 / 艾拉 act on it). The loop now ends at the first success. No content sends
  phys or arts damage with such a mask (逻各斯's execute is true and source-less). New attack-cancellation cases for both
  masks (3 stacks, probability 1: one roll, one hook, one dodge fx); they fail on the old loop.
- **重刷本局 with one human had no throttle** (nit): PR #860's per-seat cooldown is cleared by a completed reroll, and with
  one human (solo, co-op with bots) every start completes at once, so 200 starts in a row all rebuilt the opening
  (0.1–0.4 ms each) and flushed `m.public`. Restoring 0.3.1's match-wide 300 ms gap broke PR #860's own real-socket cases
  (seats starting votes milliseconds apart), so `room.rerollSetup` joins server/net.js `HEAVY_TYPES` instead (2 per
  second after a burst of 6, plain RATE). design/match.md §28.21 and design/network.md say so; a new real-socket case
  (solo: 6 rerolls pass, the 7th is RATE and rebuilds nothing) fails without the entry.
- **Tests that pinned less than claimed** (nits): the "each way out of a drag" browser case now also drags and blurs the
  range (the row returns to the stored size; it fails with `onBlur` removed), so the blur drop that the screenFit.js
  header and §35.46 name is covered; attack-effects' aura shape (keys `aura:1` / `aura:2` in group `'aura'`) is its own test, so it
  reports on its own when the assertions before it change.

Tests: `SP_E2E=1` `screen-fit` 9/9 (each new assertion fails on a scratch mutation of the code it covers),
`attack-cancellation`, `attack-effects`, `setup-reroll` + `setup-reroll-ws`, `ui-size`, `screen-fit`, `lobby-hub`; the
full golden comparison: 288 match (the dodge loop changes only masked hits, which no scenario has), so no update and
no versions G1 refresh.
### 35.48 Enemy skill clips by the official ability (§35.19 leftover)

- **Seen:** §35.19 left six enemies whose skill slots play a clip other than their official ability's, the same cause as
  昆图斯's (the resolver pairs numbered clips with slots by name, `Skill_n` = slot n − 1, or finds no slot clip at all).
- **Official:** the EnemySkill abilities' `_animKey` of each data slot (`public/assets/local/fx/index.json`
  `units.<key>.skills.<prefabKey>.abilities`, read from the local client's `battle/enm_pfb_*.ab`), against the clips
  the client played (`data/assets.json` `anims.skills[slot]`, else the primary `anims.skill`) and the casts the sim
  makes (`castSlot`):

  | Enemy | Slot (prefabKey) | Official clip | Played before | The sim's cast |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 泥岩 | 0 RefreshShield / 1 occupy | `Skill_2` 1.333 s / `Skill_1` | `Skill_1` 1.5 s / `Skill_2` | RefreshShield, a skill window (castNow) |
  | “自在” | 0 CrossAttack | `Skill_01_02` | `Skill_01` | a skill window |
  | “自在” | 1 ShieldBurst / 2 ShieldBurstReborn | none (`MeleeAttack`, empty `_animKey`) | the `Skill_02` set / the cross's `Skill_01` (slot 2 had no clip) | both, skill windows |
  | “斩胄之剑” / “破胄之锤” | 0 "1" (掷剑 / 掷锤) / 1 "2" | `Launch_Begin` 1.367 s / `Back_End` | `Skill` 1.733 s for both | "1", a skill window |
  | 锏 | 0 CircleAttack / 2 CircleAttack2 | `A_Skill2` / `B_Skill2` (2.067 s) | the attack role (no clip; the second form's clip set also replaced the skill role) | both, skill windows |
  | 锏 | 1 Blink / 3 Blink2 | none (`ActiveBuffAbility`, no animated ability) | the attack role | both, skill windows |
  | 杰斯顿·威廉姆斯 | 0 ironsandstorm / 1 armorpiercing | `C1_Skill` 2.067 s / `C2_Skill` 1.133 s | the attack clip | inside the (sp+1)th attack (`before` hook), no skill window |
  | “碎铳之簧” (元素 / 频次护盾) | 0 "1" | `Skill_b` 3.0 s / `Skill_c` 6.967 s | the attack clip | inside the shielded (spCost+1)-th attack, no skill window |

  A sweep of every enemy slot with an official clip in its model found six more that differ — 深池逐火 `BornAnim`
  (`Start`), 圆仔 `Show` (`Special`), 架桥船工 / 扶桥老手 `BuildBridge` (`Skill_End`), 转译基底·α `ChangeToB/C/D`
  (`A_Die_B/C/D`), 水遁忍者 `EndAnim` (`Skill_End`), 异质裂兽·α `M1Start` (`Trans`) — none of which the sim casts (their
  moments are form change clips, render/units.js FORMS, or not drawn); left as they are. 卷心籽 `onfire` and 拥霜羽兽
  `switch` name a `Skill` clip their models lack.
- **Now:** `tools/assets/spine.mjs PREFAB_SPINE_ROLES` gives these models their slots (and slot 0 as the primary `skill`
  where the skill window plays it); `data/assets.json` regenerated with `node tools/fetch-assets.mjs --offline
  --add-only` (its disk-dependent `stats.bytes` kept at the committed value): only these eight models' `anims.skill` /
  `anims.skills` change. The attack-class slots carry `strike`: the sim sends ['cast', id, slot] from the attack's
  `before` hook and that attack's 'atk' in the same tick, so the client plays the slot's clip as that attack's clip
  (`UnitView.onCast`, `STRIKE_WINDOW` 0.25 game s for a batch split by a late frame; the wind-up look-ahead passes a cast
  of the attack's tick to `windUp`; `SpineActor.castStrike`); every target's 'atk' of that tick keeps it, the next
  plain attack plays the attack clip again. 锏's second-form clip set (`FORMS` `form2`) no longer carries a `skill` role,
  so `B_Skill2` plays there. Normative: design/client.md §9 (enemy skill clips), ASSETS.md (`skills`, `strike`).
- **[ASSUMED]:** 自在's 破桎而出 (both forms) plays the skeleton's one `Skill_02_Begin / _Loop / _End` set — its ability
  names no clip (by the clip names). 锏's 速杀 keeps the attack role (no clip of its own) although the skeleton has
  `A_Skill1_Begin / _End` and `B_Skill1_Begin / _End`: no prefab field ties them to the blink. A strike attack plays
  its clip by the enemy rule (its own speed, faster only when the attacks come quicker), like every enemy attack;
  “碎铳之簧”'s 6.967 s `Skill_c` is therefore compressed to its attack interval.
- **Not changed:** other attack-class casts whose official clip is a distinct skill clip (无胄盟清扫小队 / 澪
  `PowerAttack`, 纠缠藤蔓 `StunAttack`: `Skill`) have no `skills` map and still swing their attack clip; marking them
  `strike` would extend the same table. No sim change: no golden scenario moves.
- **Tests:** `test/enemy-skill-clips.test.js` (every `skills` fix in data/assets.json, the fixed slots per enemy, the
  four strike slots and the sim hooks they rely on, each fix = the official animKey when the effects index is extracted,
  no other enemy slot the sim casts off its official clip, the never-cast list absent from server/sim); new
  `test/render/enemy-strike-clip.test.js` (杰斯顿's strike on both forms, the wound-up strike not restarted, a stale cast
  and a non-strike slot plain, 碎铳之簧, 泥岩 / 锏 second form / 斩胄之剑 in their skill windows); adjusted
  `test/render/feedback1d-bombd.test.js` (the `windUp` call's third argument); `test/fixtures/versions-default-guard.json`
  regenerated for `data/assets.json` alone (the eight models' clips; G1).

### 35.49 Literal msgids at the two computed t() sites (#849)

- **Seen (#849, @zasx77771):** `tools/i18n.mjs extract` / `check` read a msgid only from a string literal inside the
  call. In `t(flag ? '甲' : '乙')` neither string is extracted nor listed as unwrapped, so a missing translation would
  pass the strict check silently; `t(name)` with a module constant is at least listed as unwrapped. 0.3.2 had two such
  sites: the 自选编队 import / export dialog title (`screens/loadout.js`, a ternary inside `t()`) and the plugin row's
  class badge (`ui/pluginPanel.js`, `t(CLASS_NAMES[p.class] || '插件')`). All four packs already had the strings, so
  nothing showed untranslated.
- **Now:** `io.mode === 'export' ? t('导出自选编队') : t('导入自选编队')` and `CLASS_NAMES[p.class] ? t(CLASS_NAMES[p.class])
  : t('插件')` (the `CLASS_NAMES` values stay `N_()`-marked, which is how extract sees them). The rule itself — a literal
  msgid, the ternary around the calls — is PR #850's text in docs/I18N.md §1 (merged on the maintainer side, closing
  #849). An extract warning for non-literal msgids is left to a separate contribution, as the triage proposed.
- **Tests:** `test/i18n.test.js` — a ternary inside `t()` yields no msgid, around two calls both; the two call sites'
  lines carry their literal msgids. Strict check: four packs, 2244 / 2244 each, 0 missing, 0 errors; extract: 0 Chinese
  literals unwrapped (`loadout.js` 242 → 244 msgids, `pluginPanel.js` 60 → 61).

### 35.50 自选 operators granted a bond by 变形同构体 (#846)

- **Seen (#846):** a 自选 operator showed the 「甄」 glyph in some views. The 作战统计 panel was already fixed by B6
  (§35.4: the DIY definition carries the pick's charId). The bond popup was not: a 自选 piece that joins a bond only
  through 变形同构体 (凯尔希 in a tier-5 slot wearing 变形同构体 + 维式重锤, a 维多利亚 row) was built from the slot id
  alone, so the row's `UnitThumb` fell back to the empty 甄选干员 slot record (charId null → 「甄」), and a watched
  teammate's row was named 「甄选干员」 too (the triage repro).
- **Now:** `ui/gameLogic/bonds.js bondMembers` gives a granted row of a 自选 piece its operator, as a 自选 member row
  already had: `rec`, the composed record's normal form (an elite copy is the same member), and `pick`, a teammate's
  unit's pick; the row's name comes from it. `ui/bondStrip.js` draws `rec` for any row that has one, skips the 补位
  stand-in lookup for it, and a teammate's row opens their operator's card with the wearer's items and the pick.
  Granted rows of regular operators are unchanged. The 变形同构体 detail card (`MorphPairings`) lists items and bonds,
  no wearer, so it needs nothing.
- **Tests:** new `test/ui/feedback846-diy-granted.test.js` — own board (name, record, the thumb's picture and glyph, no
  stand-in), an elite copy in the hand (normal form), a watched teammate (their pick; the card call with items and
  pick), a regular operator's granted row without `rec` / `pick`. `feedback6-diy-bond-members`, `diy-ui` and
  `gameLogic` unchanged and passing.

### 35.51 §35.18's UI leftovers

The leftovers listed after the text-fit work of §35.18 (its **Open** list and the screenshot round's 遗留), measured in
headless Chrome after the fixes (the room and hub matrices of `test/ui/room-fit.e2e.test.js` /
`test/ui/lobby-hub.e2e.test.js`, a survey of the other event screens in zh / en / ja at 小 / 大 / 特大 on 667×375,
640×360, 1440×900 and 1280×720: 672 findings → 355; the review below found that not all of those were in the kept
list, and that the survey's windows missed 1024×600):

- **The 机变 overlay over the corner buttons.** The overlay starts 2.8rem from the left and the HUD is below it; at
  1280–1920 px the corner's one row (交流, ⚙, 📖, ⛶) reached past it — 「玩法说明」 half and 「全屏」 wholly under the veil,
  not clickable. Under the overlay (`.gm.has-sp`) the corner now wraps upwards within the column above 767 px (as a
  phone's always does); on a coarse phone the overlay starts 2 px past the corner's wrapped width (at 2.8rem, 112 px on a
  667 px phone, it covered 8 px of ⛶), and a folded shop's one-row corner wraps there too. Measured: every corner button
  on top at 1280 / 1440 / 1920 / 667 px (`elementFromPoint` at its centre).
- **选择模拟协议 in a portrait window.** The portrait layout set the list's left padding to .1rem, and the chosen card's
  `translateX(-.44rem)` ran past the screen's edge (768×1024). The list keeps its .5rem: the chosen card's left edge at
  11 px (768×1024, 600×960).
- **The key field's placeholder on short screens.** The 13 px placeholder sat on the 28 px key's baseline, 6–9 px below
  the key icon and 观战 at 特大. The empty field (`:placeholder-shown`) takes the placeholder's size, so it is centred
  like the key will be (zh / en / ja, 667×375 and 640×360: field, icon and 观战 centres equal).
- **Player names at 特大 on phones.** Since §35.18 neither your 你 tag nor an AI teammate's 「AI·」 prefix takes room on a
  phone; a long name still kept 3–5 characters (「罗德岛…」 of 「罗德岛的凯尔希医生阁下」, 「ロドス…」). On phones at least
  370 px tall a name now takes a second line before its ellipsis (`-webkit-line-clamp: 2` in the plate's two rows; the
  plate's second row, `.rseat__doc`, is hidden there): 7 of those 11 characters at 667×375 特大, 「Mudrock the」 8 of 11
  → whole. Every plate is two lines tall there, so the cards' art keeps one height whatever the names. On the 360 px
  tall phones the art has no height to give at 大 / 特大 — with a modded room's tags, 「Standing by」, an AI frame's tag
  and 添加 AI 队友 ran past it (the room-fit matrix) — so names keep one line there. `test/e2e/text-fit.mjs` counts a line
  clamp's hidden lines as an ellipsis (allowed for a player's name only) and lets a player's name break inside a word.

  `text-wrap: balance` like the other short labels. The draft's strategy names (two lines on phones, below) balance too.

  81–320 px past its entry at 特大; it keeps 「1–4 名博士」 there (new msgid `1–{n} 名博士`, four packs).

### 35.52 The bounty leaders' and normal enemies' attack-class skills take the attack's turn

- **Seen:** the review of §35.29: its 'Not in this scope' list (normal-wave enemies and bounty leaders with an
  attack-class skill on a cooldown, still cast on their own timers on top of their attacks) conflicts with the
  maintainer's 2026-10-23 rule for 0.3.2 — nothing doable deferred.
- **Official:** a sweep of every enemy kit (`enemies.js KITS`) for a skill ability on a cooldown whose prefab ability
  (`public/assets/local/fx/index.json` units.<key>.skills, from the client's `battle/enm_pfb_*.ab`) is a MeleeAttack /
  RangedAttack / Multi*Attack, or a group of them:
  | Enemy | Skill (prefabKey) | Official ability | Clip (s) |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 重弩突袭者 | `CrossAttack` (直击) | RangedAttack, no clip | 0 |
  | 萨卡兹王庭军精锐术师 | `DeathEye` (死亡之眼) | AbilitySequenceGroup of two RangedAttacks | 0 |
  | 假想敌：黑云 | `FireWeapon` | AbilitySequenceGroup with a MultiRangedAttack `Skill_2` | 2 |
  | 高准度伦蒂尼姆城防自行炮 | `Cannon` | RangedAttack `Skill` | 10 |
  | W | `C4` | RangedAttack `Skill` | 4.8 |
  | 鼠王 | `DriftSand` (唱沙) / `SandStorm` (沙狱) | RangedAttack `Skill_1` / `Skill_2` | 2.333 / 1.333 |
  | 腐败骑士 / 凋零骑士 | `ChargeAttack` (蓄力攻击) / `TripleAttack` (爆炸箭) | MeleeAttack, no clip / RangedAttack `Skill` | 0 / 1.667 |
  | 迷路的巨像 | `StunAttack` | RangedAttack `Skill` | 2.333 |
  | 陷落雪祀 | `ChainBuff` (反自然馈赠) | RangedAttack `Skill` | 1.667 |
  | “自在” | `CrossAttack` (纬地经天) / `ShieldBurst` / `ShieldBurstReborn` (破桎而出) | RangedAttackWithExtraActions `Skill_01_02` / MeleeAttack, no clip | 1.333 / 0 |
  | 锏 | `CircleAttack` / `CircleAttack2` (肆虐风雪) | MeleeAttack `A_Skill2` / MultiMeleeAttack `B_Skill2` | 2.067 |
  | 扎罗，“狼之主” | `FearCage` (溶血骇惧, SP) | MeleeAttack, no clip | 0 |
  | 节日爵士乐手 | `fire` (狂欢式演奏) | RangedAttack, no clip | 0 |

  The clips are the models' lengths (`data/assets.json` enemies.<key>.spine.animations). Not attack turns: 暴鸰's
  `boomb` (its one bomb is its attack: no normal attack, `noAttack`), “帝国的甲胄” `MultiCombat` (it arms the next attack),
  “巨大的丑东西”'s `bomb[reborning]` (its 重生's, cooldown 9999), 锏's 速杀 `Blink` (ActiveBuffAbility), and the SP attacks
  (粉碎攻坚手, 澪, 码头水手, 无胄盟清扫小队, 纠缠藤蔓, 杰斯顿 …: every (spCost + 1)-th attack, already the attack itself).
  PRTS's skill rules as in §35.19 / §35.29.
- **Now:** `enemies/helpers.js ENEMY_ATTACK_SKILL_CLIP` (the table above) and `attackSkillOf(key, prefabKey, own)` give
  each skill `atk`, its `clip` and `own`; `archetypes.js skill()` takes them as `turn`. Each one is cast in the attack's
  turn (`enemies.js attackTurnSkill`: at a swing's damage frame in place of the strike, or — `own`, a trigger selector
  of its own: all but 腐败骑士's charge on its blocker and, since §35.54, “自在”'s 破桎而出 — when the attack is ready with
  no swing, §35.29's review), its cooldown counting from its clip's end, the attack cooldown restarting at the cast
  (held to the clip's end when the clip is longer than the interval since §35.54). An enemy that makes no normal
  attack (`profile.noAttack`: 节日爵士乐手 hidden, the tests' isolated enemies) still has its attack cooldown run out, so
  an `own` skill keeps coming when ready. 扎罗's 溶血骇惧 keeps its SP and its 静默 (the cooldown's `left`), cast in the
  turn once full.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a group of attack abilities (萨卡兹王庭军精锐术师, 假想敌：黑云) counts as attack-class; the rest as §35.29
  (the effect at the damage frame or, with no swing, at once; the clip kept under a slow).
- **Tests:** new `test/content/enemy-attack-slot.test.js` (the table against data/enemies.json, the models' clips and —
  when extracted — the fx index's classes and animKeys; every listed skill built `atk` with its clip, `own` but 腐败骑士's
  charge; no other kit casting an attack-class ability on its own timer; W's C4 and 凋零骑士's 爆炸箭 one action per
  attack interval, ready again cooldown + clip after; 鼠王 with nothing in its attack range still casting 唱沙 / 沙狱 at
  its ready turns, the attack cooldown restarting); `enemies_bosses.test.js` (死亡之眼 and 蓄力攻击 at the first turn
  after their initial cooldown; “自在”'s burst and crosses sharing the turns).

Golden (§35.27's departure fix moves none; this section, §35.28's own grid, §35.29's aoe trigger and §35.53's traits
together; regenerated with `node tools/golden.mjs --update --family roster,bonds,fields,matches,diy`, every other
scenario recomputing to its value): roster 25 (`roster-004` … `-049`), bonds 2 (`bond-deputShip-high`,
`bond-investShip-high`), fields 2 (`boss-boss_5-pair` cleared at 90.43 s, was 104.8, no operator lost; `boss-boss_5-solo`

### 35.53 The operators' 「进入休整期时」 traits at the prep start

- **Seen:** §35.24 moved the strategies' 「进入休整期时」 grants after the 机变 draft and left the operators' traits with
  `SERVER_PREP_START` (「<进入休整期时>」) on the round start, before a 机变 draft (its [ASSUMED] note); the review asked for
  them in 0.3.2 too.
- **Official:** the same sources as §35.24: research 01 §4.1 (the 机变 choice step 4, the 「进入休整期时」 effects step 6)
  and its appendix A1 (「进入此阶段时，触发<进入休整期时>的效果」 of the 休整期). The traits (data/garrisons.json, 20
  garrisons): 余 37 (SERVER_MOST_BOND), 歌蕾蒂娅 39 (SERVER_POOL_CHAR), 铃兰 60 (SERVER_TRIGGER_ANOTHER /
  _FRONT_COUNT), 赫默 / 蒂比 80 (SERVER_ADD_BOND_CHESS_ALL), 白面鸮 86 (SERVER_FRONT_SAME_EFFECT_PREP_START), 佩佩 94
  (SERVER_POOL_EQUIP), 缄默德克萨斯 114 (SERVER_GAIN_FREE_REFRESH_COUNT), 凯瑟琳 127 (SERVER_GAIN_RANDOM_EQUIP_CHESS_IN_POOL),
  寒檀 141 (SERVER_ADD_BOND; its 「<休整期结束时>」 half is garrison 142 on the prep end), 空弦 150 (SERVER_ADD_BOND_POSITION), each normal and elite.
- **Now:** `effectsMeta.js GARRISON_HOOK` maps `SERVER_PREP_START` to `onPrepStart` (was `onRoundStart`): the traits run in
  `prep.js enterPrep`'s dispatch, after the strategy's grants of that dispatch (global → band → bonds → garrisons), so a
  piece gained in the 机变 draft or at the prep start is on the board or in the hand when they run. A handler that widens
  its hooks (`garrisonHooks`) may still name `onRoundStart`. `ctx.triggerGarrisons(uid, 'SERVER_PREP_START')` (铃兰,
  白面鸮's copies) follows the map. In a round with no 机变 draft only the order around the round start changes.
  Normative: META.md §2.2 / §2.3, design/match.md §6.1.
- **[ASSUMED]:** none beyond §35.24's (the same 休整期 entry for the traits as for the strategies).
- **Tests:** `test/match/prep-start-grants.test.js` (同盟 NORMAL: 缄默德克萨斯 on the board runs nothing at the R3
  `ROUND_START` or during the R3 draft, once at the R3 `PREP`, on `onPrepStart`); `garrisons_meta.test.js` (its helper
  dispatches the prep start), `hardening.test.js` (a widened garrison on `onPrepStart` / `onPrepEnd`, nothing on the
  round start), `bench-overflow.test.js` (the twelve prep-start gains on the prep start).

Golden: in §35.52's note (the `matches` scenarios with these traits).

### 35.54 Long clips hold the next attack; “自在”'s 破桎而出 waits for a swing

- **Seen:** the review of §35.52. After an attack-turn cast the attack cooldown restarted at the attack interval, while
  two of the new clips are longer: 高准度伦蒂尼姆城防自行炮's `Cannon` 10 s (attack interval 6) and W's `C4` 4.8 s (interval 4),
  so both made a normal attack while their skill clip still played. And “自在”'s `ShieldBurst` / `ShieldBurstReborn`
  (破桎而出, MeleeAttack) were `own` although their `cond` is only the form check: an unblocked 自在 put up its barrier,
  and later blasted, as soon as its attack was ready with no operator near — exactly when the official ability, with the
  attack's trigger (PRTS 「触发索敌和普通攻击相同」), does not fire.
- **Official:** the skills' `EnemySkill` fields in the local client (`battle/enm_pfb_11.ab` `Cannon`, `enm_pfb_17.ab` …):
  `_resetMainAbilityCdWhenCastEnd` 0 and `_castLikeAttack` 0, as on 昆图斯's four (§35.19): the attack cooldown is not
  restarted at the clip's end (59 enemy skills have 1, e.g. `Devour`). The clips: §35.52's table. The abilities of
  破桎而出 are MeleeAttacks with an empty `_animKey` (fx/index.json).
- **Now:** `enemies.js attackTurnSkill` returns the ability it cast (null when the turn goes to the attack), and both
  the swing path (`attackSlotSkill`) and the own-trigger path (`onTick`) set the attack cooldown to `attackCdAfter` =
  max(attack interval, the skill's clip): the next swing waits for a longer clip, the cooldown still counted from the
  cast. 自行炮 and W make no attack inside `Cannon` / `C4`; among the leaders, 阿利斯泰尔's 斥退 (`Suicide` 5.667 s), 管's
  裂管之奏 4.167 s (3 s interval, including its custom attack loop), 昆图斯's 物种爆发 (4.467 s, §35.55) and 崩坍 under an attack-speed gain
  that brings the interval under 3 s are held the same way; §35.19's "the next swing does not wait" is superseded.
  The custom attack loops of 管 / 弦 and “萨米的意志” keep a `heldUntil` deadline too, so they respect the same clip
  occupation even with attack-speed gains. “自在”'s two 破桎而出 are built with `attackSkillOf(…, false)`: they wait for a swing at the attack's target.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a unit cannot attack while its own skill clip plays (the ability occupies it; no prefab field says so
  directly — `_resetMainAbilityCdWhenCastEnd` 0 only says the cooldown is not restarted at its end).
- **Tests:** `enemy-attack-slot.test.js` — no strike inside the `Cannon` or `C4` clip after a cast, attacks between the
  casts; the `own` list (腐败骑士's charge and 自在's two 破桎而出 wait for a swing); 鼠王's own turns refused while 缴械 or
  恐惧, and 战栗 while blocked (the §35.29 refusals, now pinned) and an `own: false` attack-class skill never cast from a ready attack with no
  swing; the class sweep also maps the cooldown abilities built without `sk` (by cooldown and initial cooldown, failing
  on one it cannot map) and runs in CI from the committed `test/fixtures/enemy-skill-classes.json` (the extract's class
  and clip of every kit's skills; a test keeps it equal to the fx index when extracted, `SP_UPDATE_FIXTURE=1` rewrites
  it). `enemies_bosses.test.js` “自在”: the barrier only once it attacks (at or after its initial cooldown), the blast
  ATK × 6, burst, crosses and strikes one per turn, a broken barrier never blasting, and no barrier with nobody in its
  attack range. `boss-attack-slot.test.js` also refuses a strike inside 管's longer clip and inside 萨米's Doom
  with an attack-speed gain.

### 35.55 昆图斯's 断裂生殖 / 物种爆发 take the attack's turn

- **Seen:** §35.19 left 盐风主教昆图斯's 断裂生殖 / 物种爆发 on their own timers ("kept to limit this fix's scope", a 0.3.3
  option), against the maintainer's 2026-10-23 rule that nothing doable is deferred.
- **Official:** PRTS 盐风主教昆图斯's skill rules 「大多数技能自动触发会占用攻击间隔」 (no exception noted for them);
  arknights.wiki.gg: one action ("attacking once or using another ability") between two tides. Both abilities are
  `AnimatedActionToOwnerAbility` with `_allowNoTarget` 1 (§35.19's prefab reading).
- **Now:** `bosses.js kitQuintus` builds all ten skills `atk`; 断裂生殖 and 物种爆发 also `own` (`QUINTUS_ATTACK_SKILLS`
  names the two with the attack's trigger, 大潮 / 崩坍), so they take a ready attack's turn with nothing in range too
  (`_allowNoTarget`). Equal priorities (all 0) draw at random on a shared turn, so a tentacle or the burst can come a
  turn later than its cooldown allows, and the third-form tide a turn later after one of them. 物种爆发's 4.467 s clip
  holds the next attack (§35.54).
- **[ASSUMED]:** none beyond §35.19's and §35.54's.
- **Tests:** `boss-quintus-attack-slot.test.js` — the tide, 崩坍 and 断裂生殖 one per turn (断裂生殖 among the turns); the
  plain tide gaps counted only between tides with no other skill between; 断裂生殖 with no operator in range; first form:
  崩坍, 大潮 and 断裂生殖 at the first turn after their initial cooldowns; 断裂生殖's gaps cooldown + clip to one more turn
  past it; 物种爆发 at its initial cooldown to two turns after, then cooldown + clip apart, and no other action inside
  its clip; `enemies_bosses.test.js` 盐风主教昆图斯 (the tentacles and the burst a turn later).

### 35.56 The draft header by text size; 确认本局信息 and the 机变 overlay on phones

- **Seen:** the review of §35.51 at 1600×900 and 1920×1080. The draft's step name hid by window width (≤ 1440 px or ≤ 640 px
  tall): at 大 / 特大 in wider windows English 「Choose Your Strategy」 still ran over 「2/2 Choose Strategy」 and 「How to
  Play」 past its button into the countdown (draft and solo draft, 1600×900 and 1920×1080, the most common desktop size),
  while 小 / 中 laptop windows (1280, 1366, 1440 px) lost the name with nothing in the way. §35.51's kept list also held
  visible overlaps: 确认本局信息's step name under 玩法说明 and COUNTDOWN (English / Korean, 大 / 特大, 640–740 px phones),
  its reroll note over 已就绪 2/4 (English / Japanese / Korean, 大 / 特大), and the 机变 overlay's timing line under 「Your
  turn to decide」 (English, 中 – 特大, phones up to 844×390, both variants: 18 cases); the overlay's Korean card texts broke
  inside words on every phone (401 findings, pre-existing).
- **Now:** `draft.css`: while the status shows, the step's name gives way at 大 / 特大 in any window and in windows up to
  640 px tall (short screens as before); 小 / 中 keep it at 1280–1920 px; 玩法说明 no longer shrinks (`flex: none`), so its
  label stays in its box. `briefing.css`: on short screens 确认本局信息's step name may take two lines (balanced, Korean by
  words, Japanese strict) and the header grows; the reroll banner's bottom margin follows the foot's height (max(1.6rem,
  .76rem + .58 × --t)). `game-panels.css`: on short screens the overlay's timing line goes under the title across the
  title block, the title block may shrink, and the title, description and timing line are capped; the cards' texts keep
  the Korean keep-all (with `overflow-wrap: break-word`) and Japanese strict rules.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the caps (the overlay's title 26 px + 2 px a step above 中, its description 15 px + 1, the timing line
  10 px + ½) and the margin's coefficients (measured: the foot is about .73rem + .56 × --t above its content).
- **Survey** (`text-fit.mjs` on the mock harness, zh / zh-TW / en / ja / ko × 小 – 特大 × 640×360 – 1920×1080, eleven

  draft's name shown at 小 / 中 from 1280×720 up; on the overlay no timing line over the turn text and no Korean word

- **Tests:** `mock.e2e.test.js` — 选择策略 (team, solo) in English at 大 / 特大 in 1600×900 / 1920×1080 windows: nothing over
  2/2 or the step's name, 「How to Play」 inside its button; at 中 in a 1280×720 window the name shown; 确认本局信息 on
  640×360 / 667×375 phones in English and Korean at 大 / 特大: nothing over the header's parts or 2/4; the 机变 overlay
  (supply and tactic) on 640×360 / 844×390 in English at 中 / 特大: the timing line clear of the turn text, and in Korean
  no card text broken inside a word. `ui-leftovers-3533.test.js` follows the rules.

### 35.57 Attack-turn casts play their clip

- **Seen:** the review of §35.52. The attack-class casts moved into the attack's turn played no Spine clip: a content
  cast only changes the view's skill slot, and the snapshot's 0.5 s skill window maps to `idle`. Where the official
  enemy plays its `Skill` clip in place of the attack, ours dropped the swing and stood idle (迷路的巨像 in a recording:
  `Idle`, then `Move`, never `Skill`); only the two `strike` slots (杰斯顿, 碎铳之簧) had a clip.
- **Official:** each ability's `_animKey` (§35.19 / §35.29 / §35.52 tables: `Skill`, `Skill_1`, `Skill_02` …; the models'
  clips in `data/assets.json` are the client's `anims.skills[slot]` or the primary `anims.skill`, the same names).
- **Now:** the sim's cast of an attack-class skill (`enemies.js castNow` → `helpers.js castSlot`) sends ['cast', id, slot,
  dur, clip], `clip` the official clip's length (0 when the ability names none). The client (`render/app.js` 'cast' →
  `UnitView.onCast`) plays the slot's clip once at its own speed in place of the attack (`SpineActor.castStrike` +
  `attack(…, once)`), or the attack clip when `clip` is 0; the look-ahead (`render/app/windup.js`) winds the clip up when
  it sees the cast ahead (`UnitView.windUpCast`), so its strike frame lands with the cast. Display only: the tuple's
  extra field alone changes no golden value (events are counted by kind). 节日爵士乐手's channel now carries its data
  skill (`sk`) too, so it emits the cast event and plays the attack clip fallback; 阿利斯泰尔's 王权号令 appends the clip
  to its manually emitted cast after choosing equip / equipA.
- **[ASSUMED]:** an attack-class ability with an empty `_animKey` (重弩突袭者's 直击, 腐败骑士's 蓄力攻击, “自在”'s
  破桎而出, 扎罗's 溶血骇惧, 节日爵士乐手, “萨米的意志”'s 自然涌动) shows the unit's attack clip.
- **Tests:** new `test/render/enemy-turn-clip.test.js` (the sim's 5-field tuple for 迷路的巨像 with no 'atk' after it; its
  `Skill` played once then the attack clip again; 鼠王's 唱沙 wound up ahead and not restarted when rendered; 重弩突袭者's
  clip-0 cast on the attack clip; 昆图斯's tide on `Skill_02`; the look-ahead winding a turn cast once and ignoring a
  plain cast); plain cooldown casts keep their 3-/4-field tuples. The class/slot check covers 节日爵士乐手 too.

Golden (§35.57): only `events.cast` changes for 节日爵士乐手 — `roster-010` 13 → 14, `roster-023` 6 → 9,
`standin-01` 7 → 8. Regenerated only these three scenarios; no damage, snapshots or outcome changes. G1 golden hashes
refreshed without changing its room-state fixture (the canonical refresh needs the blocked localhost server).

Validation in this completion run: the simulation and render unit checks and the complete golden corpus pass. The
new browser matrix could not start here: the sandbox rejects its localhost test server with `listen EPERM`. The survey
figures above were recorded by the interrupted review-fix run and were not re-measured in this environment. The full
CI command passed setup but its server tests hit the same permission error and the test phase stopped progressing;
the run was interrupted. Separate lint, import and type checks pass; smoke is blocked by the localhost restriction.

### 35.58 Browser regression checks after the 0.3.2 merges

- **Reported:** `feedback1-offers.e2e.test.js` could not click 凯瑟琳, `standin.e2e.test.js` rejected the stored ownership
  object, and `versions-fit.e2e.test.js` required a ready tag on phones. The initial failures are stale test assumptions, with the
  intended product behaviour recorded in §35.18 and §35.23. The later reload exposed the data-hook race in §35.59.
- **History:** the four-column scrolling strategy wall came from `164a380c` (§35.18) and already exists at the review's
  `1fb9c004` baseline. 凯瑟琳 is the 21st solo strategy, below the initial viewport; the test now scrolls her tile into
  view before its real mouse click. M1's `a4ed6c2f`, merged by `6deab17d`, intentionally keeps the v1 `notOwned` copy
  beside `notOwnedChars` for older builds on the same origin (§35.23); the stand-in test checks both. The room fit work
  `02eebe5b`, merged by `69eb6580`, hides `.seat__content.is-ready` on short screens to leave room for the seat's state
  (§35.18). The version matrix permits that ready tag to be hidden on phones and continues checking loading / failed
  tags and desktop ready tags when drawn.
- **Click helper:** after 干员调配 closes, `LoadoutHost` restores the underlying screen's visibility in an effect.
  `Client.point` now waits while an element inherits `visibility: hidden`; the document root containing that element
  is not evidence that the element can receive a click. This also unblocks the stand-in case's solo dialog.
- **Scope:** these test corrections add no gameplay rule or [ASSUMED] behaviour. Default golden results stay
  unchanged. The complete per-file browser results and investigation are in the round51 handoff report.

### 35.59 A resumed match leaves the data-loading screen

- **Seen:** after unblocking 凯瑟琳's strategy pick (§35.58), the queued-offer browser case reproducibly stayed on
  正在载入模拟数据… after touch emulation reloaded its page. The match and both offers survived on the server; every
  `GAME_FILES` entry was already `ready`, but the game screen had rendered while some were still loading.
- **Cause:** `useData` subscribed in a passive effect. A warm-up could settle between its render and that subscription,
  so the hook missed the last notification; requesting already loaded files emitted nothing further. The hook dates
  to the initial import, before `1fb9c004`; this is a pre-existing race exposed by the merged browser flow, not an
  intentional version rule.
- **Now:** after subscribing and starting its loads, `useData` schedules another render to read the current statuses.
  Later notifications and unmount cleanup keep their existing behaviour. `mock.e2e.test.js` settles a file in the same
  task as the first render, before the passive effect can subscribe, and checks that the hook leaves loading;
  `feedback1-offers.e2e.test.js` covers the real reconnect with both offers still queued.
- **Scope:** client readiness only; no gameplay or data change, no new [ASSUMED] rule, and no golden move.

  wrapping contract of §35.18 requires the descriptions to remain readable.
