- **Now:** at widths up to 900 px and heights above 520 px, both descriptions wrap inside their available width. A
  shared two-line minimum keeps the two cards aligned when only one description wraps. Phones keep §35.51's short
  labels. The existing full hub-label matrix checks the fix in all five languages at all four text sizes.
- **[ASSUMED]:** the two-line minimum in this desktop layout. No gameplay, data or golden change.

### 35.61 Room-card browser checks wait for the synchronized selection

- **Seen:** the full browser sweep first hit a 30 s navigation timeout when Puppeteer changed the guest to phone
  emulation. That timeout did not recur in the isolated run, which instead exposed an assertion race: the teammate's
  portrait already matched 浊心斯卡蒂 while its `room.state.seats[].card` was still absent.
- **Cause:** the default portrait is chosen from 5★/6★ operators by player id (§35.18), so it can be the same operator
  the test picks. The image is then ready before the asynchronous `room.card` update arrives. The image-only wait
  dates to `cba2372ea`, before `1fb9c004`; the product's synchronization contract is unchanged.
- **Now:** `room-cards.e2e.test.js` waits for the teammate's selected `card` value and for its removal on 默认, in
  addition to checking the portraits. No larger timeout, product change, new [ASSUMED] rule or golden move.

### 35.62 终极 (ABYSS) with LLM-played seats and bots, cycle 1

Maintainer goal 2026-10-23: when the example LLM plays (plan mode, every seat), it should clear the 终极 Final Assault
(round 14) close to always, solo and 4-player co-op, and what it learns should raise the bots' rate too. Fair play:
seats see only seat-visible state and the official rules; no rule changes. Benches, per-match notes and the research
behind this section are local maintainer material (`handoff/round51/abyss/`, not committed).

- **Seen (baseline, 2026-10-23, `1fb9c004`):** Bots: solo ABYSS 0/20, co-op ABYSS 0/20 (seeds 1–20). Solo reached the
  Final Assault 13/20 times and dealt 5.9–38.8 % of the leader pool; co-op lost 12/20 teams before it. The example
  agent (deepseek-flash, plan mode, solo seeds 1–6, co-op seeds 1–3): 0/9, and it advised only the band and R1–R4.
  - Reply time grew from 6–14 s (R1) to 16–27 s (R3–R4); from R5 every solo prep hit the agent's 28 s HTTP limit. The
    agent then answered `{plan: null}`, which counted as a failure; three in a row switched the seat to legacy for the
    match (`failureLimit`), and every failure also cleared the round's plan.
  - Co-op: every host-side cancel (`drop()` aborts) went to `StdioAI`'s `breakChild()`, which SIGKILLed the one agent
    process the four seats share. Every other seat's request in flight failed with it, and for 1 s after (backoff)
    new requests failed without reaching the model: 70 calls logged, 41 reached the agent; seed 1's twelve calls
    all failed in about 2 s each with 0 tokens. A local fake endpoint answering 机变 picks in 12 s (the co-op card
    budget is 8 s) reproduced it: 45 calls, 32 reached the endpoint, two seats switched to legacy at R3.
  - Band picks: 玛恩纳 / 贾维 / 哈洛德 (23–24 LP) in 6/6 solo matches, following candidates that had no LP term
    (`slimView` dropped `bands[].lp`). On seeds 4–6 they died in R11–R13; legacy on the same seeds took 25–45 LP bands
    and reached the Final Assault with 5–6 LP left.
  - `holdLevel` in 15 of 23 solo prep plans ("L3 costs 7 now vs 6 next round"); elites trailed legacy at R4/R7/R10
    (0.3 / 0.7 / 1.7 against 0.8 / 1.4 / 1.9). Plans could not name 卡西米尔 for 玛恩纳 before a member was owned
    (seed 2 switched at R6). Four item ids written into `buy` were dropped and never bought.
  - Legacy lineups never fielded a 6-member core bond, even owning 6 distinct members (seeds 2, 5, 12: 拉特兰 /
    叙拉古 deployed at 3–4). Going from 3 to 6 needs three swaps at once and each single swap scored lower.
- **Official:** Final Assault overtime: from 150 real seconds the team loses 1 LP per real second, and LP 0 loses;
  no in-battle layer gains in the Final Assault; a leader hit of 300000 or more is voided (`finalAssault.js`,
  `gamedata.js bossOvertimeDue`, `BOSS_HIT_LIMIT`). Starting LP is `bands.json totalHp` (`gamedata.startLp`).
  Core bond thresholds 3 / 6 (炎 3 / 6 / 9, 阿戈尔 3 / 5) from `bondInfoDict`.
- **Community (each with its source in the local research notes):** the slow level rhythm 3-5-7-9 (L2 by R3 … L5 by
  R9; BV19UXZBJExQ reply) and 二本叙's deliberate L2 hold (BV1AdXKB9Ebt pinned); boards are built around a 6-member
  main and reach several hundred layers (「400层保底」, BV1ohDNBPEcx comment; 999-layer 谢拉格, BV1xVdBBWEuu).
- **Now (adapter, every external agent):**
  - `StdioAI` cancels one request at a time: a host abort or a timeout ends only that request, its ID goes to a
    bounded "abandoned" set (256), and its late reply is dropped by ID. A reply ID that was never sent is still a
    protocol error. The child is killed only for a protocol error, a crash, or a timeout with no line from the child
    since the request was written (a real hang).
  - Plan mode: only protocol and transport errors count toward `failureLimit`. A budget timeout and an agent's
    `{plan: null}` are soft: legacy continues that decision as before, the streak is unchanged. A prep that fails or
    is still waiting keeps the last accepted plan's direction (`carryPlan`: bonds, buy / keep / sell, items, economy
    preferences; never `choice`, `placements`, `holdLevel` or its `why`). Direct mode keeps §35.8's rule (every
    failure counts and clears the plan). A failed 机变 request no longer clears that round's accepted prep plan.
  - `timeoutMs` accepts up to 300000 (was 30000); the decision budget stays half the remaining phase.
- **Now (example agent and its candidates):**
  - The example's HTTP limit `SP_LLM_TIMEOUT_MS` accepts up to 300000; `serveAgent` takes 16 concurrent requests (was 4:
    four seats plus abandoned requests still finishing). Every diagnostic line carries the provider's usage, so a
    reply the host abandoned still shows its tokens. A reply wrapped in a code fence or a sentence is parsed from its
    outermost `{…}`.
  - The model sees at most two prep candidates and one copy of the level-curve basis sentence (`levelCurveBasis`)
    instead of one per checkpoint; the band draft still sees up to eight.
  - Bands carry `lp` (playbook and the model's knowledge); bosses carry `hpByDifficulty`. Every candidate kind
    (generic, community build, cycle) has `scoreParts.survival = (lp − 26) × 1.5`. The draft keeps one of its eight
    slots for the best direction of a band without a community main: every community band starts with 23–29 LP,
    so before this no 45 LP opening was ever listed.
  - `repairPlan` drops a `holdLevel` when the level held (the higher of the shop level and `economy.level`, which
    legacy reaches first) is below the slow curve (L2 by R3, L3 by R5, L4 by R7, L5 by R9) unless one of the seat's
    candidates holds that level (`shopHold`). Levelling to the floor and holding there is kept (cycle-1 review). Item ids found in `buy` move to
    `items.buy`.
  - A strategy candidate's main may lead the plan before any member is held; a primary change still needs
    `pivot:true` and a reason.
  - The prompt states the level trade honestly (waiting saves exactly 1 fund and costs a round of higher-tier rolls),
    the hold floor, starting LP as leak budget and Final Assault clock, and the Final Assault facts above.
- **Now (legacy bot; plan seats run the same executor):**
  - `chooseLineup` also climbs from each core bond's full threshold above 3 when enough distinct members are owned,
    and keeps the best climb. `lineupScore` adds 30 for a core bond at its second tier or above. Bench members of a
    core bond with at least 4 distinct owned members are not sold as junk.
  - An accepted plan's primary core bond is `bondPlan`'s focus, owned members or not; without a plan nothing changes.
- **[ASSUMED]:** The 26 LP centre and 1.5 per LP; the sturdy slot; the hold floor and its `shopHold` exemption; the
  carried fields; treating `{plan: null}` as soft; the abandoned-set cap and the hang test; 16 concurrent requests;
  two prep candidates; `CORE_TOP_TIER` 30 and `CORE_KEEP_OWNED` 4. A 助力 layer-engine weight (every active bond's
  per-prep layers × preps left) was tried and dropped: solo ABYSS layers did not move (237 → 230) and leader damage
  fell (515k → 463k mean).
- **Measured (bots, seeds 1–20 ABYSS / 1–10 others, rehearsal default):**
  - Solo ABYSS 0/20 → 0/20, rounds passed 11.95 → 12.05, leaks 31.4 → 29.9 per match, mean leader damage 504k → 515k.
    Co-op ABYSS 0/20 → 0/20, leader damage per seat 108k → 128k, leaks 217.4 → 215.3.
  - Wins before → after: solo FUNNY 8 → 8, NORMAL 6 → 7, HARD 4 → 4; co-op FUNNY 10 → 10, NORMAL 9 → 9, HARD 4 → 4.
  - The 12 s-card fake endpoint (co-op ABYSS seed 1, `SP_EXTERNAL_AI_TIMEOUT_MS=120000`): 67 calls, all 67 reached the
    agent, one hello (the child never restarted), 9 soft card timeouts, and every seat stayed on the agent until it was
    eliminated (R12–R14).
  - A fake endpoint answering every call in 31 s (above the old 30 s caps; solo ABYSS seed 1,
    `SP_EXTERNAL_AI_TIMEOUT_MS=120000`, `SP_LLM_TIMEOUT_MS=110000`): 18 of 18 calls accepted (band, 14 preps, three
    机变 picks), 0 fallbacks, the agent planned R1–R14.
  - No real model call in this cycle (0 tokens). Winning still needs far more Final Assault damage: the research puts
    boards at 4–6× the bots' 230 banked layers.
- **Golden:** the bot change moves ten `matches` scenarios (regenerated): solo-NORMAL-2 (team LP 29 → 11, still a
  win; R10 fields 6 拉特兰 at tier 2 instead of 炎 + 2 奥术), solo-HARD-2 (an extra item equipped), solo-ABYSS-2 (leader
  pool left 2175584 → 2283835), coop3-ABYSS-5 (3081194 → 3117429), coop4-FUNNY-6, coop2-NORMAL-8-serverrun,
  coop2-NORMAL-14-standins (boss damage 194742 → 146607), coop2-NORMAL-70-diy, coop2-NORMAL-1-coverage-standins (team LP
  32 → 34), coop2-NORMAL-5-coverage-diy. Roster, bonds, fields, standins and diy are unchanged. §35.22.2's G1 guard
  pins the corpus hash: `test/fixtures/versions-default-guard.json` is regenerated for `matches.json` only (an intended
  bot change on the default version, not a data change).
- **Tests:** `external-transports` (one cancel leaves the child serving, a slow request beside a quick one, a silent
  child is killed, an unknown reply ID is a protocol error), `external-advisor` (carried plan through a timeout,
  `{plan: null}` and a protocol error; the plan's primary bond as legacy's focus), `external-llm-continuity` (hold
  floor and exemption, moved item buys, intent bonds, two prep candidates, fenced replies), `advisor-input` (the
  sturdy draft slot and `survival` on every candidate).

### 35.63 终极 (ABYSS) with LLM-played seats and bots, cycle 2

Same goal and fair-play rules as §35.62. This cycle made no external-model call (0 tokens): the LLM side is checked
with unit tests and by replaying the eight recorded cycle-2 solo plans through the executor; the bots with botbench.
Benches, traces and the diagnoses are local maintainer material (`handoff/round51/abyss/cycle2`, `cycle3`).

- **Seen (cycle 2 bench at `631fc61f`, deepseek-flash, plan mode, every seat; solo seeds 1–8, co-op 1–4):** 0/8 and
  0/4. The model fell behind legacy for its own reasons, not only for cut-off replies:
  - Output limit: 126 of 365 requests ended with `finish_reason: length`, 54 of the 58 solo ones with no text at all.
    Input stayed at 75–78k tokens (71k of them the cached system prefix); what grew was reasoning (accepted replies'
    median output 2.6k tokens in R1–R4, 4.8k in R9–R14). 机变 / offer calls failed most (20 of 62) although only their
    `choice` is executed: the model re-planned the whole prep inside them. Only about 35 % of R9–R14 preps got a plan.
  - Mains: 迅捷 ×5, 精准, 坚守 and 维 in the solo matches. 迅捷 has a single threshold (2), so late money went to
    survival sub-bonds; bond tiers at R13 were 5.5 against legacy's 7.3. The two boards that carried 卡西米尔 dealt 87.5 %
    and 28.7 % of the leader pool, every board without a core bond 2–6 %.
  - The R3 hold floor read as an order ("Dropping to L2 now is mandatory"): board value at R3 10.4 against legacy's
    15.1, elites at R4 0.1 against 0.9, third copies skipped for a new distinct member. Late, 1-fund holds in R8–R13.
  - Executor (replaying the recorded plans): 11 tier 5/6 and 5 elite sales for 1 fund each (the full-hand shed fell
    through to legacy's sale, which ignored the plan's protection); 25 % of preps ended with at most one free hand slot,
    which also stopped the spend pass from refreshing; a carried `maxRefreshes: 0` met the next round's shop (failed
    preps lost 1.19 funds each, accepted ones 0.37); 27 of 38 preps from R10 benched a tier 5/6 carry for a tier 1–2
    plan member.
  - Bots (seeds 1–100): solo 0/100, reached the Final Assault 56 times; co-op 0/100, 30 teams reached it. Boards owned
    3.9 members of their best core at R9–R13, fielded 0.1–0.5 healers, and banked about 210 layers at R13.
- **Official:** `config.json` modes' `enemyScale` (co-op 终极 enemy HP ×3.58 at R7, ×4.30 at R8–R10; solo ×1.73 at R7);
  core bonds `isCore` with thresholds 3 / 6 (阿戈尔 3 / 5); the 获得时 traits SERVER_GAIN SERVER_ADD_BOND_CHESS_ALL
  (`garrisons.json`: 野鬃 / 角峰 / 艾丝黛尔 / 深靛 +2, 雪猎 / 灵知 +5, 忍冬 +6, 星熊 / 锏 +8 layers on every bond of the operator);
  leftover funds expire at the prep end unless the band carries them.
- **Community (sources in the local research notes, RESEARCH §1, §3.3, §5):** buying and selling 灵知 / 野鬃 / 忍冬 / 角峰 /
  雪猎 stacks their layers again (BV1qkDsBtEER pinned; cv47352364); fear (叙拉古) holds 卢西恩 (Bahamut 12316 6F;
  BV118QcBGEmZ) and true damage (卡西米尔, 叙拉古) is the main output against 铳's 80 % reduction (BV1YTX5BbEGW reply;
  BV1W5XMB8Exj; BV1eBSdBBEig reply); multiplier layers (灵巧 / 奥术 / 精准) carry the damage (Bahamut 12316; cv47352364).
- **Now (example agent):**
  - `SP_LLM_MAX_TOKENS` defaults to 16000 (was 6000; still 256–32000). A reply cut off by the limit before any JSON is
    asked once more with a short "decide now, JSON only" turn while half the HTTP limit remains; `SP_LLM_RETRY_EFFORT`
    optionally sends a lower `reasoning_effort` with it. The HTTP limit covers both requests; usage adds up. A reply
    that is not JSON logs its tail as well as its head.
  - 机变, offer and reward calls get a choice-only task (`CHOICE_TASK`: the choice table and the digest, no strategy
    candidates or continuity) and answer `{why, choiceName, plan: {choice}}`. When `choiceName` names exactly one other
    card of the observation, the choice follows the name (co-op seed 3 R9: the why named 萨卡兹百夫长, the ID picked a slime).
  - Digest: shop rows carry `copiesHeld` and `completesMerge`; `economy.levelOptions` compares holding with levelling
    now (funds after, merges still affordable, next round's price); `enemyScale` is the round's official ATK / HP
    multiplier; bonds carry `kind: core | class` (the knowledge pack's bonds gain `core`); teammates carry their public
    `main` core bond.
  - Prompt: the main of a carry is a core bond brought to 6 distinct members by R11–R13, class bonds are subs and
    multipliers; spend on merges first, then the nearest threshold, then new members; the hold floor limits holding and
    is no order to level; items and multiplier layers go on the Final Assault carries; in co-op one seat covers R3–R9.
    `refreshAbove` is no longer advertised (bots with `refreshAbove: 100` played exactly like `maxRefreshes: 0`); the
    server API still accepts it.
  - `holdFloor`: L2 by R4 (was R3), L3 by R5, L4 by R7, L5 by R9, L6 by R11 (new).
- **Now (candidates):** a generic carry candidate's main is a core bond; a class bond leads only a band engine tied to it
  or the safety-net / supplier / economy roles. Every candidate kind (generic, community build, cycle) has
  `scoreParts.finalAssault`: +6 when the core bond can field its top threshold, +3 for a 灵巧 / 奥术 / 精准 sub among its
  community pairs, +8 when the community names the main against the announced leader (`strategy-community.js`
  `bossCounters`, matched by enemyKey so the 隐秘 copies count).
- **Now (executor, plan seats only):** the full-hand shed never sells an elite, a tier 5/6 piece or a merge piece the
  plan protects (a tier ≤ 4 protected piece may still go, last, for legal room); from R9 with at most one free hand
  slot a benched normal piece of tier ≤ 4 that completes no merge and was not bought this prep loses its protection;
  the spend pass refreshes with one free slot (was two); a carried plan keeps only `saveForInterest` / `reserve` of
  its economy; a plan's unit weight counts a quarter in lineup and bench values (`PLAN_UNIT_SHARE`).
- **Now (bots; plan seats run the same executor):**
  - `LEVEL_TARGET`: L5 at R9 and L6 at R11 (were R10 and R12).
  - Layer trade (`tradeOnce`): from R4, outside banking bands, buy a 获得时 layer unit whose layers per net fund × the
    focus / active bonds it reaches is at least 3, and sell it again unless it starts a pair, fills the board or makes
    the lineup.
  - Core commitment (`coreGap`): while the focus core bond has 2 owned members up to its top threshold, a new member
    is worth 10 more, last round's focus keeps 16 (was 6) once 3 are owned, a refresh is worth 20 × P(it shows a missing
    member), and with a full board a missing member is bought without a lineup gain while 3 hand slots are free.
  - Healer floor: from R5 the lineup values its first healer at 16 and a second from R9 at 6; buying the first healer
    is worth 14 (was 6), a second from R8 4 (was −3).
  - `spendDown`: after the buy loop, up to 4 refreshes with the funds that would expire, never past a copy completing a
    held pair (maybeFreeze keeps it) or a frozen shop.

### 35.64 Boss-round layout rehearsal (bots, #315)

Closes the open item in §35.63: the Final Assault is rehearsed, on a seed that is not the live fight's. Brought in with the abyss merge (the rehearsal commit on that branch was `a8c8aa1e`).

- **Seen:** at `c028065f`, seeds 1–20, the bot never rehearses a boss round (`m.wave` is null). Mean leader damage per seat on that tree is 547641 solo and 639442 co-op (0/20 and 1/20 wins). A 40-game-second rehearsal raised solo damage to 616520 and lowered co-op damage to 632041. Co-op round-13 leaks went from 9.5 to 17.0 per alive seat. That prefix ends before the escorts leak, so the leak counts tie and the damage tie-break keeps the early burst. The same layouts, played for the whole visible countdown, still leak 17.0 on the live co-op fight of that tree: the choice is already fixed inside the first 40 game seconds.
- **Official:** the boss countdown is `levelMaxPlayTime` 120 real seconds and the battle continues after it (research 01 §10; `gamedata.js` `bossLevelTime`). The combat clock is `combatTimeScale` 2, so the countdown is 240 game seconds. Leader pools and leader / escort stats match `activity_table.json` `bossInfoDict` and `enemy_database.json` level 0; per-round `enemyScale` matches research 01 §A3. The remake is not harder on those numbers. Three non-leader diffs are recorded in the cycle notes and were not edited.
- **[ASSUMED] #315:** the rehearsal seed is `deriveSeed(0xA11CE, fa:round:seat)`. It does not read the match seed or the live field seed. Each candidate builds a fresh pool from `bossPoolHp`. The teammate's board is whatever that seat has deployed when this one arranges. Dispatch is preview only. The cap is the visible countdown on the combat clock (`faRehearsalTicks`), not the overtime after it and not the unbounded fight. Counted leaks rank first; `perPlayer.bossDamage` breaks a tie. One counted leak still outranks any leader-damage difference, including on ordinary rounds. The preview event also carries `waveSpawns`, the field's wave before bounties, the same copy the live boss start passes (GitHub #839).

### 35.65 CI: research-text paths and the golden list on a POSIX pipe

- **Seen:** GitHub CI on dev `23b484c2` failed in two places. windows-latest (Node 22 and 24) reported
  `public\js\ui\detailPanel.js:524` twice from `test/ui/versions.test.js` ("the client renders no research texts").
  ubuntu-latest Node 22, and only that job, stopped `act2autochess@V2_1`'s scenario list at `diy-103`; the committed
  corpus continues through `diy-126`. The same runner on Node 24, Windows on Node 22 and 24, and a local Node v22.22.0
  printed the full list. The child exited 0 in about 1.4 s, not the 120 s spawn limit.
- **Cause:** `path.relative` uses backslashes on Windows, so the allow-list (`public/js/ui/detailPanel.js`) missed 突变细胞's
  rule line. That line names `item.note` twice, which is why the failure listed it twice. The short golden list is not
  a missing file: `data/versions/` is fully tracked, a clean Windows checkout of the same commit printed every id, and
  `--list` does not read `data/local-assets.json`, `data/local-fx.json` or `.cache`. `golden --list` printed with
  `console.log` and then called `process.exit(0)`. On a POSIX pipe those writes are asynchronous, and `process.exit`
  drops whatever is still queued (Node's documented behaviour). The V2_1 list is the longest of the three (about 57 KiB);
  the two lists that passed on that job are shorter than the prefix that survived. Windows pipes write synchronously.
- **Now:** the scan stores paths with `/`. `--list` writes the whole list with a blocking `writeSync` before it exits.
  The test points the child's stdout and stderr at files (a file write is synchronous on every platform) and rejects a
  stdout that ends mid-line or a kit file that failed to load (`[content] failed to load`). The committed corpus is unchanged.
- **Scope:** no gameplay, no data change, no golden move, no new [ASSUMED] rule.

### 35.66 The LLM integration stays out of the release

Maintainer decision 2026-10-23: until LLM-played seats win 终极 close to 100%, the LLM integration must not appear in a release package. It stays on the development checkout.

- **What is held back:** the external-AI strategy and the advisor / plan mode (§35.8–§35.15, §35.62–§35.63): the server-side host, transports, observations and plan execution (`server/match/externalAI.js`, `externalConfig.js`, `aiDigest.js`, `server/ai/`), the example agent and its tools (`tools/ai/`), the example fixture `test/fixtures/llm-advisor.json`, and the strategy docs `docs/AI-STRATEGY.md`, `docs/EXTERNAL_AI.md`, `docs/EXTERNAL_AI_ZH.md`.
- **What still ships:** @HankDUMPLINGZhong's strategy framework (#673, §35.7) and the built-in bot, including the 终极 improvements (§35.62–§35.64). [ASSUMED] `server/match/aiPlan.js` and `server/match/aiEconomy.js` stay in the package. The built-in bot calls them on the ordinary path (`ownsElite` and the no-plan result); leaving them out would fork that bot. No host ships with them, so a release never attaches an external plan.
- **Gate:** `server/match/llmRelease.js` is the one exclusion list. `tools/package.mjs` refuses those paths and writes `server/release-without-llm` into every full, lite and update stage. The Docker build deletes the same files and writes the same marker after copying `server/`. `server/match/llmGate.js` treats the marker, or a missing `externalAI.js`, as off. A release server does not register `external`, maps that id back to `legacy`, and logs once: `[llm] external AI is development-only and is not in this release; ignoring the external-AI configuration and using the built-in bot`. Settings `SP_BOT_STRATEGY=external`, `SP_EXTERNAL_AI_*` and `SP_LLM_*` do not crash the process and do not open a connection. The shipped source has no import specifier that names an excluded file; the dev checkout loads the host through a computed URL and behaves as before.
- **Golden:** unchanged. The built-in bot's decisions are not edited.
- **Tests:** `test/package-llm-gate.test.js` (the release file list, the import graph, the marker, a packaged server booting an all-bot match with the external-AI variables set). The existing external-AI tests still pass on a dev checkout.

The README points to the complete and fast scenario lists. No simulation or corpus changes. Validation: both list commands succeed; docs consistency checks pass.

CI compares the complete committed corpus in a separate job; it never regenerates it. Python 3.8 setup documentation is retained. Validation: test/ci.test.js passes. Golden files unchanged.

Gate collection scans the full grid before filtering undrawn tiles; boss fields use AREAS.boss. This changes rendering only. Validation: test/render/board3d.test.js passes; golden unchanged.

### 35.70 Configurable shutdown deadline (PR #775, @kanomahoro1224)

The shutdown timeout accepts integer milliseconds from 1000 to 300000, default 5000. Successful close exits 0, timeout or a repeated signal exits 1. No save-stack changes. Validation: test/shutdown-timeout.test.js passes; golden unchanged.

### 35.71 Chunked connection heartbeats (PR #784, @kanomahoro1224)

Heartbeats stop on close, avoid overlapping sweeps, and recheck connection identity after yielding. Validation: test/net-heartbeat-chunked.test.js passes. No simulation or golden changes.

### 35.72 Optional online session cap (PR #760, @kanomahoro1224)

Only new sessions count against the optional cap. Version vote/retry settings remain in LOBBY_OPTION_KEYS. Validation: test/net-online-cap.test.js passes. Golden unchanged.

### 35.73 Last completed battle metrics (PR #820, @zasx77771)

The local metrics panel gains scope last; replacing a longer snapshot refreshes history order. No server advisor change. Validation: test/match/runner.test.js passes. No golden changes.

### 35.74 Four-star Theresa mote display (PR #576, @Convey123)

Only the four-star ring is rendered. Official orbit parameters are 1.15 tiles and 30 degrees per game second; the S2 radius of 2 tiles from outside_radius remains [ASSUMED]. Snapshot/interpolation wiring is display-only and the hidden five-star kit is untouched. Follow-up corrects speed, pause, y orientation and model-swap cleanup. Validation: mote and HUD suites, 19 tests pass; full golden comparison recorded in the batch report.

### 35.75 Replay every fuzz group by seed (PR #579, @Xinglan233)

Failure diagnostics retain seed, engine, mode, difficulty, seat counts and enabled rules. The four-rule group has separate replay names for fake and real engines; its default seed ranges remain 201–212 and 301–304. Single-seed replays skip the corpus-wide intent total. Validation: six controlled-fault/replay diagnostics pass; complete fuzz suite results are recorded in the batch report. No gameplay or golden changes.

### 35.76 Whole-row loadout and DIY selection (PR #610, @Flotiarenor)

Ported the row-anchored control guard to the current roster and shared DIY rows. Empty slots and padding select the operator; skill/module/cultivation controls retain their actions and narrow-screen quick choices remain in the list. The card view, personal skins, version-aware data and UI size rules remain. Validation: 72 unit checks pass; changed browser suites are recorded in the batch report. No gameplay or golden changes.

### 35.77 Scrollable current bond strip (PR #678, @resilenceljp)

Removed the fourteen-entry truncation and added focus, touch scrolling and arrows in every shipped translation pack. Current bond semantics and collapse behavior are preserved. Published 0.3.0 history is unchanged. Validation: bond unit suites and strict i18n checks; browser scroll suite recorded in the batch report. No simulation or golden changes.

### 35.78 Explicit extraction aliases (PR #708, @DuolaCCCCat)

Only enemy and token are aliases. fx never selects map/fx, and common never selects both ui/common and map/common. Nested selections retain their parent job. Validation: 12 local-extract tests pass. No official art, generated data or golden changes.

### 35.79 Context-preserving WebSocket compression (PR #721, @mxdyzmx)

The sending threshold is 1024 UTF-8 bytes. Clients requesting server_no_context_takeover retain per-message compression; clients limited below windowBits 13 receive plain frames. Per-connection zlib memory rises from approximately 72 KB to 105 KB, bounded by the configured connection cap. Validation: ws-compression suite passes, including RSV1, threshold boundaries, mixed streams and independent connections. No golden changes.

### 35.80 Owned vanilla data reuse (PR #722, @mxdyzmx)

Both reuse and reuseOwner must be functions; reuseOwner must return null before and after each retrieval. Without an owner hook reuse is ignored. The historical config.version marker and content-set cloning remain. Validation: nine loader tests pass, including missing owner, ownership switches, localization and retry. No gameplay or golden changes.

### 35.81 Tick scratch preserves all version corpora (PR #723, @mxdyzmx)

Scratch buffers reduce allocations only. Buff removal retains its identity snapshot; nested hook, buff and scheduled callbacks have distinct buffers cleared in finally. [ASSUMED] allocation follows actual nesting depth under the existing recursion guard. Validation: seven scratch tests pass; all six families match in the default (288), act1autochess (220), act1autochess@V1_1 (218), and act2autochess@V2_1 (269) corpora. No golden files regenerated.

Birth UnitInfo seeds _rateSent at millisecond precision. ### 35.83 Optional bot strategies (PR #742, @Lidong1793)

Three strategies remain opt-in. Framework: @HankDUMPLINGZhong; strategy author: @Lidong1793. Preserve llmGate, package exclusions, legacy spendDown and its yield boundaries, Final Assault cap and version-aware delegation. [ASSUMED] Ioleta owns its refresh budget and omits the legacy expiring-funds sweep; adaptive retains it. Sources: existing bot rules and author research summary; benchmark claims were not independently remeasured. No intended default gameplay or golden moves.

### 35.84 SilverAsh S3 air targeting (PR #749, @Misyra)

Source: [PRTS 凛御银灰](https://prts.wiki/w/凛御银灰), S3 notes explicitly allow air targets (checked 2026-10-24). Add air profile to skill targeting and the existing trigger-range API. [ASSUMED] Activation uses skill grid plus permanent range extension, not the whole projectile path. Published 0.3.1 history and incoming old golden are discarded; regenerated corpus consequences below.

Golden regenerated in all four corpora. Only the following scenarios move because S3 now starts against flyers and damages them; both act1 corpora are unchanged.
- `test/golden/act2autochess-v2_1/roster.json` (2): `roster-043`, `roster-044`. Fields: enemies, events, fx, hooks, killed, players, rngDraws, snaps, ticks, time, units.
- `test/golden/roster.json` (1): `roster-043`. Fields: enemies, events, fx, hooks, killed, players, rngDraws, snaps, ticks, time, units.

### 35.85 Opt-in pool and loop sampler (PR #750, @kanomahoro1224)

Pool by @xinhai-ai (author permission recorded in #750), integration by @kanomahoro1224. SP_WORKERS remains unset/off by default; SP_LOOP_SAMPLE=1 is required for the local sampler. Actual room phases and pending rehearsals are read per sample, including the first window. Preserve M1 version sets/hosts and their shutdown. Production rehearsal submission is connected by #756. No gameplay or golden moves; operational limits retain the author tests.

### 35.86 Connect the rehearsal worker pool (PR #756, @kanomahoro1224)

Production startServer passes one RoomWorkerPool to default-version vanilla matches; its submit forwards to the bounded pool. Tests exercise that exact wrapper through a real lobby and Match and observe submitted/completed/accepted counters. Plugin and historical matches are excluded before host creation and serialization. BattleSpecs remove live sharedBoss objects; workers construct candidates lazily and preserve cap/adaptive limits. The shared prep executor forwards pooled, so all three opt-in strategies and legacy reach the same path. Settled/cancelled work releases room bookkeeping. Sources: existing rehearsal/spec contracts and #750/#756; no new gameplay assumptions or intended golden changes. Verification: 28 pool/prep/adaptive tests pass, real startup passes, 36 regression tests pass (versions, sampler, real pool and Boss limits); initial version-key expectation was corrected to test the default-off case.

### 35.87 Document the connected rehearsal pool (PR #763, @kanomahoro1224)

Architecture §7 follows the verified #756 path: serialized BattleSpecs, lazy construction, cap/limits, default-version vanilla-only submissions, cancellation at prep end and local fallback on refusal/error. Correct the sampler to opt-in, distinguish rehearsal pool from plugin/version match-host transports, and retain existing browser data-reuse ownership notes. No gameplay/golden changes.

### 35.88 Deployment capacity and independent grace clocks (PR #762, @kanomahoro1224)

Only the deployment guidance is new; #760 implementation was already integrated. Capacity is §12 after the existing §11 official-version guidance. Distinguish lobbyGraceMs from the in-match reconnect window; room quota is released only on actual disposal. Sources: current net/lobby limits; no new assumptions or golden moves.

### 35.89 Negotiated public-state deltas (PR #772, @kanomahoro1224)

This is the sole delta tip; #751/#771 are carried as ancestors, not separately integrated. Preserve M1 version proposal/vote/cancel messages and heavy-intent budgets, versionHosts, requireCs, soloUnlimited and the gated rehearsal pool. Browser hello retains noReplace/claimAt. Same-socket room changes clear sender and receiver baselines while sender sequence stays monotonic; repeated hello in the same room retains them. Bad JSON falls back to ordinary m.public and does not commit or advance StateReceiver. Replays retain full frames; unnegotiated clients retain ordinary m.public/m.private; only m.public is delta-enabled. Sources: existing protocol/resync contract; no gameplay assumptions. Tests: 715 targeted assertions pass; live-socket and browser-Net room-switch tests pass. Full default golden verification follows.

Verification: `# tests 715; # pass 715; # fail 0`; socket/Net E2E `# tests 6; # pass 6; # fail 0`; docs 32 pass; default golden all 288 scenarios match.

### 35.90 Front fallback for missing back skill clips (PR #786, @xcdoge)

Merge into current _modelDirty/setSkill and attack-rate renderer. Front-only skill clips retain their full visible window, including late model loads and End; Back-authored skills keep their model. [ASSUMED] Front selection is inferred from official animation roles, not a simulation-facing change. Pause leaves the hold timer unchanged. Published 0.3.1 history is preserved. Sources: official asset animation roles and #785 report. No golden moves.

S3 overrides trigger to DEFAULT, including at full HP; rawRule remains the official TAKE_DAMAGE guardian row. Other guardian skills retain the class rule. The attack core and healing settlement remain current. No new [ASSUMED] point; existing range/target selection is reused. Incoming old goldens discarded; all four corpora regenerated below.

### 35.100 Default-off spectator AI takeover (PR #691, @resilenceljp)

维护者 2026-10-24 明确接受本作自有协作规则 [ASSUMED]：房主仅在开局前开启，默认关闭，五语言设置与提示齐全。整间房在线且未托管真人过半同意/拒绝；无合格投票人才由在线房主决定。申请 30 秒、绑定对局/回合/选民，资格或阶段变化取消。只允许默认版本原版合作房；插件与历史版本隔离对局不支持。继承 AI playerId、资金、阵容和库存，保留观战者 token；旧 AI 预演取消，合成 welcome 保留已协商的 delta 版本并全量重同步。继承记录标注回合与旧 AI 数据，不计个人统计、称号、奖杯或奖励。默认 room.state 不增加键，普通模拟黄金不变。来源为维护者决定与 PR #691，所有协作/归属政策均 [ASSUMED]，非官方规则。

验证：接替与统计/文档测试 107 pass；默认关闭、房主权限、隔离版本/插件拒绝、同 socket delta 与席位预演取消回归 24 pass；真实浏览器申请/多数批准/刷新/记录、房主设置开关 2 pass；五语言 2334/2334 msgids，类型检查通过，定向 lint 0 errors（既有 seed 警告 1）。

### 35.101 Last attack interval in a batched event frame (PR #729 follow-up)

Final audit found that multiple rate updates without an intervening attack retained the first value and discarded later changes. The renderer now coalesces to the last interval per unit, even when no attack appears in that batch. [ASSUMED] b.ev has one timestamp for its drained batch (potentially several simulation ticks), so that timestamp uses its latest interval; it never borrows from a later timestamp. Spawn/skill/attack order is preserved. This corrects §35.82’s wording about individual ticks; no simulation or golden change. Regression tests cover batches with and without an attack.

### 35.102 Local CI guard dispatcher (PR #836 follow-up)

The first full CI run passed all 10,129 executed tests (27 reported skips), lint, imports and types, but failed at guards because the incoming PR declared the step without adding its runStep branch. The driver now dispatches all three guard files and routes the full test step through tools/test-run.mjs with SHARD/JUNIT removed. A dispatcher regression checks the executed arguments against each declared command; the complete CI is rerun below. No gameplay or golden change.

### 35.104 setup --game accepts an install directory (#572)

- **Seen:** `--game` was inspected as an AssetBundle root. An install directory (the folder that contains `Arknights.exe` or `Arknights_Data`, including the launcher's `Arknights Game` name) was reported as missing the mode's bundles. Auto-search did not look for `Arknights Game`. A successful extract wrote `localExtractedFrom` and the next lookup ignored it. `extract.py` kept only two `C:\Program Files` candidates.
- **Now:** `tools/setup.mjs` and `tools/local-extract/extract.py` both read `tools/local-extract/client-candidates.json`. A directory that is already the AssetBundle root is unchanged. A directory with `Arknights.exe` or `Arknights_Data` resolves to `Arknights_Data/StreamingAssets/AB/Windows`; a PlayCover container resolves to `Documents/Bundles`. The resolved path is what gets saved, and the next `findClient()` with no `--game` tries it first while it still exists. Windows search adds `Arknights Game` beside `Arknights` on every existing base, drives C–H. `enemy_scales.py` and `enemy_model_offsets.py` use the same resolver.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a parent of the install is searched one level down (named `Arknights` / `Arknights Game`, then any other child with the install marker). A path inside the install walks up at most eight levels to the directory that has the marker. No gameplay rule is involved.
- **Tests:** `test/setup-game-dir.test.js` (install directory, parent, AssetBundle root, PlayCover, remembered path, and the same Windows list from Python). No golden change.

#### #572 回归检查：FX 编码测试的客户端目录

`test/fx-texture-format.test.js` 的 CLI 用例原先把没有客户端标记的临时目录传给 `--game`；新增的路径解析因此在调用 FX 导出前返回 2，触发 `extract.main() == 0` 断言。测试现补建 `ui/autochess` 标记，与其他模拟客户端的提取测试一致；保留所有 WebP 字节一致性、PNG 混合清单及默认编码断言。生产解析逻辑不变，安装目录、非默认位置与已记住路径的支持保留；无新增 [ASSUMED] 点，无玩法或黄金变化。

验证（macOS，Node 22.22.0）：FX 编码、#572 路径、提取/setup 及文档的 9 个定向测试文件为 `113 tests / 112 pass / 0 fail / 1 skipped`（跳过旧版 projectile 索引检查）。`node tools/ci.mjs --keep-going` 运行一次，七步骤全部通过：完整测试 `10172 tests / 10145 pass / 0 fail / 27 skipped`，独立 guards 为 `28 pass`，lint 为 `0 errors / 145 warnings`，无失败需要单独重跑。`node tools/golden.mjs` 默认版本完整 288 场景全部匹配，未更新任何黄金文件。

### 35.105 One simulation Worker per plugin content set (#864)

- **Seen:** @1nsaneeee's #864. `prepare` built a new `WorkerSimHost` and `load`ed the whole content set every round. `clear` (the next prep, not only leaving the room) called `stop` on it. The worker runtime accepts `load` once, so the next round could not reuse the process. A `b.result` that was still pending or undelivered lived only on the entry map, which `clear` dropped.
- **Now:** one remote host stays parked for the content set it loaded. The next round of that set calls `start` only. `WorkerSimHost.start` drops the previous battle's events and stat cache after the new battle is built, and ignores stat replies from the one before. The parked host stops when the content set changes, the public match is gone, the phase is `LOBBY`, the worker fails, or `dispose` runs. `clear` does not stop a host that already started a battle and is still healthy. A pending or undelivered result stays in a side list that `redeliver` still walks.
- **[ASSUMED]:** two fields live at the same time still get two workers. Only one idle host is kept per content set; the extra is stopped at `clear`. An unfinished remote battle is not `forceEnd`ed on `clear` — the server already takes that field. No step result changes because of the lifecycle itself.
- **Tests:** `test/match/sim-host.test.js` (one load and two starts on a real worker; clear parks; content change, failure and lobby stop; undelivered result after clear; `start` drops stale events). `test/match/runner.test.js` still passes. No golden change.

### 35.106 信仰搅拌机 S3 is planned on its two-side range (#31)

- **Seen:** The default skill 退休前布道 (`skchr_rmixer_3`) widens to both sides. The planner scored the base line of three tiles. Healers standing in the road, and 古米 / 角峰 holding the gate, are placement quality and are unchanged.
- **Cause:** `attackRangeGrid` replaces the grid only for `passiveRange` or the line 「被动效果：攻击范围扩大」. This skill's text is 「攻击范围扩大」 without that phrase, so the deploy overlay correctly keeps the base line. `rangeRec` used that overlay for scoring and facing. The range cache key read `loadout.skill` / `loadout.module`, which `loadoutFor` does not set (`skillIndex` / `moduleId`), so a later skill change reused the first grid.
- **Now:** When the resolved skill id is `skchr_rmixer_3` and that skill has a `rangeGrid`, `rangeRec` uses it as-is. SPT-Y's 攻击距离 +1 does not add tiles (PRTS 备注: 此技能的攻击范围不受「攻击距离」属性影响; the kit already sets `targeting.noRangeExtend`). S1 and S2 still go through `attackRangeGrid`, so S1 with SPT-Y is still the line plus one. The cache key uses `skillIndex` and `moduleId`.
- **[ASSUMED]:** The skill is ammo and is not up for the whole fight. Scoring and facing still use the skill-on grid for the deployment. The deploy overlay, the card and `summonRange` stay on the base line.
- **Tests:** `test/match/bot-smarter.test.js` (default and explicit S3 include `[1,1]`, `[-1,1]` and `[0,3]`; S1 and S2 do not; SPT-Y adds no further cell on S3 and still adds `[0,3]` on S1). The existing SPT-Y length check still passes (8 tiles is longer than the base line of 3).
- **Golden:** battle families are unchanged (default roster 49, bonds 46, fields 22, standins 10, diy 140). Only full matches move, because the planner's cover changed. `node tools/golden.mjs --jobs 4` then `--family matches --update`, and the same for `act2autochess@V2_1`, `act1autochess` and `act1autochess@V1_1`.
  - `test/golden/matches.json` (6 of 21): `solo-HARD-2`, `solo-ABYSS-2`, `coop2-NORMAL-3`, `coop3-ABYSS-5`, `coop4-FUNNY-6`, `coop2-NORMAL-14-standins`.
  - `test/golden/act2autochess-v2_1/matches.json` (5 of 16): `solo-HARD-1`, `coop2-NORMAL-3`, `coop2-NORMAL-14-standins`, `coop2-NORMAL-1-coverage-standins`, `coop2-NORMAL-1-unowned-roster`.
  - `test/golden/act1autochess/matches.json` (1 of 16): `solo-NORMAL-2`.
  - `test/golden/act1autochess-v1_1/matches.json` (3 of 16): `solo-HARD-2`, `coop2-NORMAL-14-standins`, `coop2-NORMAL-70-diy`.

### 35.107 血珀 loses 10% max HP per second (#720)

- **Seen:** @FrogThai's #720. 血珀 (`enemy_1367_dseed`) stood forever after 萨卡兹骸骨拷打者 died. PRTS 天赋: 自缚，免疫失衡，不进行攻击，无法被阻挡，嘲讽等级 −2，0% 庇护; 「场上不存在唤血祭坛/沥血王座时，每秒流失最大生命值 10% 的生命」. The pulse line is 「受唤血祭坛脉冲波影响时，覆盖 1 次唤血祭坛的召唤效果，随后自身退场」 (the handbook ability is 「在祭坛处重生为新生血裔」). This mode has neither device.
- **Cause:** the enemy was stats-only. `dmgType` none already skips attacks, and the torturer's death still spawns two with `countInTotal: false` on a stay route. Nothing read `Passive.hp_ratio`.
- **Now:** the kit is 自缚 (`selfBound` + `noMove`), 失衡免疫 (`noDisplace`), 无法被阻挡, and no attack. Every second it loses `Passive.hp_ratio` (0.1) of max HP as true 流失 (`loseHp`, ignores DEF 3000 / RES 90) and can die. The first loss is at 1 s; 3000 HP is gone at 10 s. A ratio of 0 would lose nothing. `Passive.damage_resistance` 0 is unused. The altar pulse is not modelled. The torturer's two stay uncounted, so their deaths do not change `killed` or `total`. A counted extra spawn (the roster) dies in place instead of walking off.
- **[ASSUMED]:** none. The 10% and the flags are the PRTS talent and `Passive.hp_ratio`. The pulse is omitted because this mode has no 唤血祭坛.
- **Tests:** `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (the torturer still leaves 2 uncounted 血珀 where it fell; they bleed out without moving and without a kill; a placed 血珀 loses nothing in the first 0.5 s, 10% at 1 s, dies at 10 s, a push does not move it, and it never attacks). The same `Passive.hp_ratio` 0.1 / `damage_resistance` 0 / max HP 3000 is on all four enemy tables.
- **Golden:** bonds, fields, matches, standins and diy are unchanged on every corpus (a torturer's leftovers are uncounted). Only roster battles move, because they extra-spawn every non-leader kind, 血珀 included, and that copy is counted. `node tools/golden.mjs --jobs 4` then `--family roster --update`, and the same `--family roster` update for each historical version that differed.
  - `test/golden/roster.json` (3 of 49): `roster-018`, `roster-019`, `roster-040`.
  - `test/golden/act2autochess-v2_1/roster.json` (2 of 49): `roster-011`, `roster-022`.
  - `test/golden/act1autochess/roster.json` (3 of 41): `roster-005`, `roster-034`, `roster-041`.
  - `test/golden/act1autochess-v1_1/roster.json` (3 of 41): `roster-005`, `roster-034`, `roster-041`.
  - First consequences: `roster-018` `players.p1.dmg` 1552725 → 1551729 (snaps share `18ad72a1 484ec177 91c84313 38eb29d1 e3b68649`, then `c3746da1` → `86e31cf0`); `roster-019` `time` 197.23 → 196.43 and `players.p1.deaths` 0 → 1; `roster-040` `killed` 33 → 34 and `players.p1.leaks` 32 → 31. `act2autochess@V2_1` `roster-011` `rngDraws` 232 → 169 (`players.p1.deaths` 10 → 15); `roster-022` `rngDraws` 47 → 62 (`players.p1.dmg` 352066 → 350532). `act1autochess` `roster-005` `players.p1.dmg` 329658 → 327979; `roster-034` `time` 168.47 → 185; `roster-041` `killed` 13 → 14 and `enemies.enemy_1367_dseed.killed` 0 → 1 (`players.p1.leaks` 33 → 32). `act1autochess@V1_1` `roster-005` `players.p1.dmg` 308834 → 305504; `roster-034` `time` 168.47 → 185; `roster-041` `killed` 13 → 14 and `enemies.enemy_1367_dseed.killed` 0 → 1 (`players.p1.leaks` 33 → 32).

### 35.108 心虚设计师 faces its blocker while blocked (#869)

- **Seen:** @YinqiMargatroid's #869. 心虚设计师 (`enemy_10097_crshd`) keeps an 80% physical and arts cut on its front (PRTS 天赋, `weakness.damage_resistance` 0.8). While a defender stood behind a leftward walk, the cut stayed on the old walk side. Measured at DEF 450 / RES 30 / ATK 1000: the front is about 110 physical and 140 arts, the back about 550 and 700.
- **Cause:** `frontGuard` updated `a.facing` only from horizontal movement (`faceMove`). A block stops that movement, so the sign froze. The enemy has no normal attack, and the model turns from velocity, which is also ~0 while blocked. `Unit.dir` stayed at its default, so the published facing did not match the cut.
- **Now:** while `blockedBy` is alive and off the same column, the front is that side (`blocker.x − enemy.x`). Otherwise it still follows the walk. The same sign is written to `dir` and to `b.snap` `face` (`[[id, ±1]]`, only this enemy, only while it is alive and deployed). The client applies `face` to the model when the view is not sliding. The nine-field unit tuple and the golden canon do not include it. Unblocked, a leftward walk is still the front.
- **[ASSUMED]:** a blocker on the same column keeps the last sign. PRTS names the front and does not state a tie.
- **Tests:** `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (unblocked walk: facing −1, left 110 / right 550; blocked from behind: facing +1, right 110 / 140 and left 550 / 700, and `snapshot().face` is `[id, 1]`; the same column keeps +1). `test/render/interp.test.js` (the list keeps ±1 and `sample()` uses the older snapshot). `test/render/unitview.test.js` (a standing enemy with `face: 1` turns right; a slide ignores it). The older front-cut test (walk left, 圆仔 faces the crowd) still passes.
- **Golden:** the default corpus is unchanged (`node tools/golden.mjs --jobs 4`: roster 49, bonds 46, fields 22, matches 21, standins 10, diy 140). `act1autochess` and `act1autochess@V1_1` match in full. On `act2autochess@V2_1`, bonds 46, fields 22, matches 16, standins 10 and diy 126 match. Only `roster-014` moves: a blocker there stands off the walk direction, so the front cut follows it. `face` is outside the golden canon. Updated with `node tools/golden.mjs --version act2autochess@V2_1 --family roster --only roster-014 --update`, then the roster family matched (49).
  - `test/golden/act2autochess-v2_1/roster.json` (1 of 49): `roster-014`.
  - First consequences: `units[p1#4:chess_char_5_13_a].kills` 5 → 6 (`dmg` 83703 → 82467); `units[p1#10:chess_char_5_15_a].kills` 16 → 15 (`dmg` 285835 → 286516); snaps share `2bcbdbc0 627e7253 a4b0832f 19e3ca31 8998fc77 46f7e0aa`, then `1fc13ca9` → `bc6921f9`.

### 35.109 历史版本的官方资源并集与本地补提取

- **问题：** 资源生成器只读取今日数据，第一期独有的 19 名干员、六种敌人及森蚺策略没有清单条目；大厅和战斗无法请求对应资源。
- **改动：** `tools/assets/versions.mjs` 按 charId／enemyKey／iconId 合并四版素材需求，避免同一 chessId 跨期指向不同干员。`fetch-assets` 覆盖自选、补位、召唤物、技能、模组、装备、盟约和策略；`--add-only` 保留既有字段。`extract.py --only versions` 从客户端暂存提取，只补不存在的文件；常规本地提取包含六种旧敌人。FX 从四版数据读取单位，`--fx-add-only` 合并新单位与特效并保留原记录。
- **来源与结果：** 维护者本地 Windows 官方客户端 `26-09-13-15-28-10-30688348`；缺失方向同时核对 iOS PlayCover 客户端。新增 76 张基础／精二头像和半身像、37 个干员方向模型（111 文件）、六个敌人模型（18 文件）及六张图标、32 张技能图标、一张森蚺策略图标；新增 25 个 FX 单位、153 个特效 prefab。海蒂在两端都只有 Front，与吟游者的单向模型一致；没有用其他干员的 Back 代替。所有官方文件仍在 gitignored 的本地资源树。
- **边界：** 今日 210 名干员、252 个敌人等所有既有清单字段逐项保持不变；仅增加新实体和清单 hash／stats。现有整幅立绘、时装属于外观插件，历史版本沿用这一规则。旧 FX 投射物记录也保留；新增记录以 `rolesVersion: 1` 区分，混合索引的旧记录继续由运行时读取 behaviours。
- **效果映射：** 万顷一技能的记录为通用 `skcom_assist_cost[3]`，官方角色表的 `overridePrefabKey` 明确指向 `skchr_wanqin_1`。提取器按该字段写入单位内技能别名，不覆盖其他干员的同名通用技能，也不改玩法数据。
- **验证：** `tools/assets/coverage.mjs` 枚举所有棋子、干员、召唤物、敌人、装备、策略、盟约、首领和场地；`--files --fx` 核对实际文件和特效索引。回归测试删除历史独有图像／模型／图标时必须失败。真实浏览器分别进入三版大厅各入口、对局和结算；固定渲染场景补足随机对局无法覆盖的旧干员、六种敌人和召唤物。完整逐项清单和截图交接在 round52。
- **[ASSUMED]：** 无新增玩法假设；沿用今日资源的明确降级规则。只补资源，不改战斗逻辑或 golden。

`fetch-assets --add-only --strict` 下载 1,144 个缺失文件（28,948,723 bytes），但因 284 个计划叶子没有可用来源退出 1：EN/KR 配音各 140 项、敌人图标 1 项、召唤物头像 3 项；requiredMisses、downloadErrors、fontErrors 均为 0。再生成候选给六个历史敌人新增远端 `spine`、给 19 名旧干员补配音及相关音效，未覆盖已有字段；然而 `build-data` 读取 `spine` 生成 attackAnim，导致第一期上下半的离线数据重建校验失败（120/122，通过的默认数据重建保持不变）。这是超出本次测试打包的玩法数据联动，不能仅提交新素材清单而保留不一致的历史数据，也不为出包改黄金。

因此保存候选与完整报告到 round52，恢复本次进入预检前的已提交清单，继续使用 §35.109 已验证的本地历史模型与资源；未改生成数据或删除任何已有资源。保留清单下同六文件复查 122/122。严格预检的 284 项缺失仍作为发行余项记录，不能称作严格素材全通过；四版本、19 干员与安装包内清单所引用文件另外逐项验收。

### 35.111 版本胶囊与难度胶囊等高（fix53）

- **问题与依据：** 维护者测试截图 shot5 中，第一期上半版本胶囊明显小于相邻的绝境模拟胶囊。
  当前浏览器复现：1992×1248 下难度按钮约 53.45 px，版本按钮约 24.89 px。两者虽都写 `height: 78%`，
  版本按钮多了一层自动高度的 Tooltip，百分比高度因此退回内容高度；字号、内边距也各自定义。
- **修改：** Tooltip 明确继承外层高度；两个按钮共用高度、内边距、字号、字重、圆角及手机字号规则。
  两者垂直居中；保留窄屏版本名省略和完整可访问名称，放宽手机版本宽度使常规中文期次完整显示，整组宽度
  仍限制在底栏内。版本入口复用项目 `Icon book`（房间底部、同盟设置和大厅一致），
  不新增外部资源；大厅保持自己的顶部工具条尺寸，触屏 40 px 下限随 `--sp-ui` 缩放并保留 32 px 最低点击区。
- **[ASSUMED]：** 以现有书册图标表示归档的活动期次，属于界面图标选择；胶囊等大来自维护者的明确要求。
- **验证：** `version-pills.e2e.test.js` 使用 `test/e2e/text-fit.mjs`，检查五语言、四档文字大小、
  小字时 75/80/85/90/95/100% 界面大小、四种横屏手机和两种桌面，共 540 个大厅／房间布局组合；
  对比两个按钮实测高度、内边距、字号、圆角，检查文字和点击命中。中及以上按现有设置规则使用 100%。
- **黄金：** 不改模拟或生成数据；无场景应变动。前后截图及实测记录放在本轮 handoff/round52/fix53/，不入库。

### 35.112 S.W.E.E.P. 等待所选版本的校验结果（fix53）

- **已复现的根因：** `useData` 只表示文件已结束加载，不表示文件属于当前选择的版本。
  `content.js` 校验新版本期间会保留上一版本的文件。冷缓存、CDP 网络限速，并延迟新版本的 `bands.json`
  响应时，切到第二期上半后立刻打开策略报告，旧代码仍显示第二期下半的 40 张策略卡；新增断言实测失败
  （预期读取状态且 0 张卡，实际无读取状态且 40 张卡）。这与 §35.59 的订阅时机修复是两个问题。
- **修改：** 报告订阅所选版本和内容集状态，同时要求已校验的页面版本一致；等待期间显示现有读取文案，
  校验失败显示现有失败文案及重试按钮。成功后正常展示该版本的全部列表，不改变 `data.js` 的共享加载契约。
- **回归：** `sweep.e2e.test.js` 连接真实服务器和 Chrome，关闭 HTTP 缓存，使用 CDP 120 ms 延迟、
  1 MiB/s 下载限速；四版本共 64 次打开、192 次页签检查，每次核对各分组数量及首末卡片的点击和详情，
  并在每个版本中加入另一版本房间后离开恢复。延迟一个真实版本响应使加载窗口确定可测；另注入一次
  HTTP 200 但校验错误的文件，验证报告内重试恢复。控制台和数据请求状态随截图留档。
- **调查边界：** 维护者 shot2–shot4 的大片空白、半行领袖、附加盟约不可见，尚未在此机复现，不能据此
  宣称上述修复已消除截图中的绘制问题。额外调查包括初始 55 次打开、四版本冷缓存限速各 15 次打开，以及
  2× 像素密度和 6× CPU 降速，另在有窗口的 Chrome 和 Edge 中按同样条件检查四版本；未见列表缺失或控制台错误。
  未据推测改动懒加载、列表布局或绘制层级。
  若原现象仍存在，需要原浏览器／设备及空白时的 DOM、控制台记录继续定位。
- **来源与 [ASSUMED]：** 依据维护者截图、当前 `data.js`/`content.js` 的契约和上述浏览器复现；没有新增玩法假设。
  不改官方数据和模拟；黄金场景无变动。

### 35.113 S.W.E.E.P. 内容不再等待入场动画（fix53b）

- **确定复现：** 在真实服务器页面中先等大厅入场结束，再用 CDP `Animation.setPlaybackRate(0)` 打开报告，
  `.sw` 和 `.sw-detail` 的计算透明度为 0；DOM、标题、卡片数量和尺寸均存在，单纯检查元素存在或点击命中不能
  发现问题。另一组直接对 Web Animations 执行 `pause()` 并设 `currentTime = 0`，同样失败。
  Chromium 与 Playwright WebKit 单独暂停详情动画时，三页签均出现左侧整块空白，右侧保持透明度 1。
  同一已就绪数据集的通知保留 DOM 节点与动画对象，未发现普通重渲染反复重启动画；切页、换选中项会重挂详情，
  重新进入透明的首帧。
- **根因与修改：** `sw-in` 首帧为 `opacity: 0`，用于报告、敌方图鉴、报告详情及图鉴详情；虽都写了
  `backwards`，实际没有延迟，暂停在活动区间的首帧也足以隐藏内容，改成 `both` 或去掉填充模式不能解决。
  外层共享 `.modal` 的 `modal-fade` 也有透明首帧。移除上述内容入场动画，两个 Modal 通过既有 `veilClass`
  接口使用仅作用于本报告的 `sw-veil`，取消外层淡入；不改其他 Modal。保留无内容遮挡作用的 `sw-mist`
  装饰漂移和卡片 hover 位移；减少动态效果时两者仍关闭。卡片和右侧分组原本没有入场动画或分批延迟。
- **边界：** 本次可确定解决暂停动画造成的透明报告、详情和图鉴；未复现维护者 shot2–shot4 右侧列表的
  锐利水平截断。单独暂停左侧不能解释右侧，暂停整个报告则连顶栏一起隐藏，与原截图也不完全相同。
  Chromium、WebKit 的切页及另开页后返回检查没有产生该截断；这不等于覆盖实际 macOS 窗口遮挡或低电量模式。
  不将推测写成该截图的完整根因，也不据此改动列表布局或共享数据加载。
- **回归：** `test/ui/sweep.e2e.test.js` 在 CDP 冻结、Web Animations 暂停、禁用动画和减少动态效果四种条件下，
  检查三页签所有卡片、祖先透明度、详情、首末选中项的点击命中、数据通知后的节点及动画对象保留、图鉴打开与
  敌人切换、Esc 返回。同一测试修复前两种暂停条件失败，禁用／减少动态效果通过；修复后全部通过。
  已有四版本慢网络、读取／失败／重试及 64 次打开检查继续保留，不再依靠等 160 ms 淡入后才验收详情。
- **来源与 [ASSUMED]：** 维护者的动画排查要求、上述实际浏览器复现及当前 CSS／组件生命周期。
  没有新增玩法或无来源数值假设；默认版本数据、战斗模拟和黄金场景均不变。当前契约见 `docs/design/client.md`，
  文件入口见 `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`。

其他 `backwards` 入场效果的源码审计（本次只改 S.W.E.E.P.，不宣称其他页面也已加固）：

| 文件 | 内容容器或可见内容的入场效果 |
|---|---|
| `public/css/theme.css` | 通用 `.screen` 的 `screen-in` 透明淡入 |
| `public/css/screens/title.css` | `.emblem`、标题、标语、登录区及页脚的 `rise-in`；另有装饰山脊淡入 |
| `public/css/screens/lobby.css` | 大厅顶栏、中央、两侧和模式入口；模拟协议／同盟方式选择的顶栏、标题、场景、难度卡、创建／加入卡、提示与页脚；另有背景、场景图形及选中印章。`tr-veil` 只改背景色，本身不隐藏子内容 |
| `public/css/screens/room.css` | 座位卡、顶栏、简报及页脚；另有背景与准备状态反馈 |
| `public/css/screens/loadout.css` | 调配整页、弹出详情、快捷栏、抽屉、卡片模式顶栏／分组／底栏及持有／自选区域；另有背景 |
| `public/css/screens/stats.css` | 统计整页、成就弹层及其滚动内容子项 |
| `public/css/screens/briefing.css` | 简报／轮选标题、简报分组及其中盟约／禁用干员、底部按钮、开始提示 |
| `public/css/screens/draft.css` | 选择顺序栏及卡片、策略卡、详情及详情正文、完成提示、轮到自己提示；另有选中、已选和状态反馈 |
| `public/css/screens/result.css` | 结局玩家卡片 `card-in` |
| `public/css/screens/match-popups.css` | `.mpop__plate` 的 `mpop-in`；`.mpop__old` 是旧内容离场，不是入场读取屏障 |
| `public/css/components.css` | 阶段胶囊 `pb-open`／`pb-wipe`、回合提示 `rd-open`／`rd-wipe`；另有装饰线和刻意延迟的弱连接提示。共享 `.modal`／`.modal__box` 虽不带 `backwards`，透明首帧同样受暂停影响，报告已局部取消 |

透明度 0、缩放到 0 或完全裁剪的首帧都可能把内容留在不可见状态；填充模式本身不是是否安全的充分条件。
